### [1] [Exact Mixed-Integer Conic Liftings for Queueing-Based CDN Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30491)
*Víctor Blanco,Miguel Martínez-Antón*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于混合整数锥提升的框架，将排队论中的拥塞效应（响应时间）显式纳入内容分发网络设计，通过McCormick包络和二阶锥规划精确重构非线性表达式，同时优化服务器位置、容量分配和路由决策，实验表明拥塞感知建模对网络设计有显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统内容分发网络设计依赖距离近似，忽略了服务过程中的拥塞效应和随机动态，导致模型与实际性能偏差较大。本文旨在将排队论中的响应时间显式建模到网络设计中，以更准确地反映真实服务性能。

Method: 提出混合整数锥提升框架，结合McCormick型包络和二阶锥表示，将涉及仿射映射比值、倒数项和多类流量交互的非线性表达式精确重构为可处理的混合整数锥规划问题。从单服务器场景（与经典Fermat-Weber问题关联）扩展到多服务器场景，联合优化服务器位置、容量分配和路由决策。

Result: 在真实案例实验中，拥塞感知建模显著改变了网络设计方案，且所提出的混合整数锥规划公式在使用现代锥优化求解器时表现出良好的计算效率。

Conclusion: 所提框架能够将排队论中的拥塞效应与网络设计中的几何决策和随机服务动态统一在同一优化范式内，为更真实的内容分发网络设计提供了可行的求解途径。

Abstract: We study a class of content delivery network design problems in which service performance is explicitly modeled through queueing-based response times. In contrast to standard formulations relying on distance-based approximations, the resulting models incorporate congestion effects and give rise to nonlinear expressions involving ratios of affine mappings, reciprocal terms, and interactions across multiple traffic classes. To address these challenges, we develop a systematic framework based on mixed-integer conic liftings that enables exact reformulations of such expressions within a tractable optimization paradigm. The proposed approach combines McCormick-type envelopes and second-order cone representations to derive mixed-integer conic programming formulations that jointly capture geometric design decisions and stochastic service dynamics within the same system. The framework is first introduced in a single-server setting, where the optimal solution exhibits a connection with the classical Fermat--Weber problem, and is then extended to the multi-server case, leading to a general formulation that simultaneously optimizes server locations, capacity allocation, and routing decisions. Computational experiments on a realistic case study illustrate the impact of congestion-aware modeling on network design and demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed formulations when solved using modern conic optimization solvers.

</details>


### [2] [A Parameter-Free Restart Scheme with Only a Parallelizable $\log\log(1/ε)$ Overhead](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30502)
*Yue Wu,Benjamin Grimmer*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种无参数的重启策略，通过并行实例通信避免传统重启方法的三大缺陷，在亚线性加速率下仅需O(log log(1/ε))个并行进程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有重启方法要么需要不切实际的问题先验知识，要么在预言机复杂度上产生对数开销因子，要么存在非平凡的初始预热阶段，本文旨在消除这三个缺陷。

Method: 采用Renegar和Grimmer风格的并行实例通信机制，动态部署多个一阶方法实例，通过进程间通信实现参数无关的自适应重启。

Result: 所提方案避免了昂贵的预热阶段，在亚线性加速率场景下仅需O(log log(1/ε))个并行进程即可实现加速。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一种无参数、无预热开销、无对数因子负担的通用重启框架，可有效加速一阶方法在具有增长结构问题上的收敛。

Abstract: It is well-known that first-order methods can offer accelerated convergence rates in the presence of growth structures. Restarting schemes provide a general tool for such speed-ups. These schemes typically either require unrealistic problem knowledge, incur logarithmic overhead factors in oracle complexity, and/or have a nontrivial initial burn-in phase. We present a parameter-free approach for restarting any first-order method, avoiding these three drawbacks. Our approach dynamically deploys parallel instances of a given first-order method communicating progress in the style of Renegar and Grimmer. Our optimized scheme avoids expensive burn-ins and only requires $O(\log\log(1/ε))$ parallel processes when the accelerated rate is sublinear.

</details>


### [3] [Benchmarking Bilevel Derivative-Free Optimization Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30531)
*Charles Audet,Valentin Dijon,Youssef Diouane*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对双层无导数优化（BL-DFO）算法，提出了一套基准测试方法，包括引入后优化裁判程序以排除非可行点，并综合考虑上下层优化器的计算成本，确保算法间公平比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有BL-DFO基准测试技术未能严格验证所得解的可行性（即满足下层优化最优性），也未充分考虑上下层求解器的计算开销，导致算法比较不公平。

Method: 提出了一套BL-DFO基准测试方法论，引入名为"裁判程序"（refereeing procedure）的后优化步骤，用于剔除不可行点；同时将上下层求解器的计算开销纳入总体计算成本核算。

Result: 通过数值实验展示了所提基准测试方法的效果，验证了裁判程序能有效排除不可行解，且考虑了上下层计算开销后的成本度量能更公平地比较算法。

Conclusion: 该工作为BL-DFO领域提供了更严谨的基准测试框架，裁判程序和综合计算成本度量有助于提升算法比较的公平性和可靠性。

Abstract: Bilevel optimization involves an upper-level and a lower-level decision maker. The lower-level optimization problem is nested within the constraints of the upper-level one. A point is said to be admissible for the bilevel problem if it satisfies all constraints and is optimal for the lower-level decision-maker. Bilevel derivative-free optimization (BL-DFO) algorithms address bilevel optimization problems in which either the upper-level or the lower-level problem is solved using a derivative-free optimization method. In this context, existing BL-DFO benchmarking techniques often do not rigorously validate the admissibility of proposed solutions, and do not adequately account for the computational effort deployed by the upper- and lower-level solvers. This work proposes a benchmarking methodology for BL-DFO algorithms. A post-optimization procedure, named refereeing procedure, is introduced to discard non-admissible points and ensure a fair comparison between the algorithms. The computational effort deployed by upper- and lower-level solvers are also taken into account into the overall computational cost. Numerical experiments illustrate the benchmarking methodology.

</details>


### [4] [A characterization of maximal inhomogeneous-quadratic-free sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30602)
*Gonzalo Muñoz,Joseph Paat,Felipe Serrano*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了由非齐次二次不等式定义集合S的极大S-自由集的特征刻画，通过非扩张函数给出了完整表征，与齐次情形结果共同完成了所有极大二次自由集的刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在整数规划中，极大格自由集已有明确刻画；在二次优化中，Muñoz等人（2025）刻画了齐次二次不等式定义S时的极大S-自由集。本文旨在将这一刻画推广到非齐次二次不等式情形，从而完善整个理论框架。

Method: 采用交截割框架，利用非扩张函数（non-expansive functions）对由非齐次二次不等式定义的集合S的极大S-自由集进行特征刻画。

Result: 给出了非齐次二次不等式定义S时极大S-自由集的完整表征，该表征基于非扩张函数构建。

Conclusion: 与齐次情形的结果相结合，本文完成了所有极大二次自由集通过非扩张函数的完整特征刻画。

Abstract: The intersection cut framework is a versatile tool for generating valid inequalities in optimization. Its main ingredients are so-called $S$-free sets: convex sets whose interiors do not intersect a given set $S$. Among these, inclusion-wise maximal $S$-free sets are particularly important, as they yield the strongest intersection cuts. In the integer programming setting, maximal lattice-free sets are well studied and admit explicit characterizations. In the quadratic optimization context, Muñoz, Paat, and Serrano (2025) characterized maximal $S$-free sets when $S$ is defined by a homogeneous quadratic inequality. In this work, we characterize maximal $S$-free sets when $S$ is defined by an inhomogeneous quadratic inequality. As in the homogeneous case, our characterization is built using non-expansive functions. Together with the results in the homogeneous case, our results complete a characterization of $every$ maximal quadratic-free set via non-expansive functions.

</details>


### [5] [Distributed optimal control problems governed by poroelasticity equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30839)
*Arbaz Khan,Jeonghun J. Lee,Harpal Singh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对Biot固结模型提出了一种以固体位移和流体压力为主要未知量的双场对称新公式，证明了其适定性及最优控制的存在唯一性，并给出了全离散格式的先验误差估计和数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为Biot多孔弹性固结模型建立一种适定的双场对称新公式，并研究其最优控制问题。

Method: 提出以固体位移和流体压力为未知量的双场对称公式；采用向后欧拉时间离散和变分近似控制变量；进行数值实验验证。

Result: 证明了新公式的适定性；证明了以流体源为控制变量的最优控制的存在唯一性；推导了全离散格式的先验误差估计；数值实验验证了方案性能。

Conclusion: 该双场对称公式在理论上是适定的，对应的最优控制问题存在唯一解，数值方法有效可靠。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose and analyze a novel two-field symmetric formulation with solid displacement and fluid pressure as main unknowns for the Biot's consolidation model in poroelasticity. Firstly, we prove the well-posedness of the new formulation and then show the existence and uniqueness of optimal control where the fluid sources in the model act as a control variable. We prove a priori error estimates for the fully discrete scheme with backward Euler time discretization and a variational approximation of the control variable. A numerical example is presented to validate the performance of the proposed novel scheme.

</details>


### [6] [BUP-TR: Bayesian Underdetermined Projection Trust-Region Methods for Derivative-Free Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30841)
*Wei Hu,Pengcheng Xie,Ya-Xiang Yuan,Li Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: BUP-TR是一种贝叶斯欠定投影信赖域方法，用先验正则化替代经典的最小Frobenius范数Hessian更新来完成欠定二次插值建模，在标准假设下达到全局一阶收敛和O(ε^{-2})评价复杂度，其实现BUP-NEWUOA在固定预算下优于NEWUOA等标准无导数求解器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统无导数信赖域方法中，欠定二次插值存在模型不唯一的问题，经典方法通过预设范数或模型变化度量（如最小Frobenius Hessian更新）来消除歧义，但这种固定规则可能不是最优选择。本文提出用贝叶斯先验正则化替代这种人为规定的完成规则，使模型选择更灵活且可融入先验知识。

Method: 提出BUP-TR框架：将插值方程作为精确约束，在仿射族中选择最大后验(MAP)二次模型，等价于在精度矩阵定义的范数下将先验模型投影到插值族上。精度矩阵还用于定义插值集的几何检验（MAP-poisedness）及修复过程。实现上采用NEWUOA风格算法BUP-NEWUOA。

Result: 在标准光滑性、几何和先验精度假设下，硬-MAP模型具有完全线性性质；信赖域方法达到全局一阶收敛，评价复杂度为O(ε^{-2})（包含修复评价）。BUP-NEWUOA在固定评价预算下性能优于NEWUOA及其他标准无导数求解器。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯先验正则化为欠定二次插值提供了一种灵活有效的替代方案，不仅保持了理论收敛保证，还在实际测试中展现出性能提升，为模型构建规则的设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Underdetermined quadratic interpolation is a standard model-construction tool in model-based derivative-free trust-region methods: it keeps the number of function evaluations manageable, but leaves many quadratic models consistent with the sampled values. Classical solvers remove this ambiguity by prescribing a norm or a model-change measure, such as the least-Frobenius Hessian update in Powell-type methods. We propose BUP-TR (Bayesian Underdetermined Projection Trust-Region), which replaces this prescribed completion rule by a prior-regularized one. At each iteration, the interpolation equations are imposed as exact constraints, and the model is chosen as the maximum-a-posteriori (MAP) element of the affine family of interpolating quadratics, equivalently as the projection of a prior model onto this family in the norm defined by the precision matrix. The precision matrix used in this completion also defines a geometry test for the interpolation set, leading to MAP-poisedness and a corresponding repair procedure. Under standard smoothness, geometry, and prior-accuracy assumptions, the hard-MAP models are fully linear, and the resulting trust-region method attains global first-order convergence with O(epsilon^{-2}) evaluation complexity, including repair evaluations. A NEWUOA-style implementation, BUP-NEWUOA, improves performance under fixed evaluation budgets over NEWUOA and other standard derivative-free solvers on the test set considered.

</details>


### [7] [MATRO: Metric-Aware Fully Quadratic Trust Regions for Derivative-Free Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30845)
*Wei Hu,Pengcheng Xie,Ya-Xiang Yuan,Li Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出MATRO（度量感知信任域优化），一种全二次插值框架，其信任域采用椭球形状s^T M_k s ≤ Δ_k^2，通过海森矩阵定义的曲率度量来塑造信任区域，在保持一阶评估复杂度O(n²ε⁻²)的同时，有效处理黑箱目标函数的坏尺度或局部各向异性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当黑箱目标函数存在坏尺度或局部各向异性时，基于欧几里得球的信任域方法会被最陡局部方向限制，阻碍沿慢变方向的收敛进度。现有方法缺乏对信任区域形状的系统性设计，因此需要一种能够自适应度量空间的方法来克服这一限制。

Method: MATRO采用双层结构：第一层，对任意正定度量M_k，通过线性变换y = M_k^{1/2} s将椭球子问题转化为标准欧几里得信任域子问题，在诱导坐标下统一度量约束进行模型下降、比率测试、半径更新和全二次插值理论分析；第二层，从插值模型的海森矩阵选择度量——正定二次型使用体积归一化曲率度量使诱导海森矩阵各向同性，对应截断牛顿步；不定海森矩阵使用绝对曲率度量平衡曲率大小并保留符号。

Result: 在标准全二次假设和度量约束下，MATRO保持了全二次插值的一阶评估复杂度O(n²ε⁻²)。More-Wild基准测试、受控各向异性测试和二维轨迹实验表明，曲率形状的信任域在插值海森矩阵捕捉到稳定局部各向异性时最为有效，但其密集线性代数成本在宽松精度或廉价解析测试中最明显。

Conclusion: 基于曲率的椭球信任域能够有效处理目标函数的各向异性，在保持理论复杂度阶次不变的同时提升实际收敛性能，但额外计算成本主要体现在高精度需求或昂贵函数评估场景中。

Abstract: Model-based derivative-free trust-region methods build local models from function values and accept trial steps only where these models are expected to be reliable. This paper studies the shape of that trusted region. When a black-box objective is badly scaled or locally anisotropic, a Euclidean ball can be forced by the steepest local direction and can therefore restrict progress along directions of slow variation. We propose MATRO (Metric-Aware Trust-Region Optimization), a fully quadratic interpolation framework in which the trust region is the ellipsoid s^T M_k s <= Delta_k^2. The method is built in two layers. First, for any positive definite metric M_k, the induced variable y = M_k^{1/2} s converts the ellipsoidal subproblem into a standard Euclidean trust-region subproblem. Model decrease, ratio tests, radius updates, and fully quadratic interpolation theory can then be stated in induced coordinates under a uniform metric contract. Second, the metric is selected from the Hessian of the interpolation model. For positive definite quadratics, the volume-normalized curvature metric is the unique shape that makes the induced Hessian isotropic, and the corresponding metric trust-region step is a truncated Newton step. For indefinite fitted Hessians, an absolute-curvature metric balances curvature magnitudes while preserving the signs of the model curvature. Under the standard fully quadratic assumptions and the metric contract, MATRO retains the first-order evaluation-complexity order O(n^2 epsilon^{-2}) for full quadratic interpolation. Experiments on More-Wild benchmarks, controlled anisotropy tests, and two-dimensional trajectories show that curvature-shaped trust regions are most useful when the interpolation Hessian captures a stable local anisotropy, while their dense linear-algebra cost is most visible at loose accuracies or on inexpensive analytic tests.

</details>


### [8] [Diffusion-Robust Optimization over Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30853)
*Liviu Aolaritei,Ricky Huang,Michael I. Jordan,Paul Grigas*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于扩散的不确定性模型，用于有向图上的鲁棒优化。在该模型中，边权重的扰动沿相邻边传播并满足节点处的守恒约束。针对凸网络问题（最小成本流、最大流），鲁棒优化仍为凸问题且可多项式时间求解。对于组合问题，最短路径的传播深度会导致可解性与难解性之间的尖锐相变；而对于旅行商问题，鲁棒性通常不增加额外的计算复杂度。结果表明，拓扑感知的不确定性可以从根本上改变鲁棒组合优化的计算复杂性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有鲁棒优化模型通常假设不确定性独立作用于每条边，忽略了网络系统中不确定性通过流和局部相互作用传播的实际物理特性。作者希望引入一种拓扑感知的扩散不确定性模型，更真实地刻画交通、物流、通信和能源网络中的不确定性传播机制。

Method: 提出扩散不确定性模型，其中边权重扰动沿相邻边传播并满足节点守恒约束；在凸网络问题（最小成本流、最大流）和组合问题（最短路径、TSP）上分别分析鲁棒对应问题的计算复杂性，考察传播深度、预算几何与可行解结构之间的相互作用。

Result: 1) 凸网络问题在所有扩散机制下仍保持凸性和多项式时间可解性；2) 最短路径的传播深度引发可解性与难解性之间的尖锐相变；3) TSP的鲁棒版本不增加额外计算复杂性，因为哈密顿回路结构使固定路径对抗问题可化为显式公式。

Conclusion: 拓扑感知的扩散不确定性模型能够从根本上改变鲁棒组合优化的计算性质，其可解性由传播深度、预算几何和可行解结构三者之间的相互作用共同决定。

Abstract: We introduce a diffusion-based uncertainty model for robust optimization on directed graphs, in which perturbations of edge weights propagate along adjacent edges and satisfy conservation constraints at nodes. This topology-aware structure is natural in networked systems where uncertainty is induced by flows and local interactions, including transportation, logistics, communication, and energy networks. We analyze how such diffusive uncertainty reshapes the computational landscape of robust graph optimization. For convex network problems, such as minimum-cost flow and maximum flow, the resulting formulations remain convex and admit polynomial-time solution methods across all diffusion regimes considered. For combinatorial problems, the effect is more delicate. We focus on two canonical combinatorial graph problems, shortest path and the traveling salesman problem (TSP), which provide complementary benchmarks: shortest path is polynomial-time solvable in the nominal setting, whereas TSP is already NP-hard. We show that, for shortest path, propagation depth induces a sharp transition between tractable and intractable robust counterparts. For the traveling salesman problem, robustness often adds no computational complexity beyond ordinary TSP, because the structure of Hamiltonian cycles makes the fixed-tour adversarial problem collapse to explicit formulas. Together, these results show that topology-aware uncertainty can fundamentally change robust combinatorial optimization, with tractability governed by the interaction between propagation, budget geometry, and the structure of feasible solutions.

</details>


### [9] [Near-Optimal Mixed Strategy for Zero-Sum Linear-Quadratic Differential Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30886)
*Tao Xu,Wang Xi,Jianping He*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对零和线性二次型微分博弈(ZSLQDGs)中最优混合策略的解析求解问题，通过构造代理纯策略随机微分博弈(SDG)匹配混合策略的前两阶矩，实现了近最优混合策略的解析综合与严格性能认证，证明了状态分布与期望成本的$\mathcal{O}(\barπ^2)$弱近似误差以及全局值函数与策略次优性间隙的$\mathcal{O}(\barπ^{\frac{1}{2}})$上界，并通过双积分器追逃博弈数值实验验证了理论界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零和线性二次型微分博弈中最优混合策略的解析解至今未解，现有方法缺乏对混合策略的解析综合与性能保证。

Method: 构造代理纯策略随机微分博弈匹配混合策略的一阶矩和二阶矩，解析求解代理博弈得到闭式最优控制律，揭示代理博弈由广义Riccati微分方程(GRDE)控制并显式决定方差注入的动态能量分配律，提出鲁棒双路由架构执行近最优混合策略。

Optim., 2010) conjectured that the smallest constant $C_n$ such that the polynomial $x_1\cdots x_n +C_n$ is contained in the degree-$n$ truncated quadratic module $M_{n,n}(x_1-x_1^2,\ldots,x_n-x_n^2)$ of the hypercube is $C_n=1/(n(n+2))$, for $n$ even. Abstract: We introduce the notion of worst-case posterior and worst-case likelihood sensitivity. These measure, respectively, the sensitivity of the expected cost to worst-case perturbations of the posterior distribution and worst-case perturbations of the likelihood of a Bayesian model. Each defines a quantitative measure of robustness. A decision maker concerned about the sensitivity of the out-of-sample expected cost to deviations from her assumptions will want a decision for which both sensitivities are small. We derive posterior and likelihood sensitivities for uncertainty sets defined in terms of deviation measures. Posterior sensitivity vanishes when the posterior variance shrinks to zero, which occurs when parameter uncertainty is eliminated from learning. Parameter learning does not eliminate likelihood sensitivity. A distributionally robust formulation of a Bayesian optimization problem makes a near-Pareto-optimal tradeoff between performance (expected cost) and robustness (posterior and likelihood sensitivity).

</details>

For instance, we demonstrate that accelerated methods can be wall-clock inferior to non-accelerated schemes. Our framework provides a unified methodology to translate convergence guarantees into practical fidelity and batching recommendations.

Abstract: Can gradient descent be accelerated by just choosing better stepsizes? Surprisingly, the answer is yes. This short expository article provides an accessible introduction to this phenomenon of stepsize hedging.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 混合精度GPU优化框架为解决大规模最小包围球问题提供了高效且高精度的方案，混合精度策略有效降低了计算开销而不牺牲最终精度。

Abstract: A mixed-precision GPU-oriented optimization framework is developed for computing the minimum enclosing ball of a collection of balls. The approach is built on an equivalent second-order cone programming reformulation and a relative-type inexact proximal augmented Lagrangian method (ripALM), which provides a high-accuracy optimization backbone while solving the inner subproblems only to a progress-dependent relative accuracy. The proximal augmented Lagrangian inherits a constraint-wise separable structure: its objective, gradient, generalized Hessian, and multiplier updates can be efficiently evaluated on GPUs as parallel maps over the input balls followed by reductions. To further improve efficiency, a mixed-precision reduction strategy is introduced. A low-precision ripALM run identifies balls near the approximate boundary, a high-precision ripALM run refines the reduced problem, and a full a posteriori feasibility check detects and reintroduces any violated discarded balls. Thus, low precision is used only for screening and warm starting, while the final feasibility is enforced against the original problem. Numerical experiments show that ripALM and mixed-precision ripALM achieve high accuracy and are substantially faster than the tested CPU-based geometric software and general-purpose conic solvers on large-scale instances.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进超立方体上非负多项式的多项式核近似精度，从而提升Lasserre层级在多项式优化中的收敛速率。

Method: 构造了一族新的多项式近似核，生成$r$次多项式平方和（SOS），通过理论分析证明其在系数$\ell_1$范数下达到$O(\log^3 r/r^2)$的误差界。

Result: 新核的$\ell_1$范数误差为$O(\log^3 r/r^2)$，相比已有$O(1/r)$的误差界有显著改进。

Conclusion: 该核的提出将Lasserre层级在超立方体多项式优化上的收敛速率从$O(1/r)$提升至$O(\log^3 r/r^2)$。

Abstract: We propose a new family of polynomial approximation kernels for approximating nonnegative polynomials on the hypercube $[-1,1]^n$. Our Kernels produce polynomial sums-of-squares of degree $r$, achieving an $O(\log^3 r/r^2)$ error in the $\ell_1$-norm of the coefficients. This improves on the known error bound $O(1/r)$ from the literature. As a corollary, we obtain an improved convergence rate for the Lasserre hierarchy for polynomial optimization on the hypercube, again improving a known rate by Baldi and Slot from $O(1/r)$ to $O(\log^3 r/r^2)$.

</details>

*Florentin Goyens,Simon Mataigne,Pierre-Antoine Absil*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种随机化变体的着陆方法（sketched landing method），通过引入低维随机草图矩阵降低正交约束优化问题的每迭代计算成本，同时保持期望收敛性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准着陆方法在处理正交约束优化时，每次迭代中法向分量和切向分量的计算成本较高，尤其在高维问题中成为瓶颈。作者希望引入随机化技术（草图矩阵）来降低这些方向的计算复杂度。

Method: 在着陆方法的每次迭代中，法向分量（减少不可行性）和切向分量（降低目标函数）的计算中引入低维随机草图矩阵。考虑了密集（高斯）和稀疏（子采样）两种草图矩阵，分别分析其计算效率提升及收敛性保障。

Result: 密集和稀疏草图矩阵均能显著降低每迭代计算成本，同时在期望意义下保持了收敛保证。

Conclusion: Sketched landing method 为大规模正交约束优化提供了一种高效且理论上可保证收敛的随机化求解方案。

Abstract: We propose the \emph{sketched landing method}, a randomized variant of the landing method for optimization under orthogonality constraints. Each landing step consists of the sum of a \emph{normal} component, which reduces infeasibility, and a \emph{tangent} component, which decreases the objective function. Our main contribution is the introduction of low-dimensional random \emph{sketch matrices} to reduce the computational cost of these directions. We consider both dense (Gaussian) and sparse (subsampling) sketch matrices, and show how they reduce the per-iteration cost while preserving convergence guarantees in expectation.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典共识基优化方法拓展至无限维优化问题，解决无限维动力学分析中的理论挑战。

Conclusion: 该方法将共识基优化从有限维推广到无限维优化问题，拓展了方法的应用范围。

Abstract: We introduce a stochastic interacting particle system in separable Hilbert spaces together with its associated mean-field formulation. The model is shown to retain the characteristic consensus-driven structure of classical Consensus-Based Optimization, while accounting for the analytical challenges of infinite-dimensional dynamics. We establish well-posedness of the proposed dynamics and analyze the associated consensus mechanism. Furthermore, we derive convergence guarantees under suitable assumptions on the objective functional, showing concentration of the dynamics toward the minimizer in the long-time regime. This extends the applicability of the method to a broad class of infinite-dimensional optimization problems. In addition, we study the corresponding finite-particle system relevant for numerical implementation and propose a practical algorithm.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对M/M/1队列系统，研究一种一次性临时服务速率控制选项的成本优化问题，填补该特定控制策略下期望总节省成本近似方法的空白。

Method: 采用价值迭代算法结合M-均匀几何递推方法，分别近似期望总节省未来成本和期望总节省折现未来成本，并推导最优策略结构与强Blackwell最优性理论结果。

Result: 提出了基于起始状态或起始分布的期望总节省成本近似方法，获得了最优策略的结构性质，验证了强Blackwell最优性，并通过数值实例展示了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 论文成功为M/M/1队列的一次性临时服务速率控制问题提供了理论框架和数值求解方法，所提出的近似算法具有良好的性能，理论结果为后续研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: In this article, a one-off option for temporary service rate control for the M/M/1 queue is considered.
  After taking this option, during a single exponentially distributed period, two service rates are available for use. Once service rate control is lost, the system continues with a fixed service rate $μ$. The objective is to minimise the sum of holding costs and service costs.
  We approximate the expected total saved cost by taking the one-off option, depending on the starting state or starting distribution.
  Using the Value Iteration algorithm with $M$-uniform geometric recurrence, we present methods to approximate the expected total saved future cost, as well as the expected total saved discounted future cost. Furthermore, we obtain theoretical results on the structure of optimal policies and strong Blackwell optimality. The paper is concluded by numerically applying the methods to various instances of the model.

</details>

### [24] [PhyDrawGen: Physically Grounded Diagram Generation from Natural Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30512)
*Nafiul Haque,Syed Nazmus Sakib,Shifat E Arman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PhyDrawGen 是一个神经符号管道，通过解耦语义理解与物理约束满足，结合大语言模型、确定性求解器和视觉验证循环，在物理图生成任务上显著超越现有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型虽能产生视觉上合理的输出，但系统性出现力向量幻觉、忽略守恒定律和违反几何约束的问题，亟需一种能严格遵循物理定律的图生成方法。

Method: 1) 大语言模型从文本中提取类型化场景图；2) 确定性求解器将场景图转换为平面直线图(PSLG)，编码力平衡、光路和场拓扑；3) 微调后的Qwen-VL模型通过视觉化"提出-验证"循环迭代修正约束违反。

Result: 在涵盖力学、光学和电磁学的1,449个问题基准上，PhyDrawGen显著优于GPT-5-image、Gemini 2.5 Flash和Gemini 3 Pro，在非常规物体问题上仍保持鲁棒物理准确性。

Conclusion: 神经符号方法通过语义理解与物理约束的解耦，能有效解决生成模型在物理图生成中的系统性幻觉问题，实现精确的物理合规性。

Abstract: Generating physics diagrams from text requires strict adherence to physical laws. While current generative models produce visually plausible outputs, they systematically hallucinate force vectors, ignore conservation laws, and violate geometric constraints. We present PhyDrawGen, a neuro-symbolic pipeline that decouples semantic scene understanding from physical constraint satisfaction. First, a large language model extracts a typed scene graph from the problem text. A deterministic solver then converts this graph into a Planar Straight-Line Graph (PSLG), encoding force balance, optical paths, and field topologies as exact geometric primitives. Finally, a fine-tuned Qwen-VL model implements a visually grounded propose-verify loop to iteratively correct any constraint violations. Evaluated on a benchmark of 1,449 problems spanning mechanics, optics, and electromagnetism, PhyDrawGen significantly outperforms GPT-5-image, Gemini 2.5 Flash, and Gemini 3 Pro, demonstrating robust physical accuracy even on unusual-object problems.

</details>


### [25] [Physically Viable World Models: A Case for Query-Conditioned Embodied AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30542)
*Adam J. Thorpe,Stepan Tretiakov,Cheng-Hsi Hsiao,Su Ann Low,Xingjian Li,Hassan Iqbal,Neel P. Bhatt,Ufuk Topcu,Krishna Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出具身AI的现有观测预测型世界模型虽然在视觉上合理，但常常产生物理上不可行的预测，提出应构建“物理可行”的世界模型——即识别足以回答干预查询的最简物理抽象，包含模块化组件和自主编排器，并通过实验证明该方法能解决现有系统失败的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有观测预测型世界模型能生成视觉上看似合理的预测，但常产生物理上错误的结果（如推荐不可行动作、错误预测交互结果或认证不安全行为），这是因为不同物理系统可能在视觉上相同但在干预下行为不同，因此亟需一种能确保物理可行性的世界模型设计原则。

Method: 提出物理可行世界模型的概念，包含模块化组件（环境表征、潜在状态与参数估计、动作规范、干预动力学、查询级响应），以及一个自主编排器根据查询动态组合兼容的学习型或结构化组件，保证转移模型（解析、模拟、学习或混合）保留决定干预结果的结构。

Result: 通过控制基准实验（固定可见场景、变化潜在物理参数）证明现有模型失败，并展示所提方法能正确回答现有系统无法正确处理的查询，实现规划、控制和验证任务。

Conclusion: 正确的世界模型抽象不是最详细的模型，而是能保留与查询相关的区分的最简模型。该分解使模型可解释、组件可验证、输出可审计，为世界模型设计提供新原则，并为现有模型提供可行性测试标准。

Abstract: World models for embodied AI must be physically viable: constructed to answer intervention queries by representing the physical structure governing action outcomes, rather than merely predicting future observations. Existing observation-predictive world models can produce visually plausible but physically wrong rollouts. This failure is structural; distinct physical systems can look identical yet diverge under intervention. We expose this problem with controlled benchmarks that fix the visible scene while varying latent physics. We show that such models may recommend infeasible actions, mispredict interaction outcomes, or certify unsafe behavior. We argue that embodied AI requires world models that identify the simplest physical abstraction sufficient to answer an intervention query. Such a model comprises modular components, including environment representation, latent state and parameter estimation, action specification, interventional dynamics, and query-level response. An autonomous orchestrator should identify the relevant abstraction and compose compatible learned and structured components per query. When closed-form physics is unavailable, uncertain, or costly, the transition model may be analytic, simulated, learned, or hybrid, but it must preserve the structure that determines interventional outcomes. This decomposition makes the model interpretable, its components verifiable, and its outputs auditable against the query. It also provides a design principle for new world models and a feasibility test for existing ones: the right abstraction is not the most detailed model of the world, but the simplest model that preserves the distinctions relevant to the query. We demonstrate this approach on queries that existing systems fail to answer correctly, and outline how an orchestrator can dynamically assemble and adapt physically viable models for planning, control, and verification.

</details>

*João Filipe,Álvaro Torralba,Gregor Behnke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了如何将因子化任务（factored tasks）编码为SAT问题，提出了多种将因子化转移关系转换为命题逻辑的编码策略，并分析了不同层次并行性的利用以及常见任务转换对SAT规划器性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因子化任务是一种比STRIPS或SAS+更紧凑的规划表示形式，但现有规划方法局限于启发式搜索。为了拓展规划方法，作者希望探索如何将因子化任务编码为SAT问题，从而利用SAT求解器进行规划。

Method: 提出了多种将因子化转移关系（factored transition relation）编码为命题逻辑（propositional logic）的方法，分析了不同编码策略；研究了在SAT规划中利用不同层次并行性的方式；并评估了常见任务转换（task transformations）对基于SAT的规划器性能的影响。

Result: 提出了多种可行的SAT编码方式，并分析了并行性和任务转换对SAT规划性能的影响。具体实验结果表明不同编码策略在规划效率上存在差异。

Conclusion: 因子化任务可以有效地编码为SAT问题，通过合理的编码策略和并行性利用，能够提升SAT规划器的性能。这为因子化任务提供了除启发式搜索之外的新规划途径。

Abstract: Factored tasks are a classical planning representation that extends SAS+ with limited forms of disjunctive preconditions, conditional effects, and angelic nondeterminism. This allows for a more compact representation of tasks than traditional formalisms such as STRIPS or SAS+, and supports a wide range of task transformations. However, existing planning approaches for factored tasks have been limited to heuristic search methods.
  In this work, we investigate how to encode factored tasks in SAT. We propose several ways to encode the tasks, focusing on different strategies for translating the factored transition relation into propositional logic. We also analyze how to exploit parallelism at various levels in this setting and study the impact of common task transformations on the performance of SAT-based planners.

</details>


### [27] [Procedural Generation of First Person Shooter Maps using Map-Elites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30570)
*Simone de Donato,Pier Luca Lanzi,Daniele Loiacono*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究将MAP-Elites（一种质量多样性算法）应用于第一人称射击（FPS）游戏关卡设计，提出了两种新的地图表征方法（Point-Line和Spatial-Layout），并通过拓扑属性和涌现属性指标进行评估，结果表明新表征能生成比传统方法更多样化且更高质量的FPS地图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FPS游戏关卡设计方法缺乏多样性和质量平衡，传统地图表征（All-Black和Grid-Graph）对FPS地图的特性刻画不足，需要更有效的表征方法和特征指导来生成高质量且多样化的关卡。

Method: 采用MAP-Elites with Sliding Boundaries (MESB)算法，引入Point-Line和Spatial-Layout两种新型地图表征；定义拓扑属性指标（仅依赖布局）和涌现属性指标（需实际游戏评估）；通过深入分析识别最适合指导MAP-Elites光照过程的行为特征。

Result: 两种新表征（Point-Line和Spatial-Layout）相比传统表征（All-Black和Grid-Graph）能生成具有更高多样性和更高质量的FPS地图。

Conclusion: 新型地图表征方法显著提升了FPS关卡设计的多样性和质量，证明了MAP-Elites结合适当表征在游戏内容生成中的有效性。

Abstract: We investigate the application of MAP-Elites (a well-known quality diversity algorithm) to design levels for First-Person Shooter (FPS) games. We consider two well-known map representations (All-Black and Grid-Graph) and introduce two novel representations (Point-Line and Spatial-Layout) that improve the characterization of FPS maps. We define a series of metrics to describe maps' topological properties (which solely depend on maps' layout), and emergent properties (which must be evaluated through actual gameplay). We perform an in-depth analysis to identify the most suitable features to guide MAP-Elites illumination process. We apply MAP-Elites with Sliding Boundaries (MESB) to evolve populations of FPS maps. Our results show that the new representations can generate maps with higher diversity and quality than the representations previously used for evolving FPS maps.

</details>


### [28] [Uncertainty-Aware and Temporally Regulated Expert Advice in Reinforcement Learning for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30576)
*Ahmed Abouelazm,Felix Klingebiel,Philip Schörner,J. Marius Zöllner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种不确定性感知的框架，通过专家建议引导强化学习中的探索，结合自适应阈值、承诺冷却策略和随机早停启发式，在CARLA实验中实现更安全高效的自动驾驶导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶强化学习的探索过程存在固有安全性问题：智能体必须经历新行为来学习，但探索可能导致碰撞或偏离道路。

Method: 1) 基于滚动缓冲区的自适应阈值触发专家建议（不确定性包括认知不确定性和偶然不确定性）；2) 承诺-冷却策略和随机早停启发式控制引导时长与频率；3) 在离线IQN骨干网络中使用共享经验回放池混合专家与智能体经验。

Result: 在CARLA无信号交叉口导航任务中，相比IQN基线，成功率提升5-7%，并减少故障次数。

Conclusion: 风险敏感的不确定性结合受控的专家集成，能够实现更安全高效的基于传感器的RL策略学习探索。

Abstract: Exploration in reinforcement learning for autonomous driving is inherently unsafe: agents must experience novel behaviors to learn, yet exploration can lead to collisions or off-road driving. We propose an uncertainty-aware framework that leverages expert advice to guide exploration while avoiding long-term dependence. Advice is triggered when epistemic or aleatoric uncertainty exceeds adaptive thresholds derived from rolling buffers, ensuring advice evolves with the agent's confidence. A commitment-cooldown strategy with a stochastic early-stop heuristic regulates the duration and frequency of guidance, exposing the agent to coherent maneuvers without exhausting the advice budget. Expert and agent experiences are combined in a shared replay buffer within an off-policy implicit quantile network (IQN) backbone, enabling efficient reuse of expert trajectories. Experiments in CARLA show that our method outperforms the IQN baseline, improving success by 5-7% and reducing failures, demonstrating that risk-sensitive uncertainty coupled with regulated expert integration enables safer and more efficient exploration for sensor-based RL policy learning in unsignalized intersection navigation.

</details>


### [29] [Harness Updating Is Not Harness Benefit: Disentangling Evolution Capabilities in Self-Evolving LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30621)
*Minhua Lin,Juncheng Wu,Zijun Wang,Zhan Shi,Yisi Sang,Bing He,Zewen Liu,Tianxin Wei,Zongyu Wu,Zhiwei Zhang,Dakuo Wang,Xiang Zhang,Benoit Dumoulin,Cihang Xie,Yuyin Zhou,Suhang Wang,Hanqing Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了LLM智能体的“框架自进化”（harness self-evolution）能力，发现两个反直觉结论：（1）框架更新能力与模型基础能力无关，弱模型也能产出与强模型效果相当的框架更新；（2）框架收益能力呈现非单调性，中等能力模型受益最大，弱模型受益小，强模型反而不如中等模型。原因在于弱模型存在“无法激活框架组件”和“无法忠实遵循框架指令”两种失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体系统通过可编辑的外部框架（提示词、技能、记忆、工具）来塑造任务执行，但模型在“框架自进化”方面——即从执行经验中更新框架并从中获益的能力——与模型基础能力之间的关系尚不明确。本文旨在探究哪些模型能产生有用的框架更新，哪些模型真正能从更新中受益。

Method: 本文定义了两种框架自进化能力：（1）框架更新能力：从执行证据中产生有用的持久框架更新的能力；（2）框架收益能力：在任务求解中使用更新后的框架获益的能力。通过对比不同能力层级的模型（包括Qwen3.5-9B到Claude Opus等），系统评估这两种能力与模型基础能力之间的关系。

Result: （1）框架更新能力与模型基础能力呈“平坦”关系：不同能力层级的模型产生的框架更新带来的增益惊人地相似，Qwen3.5-9B的更新收益可与Claude Opus相媲美。（2）框架收益能力呈非单调性：弱模型获益少，中等模型获益最大，强模型收益反而低于中等模型。（3）弱模型收益低的原因有两种失败模式：无法激活相关框架组件，或激活了组件但无法忠实遵循其指令。

Conclusion: 建议将能力预算投入到任务求解智能体而非进化器上，并应在智能体训练中重点提升框架调用能力和长程指令遵循能力。

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly deployed as systems built around editable external harnesses, including prompts, skills, memories and tools, that shape task execution without changing model parameters. Harness self-evolution adapts such agents by updating these harnesses from execution evidence. Yet it remains unclear whether a model's base capability in task-solving predicts its capabilities in harness self-evolution: which models produce useful harness updates, and which actually benefit from them? We analyze two harness self-evolution capabilities: (i) harness-updating, the capability to produce useful persistent harness updates from execution evidence; (ii) harness-benefit, the capability to benefit from updated harnesses during task solving. Our analysis reveals two findings. First, harness-updating is flat in base capability: models from different capability tiers produce harness updates that lead to surprisingly similar gains; even Qwen3.5-9B's updates yield gains comparable to those of Claude Opus~4.6. Second, harness-benefit is non-monotonic in base capability: weak-tier models benefit little from updated harnesses, mid-tier models benefit most, and strong-tier models benefit less than mid-tier. We trace low gains at the weak tier to two failure modes: weak-tier models may fail to activate relevant harness artifacts, or activate them but fail to follow them faithfully. These findings suggest investing capability budget in the task-solving agent rather than the evolver, and targeting harness invocation and long-horizon instruction following in agent training. Our source code is publicly available at https://github.com/A-EVO-Lab/a-evolve/tree/release/harness-evolution.

</details>


### [30] [EHRBench: An Automated and Reliable EHR-based Benchmark for Clinical Decision Making with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30637)
*Yuzhang Xie,Keqi Han,Yunpeng Xiao,Hejie Cui,Guanchen Wu,Ziyang Zhang,Kai Shu,Jiaying Lu,Xiao Hu,Carl Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EHRBench，一个基于电子健康记录（EHR）和海量知识库的自动化、可扩展的临床决策基准，用于评估大语言模型在诊断、治疗和预后三项核心临床决策任务上的表现。通过EHR-LLM-KB交互流水线构建了近100万个QA样本，对30多个代表性LLM进行评测，揭示了当前模型在实际临床决策中的能力差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有临床决策评估缺乏一个既自动化又可靠的、基于真实EHR的大规模基准，无法充分评测LLM在需要生物医学知识和临床推理的实际决策任务上的可靠性。

Method: 提出EHR-LLM-KB交互流水线：先用专用LLM将EHR轨迹自动转换为结构化模板，再确定性实例化为QA条目，同时通过系统性的知识库验证和丰富来过滤幻觉或模糊关系，构建了包含诊断、治疗、预后三类任务的近100万QA样本。

Result: 构建了960,067个QA条目，对30多个代表性LLM进行基准测试，结果显示不同设置下能力趋势一致，验证了EHRBench的可靠性，并揭示了通向临床可靠LLM系统的可操作差距。

Conclusion: EHRBench是一个可靠、可扩展的EHR驱动临床决策基准，能够有效评估和揭示LLM在真实临床决策任务中的能力与不足。

Abstract: Clinical decision-making (CDM) is central to real-world clinical workflows, where clinicians infer diagnoses, select treatments, or anticipate future health outcomes under incomplete evidence. LLMs are increasingly used to support these decisions due to strong language capabilities, broad biomedical knowledge, and efficiency, yet the reliability of LLMs on real-world clinical decision tasks remains insufficiently understood. To evaluate CDM models, especially LLM-based models, an ideal and practical medical decision benchmark should be constructed via an automated yet reliable pipeline to ensure both scale and quality. Moreover, the grounding of a CDM benchmark in real patient EHRs can better support evaluation on practical CDM tasks that require substantive biomedical knowledge and clinical inference. To fill the gaps, we introduce EHRBench, an automated and reliable EHR-grounded benchmark for evaluating LLM-based clinical decision-making at scale. To ensure scalability and reliability, EHRBench is constructed through an EHR-LLM-KB(knowledge-base) interaction pipeline. For efficiency, we use a specialized LLM to automatically convert encounter-level EHR trajectories into structured templates and deterministically instantiate the templates into QA items. In parallel, we apply systematic KB-based verification and enrichment to filter hallucinated or ambiguous relations and to improve reliability. Using this pipeline, we construct nearly 1M (960,067) QA items spanning three core inference-required clinical decision tasks: diagnosis, treatment, and prognosis. We benchmark more than 30 representative LLMs on EHRBench and provide detailed analyses of performance and robustness. The results show consistent capability trends across settings, further validating the reliability of EHRBench and highlighting actionable gaps toward clinically reliable LLM systems.

</details>


### [31] [Structure-Induced Information for Rerooting Levin Tree Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30664)
*Jake Tuero,Michael Buro,Laurent Orseau,Levi H. S. Lelis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过$\sqrt{\text{LTS}}$算法中的学习型“rerooter”隐式分解软子任务，提出了三种rerooter设计（聚类、启发式、混合），避免了显式子目标生成的开销，实现了可扩展的搜索分配，在复杂环境中达到SOTA在线训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于子目标的策略树搜索依赖显式子目标生成，计算开销大且可扩展性差，阻碍了在复杂问题中的应用。

Method: 基于$\sqrt{\text{LTS}}$算法，提出三种rerooter设计：(i) 利用全局状态空间结构的聚类式rerooter，(ii) 利用学习到的代价估计的启发式rerooter，(iii) 结合两者的混合式rerooter，隐式分解软子任务。

Result: 所提方法能扩展到基于子目标的策略树搜索失败的复杂环境，并在测试领域中实现了最先进的在线训练效率。

Conclusion: 使用学习型rerooter隐式分解子任务可以避免显式子目标重构与推理的开销，显著降低计算负担，同时提升可扩展性和搜索效率。

Abstract: Subgoal-based policy tree search, which uses a policy to guide search, is effective for complex single-agent deterministic problems but often relies on explicit subgoal generation that can incur substantial overhead and hinders scalability. In this paper, we overcome these limitations by using a learned ``rerooter'' through the recently-introduced $\sqrt{\text{LTS}}$ algorithm. A rerooter implicitly decomposes the problem into soft subtasks. While previous work focused on the formal guarantees for given or handcrafted rerooters, in this work we propose three rerooter designs: (i) a clustering-based rerooter that exploits global state-space structure, (ii) a heuristic-based rerooter that leverages learned cost-to-go estimates, and (iii) a hybrid that combines both signals. Our framework avoids having to explicitly reconstruct and reason over generated subgoals, thereby enabling scalable allocation of search effort with significantly lower computational overhead. Empirically, our rerooting-based methods scale to complex environments where subgoal-based policy tree search fails, and achieve state-of-the-art online training efficiency on the domains tested.

</details>


### [32] [Healthcare Mechanisms from Policy-as-Code Search under Strategic Provider Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30680)
*Zihan Wang,Xiang Xu,Hongyuan Zha,Wenhao Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将医疗机制设计重新定义为语言模型的程序合成问题，通过多智能体模拟器Medi-Sim评估规则程序产生的均衡结果，并利用LLM引导的进化代码搜索合成可检查的混合目标程序，该程序能消除上码行为、减少一半拒诊率，同时保留大部分利润导向基线的资金。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医疗AI基准将提供者响应视为固定不变的，无法评估医疗机制在均衡状态下产生的效果，忽略了医疗机制与战略提供者响应之间不可分割的相互作用。

Method: 将医院机制设计重新表述为语言模型的程序合成问题，使用类型化、可检查的规则程序，通过多智能体模拟器Medi-Sim执行并评分，该模拟器包含五种战略提供者渠道（编码、选择、延迟、努力、分诊）。采用激励扫描恢复经典健康经济学发现，并使用LLM引导的进化代码搜索合成优化程序。

Result: 激励扫描恢复了经典健康经济学发现：利润压力下的上码和低复杂度患者选择，以及古德哈特漂移现象。单一审计杠杆暴露了压力迁移：关闭编码渠道使低复杂度选择增加两倍以上。LLM引导的进化搜索合成的混合目标程序消除了上码、使拒诊率减半，同时保留了利润导向基线的大部分资金。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了将机制设计与程序合成相结合的有效性，能够通过可检查的规则程序在激励兼容性与财务可持续性之间取得平衡。

Abstract: Healthcare mechanisms are inseparable from the strategic provider response they induce: existing healthcare AI benchmarks hold this response fixed and so cannot evaluate mechanisms by the equilibrium they produce. We recast hospital mechanism design as program synthesis for language models: typed, inspectable rule programs are executed and scored by Medi-Sim, a multi-agent simulator with five strategic provider channels (coding, selection, delay, effort, triage). An incentive sweep recovers classical health-economics findings as adjacent regimes -- up-coding and low-complexity-patient selection under profit pressure, and Goodhart-style drift where measured performance becomes anti-correlated with true outcomes -- and a single audit lever exposes pressure migration: closing the coding channel more than doubles low-complexity selection. LLM-guided evolutionary code search over the same rule-program space then synthesizes an inspectable mixed-objective program that eliminates up-coding, halves rejection, and retains most of the profit-oriented baseline's funds.

</details>


### [33] [MAVEN: Improving Generalization in Agentic Tool Calling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30738)
*Omkar Ghugarkar,Vishvesh Bhat,Muhammad Ahmed Mohsin,Asad Aali*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MAVEN是一个轻量级符号推理框架，通过结构化分解、自适应工具编排和中间验证来增强大语言模型的工具调用能力，在多个基准测试上无需额外训练即可将GPT-OSS-120b的准确率从48%提升至71%，并以约1/10的成本达到与前沿闭源模型竞争的水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型在跨域工具调用环境中缺乏组合推理策略、保持中间状态和协调工具的泛化能力，而当前基准测试无法充分暴露这一问题。

Method: 提出MAVEN（模块化智能体验证与执行网络），一种轻量级符号推理脚手架，包含结构化分解、自适应工具编排和中间验证三个核心模块；并构建了MAVEN-Bench压力测试基准。

Result: 在MAVEN-Bench上，MAVEN将GPT-OSS-120b基础模型准确率从48%提升至71%（无需额外训练）；以约1/10的成本达到与前沿闭源模型竞争的水平。

Conclusion: 轻量级、以验证为中心的推理脚手架能够有效增强组合推理能力，并推动更具过程意识的智能体评估方法。

Abstract: Generalization across agentic tool-calling environments remains a central challenge for reliable agentic reasoning systems. Although large language models achieve strong results on individual benchmarks, their ability to compose reasoning strategies, preserve intermediate states, and coordinate tools across domains remains underexplored. We present MAVEN (Modular Agentic Verification and Execution Network), a lightweight symbolic reasoning scaffold for structured decomposition, adaptive tool orchestration, and intermediate verification. We evaluate MAVEN across established tool-calling benchmarks, including BFCL v3, TauBench, Tau2Bench, AceBench, and introduce MAVEN-Bench, a stress-test benchmark for multi-step mathematical and physical reasoning with explicit verification and adversarial task composition. MAVEN-Bench exposes a substantial gap between partial reasoning quality and end-to-end task success; in direct MAVEN-Bench runs, MAVEN improves its GPT-OSS-120b base model from 48% to 71% accuracy without additional training. It also remains competitive with frontier proprietary baselines while using an open-weight backbone with an estimated cost ratio of roughly 1/10, suggesting that lightweight verification-centered scaffolds can strengthen compositional reasoning and motivate more process-aware evaluation of agents in the wild.

</details>


### [34] [Generating Graph-like Rules for Knowledge Graph Reasoning via Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30747)
*Haoxiang Cheng,Yunfei Wang,Chao Chen,Kewei Cheng,Zhipeng Lin,Haoxuan Li,Changjun Fan,Shixuan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GRiD是一个将图状规则发现重新表述为以目标关系为条件的离散生成过程的框架，采用监督预训练捕获结构先验，再通过强化学习直接优化不可微的规则质量指标，在六个基准数据集上取得了有竞争力的知识图谱补全性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有规则挖掘方法主要关注简单链式规则，忽略了图结构中蕴含的丰富关系信息（如循环和分支）；同时，搜索空间的组合爆炸导致计算瓶颈，而扩散模型等生成方法因训练目标与规则质量不对齐、不可微的评价指标无法直接指导优化，难以应用于规则挖掘。

Method: GRiD框架将图状规则发现重新表述为以目标关系为条件的离散生成过程。采用两阶段训练策略：第一阶段通过监督预训练从KG元图采样子图中捕获结构先验；第二阶段通过强化学习（策略梯度优化）直接由不可微的规则质量指标指导微调。

Result: 在六个基准数据集上，GRiD在知识图谱补全任务中取得了有竞争力的性能。消融研究验证了GRiD的效率和鲁棒性，并表明图状规则与链式规则在KG补全中具有互补性。

Conclusion: GRiD通过将图状规则发现转化为受控离散生成过程并利用强化学习优化非可微指标，有效突破了传统规则挖掘方法仅限链式规则的局限，为知识图谱推理中的图状规则学习提供了可行的新范式。

Abstract: Logical rules constitute a cornerstone of knowledge graph (KG) reasoning, valued for their interpretability and ability to model relational patterns. However, existing rule mining methods predominantly focus on simple chain-like rules and therefore neglect the richer relational information encoded in graph-like structures, such as cycles and branches. This limitation is further exacerbated by computational bottlenecks caused by the combinatorial explosion of the search space, which is especially challenging for graph-like rules. Meanwhile, generative approaches such as diffusion models, despite their success in other domains, can not be directly applied to rule mining because their training objectives are not aligned with the goal of learning high-quality rules, and non-differentiable KG rule quality metrics cannot directly guide model optimization. To address these limitations, we propose GRiD, a framework that reformulates graph-like rule discovery as a discrete generative process conditioned on the target relation. GRiD employs a two-phase training strategy. First, supervised pre-training enables GRiD to capture structural priors from subgraphs sampled from the KG meta-graph. Subsequently, reinforcement learning is applied to fine-tune GRiD through policy gradient optimization guided directly by non-differentiable rule-quality metrics. Experiments on six benchmark datasets show that GRiD achieves competitive performance on KG completion tasks. Ablation studies confirm the efficiency and robustness of GRiD and further show that graph-like rules complement chain-like rules in KG completion. Our codes and datasets are available in https://github.com/Haoxiang-Cheng/GRiD

</details>


### [35] [Learning Agent-Compatible Context Management for Long-Horizon Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30785)
*Lu Yi,Runlin Lei,Liuyi Yao,Yuexiang Xie,Yuyang Li,Wenhao Zhang,Zhewei Wei,Yaliang Li,Jian-Yun Nie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AdaCoM通过外部LLM和强化学习为冻结的智能体自适应管理上下文，在长期任务中显著提升性能，并发现保真度-可靠性权衡规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在长期任务中面临上下文积累导致的长上下文退化和推理失败问题，现有方法（如智能体端上下文控制或固定摘要策略）需要训练智能体本身进行适应，不适用于闭源智能体，且忽略了不同智能体可能需要不同策略。

Method: 提出AdaCoM，训练一个外部LLM作为上下文管理器，通过灵活的修改操作（如裁剪、摘要等）和端到端强化学习，为冻结的智能体（不修改其参数）管理上下文。

Result: 在多个智能体和基准测试（网页搜索、深度研究）上，AdaCoM显著提升性能；发现保真度-可靠性权衡：高基线性能的智能体受益于高保真上下文保留，低性能智能体需要更激进的压缩以保持在可靠推理区间；迁移实验表明AdaCoM在同能力水平的智能体间泛化最好。

Conclusion: AdaCoM提供了一种无需修改智能体即可自适应管理上下文的实用方案，并揭示了上下文管理策略与智能体能力之间的关系，为可重用的上下文管理器指明了方向。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly face long-horizon tasks such as web search and deep research in real-world applications, where accumulated context can cause long-context degradation and reasoning failures. Prior work mitigates this through context management with agent-side context control or fixed strategies such as summarization, which require training the agent itself for adaptation - making it impractical for closed-source agents and ignoring that different agents may require different strategies. We introduce Adaptive Context Management (AdaCoM), which trains an external LLM to manage the context of a frozen agent through flexible modification actions and end-to-end reinforcement learning. Across diverse agents on web search and deep research benchmarks, AdaCoM substantially improves performance by preserving task constraints and progress while pruning stale content. The learned strategies reveal a Fidelity-Reliability Trade-off: agents with higher vanilla ReAct performance benefit from higher-fidelity context preservation, whereas lower-performing agents require more aggressive compression to stay within a reliable reasoning regime. Transfer experiments show that AdaCoM generalizes most effectively across agents with similar capability (measured by vanilla ReAct performance), suggesting a practical path toward reusable context managers for agent systems.

</details>


### [36] [PReMISE: Policy Rubrics as Measurement Specifications for LLM Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30803)
*Swastik Roy,Rajkumar Pujari,Tharindu Kumarage,Charith Peris,Rahul Gupta,Anna Rumshisky,Pradeep Natarajan,Venkatesh Saligrama*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PReMISE框架，用于基于人类偏好数据发现和审计用于LLM评测的评分标准（rubric）。研究发现，现有评分标准在可靠性、偏好预测性和对抗鲁棒性上无法兼得，而PReMISE是唯一同时在适用性、具体性和有效维度上表现非平凡的标准来源。通过偏好排序选择和可靠性约束精炼两种修复操作，可显著提升评分准确率并降低对抗利用风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM裁判对开放回答的评分高度依赖于所使用的评分标准，模糊的评分标准（如要求回答"有帮助且符合事实"）会奖励那些编造事实或违反用户意图的精心修饰的回答。现有评分标准缺乏系统性的审计和评估，需要一种框架来发现和审计评分标准集。

Method: 提出PReMISE框架，包含两个部分：(i) 基于成对人类偏好数据发现策略级评分标准集；(ii) 沿四个维度（结构充分性、可靠性、偏好拟合度、对抗鲁棒性）审计任意评分标准集。同时提出两种修复操作：偏好排序选择和可靠性约束精炼。

Result: 没有任何原始评分标准源能同时满足可靠性、偏好预测性和对抗鲁棒性；高评分者间一致性不意味着低可利用性。PReMISE是唯一同时在适用性、具体性和有效维度上表现非平凡的标准源。偏好排序选择将配对回答的评分准确率从65.0%提升至68.6%；可靠性约束精炼将对抗性利用回答获得高分的比率从46.4%降至36.0%，而评分者间一致性变化很小（α=.531→.519）。

Conclusion: 评分标准的选择对LLM评估结果有决定性影响，需要系统性的审计框架。PReMISE提供了一种有效的评分标准发现和审计方法，并通过修复操作提升了评估的准确性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: LLM judges are increasingly used to evaluate open-ended responses, but their scores depend strongly on the rubrics that condition them. A vague rubric asking for a response to be ``helpful and factual'' can reward polished answers that invent facts or violate user intent. We treat reusable rubrics as measurement specifications: changing the rubric changes the response quality measurement induced by a fixed judge. We introduce PReMISE, a framework that, given pairwise human-preference data, (i) discovers a policy-level rubric set, and (ii) audits any rubric set under LLM-judge use along four axes: structural adequacy, reliability, preference fit, and adversarial robustness. Across rubric sources no raw source is simultaneously reliable, preference-predictive, and adversarially robust; and high inter-rater agreement does not imply low exploitability. PReMISE is the only rubric source to score non-trivially on applicability, specificity, and effective dimensionality simultaneously. We contribute two audit-targeted repair operations: preference-rank selection raises judge accuracy on paired responses from $65.0\%$ to $68.6\%$, competitive with the strongest rubric-discovery baselines and leading on two of three judges in our cross-judge sweep; reliability-constrained refinement reduces the rate at which exploit responses receive high scores from $46.4\%$ to $36.0\%$ with little change in inter-judge agreement ($α{=}.531\to.519$).

</details>


### [37] [Planner-Centric Reinforcement Learning for Deep Research with Structure-Aware Reward](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30824)
*Mustafa Anis Hussain,Xinle Wu,Yao Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DecomposeR通过将研究计划表示为类型化有向无环图（DAG），采用两阶段强化学习（规划器RL+回答器RL）训练Qwen3-8B模型，在长形式基准上超越强基线5.1-8.0分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM深度研究方法存在规划与执行难以解耦、规划过程信用分配薄弱的问题，且现有训练范式要么依赖短形式可验证QA作为代理，要么优化难以区分的单一日志轨迹。

Method: 提出DecomposeR框架，将研究计划表示为类型化DAG；两阶段训练：第一阶段规划器强化学习学习图结构和查询分解，第二阶段回答器强化学习在计划条件下学习分支级执行和最终合成；奖励分配到显式规划token和结构化组件。

Result: DecomposeR-8B在多个长形式基准上比强开源基线提升5.1-8.0个点。

Conclusion: 通过将规划显式化为结构化DAG并采用两阶段强化学习，实现了对规划过程的细粒度优化，有效提升了深度研究任务的规划与回答能力。

Abstract: Deep research tasks require LLMs to plan what to investigate, retrieve evidence, and synthesize long-form answers across multiple branches of inquiry. Existing training paradigms either rely on short-form verifiable QA as a proxy or optimize monolithic long trajectories, which makes planning and execution difficult to disentangle and yields weak credit assignment for the planning process. We propose DecomposeR, a planner-centric deep research framework that represents research plans as typed directed acyclic graphs (DAGs), allowing planning to be made explicit, structured, and rewardable. We train a Qwen3-8B model in two stages: planner reinforcement learning (RL) first learns graph structure and query decomposition to improve research planning, and answerer reinforcement learning (RL) then learns branch-level execution and final synthesis conditioned on the learned plan. By assigning rewards to explicit planner tokens and structured components rather than to a flat trajectory, DecomposeR enables finer-grained optimization of planning while reducing the ambiguity of end-to-end training. Experiments show that DecomposeR-8B improves over strong comparable open baselines by 5.1-8.0 points on popular long-form benchmarks due to improved planning and answering capabilities.

</details>


### [38] [SLAT: Segment-Level Adaptive Trimming for Efficient CoT Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30832)
*Jian Yao,Xiongcai Luo,Ran Cheng,Kay Chen Tan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SLAT框架，通过理论驱动的段级自适应剪枝策略，在大语言模型推理中减少冗余思考（overthinking），在保持准确率的同时将推理长度压缩50%，建立了更优的准确率-效率帕累托前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大推理模型在RL训练中产生冗长的链式推理（CoT），存在大量结构冗余（即过度思考），增加了计算开销却不提升答案正确性。已有的缓解方法（如token级均匀长度惩罚）过于粗粒度，会无差别压制有用推理和冗余内容。

Method: 作者首先从理论上刻画了在正确性-长度权衡目标下，推理序列中段（segment）的次优性，并发现低效推理集中出现在高概率但低边际效用的段中。基于此，提出SLAT（Segment-Level Adaptive Trimming），一个段级自适应剪枝的RL框架，根据理论判据选择性地抑制冗余段。

Result: 在标准基准测试上，SLAT相比未压缩基线将推理长度减少50%，同时保持有竞争力的准确率，建立了更优的准确率-效率帕累托前沿。

Conclusion: 理论驱动的段级感知剪枝是实现大规模语言模型高效CoT推理的一个有前景的方向。

Abstract: Recent advances in Large Reasoning Models have significantly improved chain-of-thought (CoT) capabilities via reinforcement learning (RL). However, generated reasoning chains frequently suffer from structural redundancy (i.e., \emph{overthinking}), incurring high computational overhead without improving answer correctness. Existing mitigation strategies typically rely on token-uniform length penalties, which provide coarse, segment-agnostic pressure toward shorter outputs and can inadvertently suppress useful reasoning alongside redundancy. To address this, we demonstrate that inefficiency concentrates in high-probability segments with low marginal utility. We derive a theoretical characterization of segment suboptimality under the correctness-length trade-off objective and propose \textsc{SLAT} (Segment-Level Adaptive Trimming), an RL framework that selectively suppresses redundant segments based on this criterion. Empirical results on standard benchmarks indicate that \textsc{SLAT} establishes a superior accuracy-efficiency Pareto frontier, reducing reasoning length by $50\%$ relative to uncompressed baselines while maintaining competitive accuracy. Overall, our results suggest that theoretically grounded, segment-aware trimming is a promising direction for efficient CoT reasoning in large language models.

</details>


### [39] [COMPASS: Cognitive MCTS-Guided Process Alignment for Safe Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30838)
*Wenkai Shen,Pengyang Zhou,Jiahe Xu,Jiaming Qian,Haozhe He,Zhihao Huang,Chaochao Chen,Xiaolin Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 COMPASS 框架，通过认知树搜索（CTE）合成隐蔽攻击轨迹，并结合内省逐步对齐（ISA）对多步智能体交互进行细粒度过程监督，在保持实用性的同时实现鲁棒安全对齐，且训练数据需求显著降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的搜索智能体在多步推理和工具使用过程中存在"检索诱导的安全退化"问题——有害意图可分解为看似无害的子查询，导致不安全后果。现有对齐方法难以捕捉稀疏安全信号，也无法有效监督多步交互中的各类违规行为。

Method: 提出 COMPASS 框架，包含两个核心模块：1) 认知树探索（CTE），高效合成隐蔽攻击轨迹；2) 内省逐步对齐（ISA），隔离风险中间动作以实现细粒度过程监督。

Result: 实证结果表明，COMPASS 在安全-效用权衡上表现优越，同时所需训练数据显著减少。

Conclusion: COMPASS 通过过程级安全对齐有效解决了多步智能体交互中的安全退化问题，在保证通用效用的同时实现了更鲁棒的安全性能，且数据效率更高。

Abstract: LLM-powered search agents enable multi-step reasoning and tool use. However, these capabilities introduce retrieval-induced safety degradation, as harmful intents may decompose into seemingly innocuous sub-queries that lead to unsafe outcomes. Existing alignment methods struggle to capture sparse safety signals and fail to supervise diverse violations across multi-step interactions. We propose COMPASS, a Cognitive MCTS-Guided Process Alignment framework designed to achieve robust safety alignment throughout the agent workflow while preserving general utility. COMPASS integrates cognitive tree exploration (CTE) to efficiently synthesize stealthy attack trajectories, and introspective step-wise alignment (ISA) to isolate risky intermediate actions for fine-grained process supervision. Empirical results show that COMPASS achieves a favorable safety-utility trade-off while requiring substantially less training data.

</details>


### [40] [Distilling LLM Feedback for Lean Theorem Proving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30861)
*Gaetan Narozniak,Gérard Biau,Rémi Munos,Ahmad Rammal,Pierre Marion*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出反馈蒸馏（Feedback Distillation）方法，通过在token级别使模型匹配自身在特权反馈条件下的分布，解决了GRPO在推理模型后训练中奖励稀疏、探索不足和模式 collapse 的问题。在Lean4定理证明任务上，该方法相比GRPO保持了更高的轨迹多样性，且两者互补：用反馈蒸馏初始化GRPO优于单独使用任一方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO等强化学习方法在推理模型后训练中存在奖励稀疏、探索受限和模式崩溃等问题，亟需更高效的训练方法。

Method: 反馈蒸馏（Feedback Distillation）：在token级别训练模型，使其分布匹配自身在语言模型产生的特权反馈条件下的分布，提供token级监督并注入外部知识。

Result: 在Lean4定理证明任务上，反馈蒸馏比GRPO保持更高的轨迹多样性（更高策略熵、更好pass@k缩放）；两者互补，用反馈蒸馏初始化GRPO超越单独任一方法。

Conclusion: 反馈蒸馏为复杂推理的后训练提供了有前景的改进方向。

Abstract: Post-training for reasoning models typically combines supervised fine-tuning with reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards, most commonly with GRPO. However, this algorithm suffers from sparse rewards, limited exploration, and mode collapse. Building upon recent works on self-distillation, we propose Feedback Distillation, a training method where the model is trained to match, at the token level, its own distribution conditioned on privileged feedback produced by a language model. Feedback Distillation offers token-level supervision and can inject external knowledge. Evaluating our method for Lean4 theorem-proving, we find that Feedback Distillation maintains greater diversity in generated trajectories than GRPO, yielding higher policy entropy and better pass@k scaling. The two methods are complementary: initializing GRPO from a Feedback Distillation checkpoint outperforms either method alone. All in all, our results suggest a promising avenue to improve post-training for complex reasoning.

</details>


### [41] [UniScale: Adaptive Unified Inference Scaling via Online Joint Optimization of Model Routing and Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30898)
*Kaiyu Huang,Xingyu Wang,Mingze Kong,Zhubo Shi,Yuqian Hou,Hong Xu,Zhongxiang Dai,Minchen Yu,Qingjiang Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出统一推理缩放框架UIS，将模型路由与测试时缩放整合为单一优化空间，并基于LinUCB在线学习策略UniScale，实现细粒度、更优的质量-成本权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将模型路由（离散切换不同规模模型）与测试时缩放（固定模型内调整推理计算量）分离设计，导致前者粒度粗、性能跳跃，后者存在容量上限与边际收益递减，两者割裂限制了动态推理环境下的适应性。

Method: 将UIS建模为上下文多臂老虎机问题，采用LinUCB算法在线学习推理策略；引入效率感知学习与成本建模，确保高维动作空间下的稳定可扩展优化。

Result: UniScale在多种动态推理场景下有效利用UIS空间的协同效应，实现了比现有方法更细粒度且一致更优的质量-成本权衡。

Conclusion: 统一模型路由与测试时缩放能够突破各自局限，通过在线学习实现更灵活高效的推理决策。

Abstract: In real-world deployments of large language models (LLMs), balancing inference quality and computational cost has become a central challenge. Existing approaches tackle this trade-off along two largely independent dimensions: model routing, which switches among models of different scales to match request complexity, and test-time scaling (TTS), which adjusts inference-time compute within a fixed model for fine-grained control. However, this decoupled design introduces inherent limitations. Model routing yields coarse-grained, discrete performance changes due to the sparse set of model scales, while single-model TTS often encounters capacity ceilings and exhibits diminishing returns as compute increases. Moreover, treating the two mechanisms separately restricts adaptability in dynamic inference environments. To overcome these limitations, we introduce Unified Inference Scaling (UIS), which unifies model routing and TTS in a single optimization space. Building on this formulation, we propose UniScale, an online framework that models adaptive UIS as a contextual multi-armed bandit problem and learns inference policies via LinUCB. The framework incorporates efficiency-aware learning and cost modeling to ensure stable and scalable optimization over high-dimensional action spaces. Evaluation shows that UniScale effectively exploits the synergy in the UIS space to deliver a fine-grained and consistently better quality-cost trade-off across diverse, dynamic inference scenarios.

</details>


### [42] [BilliardPhys-Bench: Benchmarking Physical Reasoning and Visual Dynamics of Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30900)
*Ben Wang,Xiaogang Li,Ruochen Gao,Peiyao Xiao,Chengliang Xu,Zeyu Wang,Zichao Chen,Bing Zhao,Hu Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 BilliardPhys-Bench，一个基于台球模拟的物理推理基准，用于评估多模态大模型在物体运动预测方面的能力。实验发现，现有 MLLM（GPT、Claude、Gemini、Qwen 系列）在碰撞预测、轨迹推理等任务上表现不佳，存在“静态偏差”——当物理结果难以推断时，模型倾向于预测无交互。该基准揭示了当前视觉动态推理的短板，强调了引入物理归纳偏置的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态模型在静态图像识别上表现优异，但在直观物理推理（如物体如何运动与交互）方面仍然薄弱，缺乏系统化的基准来评估和诊断这一问题。

Method: 构建 BilliardPhys-Bench 基准，基于程序化台球模拟引擎生成随机场景，包含摩擦和弹性碰撞物理；设计三类推理任务（球间碰撞、墙壁反弹、最终位置估计）；在 GPT、Claude、Gemini、Qwen 等主流 MLLM 上进行评估。

Result: 模型性能随模拟时间增长和场景几何复杂度增加而下降；发现一致的失败模式——"静态偏差"（stasis bias），即模型在物理结果难以推断时倾向于预测无交互。

Conclusion: 当前多模态大模型在视觉动态推理上存在明显局限，需要在多模态架构中引入更好的物理归纳偏置以提升物理推理能力。

Abstract: Current multimodal models handle static image recognition well, but intuitive physical reasoning remains a weakness. Predicting how objects will move and interact from a single image is still difficult for these systems. We present BilliardPhys-Bench, a benchmark for physical reasoning in synthetic billiards environments. Its procedural engine generates randomized scenarios with friction and elastic collisions. The benchmark tests three abilities: (1) predicting ball-to-ball collisions, (2) reasoning about wall bounces, and (3) estimating final ball positions after motion stops. We evaluate recent MLLMs from the GPT, Claude, Gemini, and Qwen families. Performance drops as simulation time increases and scene geometry grows more complex. We also observe a consistent failure mode we call "stasis bias": when the correct physical outcome is harder to infer, models tend to predict no interaction. These findings show where current MLLMs break down on visual dynamics and point toward the need for better physical inductive biases in multimodal architectures.

</details>


### [43] [A Persona-Based Evaluation Framework for Pluralistic Alignment in Generative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31021)
*Atahan Karagoz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于状态空间约束的AI评估框架，使用合成认知画像（evaluative personas）替代单一评估函数，实现多元视角的基准测试。同时揭示了这些模拟评估者在连续推理和随机提示扰动下存在一致性退化（状态空间漂移和语义不一致），论证了需要在生成系统中嵌入动态、可存活性驱动的调节机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI对齐范式依赖单一基准测试框架，将人类判断的多元性简化为聚合统计基线，忽视了文化、人口统计和语境差异，无法反映真实世界中的人类观点多样性。

Method: 提出状态空间约束的仿真框架，在潜在表征流形上构建结构化合成认知画像集合，利用现代生成架构实例化并维持多种评估者角色（evaluative personas），实现多元视角依赖的基准测试。

Result: 现代生成架构能以较高一致性实例化和维持评估者角色；但在连续推理和随机提示扰动下，角色一致性出现系统性退化，表现为状态空间漂移和语义不一致。静态对齐约束不足以维持稳定的评估行为。

Conclusion: 需要在生成系统中嵌入动态、可存活性驱动的调节机制；将基于角色的评估视为潜在表征流形上的结构化动力系统，是实现更自适应、更对齐、更上下文敏感的AI评估的基础方向。

Abstract: Current alignment paradigms for generative artificial intelligence rely predominantly on monolithic benchmarking frameworks that reduce the plurality of human judgment to aggregated statistical baselines, thereby obscuring cultural, demographic, and contextual variability in evaluation. We introduce a state-space constrained emulation framework for AI evaluation that replaces singular assessment functions with a structured manifold of synthetic cognitive profiles representing diverse human perspectives. We show that modern generative architectures can instantiate and maintain these evaluative personas with high consistency, enabling a form of pluralistic, perspective-dependent benchmarking that more closely reflects real-world consensus variability. However, we further analyze the stability of these simulated evaluators under sequential inference and stochastic prompt perturbations, revealing systematic degradation in persona coherence that manifests as state-space drift and semantic inconsistency. These findings suggest that static alignment constraints are insufficient for sustaining robust evaluative behavior over time. Instead, we argue for the necessity of embedding dynamic, viability-driven regulatory mechanisms within generative systems to preserve coherent cognitive emulation. By framing persona-based evaluation as a structured dynamical system over latent representation manifolds, this study provides a foundation for more adaptive, human-aligned, and context-sensitive approaches to AI evaluation.

</details>


### [44] [HADT: A Heterogeneous Multi-Agent Differential Transformer for Autonomous Earth Observation Satellite Cluster](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31023)
*Mohamad A. Hady,Muhammad Anwar Masum,Siyi Hu,Mahardhika Pratama,Jimmy Cao,Ryszard Kowalczyk*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Transformer架构的强化学习方法，用于异构卫星集群在地球观测任务中的自主资源管理，通过关系型观测-动作令牌化和差分注意力机制，在实验中显著优于基线方法并展现出良好的可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于数学模型的调度方法在空间任务动态变化和不确定性环境下效果不佳，因此需要一种无需模型、能实现自适应实时决策的资源管理方法。

Method: 将问题建模为序贯决策过程，采用无模型强化学习；提出基于Transformer的新型架构，包含关系型观测-动作令牌化（relational observations-actions tokenization）和差分注意力机制（differential attention mechanism）。

Result: 与现有基线方法相比，所提出架构在性能上有显著提升，并且在不同数量的卫星集群场景下表现出强适应性和迁移能力。

Conclusion: 基于Transformer的强化学习架构能有效解决异构卫星集群自主地球观测中的资源管理问题，具备良好的可扩展性和迁移性，为未来自主卫星任务管理提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: This work addresses the problem of autonomous resource management in heterogeneous satellite cluster conducting Earth Observation (EO) missions including optical and Synthetic Aperture Radar (SAR) satellites. In autonomous operation mode, satellites are equipped with intelligent capabilities enabling real-time decision-making based on the latest conditions, while requiring minimal interaction with ground operators. Traditional scheduling approaches typically rely on mathematical models to represent satellite mission and resource management. Then, this problem is solved by using optimization algorithms. However, such solutions become less effective when the underlying models are not available, over complex, and inaccurate due to dynamic changes and uncertainties inherent in the space mission environment. A promising alternative is to reformulate the problem as a sequential decision-making process and apply model-free reinforcement learning techniques to enable adaptive and real-time resource management. To this end, we propose a novel transformer-based architecture tailored for heterogeneous satellite cluster autonomous EO Mission with relational observations-actions tokenization and differential attention mechanism. Our experimental results demonstrate significant performance improvements compared to the available baselines. Moreover, the proposed architecture exhibits strong adaptability and transferability with respect to varying numbers of satellite clusters.

</details>


### [45] [GraphARC: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Graph-Based Abstract Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31031)
*Saku Peltonen,August Bøgh Rønberg,Andreas Plesner,Roger Wattenhofer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GraphARC 是一个面向图结构数据的抽象推理基准，将 ARC 的少样本变换学习范式推广到图领域，涵盖局部、全局和层次图变换。实验表明，语言模型在图属性问答上表现尚可，但在完整图变换任务上存在明显的"理解-执行鸿沟"，且规模扩展时性能显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有抽象推理基准（如 ARC）局限于网格或文本格式，缺乏对图结构数据的系统性评估。图数据上的关系推理是智能的核心，但缺乏一个可规模化的、覆盖多样图变换的基准来评估模型的抽象推理和泛化能力。

Method: GraphARC 将 ARC 的少样本变换学习范式推广到图结构数据。每个任务从少量输入-输出图对中推断变换规则并应用于新测试图，涵盖局部（节点/边修改）、全局（图结构重排）和层次（子图组合）三类图变换。通过不同图家族和尺寸实现规模化生成。

Result: 评估表明，当前最先进的语言模型在图属性问答任务上表现良好，但在完整的图变换推理任务上表现有限，暴露出"理解-执行鸿沟"；在更大规模的图实例上性能进一步退化，揭示了扩展性障碍。

Conclusion: GraphARC 将节点分类、链接预测和图生成等多方面任务统一在单一框架下，为未来图基础模型提供了有前景的测试平台，并揭示了当前模型在图结构上的抽象推理能力不足。

Abstract: Relational reasoning lies at the heart of intelligence, but existing benchmarks are typically confined to formats such as grids or text. We introduce GraphARC, a benchmark for abstract reasoning on graph-structured data. GraphARC generalizes the few-shot transformation learning paradigm of the Abstraction and Reasoning Corpus (ARC). Each task requires inferring a transformation rule from a few input-output pairs and applying it to a new test graph, covering local, global, and hierarchical graph transformations. Unlike grid-based ARC, GraphARC instances can be generated at scale across diverse graph families and sizes, enabling systematic evaluation of generalization abilities. We evaluate state-of-the-art language models on GraphARC and observe clear limitations. Models can answer questions about graph properties but often fail to solve the full graph transformation task, revealing a comprehension-execution gap. Performance further degrades on larger instances, exposing scaling barriers. More broadly, by combining aspects of node classification, link prediction, and graph generation within a single framework, GraphARC provides a promising testbed for future graph foundation models.

</details>


### [46] [Vector Linking via Cross-Model Local Isometric Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31100)
*Ziying Chen,Yang Cao,He Sun,Beining Yang,Tianjian Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究向量链接问题：给定由不同黑盒编码器在部分重叠数据集上生成的两个嵌入云，仅利用向量恢复跨模型对象对应关系。实验和理论表明，独立训练的对比编码器表现出局部几何一致性（短距离近似保持比例），而长距离因模型特定失真而不一致。为此提出基于迭代参考的几何嵌入哈希方法，通过少量配对锚点种子集恢复向量链接。该方法利用到采样锚点的距离表示向量，通过哈希空间匹配提出候选链接，并在Beta-Bernoulli后验中跨视图聚合证据，将高置信度链接自举为新锚点。实验证明该方法在不同重叠度、种子预算和域外锚点下均准确鲁棒，可应用于向量数据库集成和跨模型聚类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖向量对齐或共享监督信号来建立跨模型对应关系，但现实场景中编码器是黑盒的，且仅提供嵌入向量而不暴露模型参数或训练数据，迫切需要一种仅利用向量本身恢复跨模型对象映射的无监督方法。

Method: 提出VecLinking框架：1) 理论分析证明局部几何一致性（短距离近似保持比例，长距离失真）；2) 以少量配对锚点为种子，用距离向量表示每个嵌入点；3) 在哈希空间中匹配候选链接；4) 利用Beta-Bernoulli后验跨视图聚合证据，迭代自举新锚点逐步扩展链接集合。

Result: 在多个基准和编码器对上的实验表明，该方法在不同数据集重叠率、种子锚点数量和域外锚点条件下均能准确、鲁棒地建立跨模型链接，并成功应用于向量数据库集成和跨模型聚类任务。

Conclusion: 证实了对比编码器嵌入空间存在局部几何一致性，并基于此提出了一种高效、不依赖模型内部信息的向量链接方法，为异构嵌入系统的互通提供了实用方案。

Abstract: We study Vector Linking: given two embedding clouds produced by different black-box encoders over partially overlapping datasets, recover cross-model object correspondences using only vectors. Empirically and theoretically, we show that independently trained contrastive encoders exhibit local geometric consistency: short-range distances are approximately preserved up to a scale factor, while long-range distances are not due to model-specific distortion. Building on this, we propose an iterative, reference-based geometric embedding hashing that recovers vector links from a tiny seed set of paired anchors. It represents each vector by distances to sampled paired anchors, proposes candidate links via hash-space matching, and aggregates evidence across views in a Beta-Bernoulli posterior to bootstrap high-confidence links as new anchors. Experiments across multiple benchmarks and embedding model pairs demonstrate accurate and robust linking under varying overlap, seed budgets, and out-of-domain anchors, with applications to vector database integration and cross-model clustering. Code is available at https://github.com/DBgroup-Edinburgh/VecLinking.

</details>


### [47] [LLM-FACETS: A Privacy-Preserving Framework for Evaluating LLM Transparency and Accountability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31167)
*Tom Lucas,Alessio Buscemi,Alfredo Capozucca,German Castignani,Barbara Delacroix*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLM-FACETS 是一个面向非技术人员的开源 LLM 评估框架，提供浏览器界面和插件架构，支持确定性指标本地运行与 LLM-judge 指标可控外联，通过 token 级 log 概率可视化、多裁判共识和 RAG Triad 机制实现透明度，并已在 18 个指标上完成交叉验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 审计工具对非技术从业者（如领域专家、合规官员）不友好：需要编程技能和复杂环境配置，且云平台会将评估数据传至外部服务，存在隐私和法规障碍。

Method: 提出 LLM-FACETS 框架，包含：(1) 基于三种用户画像（技术专家、领域专家、合规官员）的角色映射；(2) 确定性指标本地自托管运行，LLM-judge 指标显式调用外部 API；(3) token 级 log 概率可视化、多裁判共识、RAG Triad 指标；(4) 插件架构支持新指标/数据集集成。

Result: 通过 18 个指标实现与规范参考库的交叉验证，验证了框架在不同指标类型下的正确性。

Conclusion: 该框架降低了 LLM 审计门槛，使非技术合规人员也能进行可复现的评测，并解耦 AI 问责与模型开发团队，符合 EU AI Act 和 NIST AI RMF 的要求。

Abstract: Assessing whether Large Language Models outputs are factually grounded, epistemically calibrated, and methodologically reproducible is a prerequisite for responsible AI deployment. Yet auditing LLMs remains inaccessible to non-technical practitioners: existing tools require programming expertise and non-trivial environment setup, and cloud-hosted platforms transmit evaluation data to external services, creating barriers for domain experts and compliance officers legally responsible for AI oversight. We introduce LLM-FACETS (LLM FActuality Cross-EvaluaTion System): an open-source framework with a browser-accessible interface and a plugin architecture, structured around three practitioner profiles (technical experts, domain experts, compliance officers) that mirror the stakeholder categories identified in the EU AI Act and the NIST AI Risk Management Framework. The architecture makes data flows explicit: deterministic metrics (BLEU, ROUGE, BERTScore) run entirely within the self-hosted server with no outbound transmission; LLM-judge metrics contact external APIs explicitly, with users retaining full credential control. The framework operationalizes transparency through three mechanisms: token-level log-probability visualization for epistemic uncertainty, multi-judge consensus to mitigate judge bias, and RAG Triad metrics (Faithfulness, Answer Relevance, Context Relevance) to detect and localize hallucinations. A plugin architecture allows any new metric or dataset to be integrated without modifying the evaluation pipeline. The open-source implementation enables cross-checking across multiple metrics targeting the same property, ensuring reproducibility and decoupling AI accountability from the teams building the systems assessed. We verify the framework through cross-validation of 18 metric implementations against canonical reference libraries.

</details>


### [48] [Formalizing and falsifying causal pathways of rare events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31254)
*Anahita Haghighat,Dominik Janzing*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出因果路径的形式化定义及其在稀有事件场景下的可检验含义，并引入一种因果抽象方法，将完整因果图简化为稀有事件路径，从而桥接简单因果解释与详细因果建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有根因分析的形式化工作主要关注稀有事件（异常值）在结构方程模型中的识别，但缺乏对因果路径本身的形式化定义及其可检验含义的系统探讨，特别是当这些含义仅依赖于稀有事件路径的因果抽象而非完整因果图时。

Method: 基于稀有事件根因分析的最新形式化成果，提出因果路径的形式化定义，推导其可检验含义，并构建一种将完整因果结构抽象为稀有事件路径的因果抽象方法。

Result: 识别出因果路径的可检验含义在某些条件下仅依赖于稀有事件路径所定义的因果抽象，而非整个系统的完整因果图；引入了一种连接简单因果解释与详细因果建模的稀有事件路径因果抽象。

Conclusion: 该工作为稀有事件场景下的因果解释提供了形式化基础，通过路径级抽象降低了因果推理的复杂度，同时保持了与详细因果模型的一致性，有助于桥接定性解释与定量建模之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Building on recent formalizations of root cause analysis for rare events (``outliers'') in structural equation models, we propose a formal definition of a causal pathway and discuss its testable implications. We identify conditions under which these implications depend only on a causal abstraction defined by the pathway of rare events, rather than on the full causal graph of the underlying system. Accordingly, we introduce an abstraction of causal structure to pathways of rare events that bridges simple verbal causal explanations and detailed causal modeling.

</details>


### [49] [COLLEAGUE.SKILL: Automated AI Skill Generation via Expert Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31264)
*Tianyi Zhou,Dongrui Liu,Leitao Yuan,Jing Shao,Xia Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出COLLEAGUE.SKILL系统，一种自动将人物或角色的异构痕迹蒸馏为可检查、可修正、可被AI代理使用的版本化技能包的工作流。技能包包含能力轨道（实践、心智模型、决策启发）和有界行为轨道（沟通风格、交互规则、修正历史），支持检查、调用、自然语言更新、回滚、跨宿主部署及受控分发。开源仓库约18.5k星标，已收录215个技能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理不仅需要完成孤立任务，还需承载人类专业知识、判断和交互风格的有界表征。但构建此类人格化代理仍然困难，因为与人物或角色相关的可操作知识通常嵌入在异构痕迹中，而非以清晰指令形式存在。现有记忆与人格系统仅捕获碎片化证据，技能框架提供可移植的封装格式，但缺乏将痕迹蒸馏为可检查、可修正且代理可用的端到端工作流。

Method: 提出COLLEAGUE.SKILL自动化痕迹到技能蒸馏系统。给定目标人物或角色的材料，生成版本化技能包，包含两个协调轨道：能力轨道（实践、心智模型、决策启发）和有界行为轨道（沟通风格、交互规则、修正历史）。技能包支持检查、调用、自然语言反馈更新、回滚、跨代理宿主安装及受控分发。系统包含工件契约、生成工作流、修正生命周期、部署面和领域预设。

Result: 开源仓库约18.5k GitHub星标；画廊列出215个技能，来自165位贡献者；累计超过100k星标。

Conclusion: 该系统展示了人格化技能可以表示为可移植、可修正的包，而非不透明的提示词或隐藏的记忆，提供了一种可检查、可修正、代理可用的技能蒸馏与部署方案。

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly expected not only to complete isolated tasks, but also to carry bounded representations of human expertise, judgment, and interaction style. Building such person-grounded agents remains difficult because actionable knowledge associated with a person or role is usually embedded in heterogeneous traces rather than written as clean instructions. Existing memory and persona systems capture fragments of this evidence, while skill frameworks provide portable packaging formats; however, there is no end-to-end workflow for distilling these traces into inspectable, correctable, and agent-usable skills. We present an automated trace-to-skill distillation system for generating person-grounded AI skills via expert knowledge distillation. Given materials from a target person or role, COLLEAGUE.SKILL produces a versioned skill package with two coordinated tracks: a capability track for practices, mental models, and decision heuristics, and a bounded behavior track for communication style, interaction rules, and correction history. The package can be inspected, invoked, updated through natural-language feedback, rolled back, installed across agent hosts, and optionally prepared for controlled distribution. We describe the artifact contract, generation workflow, correction lifecycle, deployment surface, and domain presets implemented in the open-source system. At the time of writing, the public repository has approximately 18.5k GitHub stars; the gallery lists 215 skills from 165 contributors and more than 100k cumulative stars across listed skill cards. The system illustrates how person-grounded skills can be represented as portable, correctable packages rather than opaque prompts or hidden memories.

</details>


### [50] [Industrializing Prediction-Powered Inference: The GLIDE Library for Reliable GenAI and Agentic Systems Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31278)
*Grégoire Martinon,Ibrahim Merad,Mohammed Raki*
