Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GLIDE是一个开源Python库，统一了多种预测驱动推断（PPI）估计器和采样器，用于对智能体系统进行可靠、无偏的评估，并配有可复现的蒙特卡洛验证套件和方法选择决策树。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体系统的可靠评估需要无偏估计和有效的不确定性度量，但标准实践在昂贵的人工标注和有偏的LLM裁判代理之间权衡。PPI方法可以结合两者得到去偏估计和有效置信区间，但现有方法分散在各论文中，实现不完整。

Method: GLIDE将多种PPI估计器（PPI++、分层PPI、Predict-Then-Debias及其分层变体、主动统计推断）和采样器（均匀、分层、主动、成本最优）统一在scipy风格的API下，专用于均值估计。同时提供可复现的蒙特卡洛验证套件、基于经验的方法选择决策树。

Result: 通过智能体评估案例研究，GLIDE在同等精度下实现了显著的标注成本节省。

Conclusion: GLIDE提供了一个统一、开源的PPI工具库，降低了可靠评估的门槛，为智能体系统评估提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Reliable evaluation of agentic systems requires unbiased estimates with valid uncertainty, but standard practice navigates between costly human annotation and biased LLM-as-judge proxies. Prediction-powered inference (PPI) combines both into debiased estimates with valid confidence intervals, yet its various methods remain scattered across papers under partial implementations. We introduce GLIDE, an open-source Python library that unifies state-of-the-art PPI estimators (PPI++, Stratified PPI, Predict-Then-Debias and its stratified variants, Active Statistical Inference) and samplers (uniform, stratified, active, cost-optimal) under a scipy-style API specialized to mean estimation. GLIDE ships with a reproducible Monte Carlo validation suite, an empirically grounded decision tree for method selection, and an agentic evaluation case study showing substantial annotation savings at equivalent precision. The GLIDE package is available at this URL: https://github.com/EmertonData/glide

</details>


### [51] [TraceGraph: Shared Decision Landscapes for Diagnosing and Improving Agent Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31308)
*Junjie Nian,Kang Chen,Ge Zhang,Yixin Cao,Yugang Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TraceGraph是一种基于图的框架，通过构建共享决策景观来剖析多模型智能体轨迹，识别高产核心区和陷阱区域，并利用这些结构信息提升基准性能（如SWE-bench上解决率从40.4%提升至43.5%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体基准测试虽记录了丰富的交互轨迹，但评估方式往往简化为通过率或奖励分数，忽视了对轨迹过程的结构化分析，难以揭示模型在决策空间中的行为差异和失败模式。

Method: TraceGraph首先从多模型轨迹池中构建基于可观察动作-状态的有向图，在引入模型身份前完成图构建；然后叠加结果信息标记高产核心区和陷阱区；接着将每条轨迹总结为三个关键事件：访问（Access）、陷阱暴露（Trap exposure）和修复（Repair）。此外，在SWE-bench上利用陷阱区域信息设计运行时检测器，在匹配历史陷阱区域的状态上启动轻量级续写策略。

Result: 在五个基准测试分片上，TraceGraph揭示了被聚合分数掩盖的导航差异，表明不同分片在奖励避陷阱还是修复陷阱上存在差异。在SWE-bench上，最优池化单因子策略将官方解决率从40.4%提升至43.5%（按提供者划分的触发子集），从41.0%提升至44.8%（共同触发实例），且不同提供商的活跃组件各异。

Conclusion: TraceGraph提供了一种过程化语言，用于探究智能体基准测试究竟在测试什么、模型在共享景观上的分歧点，以及失败区域如何指导后续改进。

Abstract: Agent benchmarks increasingly record rich interaction trajectories, yet evaluation often reduces each rollout to a pass rate or reward score. We introduce TraceGraph, a graph-based framework that turns released multi-model agent trajectories into shared decision landscapes. For each task, TraceGraph builds a graph over observable action-observation states from pooled rollouts before model identity is introduced. It then overlays outcome-informed productive cores and trap regions, and summarizes each rollout with three events: Access, Trap exposure, and Repair. Across trajectories spanning five benchmark splits, TraceGraph profiles reveal navigation differences hidden by aggregate scores and show that splits differ in whether they reward avoiding traps or recovering from them. The same TraceGraph landscape also motivates a trap-aware recovery pipeline for SWE-bench: aruntime detector fires on states matching historical trap regions, then lightweight continuation policies are evaluated from the same prefix. On fired states, the best pooled single-factor policy raises official resolved rate from 40.4% to 43.5% on the per-provider fired subset and from 41.0% to 44.8% on common-fired instances, with provider-specific active components. Overall, TraceGraph provides a process vocabulary for asking what agent benchmarks test, where models diverge on a shared landscape, and how failure regions can guide downstream improvement.

</details>


### [52] [Diagnosing Failure Modes of Shared-State Collaboration in Resource-Constrained Visual Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31354)
*Yunpeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究弱模型（4B-8B参数）在模块化视觉推理系统中的协作失败机制，提出CoSee审计框架，发现共享工作记忆会导致噪声强化和政策崩溃两种主要失败模式，并指出资源受限场景下通信保真度比推理深度更关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模块化视觉推理系统依赖共享工作记忆进行多步协作，但低容量条件下中间状态演化的失败动力学尚未被充分研究。

Method: 提出CoSee审计框架，形式化读-写-验证循环以追踪文档视觉问答中的信息流，在跨页面、图表和网页基准上进行实验。

Result: 发现共享工作空间会放大幻觉而非解决它；识别出噪声强化（未锚定备注被当作证据重用）和政策崩溃（额外上下文使模型转向不明确短答案）两种失败模式；通过成本-精度帕累托前沿证明增加计算量可能与性能负相关。

Conclusion: 对于资源受限的智能体，瓶颈不在于推理深度而在于通信保真度，提供了追踪级诊断和可靠模块化设计的机制基线。

Abstract: Modular visual reasoning systems increasingly rely on shared working memory for multi-step collaboration, yet the failure dynamics of intermediate state evolution in low-capacity regimes remain underexplored. We study failure modes of collaborative reasoning with weak learners (4B--8B models) through the lens of noise accumulation. We introduce CoSee, an auditing framework that formalizes the read-write-verify loop to trace information flow in document visual question answering. Across multi-page, chart, and web-based benchmarks, we find a counter-intuitive degradation: naive shared workspaces often amplify hallucinations rather than resolve them. We identify two dominant failure modes: Noise Reinforcement, where ungrounded notes are reused as evidence, and Policy Collapse, where added context shifts the model toward under-specified, short-form answers. Using cost-accuracy Pareto frontiers, we show that increased compute can correlate negatively with performance without explicit verification. Our findings suggest that for resource-constrained agents, the bottleneck lies not in reasoning depth but in communication fidelity, providing trace-level diagnostics and a mechanistic baseline for reliable modular design.

</details>


### [53] [Learning to Adapt: Self-Improving Web Agent via Cognitive-Aware Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31365)
*Weile Chen,Bingchen Miao,Qifan Yu,Wendong Bu,Guoming Wang,Wenqiao Zhang,Shengyu Zhang,Juncheng Li,Siliang Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SCALE框架，通过三个对抗角色（Selector、Predictor、Judger）实现Web代理的自主认知边界扩展与探索，结合SCALE-Hop图探索策略避免局部陷阱，并构建SCALE-20k数据集，显著提升了多模态大模型在Web环境中的性能和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Web代理依赖手工执行的流程或昂贵的专家轨迹，难以适应复杂动态环境。

Method: 提出SCALE框架，包含三个对抗角色（Selector、Predictor、Judger）自主发现代理局限性并扩展认知边界；提出SCALE-Hop图探索策略实现全局规划、避免局部探索陷阱；构建SCALE-20k大规模数据集（来自19个真实网站）。

Result: 显著提升了多种多模态大语言模型在不同Web环境中的性能和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该框架为构建真正自主和自适应的Web代理提供了可扩展且可泛化的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have led to promising progress in web agents. However, existing web agents often rely on handcrafted execution pipelines or expensive expert trajectories, limiting their adaptability to complex, dynamic environments. To address these challenges, we propose SCALE (Self-Cognitive-Aware Learning and Exploration), which leverages three adversarial roles, Selector, Predictor, and Judger to autonomously discover the agent's limitations and expand its cognitive boundaries through environmental exploration. Moreover, we propose SCALE-Hop, a graph exploration strategy that facilitates global planning and helps agents avoid local exploration traps. To further support learning, we construct SCALE-20k, a large-scale dataset collected from 19 real-world websites, containing diverse task types and structured demonstrations generated from SCALE's exploration traces. Experimental results show that our approach significantly improves the performance and generalization of multiple MLLMs in various web environments. Our framework offers a scalable and generalizable solution for building truly autonomous and adaptive web agents.

</details>


### [54] [HypoAgent: An Agentic Framework for Interactive Abductive Hypothesis Generation over Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31370)
*Yisen Gao,Yixi Cai,Tianshi Zheng,Jiaxin Bai,Yangqiu Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HypoAgent是一个基于Agent框架的交互式溯因假设生成系统，包含意图识别、假设生成和根因分析三个智能体，在多轮对话中实现知识图谱上的可控假设生成与诊断，在常识和生物医学知识图谱上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可控假设生成方法在交互式场景中存在两个局限：难以在多轮对话中理解不断演化的自然语言意图，且在假设生成失败时缺乏细粒度的诊断能力。

Method: 提出HypoAgent框架，包含三个Agent：1) 意图识别Agent：将用户话语和对话历史转化为可执行的KG条件；2) 假设生成Agent：根据提取的用户意图执行可控假设生成；3) 根因分析Agent：诊断不可靠的假设片段，通过KG邻域探针识别支持的修正方案。

Result: 在常识和生物医学领域知识图谱上，HypoAgent在单轮、多轮和无条件设置下均达到最先进的语义相似度。

Conclusion: HypoAgent通过多Agent协作有效解决了交互式溯因假设生成中的意图理解和错误诊断问题，验证了其在多个领域的通用性和有效性。

Abstract: Abductive reasoning over knowledge graphs aims to generate logical hypotheses that explain observed entities or facts. Existing controllable hypothesis generation methods allow users to guide this process with explicit conditions, but they remain limited in interactive settings: they struggle to ground evolving natural-language intents across multi-turn dialogues and provide little fine-grained diagnosis when generated hypotheses fail. To address these limitations, we propose HypoAgent, an Agentic framework for interactive abductive Hypothesis Generation over knowledge graphs. HypoAgent integrates three agents: an Intent Recognition Agent that grounds user utterances and dialogue history into executable KG conditions, a Hypothesis Generation Agent that performs controllable hypothesis generation according to the extracted user intention, and a Root Cause Analysis Agent that diagnoses unreliable hypothesis fragments and leverages KG neighborhood probing to identify supported refinements. Experiments on commonsense and biomedical domain-specific knowledge graphs demonstrate that HypoAgent achieves state-of-the-art semantic similarity under single-turn, multi-turn, and unconditional settings. Our code is available at https://github.com/HKUST-KnowComp/HypoAgent.

</details>


### [55] [FAM-Bench: A Multimodal Benchmark for Condition-Aware Food-as-Medicine Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31410)
*Mingyang Mao,Bhargav Rishi Medisetti,Utkarsh Grover,Tanvir Ibrahim,Wenyan Li,Tingting Zhang,Xiaomin Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出FAM-Bench基准，用于评估AI模型在具体食物选择是否适合特定健康条件方面的推理能力，包含2500个营养专家验证的实例和两类互补任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有食物AI基准主要评估菜品识别、营养估算等，缺乏对食物选择与健康条件匹配性的测试，Food-as-Medicine需要模型超越简单识别进行健康-aware推理。

Method: 构建FAM-Bench多模态基准，含2500个专家验证实例，覆盖13种饮食相关健康条件；设计两类任务：菜品级别适宜性评估（判断菜品是否适合特定条件）和比较性菜品分析（对四个候选菜品按适宜性排序）。两类任务均需整合食材证据、视觉线索和临床营养约束。

Result: 构建了标准化的测试基准，为语言和视觉-语言模型在基于证据的健康-aware推理方面提供了统一测试平台。

Conclusion: FAM-Bench填补了食物AI在健康决策层评估的空白，为Food-as-Medicine应用提供了标准化的测试基准。

Abstract: Food-as-Medicine requires models to reason beyond what a dish is or what nutrition it contains: they must decide whether a concrete food choice is appropriate for a specific health condition. Existing food AI benchmarks primarily evaluate dish recognition, recipe understanding, nutrient estimation, or general nutrition question answering, leaving this health-aware decision layer largely untested. We introduce FAM-Bench, a multi-modal Food-as-Medicine benchmark with 2500 nutrition-expert-verified instances across 13 diet-related health conditions. The benchmark contains two complementary tasks: dish-level suitability assessment, where models judge whether a dish is suitable for a condition from its image and ingredient list, and comparative dish analysis, where models rank four candidate dishes by condition-specific suitability. Both tasks require integrating ingredient evidence, visual preparation cues, and clinical nutrition constraints, providing a standardized testbed for grounded health-aware reasoning in language and vision-language models.

</details>


### [56] [Answer-Set-Programming-based Abstractions for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31444)
*Rafael Bankosegger,Thomas Eiter,Johannes Oetsch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨使用回答集编程（ASP）替代Prolog实现CARCASS框架中的关系强化学习抽象，在Blocks World和Minigrid两个领域中的案例研究表明，基于ASP的方法在领域知识可用时为构建RL抽象提供了一种有前景的方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习面临状态空间巨大、学习与泛化困难的挑战，需要抽象和近似方法。关系强化学习（RRL）通过推理对象及其关系来应对，而CARCASS框架展示了逻辑表示建模一阶域MDP的能力。原本基于Prolog实现，但Prolog并非完全声明式，因此探索使用更丰富、完全声明式的回答集编程（ASP）来实现CARCASS抽象。

Method: 使用回答集编程（ASP）替代Prolog重新实现CARCASS框架的抽象机制，在Blocks World和Minigrid两个领域进行案例研究评估。

Result: 基于ASP的CARCASS实现在两个测试领域中成功构建了抽象，表明ASP能够有效实现CARCASS框架中的逻辑抽象，尤其在领域知识可用时表现良好。

Conclusion: CARCASS with ASP为构建强化学习抽象提供了一种有前景的方法，特别适用于具备领域知识的场景。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) enables autonomous agents to learn policies from experience, but realistic problems often involve enormous state spaces, making learning and generalisation challenging. Abstraction and approximation are therefore essential. Relational Reinforcement Learning (RRL) offers a way to reason about objects and their relations, and the CARCASS framework by Martijn van Otterlo demonstrates how logical representations can model Markov Decision Processes (MDPs) in first-order domains. Originally implemented in Prolog, CARCASS leverages domain knowledge to create powerful abstractions. We explore Answer-Set Programming (ASP), which is a rich and, contrary to Prolog, fully declarative modelling language, to realise CARCASS abstractions. We evaluate our ASP-based implementation in case studies of two domains, viz. Blocks World and Minigrid. Our results indicate that CARCASS with ASP provides a promising approach to constructing abstractions for RL, especially when domain knowledge is available.

</details>


### [57] [AutoSci: A Memory-Centric Agentic System for the Full Scientific Research Lifecycle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31468)
*Weitong Qian,Beicheng Xu,Zhongao Xie,Bowen Fan,Guozheng Tang,Jiale Chen,Xinzhe Wu,Mingtian Yang,Chenyang Di,Jiajun Li,Lingching Tung,Peichao Lai,Yifei Xia,Ziyi Guo,Yanwei Xu,Yanzhao Qin,Shaoduo Gan,Xupeng Miao,Bin Cui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AutoSci 是一个以记忆为核心的智能体系统，通过四个模块（SciMem、SciFlow、SciDAG、SciEvolve）实现从文献理解到评审回复的完整科研生命周期自动化，支持持久化记忆和自我进化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统无法同时满足完整科研生命周期支持、结构化持久记忆以及研究程序自我改进三大需求，存在自动化科学研究的系统级缺口。

Method: 提出四模块架构：SciMem（模式化研究记忆，分为长期知识记忆和活跃研究记忆）、SciFlow（五阶段科研生命周期执行框架）、SciDAG（DAG结构多智能体算子与阶段模板）、SciEvolve（基于反馈信号驱动系统演化的版本更新机制）。

Result: 构建了完整的自动化科学研究系统，代码已开源，能够执行、记忆并在研究项目间持续进化。

Conclusion: AutoSci 填补了统一自动化科研系统的空白，通过记忆中心的四模块协同实现了可持久化运行和自我改进的科研环境。

Abstract: Scientific research has traditionally been human-intensive, requiring researchers to coordinate literature, ideas, experiments, manuscripts, and review responses across long project cycles. The rise of LLM-based scientific agents creates an opportunity to automate this process. Such a system must support the full research lifecycle, maintain structured persistent memory across projects, and improve its own research procedures over time. However, existing systems either partially satisfy or fail to satisfy these requirements, leaving a gap for a unified automated scientific research system. As a result, we present AutoSci, a memory-centric agentic system for the full scientific research lifecycle. AutoSci is organized around four modules. SciMem provides schema-governed research memory, separating Long-Term Knowledge Memory for reusable scientific knowledge from Active Research Memory for project-level artifacts such as ideas, experiments, manuscripts, and reviews. SciFlow executes a five-stage lifecycle from literature understanding to rebuttal through a harness that controls state, context, verification, feedback, and orchestration. SciDAG augments difficult skills with DAG-shaped multi-agent operators and reusable stage-specific templates. SciEvolve converts feedback signals from users, experiments, reviews, and external environments into versioned updates to SciMem organization, SciFlow skills, and SciDAG templates. Together, these modules make AutoSci a persistent research environment that can execute, remember, and evolve across research projects. The code repository is available at https://github.com/skyllwt/AutoSci.

</details>


### [58] [LinTree: Improving LLM Reasoning with Explicitly Structured Search Histories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31492)
*Liwei Kang,Yee Whye Teh,Wee Sun Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLM在推理过程中生成的中间轨迹可视为线性化的搜索树。研究发现，仅提供搜索历史（隐式树结构）不足以让LLM超越启发式搜索，而通过添加显式父指针（LinTree）来明确树结构，能显著提升任务性能和搜索效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLM在推理时生成的中间轨迹（搜索历史）是否比传统启发式搜索具有优势，以及这种优势的来源——即模型能否有效利用整个搜索轨迹中的全局信息（而不仅仅是当前局部状态）。

Method: 在Blocks World、grid Navigation和Sokoban三个受控推理环境中，对比以下方法：(1) LLM基于完整搜索轨迹（隐式线性化树）的推理策略；(2) 基于LLM启发函数的Best-First搜索（仅观察当前局部状态）；(3) 添加显式父指针表示线性化树结构（LinTree）的推理模型。

Result: 原始搜索历史（隐式树结构）不足以可靠地超越LLM启发式搜索。而添加显式父指针（LinTree）后，模型在任务性能和搜索效率上均优于隐式推理模型和LLM启发式引导搜索。

Conclusion: 搜索历史在显式表示其树结构时最有价值，这为设计更结构化的LLM推理表示提供了方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often solve reasoning problems by generating intermediate traces that explore and revise partial solutions. From a search perspective, these traces can be viewed as linearized search trees, where the model extends a partial solution, abandons it when it fails, and backtracks to try alternatives. Compared with traditional heuristic-guided search, such a policy has a potential advantage: it conditions on the whole search trace rather than only on the current local state. We first test whether LLMs utilize this advantage by comparing trace-conditioned reasoning policies against best-first search equipped with an LLM heuristic that only observes the current local state. Across three controlled reasoning environments, Blocks World, grid Navigation, and Sokoban, we find that raw access to search history alone is not enough to reliably outperform heuristic search. We then study one possible reason: in LLM reasoning traces, the underlying search tree is only implicitly represented, and when the model backtracks or switches branches, the trace does not explicitly identify which earlier search state is being revisited. We show that adding simple parent pointers to explicitly represent the linearized tree (LinTree) structure improves both task performance and search efficiency relative to implicit reasoning models and LLM-heuristic-guided search. These results suggest that search history becomes most useful when its tree structure is made explicit, motivating more structure-aware representations for LLM reasoning.

</details>


### [59] [Choosing the Lens: Strategic Perspective Activation in Context-Dependent Argumentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31581)
*Albert Sadowski,Jarosław A. Chudziak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出上下文依赖论证框架（CDAFs），扩展了Dung的论证理论，通过一个随上下文变化的击败函数来决定哪些攻击成功。基于视角标签的特化方法利用相关集ρ和优先级π推导击败函数。研究表明，在某些条件下，智能体可以通过部分激活实现完全激活无法达成的论证接受结果，并定义了ACTIVATION-MANIPULATION决策问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准论证形式化框架无法直接捕捉智能体通过影响外部制度（regime）来操纵论证结果的策略性杠杆，需要一种能够刻画上下文依赖性的论证模型。

Method: 提出上下文依赖论证框架（CDAFs），扩展Dung的论证理论，引入击败函数；通过视角标签（perspective-labeled）特化方法，利用相关集ρ（智能体的行动空间）和优先级π推导击败函数。

Result: 在小型工作示例中，目标论证在完全相关单射优先级下被拒绝，但在部分激活下可被接受，且其中一种部分激活模式无法被任何VAF观众（audience）所镜像。

Conclusion: 定义了ACTIVATION-MANIPULATION决策问题并给出了基准复杂度界限，但紧致界限和多智能体变体仍有待研究。

Abstract: The same arguments often need to be evaluated under different external regimes. An agent with influence over the regime has a strategic lever that standard formalisms do not directly capture. We introduce context-dependent argumentation frameworks (CDAFs), an extension of Dung's theory in which a defeat function determines, per context, which attacks succeed. A perspective-labeled specialisation derives the defeat function from a relevance set $ρ$ and a priority $π$. The relevance set is the agent's action space. In a small worked example, the agent's target argument is rejected under every full-relevance injective priority, yet accepted under partial activations, one of which no VAF audience can mirror. We define the corresponding decision problem, ACTIVATION-MANIPULATION, and record baseline complexity bounds. Tight bounds and multi-agent variants are left open.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [60] [Improved Distribution Estimation in $\ell_\infty$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30509)
*Doron Cohen,Aryeh Kontorovich,Yonatan Livshitz*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文针对 $\ell_\infty$ 范数下离散概率分布的估计问题，提出了改进的极小极大期望界和高概率尾界，解决了 Kontorovich 和 Painsky (JMLR, 2025) 提出的若干开放问题，包括最紧风险界的全经验版本以及最坏极端分布的形式，并给出了有鼓励性的实验结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决 Kontorovich 和 Painsky (JMLR, 2025) 中关于 $\ell_\infty$ 范数下离散分布估计的开放问题，特别是获得全经验版本的最紧风险界和确定最坏极端分布的形式。

Method: 通过理论分析推导 $\ell_\infty$ 范数下离散概率分布估计的改进极小极大期望界和高概率尾界，并给出全经验版本的风险界以及最坏极端分布的形式。

Result: 得到了改进的极小极大期望界和高概率尾界，解决了 Kontorovich 和 Painsky 提出的开放问题，并提供了有鼓励性的实验结果。

Conclusion: 本文在 $\ell_\infty$ 范数下离散分布估计问题上取得了理论进展，解决了先前工作中的开放问题，实验结果支持了理论的有效性。

Abstract: We present improved bounds for estimating discrete probability distributions under the $\ell_\infty$ norm. These include minimax bounds in expectation and high-probability tail bounds. We resolve some of the open questions posed in Kontorovich and Painsky (JMLR, 2025) -- including a fully empirical version of the tightest risk bound they presented and identifying the form of the worst-case extremal distribution. Encouraging empirical results are reported as well.

</details>


### [61] [Reward Learning from Best-of-$N$ Preference Data: Targets, Tradeoffs, and Design Principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30619)
*Rattana Pukdee,Maria-Florina Balcan,Pradeep Ravikumar*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了Best-of-N采样生成的成对偏好数据在Bradley-Terry奖励学习中的理论性质，推导了独立参考变体下的闭式奖励目标，揭示了实际变体中BT可表示性的失效与渐近恢复，发现了N值对边际和连通性的权衡作用，并提出了相应的设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Best-of-N采样被广泛用于生成成对偏好数据，但从中进行BT奖励学习究竟提取了什么、以及如何选择采样规模N和基分布，这些问题尚不明确。

Method: 将偏好数据通过诱导条件分布的近期分析框架特化到Best-of-N场景，推导独立参考变体的闭式奖励目标，分析Best-vs-Random和Best-vs-Worst等实际变体的BT可表示性，研究边际和连通性随N变化的权衡关系。

Result: 独立参考变体下推导出奖励目标与N和基分布的显式函数关系，且保留潜在奖励排序；实际变体中BT精确可表示性一般失败，但随着N增长有界类最小化器趋近参考目标；N增大拓宽边际但降低连通性，形成权衡。

Conclusion: 提出两条设计原则：偏好标签为瓶颈时用大N，生成为瓶颈时用小N；调整基分布在测试时最重要的响应之间集中质量。实验验证了样本量和基分布形状的预测依赖性。

Abstract: Best-of-$N$ sampling is widely used to construct pairwise preference data: $N$ candidates are drawn from a base distribution, and the best is paired with a rejected response. Despite its widespread use, what Bradley--Terry (BT) reward learning extracts from such data, and how to choose $N$ and the base distribution, remain unclear. We specialize a recent analysis of preference data via its induced conditional distribution to Best-of-$N$. For independent-reference variants, we derive closed-form reward targets as explicit functions of $N$ and the base distribution, and show that they preserve the latent reward ranking. For the practical Best-vs-Random and Best-vs-Worst variants, chosen and rejected responses are coupled through the same candidate set, so exact BT representability generally fails; nevertheless, bounded-class minimizers approach the reference targets as $N$ grows. Although margin and connectivity are known to govern sample efficiency in pairwise preference learning, Best-of-$N$ couples them through $N$ in opposing directions: larger $N$ widens pairwise margins but reduces connectivity. This trade-off yields two design principles: use larger $N$ when preference labels are the bottleneck, smaller $N$ when generation is the bottleneck; and shape the base distribution to place mass between the responses whose comparison matters most at test time. Experiments on synthetic and real preference data support the predicted dependence on sample size and base-distribution shape.

</details>


### [62] [Is the Last Layer Sufficient for Uncertainty Quantification?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30741)
*Joseph Wilson,Chris van der Heide,Liam Hodgkinson,Fred Roosta*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文通过理论分析和大量实验证明，在深度神经网络的认知不确定性量化中，最后一层线性化近似（仅对最后一层进行贝叶斯广义线性模型处理）与全网络线性化相比，性能几乎相当，但计算效率显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络在关键任务场景中的安全部署需要有效的认知不确定性量化，现有方法常通过线性化DNN构建贝叶斯广义线性模型（GLM）来实现，其中对最后一层进行线性化近似是常用做法，但人们普遍认为这种近似会以性能下降为代价。本文旨在从理论和实证两个角度，严格比较全网络线性化与最后一层线性化在UQ性能上的差异。

Method: 采用两种互补方法：1）理论层面，利用随机矩阵理论工具对两种线性化方法进行形式化比较分析；2）实证层面，在多种现代机器学习任务上进行大规模评估，对比全网络线性化与最后一层线性化在不确定性量化性能上的表现。

Result: 理论分析表明，全网络线性化相比最后一层线性化在UQ能力上没有有意义的提升；大规模实证评估进一步证实，最后一层近似能够在保持可比UQ性能的同时，提供显著更优的计算效率。

Conclusion: 最后一层线性化近似是实践中更优的选择，它在几乎不牺牲认知不确定性量化性能的前提下，大幅降低了计算负担，为深度神经网络在安全关键场景中的实际部署提供了更高效的不确定性量化方案。

Abstract: Epistemic uncertainty quantification (UQ) for deep neural networks (DNNs) is a requirement for safe adoption of AI in mission-critical settings. Several leading methods for UQ linearize DNNs to form Bayesian Generalized Linear Models (GLMs), where epistemic uncertainty is modeled via the predictive posterior distribution. Linearizing around the parameters of the final connected layer of a DNN is a commonly used approximation for reducing the computational burden of such GLMs, though it is often believed to come at the cost of degraded performance. In this work, we compare GLMs arising from full-network and last-layer linearization using both theoretical and empirical approaches. We first employ tools from random matrix theory to conduct a theoretical comparison; this analysis reveals no meaningful improvement in the UQ capabilities of full linearization. Coupled with a large-scale empirical evaluation across a range of modern machine learning tasks, we arrive at the following conclusion: a last-layer approximation yields comparable UQ performance while offering substantially improved computational efficiency.

</details>


### [63] [Batched Stochastic Linear Bandits with 1-Bit Communication Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30976)
*Ivan Lau,Daniel McMorrow,Kevin Jamieson,Jonathan Scarlett*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究随机线性赌博机在批次和通信约束下的问题：每个批次中智能体只能通过1比特反馈向学习器传递信息。作者建立了最小最大下界，并提出了两种基于相位消除的算法，证明单比特反馈足以在广泛参数范围内接近无约束线性赌博机的后悔值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究要么关注每轮量化（per-round quantization），要么关注总比特预算（total bit budgets），但两者之间—即每个批次仅允许1比特反馈—的中间地带尚未被探索。本文旨在填补这一空白，研究在批次大小为B的设定下，单比特通信瓶颈对线性赌博机后悔值的根本限制。

Method: 建立了最小最大下界 $\Omega(B\min\{d,\log|\mathcal{A}|\})$（无噪声时不可避免），结合统计极限得到 $\widetilde{\Omega}(B\min\{d,\log|\mathcal{A}|\} + \sqrt{dT\min\{d,\log|\mathcal{A}|\}})$。提出两种基于G-最优设计和1比特均值估计的相位消除算法：算法1达到 $\widetilde{O}(dB + d\sqrt{T})$；算法2通过安全臂识别与热启动获得 $\widetilde{O}(B\log|\mathcal{A}| + d^{3/2}\sqrt{B} + \sqrt{dT\log|\mathcal{A}|})$。

Result: 算法1在臂集大小为 $\exp(\Omega(d))$ 时匹配下界（对数因子内）；算法2在 $(\mathcal{A}, B, d, T)$ 的广泛缩放场景下接近最优。证明1比特反馈足以在批次大小达 $\Theta(\sqrt{T})$ 时接近无约束线性赌博机的后悔值。

Conclusion: 单比特反馈在批次通信约束下足以在广泛参数范围内接近无约束线性赌博机的最小最大后悔值，填补了每轮量化和总比特预算两种极端模型之间的理论空白。

Abstract: We study stochastic linear bandits under a natural combination of batching and communication constraints: the time horizon is partitioned into batches of equal size $B$, and during each batch the learner sends $B$ requested arm pulls to an agent, who then observes the corresponding $B$ rewards and responds with a single bit of feedback to the learner. For each batch, the learner specifies the 1-bit quantization rule the agent uses, which may depend on all previously received bits but not on any past rewards directly. This setting addresses a significant yet unexplored ``middle ground'' between previous models having per-round quantization only or total bit budgets only. We establish a minimax lower bound showing that $Ω(B\min\{d,\log\lvert \mathcal{A} \rvert\})$ regret is unavoidable due to the 1-bit communication bottleneck, even in the absence of noise. Combined with standard statistical limits, this yields a general lower bound of $\widetildeΩ(B\min\{d,\log\lvert \mathcal{A} \rvert\} + \sqrt{dT \min\{d,\log\lvert \mathcal{A} \rvert\}})$. We develop two phased-elimination algorithms based on $G$-optimal designs and 1-bit mean estimation. The first achieves $\widetilde{O}(dB + d\sqrt{T})$ regret, matching the lower bound up to logarithmic factors when $\lvert \mathcal{A} \rvert = \exp(Ω(d))$, and the second incorporates a safe-arm identification and warm-start procedure to obtain $\widetilde{O}(B\log\lvert \mathcal{A} \rvert + d^{3/2}\sqrt{B} + \sqrt{dT\log\lvert \mathcal{A} \rvert})$ regret, which is near-optimal in broad scaling regimes of $(\lvert \mathcal{A} \rvert, B, d, T)$. Together, our results demonstrate that a single bit of feedback per batch suffices to nearly match the minimax regret of unconstrained linear bandits in broad scaling regimes, even for batch sizes as large as $Θ(\sqrt{T})$.

</details>


### [64] [Hedging on the Frontier: Learning New Tasks with Few Samples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30997)
*Tobias Wegel,Federico Di Gennaro,Geelon So,Fanny Yang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出利用近似弱单调性（若模型在多个基准上占优，则在新任务上也可能更优）来建模任务相关性，并在迁移学习和模型选择聚合中证明了其统计复杂度优势，包括剪枝模型类并自适应边界几何进行对冲。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面对少样本新任务时，需要利用已有的辅助信息（如相关任务的基准评估结果），但如何建模任务相关性使其既现实又可证明有效是关键问题。

Method: 基于经验观察的近似弱单调性假设，将其引入迁移学习和模型选择聚合两种范式，利用单调性剪枝模型类，并通过边界对冲自适应地利用可用折衷的几何结构。

Result: 不仅可以通过单调性剪枝模型类，还能进一步通过边界对冲自适应地利用可用折衷的几何结构，从而在统计复杂度上获得可证明的提升。

Conclusion: 近似弱单调性是一种现实且有效的任务相关性建模方式，能够在少样本学习场景下提供理论保证的性能增益。

Abstract: When a learner faces a new task with few samples, it must leverage any available side information. In practice, this often comes in the form of model evaluations on related tasks in public benchmarks. A key question then is how to model task relatedness such that it is both realistic and the benchmark evaluations lead to provable gains. Empirically, we observe that weak monotonicity is often approximately satisfied: if a model dominates another on many benchmarks, it also tends to outperform on the new task. We explore the statistical complexity of learning under (approximate) weak monotonicity, leveraging it within two learning paradigms: transfer learning and model selection aggregation. We show that not only can we prune the model class based on monotonicity, but we can also further adapt to the geometry of the available trade-offs by hedging on the frontier.

</details>


### [65] [Routing on the Stiefel Manifold: When Does Adaptive Subspace Selection Help for Cross-Domain EEG Decoding?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31043)

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出动态Stiefel路由机制，通过Stiefel流形上的专家投影滤波器池和交叉注意力路由实现跨域EEG解码。针对该机制会退化为集成平均的问题，引入三个结构性属性（对称锚点、冻结域判别查询编码器、解耦键对齐损失），在三个数据集上一致提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黎曼深度学习虽取得进展，但跨域EEG解码仍面临挑战：不同被试的协方差矩阵占据SPD流形上系统不同的区域，现有域适应方法要么需要目标域标定数据，要么学习无法跨域泛化的被试特定成分。

Method: 提出动态Stiefel路由：在Stiefel流形上维护K个专家投影滤波器，每个滤波器专精于SPD流形不同区域，通过交叉注意力将输入协方差路由到最合适的滤波器，实现逐样本的自适应子空间投影。进一步引入三个结构性属性打破退化为均匀集成平均的简并性：对称锚点W_base、冻结的域判别查询编码器、解耦的键对齐损失。

Result: 在三个数据集上，平衡准确率分别从0.773提升至0.823、0.757提升至0.809、0.801提升至0.839，且对齐策略由单一数据驱动规则自动确定，无需数据集特定的超参数搜索。

Conclusion: 提出了首个在SPD流形上真正具有承诺性（committed）和域结构化（domain-structured）的路由机制，通过三种结构性设计避免了朴素路由的退化问题，显著提升了跨域EEG解码性能。

Abstract: Cross-domain EEG decoding remains challenging despite advances in Riemannian deep learning: covariance matrices from different subjects occupy systematically distinct regions of the SPD manifold, yet existing domain adaptation methods either require target-domain calibration data or learn subject-specific components that cannot generalise across domains. We propose dynamic Stiefel routing: a pool of $K$ expert projection filters on the Stiefel manifold, each specialised for a different region of the SPD manifold, with each input covariance routed to the most appropriate filter via cross-attention, adapting the subspace projection per sample. A central finding is that this approach, implemented naively, provably collapses to ensemble averaging: when routing weights are uniform, the adaptive filter reduces exactly to an equal-contribution combination of experts, indistinguishable from a single fixed filter. Three structural properties break this degeneracy: a symmetric anchor $W_{\mathrm{base}} \in \mathrm{St}(n,k)$ that removes proximity bias among experts; a frozen domain-discriminative query encoder that decouples routing from task optimisation; and a decoupled key alignment loss that trains expert keys toward stable domain attractors. Together they produce the first genuinely committed and domain-structured routing on SPD manifolds, with consistent gains across three datasets: balanced accuracy improves from $0.773\to 0.823$, $0.757\to 0.809$, and $0.801\to 0.839$, with the alignment strategy determined automatically by a single data-driven rule and no dataset-specific hyperparameter search.

</details>


### [66] [Free energy Estimation on Any State Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31063)

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通用神经传输学习方法，将自由能估计从连续空间推广到任意状态空间（离散、多模态、自回归），并揭示了时间反演与Doob h-变换构成的广义二面体群代数结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自由能估计是物理学和统计学中的基础难题，现有方法（直接估计、准静态积分、有限时间平均）效率有限，近期神经传输方法在有限时间场景下取得突破但局限于连续空间，需要推广到更一般的状态空间。

Method: 基于神经传输学习框架，将其推广到任意状态空间；通过建立无穷小时间反演与广义Doob h-变换之间的代数恒等式，揭示其群论结构（构成广义二面体群）。

Result: 实验验证了该方法在离散空间、多模态空间和自回归设置中的有效性和效率，超越了连续空间的限制。

Conclusion: 提出的广义神经传输学习框架能高效处理任意状态空间的自由能估计问题，同时发现的群论结构为理解相关变换提供了代数基础。

Abstract: Free energy estimation is a fundamental yet challenging problem, from physics to statistics. Classical approaches rely on thermodynamic transformations, ranging from direct estimation, quasistatic integration, to finite-time averaging. Recent work [He and Du et al., 2025] learns neural transports to significantly accelerate the efficiency in the finite-time regime. In this paper, we generalize this framework to arbitrary state spaces. Building on this view, we develop a generalized neural transport learning approach for efficient estimation. Experiments validate the effectiveness and efficiency of the proposed method beyond continuous settings, extending to discrete and multimodal spaces as well as autoregressive settings. Beyond free energy estimation, we establish algebraic identities and reveal a group-theoretic structure linking infinitesimal time reversal and generalized Doob's $h$-transforms, showing that their compositions form a generalized dihedral group.

</details>


### [67] [Approximation and learning of anisotropic and mixed smooth functions by deep ReLU neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31152)
*Yunfei Yang,Jun Fan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了深度ReLU神经网络对光滑函数的逼近和学习效率，将超逼近率从各向同性Besov空间推广到各向异性和混合光滑函数类，并证明了在相应条件下可达到最优极小化速率（至多相差对数因子）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究在Besov空间下得到了超逼近率$\mathcal{O}((WL)^{-2s/d})$，但其依赖于维度$d$，存在维度灾难问题。为了克服这一限制，需要将该结果推广到各向异性和混合光滑函数类，从而获得与维度无关或更优的逼近速率。

Result: 各向异性Besov空间下逼近率为$\mathcal{O}((WL)^{-2\tilde{s}})$，其中平均光滑度$\tilde{s}=(\sum_{i=1}^d s_i^{-1})^{-1}$；混合光滑Besov空间下逼近率为$\mathcal{O}((WL)^{-2s})$（至多对数因子）。深度ReLU网络可在广泛光滑函数类上实现至多相差对数因子的极小化最优速率。

Conclusion: 将超逼近率从各向同性推广到各向异性和混合光滑函数类，有效克服了维度灾难，为深度ReLU网络在更广泛函数类上的最优性提供了理论保证。

Abstract: This paper studies how efficiently deep ReLU neural networks can approximate and learn smooth functions. When the error is measured in $L^p([0,1]^d)$ norm and the approximator is a network with width $W$ and depth $L$, recent works have proven the supper approximation rate $\mathcal{O}((WL)^{-2s/d})$ for Besov space $\mathcal{B}^s_{q,r}([0,1]^d)$ under the Sobolev embedding condition $s/d>1/q-1/p$. In order to overcome the curse of dimensionality in this rate, we extent this result to anisotropic and mixed smooth function classes. We establish the approximation rate $\mathcal{O}((WL)^{-2\tilde{s}})$ for anisotropic Besov space $\mathcal{B}^{\boldsymbol{s}}_{q,r}([0,1]^d)$ with anisotropic smoothness $\boldsymbol{s}=(s_1,\dots,s_d)$ under the embedding condition $\tilde{s} > 1/q-1/p$, where the mean smoothness $\tilde{s} = (\sum_{i=1}^d s_i^{-1})^{-1}$. For mixed smooth Besov space $\mathcal{MB}^s_{q,r}([0,1]^d)$ with mixed smoothness $s>1/q-1/p$, we show that the approximation rate $\mathcal{O}((WL)^{-2s})$ holds up to logarithmic factors. Using these results, we also derive approximation bounds for the composition of anisotropic Besov functions. As an application, it is shown that deep ReLU neural networks can achieve minimax optimal rates up to logarithmic factors for a wide range of smooth function classes.

### [68] [Memory by Design: Probabilistic Sequence Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31163)
*Matthew Dowling,Hyungju Jeon,Cristina Savin,Il Memming Park*

Main category: stat.ML

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从显式的记忆假设出发推导高效的递归序列模型，统一理解现有的次二次递归模型，并提升模型在训练分布外的鲁棒性和长上下文检索能力。

Method: 提出设计模型框架，将序列模型建模为精确贝叶斯滤波（写入记忆）加查询相关读出。在线性-高斯假设下实现贝叶斯层，同时传播均值和协方差，协方差用于调控写入方向和增益。

Result: 线性注意力、GLA、Mamba-2/SSD被统一为同一设计模型下的精确滤波器；DeltaNet等Delta规则模型是协方差重置的简化。恢复协方差的方法在碰撞研究、关联记忆召回和Zoology MQAR基准上提升了鲁棒性，蒸馏后的贝叶斯层在RULER长上下文检索中优于匹配计算量的Gated DeltaNet。

Conclusion: 设计模型框架为推导和理解高效递归序列模型提供了统一视角，恢复完整的协方差信息有助于提升模型鲁棒性和长上下文能力。

Abstract: We introduce the design-model framework: a way to derive efficient recurrent sequence maps from explicit assumptions about memory. A design model writes evidence into memory by exact Bayesian filtering; a query-dependent readout produces a predictive distribution whose mean is the layer output. In our linear-Gaussian instantiation, the \emph{Bayesian Layer} propagates both a mean and a covariance: the covariance tracks uncertainty over stored associations, steering writes toward uncertain directions, attenuating gains as evidence accumulates, and preserving confident memories. The same framework unifies several sub-quadratic recurrences. Linear attention, GLA, and Mamba-2/SSD are exact filters under one design model, whereas DeltaNet and related Delta-rule models arise as covariance-reset reductions under another. Restoring the covariance yields closed-form predictions for retrieval dynamics, verified empirically, and improves robustness beyond the training regime across controlled collision studies, learned associative recall, and the Zoology MQAR benchmark; distilling Bayesian Layers into a pretrained 340M Gated DeltaNet improves RULER long-context retrieval at matched compute.

</details>


### [69] [Correcting Split Selection in Online Decision Trees via Anytime-Valid Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31239)
*Salim I. Amoukou,Saumitra Mishra,Manuela Veloso*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 针对现有流数据集成学习方法中Hoeffding树分裂决策依赖固定样本浓度界、缺乏有效统计保证的问题，本文提出一种基于任意时间有效推断的原则性替代方案，能在任意数据流（包括非平稳环境）下实现错误分裂的任意时间控制，在存在预测优势时保证有限承诺时间，并在平稳i.i.d.数据下保证风险单调递减且每次分裂严格改善。实验表明，该方法在非平稳流上使用独立树及自适应随机森林时，能提升性能并生成更小的树。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Bagging的流数据集成方法（如自适应随机森林）依赖Hoeffding树作为基学习器，但分裂决策使用数据依赖的停止规则，而现有分析基于固定样本浓度界，导致其统计保证失效，甚至可能使错误分裂概率趋近于1。亟需一种具有有效统计保证的替代方案。

Method: 提出基于任意时间有效推断（anytime-valid inference）的树分裂方法，利用连续测试或赌博机不等式等工具，使得分裂决策在任意停止时间下仍保持有效统计控制，替代传统固定样本Hoeffding界。

Result: 在非平稳流数据上，该方法无论是作为独立树还是集成到自适应随机森林中，均能提升预测性能，同时生成显著更小的决策树。

Conclusion: 任意时间有效推断为流数据决策树分裂提供了原则性的统计保证框架，解决了传统方法中因数据依赖停止规则导致的保证失效问题，在理论和实证上均优于现有方法。

Abstract: Bagging-based ensembles, most notably Adaptive Random Forests, are among the strongest performers for learning from data streams. A common denominator across these methods is their reliance on Hoeffding Trees as base learners, which grow decision trees incrementally by testing whether a candidate split is significantly better than its alternatives using concentration inequalities. Despite their empirical success, existing variants lack valid statistical guarantees. Current analyses rely on fixed-sample concentration bounds, while split decisions are made using data-dependent stopping rules, which invalidates their guarantees and can drive the probabilty of incorrect splits to one. We introduce a principled alternative based on anytime-valid inference. Our method provides: (i) anytime-valid control of false splits under arbitrary data streams, including non-stationary settings; (ii) finite commitment time under a predictive advantage; and (iii) under stationary i.i.d. data, risk is monotone decreasing and strictly improves at every split. Empirically, we evaluate both standalone trees and their use within Adaptive Random Forests on non-stationary streams. Our method improves performance while producing substantially smaller trees.

</details>

*Salim I. Amoukou,Emanuele Albini,Tom Bewley,Saumitra Mishra,Manuela Veloso*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出Entropic Projection Alignment (EPA)统一框架，通过匹配选定的矩并最小化KL散度，同时解决分布偏移下的性能估计、特征归因和目标域性能提升三个关键问题，在理论和实验中均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布偏移场景中存在三个关键挑战：在无标签目标域上估计模型性能、识别导致偏移的特征、以及提升目标域性能。现有方法通常分别处理这些问题，缺乏统一有效的解决方案。

Method: EPA方法将源分布向目标分布对齐，通过匹配精心选择的矩同时最小化与源分布的KL散度。该公式导出重要性权重的闭式解，并通过隐式方差控制实现鲁棒性。基于领域自适应理论，证明矩匹配足以实现可靠的估计和自适应，无需恢复完整的密度比。

Result: 大量实验表明EPA在性能估计、特征归因和目标域性能提升三个任务上均一致优于最先进基线方法，同时具备显著的计算效率优势，并提供了强理论保证。

Conclusion: 通过矩匹配与KL散度最小化的联合优化，EPA提供了一种理论上坚实、计算高效且经验上优越的统一框架，有效解决了分布偏移下的核心挑战。

Abstract: We propose a unified framework for addressing three key challenges of distribution shift: (1) estimating a model's performance on an unlabeled target domain, (2) explaining the shift by identifying the features responsible, and (3) improving the target domain performance. Our method, Entropic Projection Alignment (EPA), aligns the source distribution to the target by matching carefully selected moments while simultaneously minimising the KL divergence from the source. This formulation yields a unique closed-form solution for importance weights, achieving robustness through implicit variance control. Drawing on domain adaptation theory, we establish that moment matching is sufficient for reliable estimation and adaptation, avoiding the need for full density ratio recovery. Extensive experiments, together with strong theoretical guarantees, demonstrate that EPA consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines while offering substantial computational efficiency.

</details>


### [71] [Log-Ratio Propagation on the Simplex: A Theory of Cellwise Contamination for Compositional Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31345)
*Matthias Templ*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文针对成分数据的细胞层面污染问题，建立了基于乘法扰动和传播定理的尺度不变污染理论，揭示了单一部分污染在log-ratio空间中引发秩一移位，并证明标准欧几里得细胞方法在单纯形上的不适用性，同时推导出单纯形上细胞崩溃值的降低因子(D-1)/D，以及变异矩阵的细胞影响函数诊断特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成分数据必须通过log-ratio分析，但中心化log-ratio变换中几何均值的引入使得单一成分污染会同时偏移所有坐标向量。现有欧几里得细胞层面鲁棒方法直接应用于log-ratio坐标时，未考虑单纯形特有的污染机制，理论上不成立。因此需要建立一套针对单纯形的细胞污染理论，为开发真正的细胞鲁棒方法奠定基础。

Method: 构建基于乘法扰动的尺度不变污染模型；提出传播定理证明单一部分污染导致log-ratio向量秩一移位；分析MCD、S、τ、坐标M估计等估计量的细胞崩溃值；推导变异矩阵的细胞影响函数。

Result: 1) 单一成分污染在log-ratio空间产生由对比矩阵方向决定的秩一移位；2) 该污染模式不等价于log-ratio坐标下的任何独立细胞污染模型；3) 单纯形上细胞崩溃值相比欧几里得对应值降低(D-1)/D因子；4) 变异矩阵的细胞影响函数中，污染单一成分会精确膨胀对应的一行一列。

Conclusion: 研究为单纯形上的细胞鲁棒方法奠定了理论基础，指出现有欧几里得细胞方法在单纯形上的不适用性，并提供了诊断污染成分的指纹工具。配套论文将基于传播几何开发细胞鲁棒PCA估计器。

Abstract: Compositional data must be analysed through log-ratios: scale invariance, the defining axiom of the field, leaves no alternative. The centred log-ratio divides by the geometric mean of every part, so a single contaminated component shifts every centred-log-ratio coordinate at once, displacing the log-ratio vector by a fixed amount that no choice of coordinates can reduce. We develop a theory of cellwise contamination on the simplex around this observation. A scale-invariant contamination model built from multiplicative perturbation combines with a propagation theorem showing that corruption of a single raw part induces a rank-one shift of the log-ratio vector, with direction determined by the contrast matrix. The resulting perturbation pattern is not equivalent to any independent cellwise contamination model in log-ratio coordinates -- so standard Euclidean cellwise methods applied to log-ratios are ill-posed under the simplex contamination mechanism. For estimators whose Euclidean cellwise breakdown is witnessed by a column-concentrated configuration -- a class including MCD, $S$-, $τ$-, and coordinate-wise $M$-estimators of location and scatter -- the cellwise breakdown value on the simplex is reduced by the factor $(D-1)/D$ relative to its Euclidean counterpart, a reduction that is tight and arises purely from the normalisation mismatch between $nD$ raw cells and $n(D-1)$ ilr cells. The cellwise influence function for the variation matrix carries a diagnostic fingerprint: contamination of a single part inflates exactly one row and column, identifying the responsible component. These results form the theoretical foundation for cellwise-robust methods on the simplex; a companion paper develops a cellwise-robust PCA estimator that exploits the propagation geometry and demonstrates it on simulated and geochemical data.

</details>

### [72] [QASM-Eval: A Dataset to Train and Evaluate LLMs on OpenQASM-3 Beyond Quantum Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30358)
*Zhenxiao Fu,Lei Jiang,Fan Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QASM-Eval是首个专门用于训练和评估大语言模型在OpenQASM-3硬件级编程（包含中电路测量、时序控制、脉冲控制等NISQ时代关键特性）能力的数据集，包含100个专家验证测试任务和4000个训练任务。评估表明现有LLM在OpenQASM-3任务上表现困难，而针对性微调可带来显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管OpenQASM-3提供了面向NISQ时代硬件的编程接口（如中电路测量、经典反馈、精确时序控制、脉冲级波形访问等），且LLM在代码生成方面进展迅速，但尚缺乏专门针对OpenQASM-3高级硬件特性进行训练和评估的数据集。

Method: 构建了一个包含100个专家验证测试任务和4000个训练任务的数据集QASM-Eval，覆盖经典逻辑、时序调度、脉冲控制和复杂真实工作流四个维度；并设计了扩展验证器，通过语法检查、量子态验证和程序时间线验证三种方式自动验证生成程序的正确性。

Result: 现有最先进LLM在OpenQASM-3硬件级编程任务上表现困难，但通过QASM-Eval进行针对性微调后，模型性能获得显著提升。

Conclusion: QASM-Eval为NISQ时代硬件层面量子编程的可靠LLM助手开发提供了关键的基准测试和训练基础，填补了OpenQASM-3硬件特性编程数据集的空白。

Abstract: Quantum computing remains in the Noisy Intermediate-Scale Quantum (NISQ) era, where the performance is highly constrained to noise. Addressing the limitation often requires hardware-facing capabilities beyond gate-sequence circuit specification, including mid-circuit measurement and classical feedback for quantum error correction (QEC), precise timing control for dynamical decoupling (DD), and pulse-level waveform access for calibration. OpenQASM-3 was introduced to expose exactly these capabilities, providing a hardware-level programming interface. However, despite the rapid progress of large language models in code generation, there is still no dataset specifically designed to train and evaluate LLMs on OpenQASM-3 programs that involve its advanced hardware-oriented features. To address this gap, we introduce QASM-Eval, the first comprehensive dataset designed to train and evaluate LLMs on OpenQASM-3. Rather than focusing on quantum algorithm design or reasoning, QASM-Eval explicitly targets the language's hardware-facing features. QASM-Eval comprises an expert-verified test set of 100 tasks and a training set of 4,000 tasks, systematically covering classical logic, timing scheduling, pulse control, and complex real-world workflows. To automatically validate generated programs, we check syntax, quantum states and program timeline using an extended verifier. Our evaluation reveals that while state-of-the-art LLMs struggle heavily in OpenQASM-3 coding tasks, targeted fine-tuning on QASM-Eval yields significant gains. QASM-Eval provides a crucial benchmark and training foundation to accelerate the development of reliable LLM assistants for hardware-facing quantum programming in NISQ era. Data and code: https://github.com/fuzhenxiao/QASM-Eval

</details>


### [73] [Gait2Hip-60: A Unified Deep Learning Benchmark for Predicting Hip Muscle Forces and Joint Moments from Multi-Cadence Gait Kinematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30374)
*Jiaqi Zhang,Ji Hou,Qing Sun,Xianzhi Gao,Bo Huo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于深度学习的框架，利用LSTM、Transformer和Mamba三种序列模型直接从下肢步态运动学预测髋关节肌肉力和关节力矩。在60名健康成年人数据上，Transformer表现最佳（肌肉力R²=0.819，力矩R²=0.862），并在股骨头坏死患者的零样本外部验证中保持中等预测能力（R²=0.537和0.569），证明了从步态运动学估计髋关节动力学的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的肌肉骨骼仿真方法虽然能估算髋关节肌肉力和关节力矩，但耗时长、难以在临床环境中推广应用，因此需要一种更高效的方法来直接从步态运动学数据预测这些动力学参数。

Method: 收集60名健康成年人在三种节拍器引导步频下的步态数据，将十项双侧下肢关节角度作为输入，OpenSim计算的髋关节肌肉力和力矩作为参考输出，在统一协议下训练并比较LSTM、Transformer和Mamba三种深度学习模型，然后将最优模型直接测试于9名股骨头坏死患者（零样本迁移）。

Result: 在健康人群基准测试中，Transformer综合表现最优：肌肉力预测RMSE=1.33 N/kg、MAE=0.57 N/kg、R²=0.819；力矩预测RMSE=0.11 Nm/kg、MAE=0.07 Nm/kg、R²=0.862。在股骨头坏死患者零样本外推中，Transformer仍保持中等预测能力：肌肉力R²=0.537，力矩R²=0.569。

Conclusion: 从步态运动学估计髋关节动力学是可行的，Transformer可作为强基线模型，但临床应用前仍需更广泛的病理数据验证和改进模型的泛化能力。

Abstract: Estimating hip muscle forces and joint moments during gait typically relies on musculoskeletal simulation, which is informative but time-consuming and difficult to apply in clinical settings. This study developed a deep learning framework to predict these hip dynamics parameters directly from lower-limb gait kinematics and compared three representative sequence models under a unified protocol. Gait data were collected from 60 healthy adults under three metronome-guided cadence conditions. Ten bilateral lower-limb joint angles were used as inputs, and OpenSim-derived hip muscle forces and hip joint moments were used as reference outputs. Three deep learning models of LSTM, Transformer, and Mamba were trained and evaluated using the same subject-level split, preprocessing pipeline, and metrics. The best model was then directly tested on an external cohort of 9 patients with osteonecrosis of the femoral head (ONFH) without retraining. In the healthy-subject benchmark, Transformer achieved the best subject-level mean performance for both hip muscle force prediction (RMSE = 1.33 N/kg, MAE = 0.57 N/kg, R2 = 0.819) and hip joint moment prediction (RMSE = 0.11 Nm/kg, MAE = 0.07 Nm/kg, R2 = 0.862), with similar advantages across walking cadences. In zero-shot external validation, Transformer retained moderate predictive ability in ONFH for hip muscle force prediction (RMSE = 1.51 N/kg, MAE = 0.70 N/kg, R2 = 0.537) and hip joint moment prediction (RMSE = 0.17 Nm/kg, MAE = 0.12 Nm/kg, R2 = 0.569). These findings support the feasibility of estimating hip dynamics from gait kinematics, identify Transformer as a strong baseline, and highlight the need for broader pathological validation and improved generalization before clinical application.

</details>


### [74] [Unicorn: Scaling High-Dimensional Time Series Forecasting via Universal Correlation Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30376)
*Haochen Yuan,Yichen Song,Yunbo Wang,Xiaokang Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Unicorn是一种用于高维时间序列的通用相关网络框架，通过潜在原型码本将相关性建模与具体通道身份解耦，实现跨异构数据集的可扩展预训练，在少样本迁移场景中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列架构存在根本性权衡：通道独立模型可扩展性强但忽略跨通道依赖关系，而通道依赖模型表达能力强但受限于维度，难以在异构数据集间泛化。作者旨在弥合这一差距，构建可扩展的多数据集预训练框架。

Method: 核心是一个潜在原型码本（latent prototype codebook），将异构通道投影到共享潜在空间，学习与具体通道身份无关的、可复用的交互模式，从而解耦相关性建模与通道身份。

Result: 在多种实验设置下，Unicorn显著优于最先进的预测架构，尤其在少样本迁移场景中表现突出。

Conclusion: Unicorn提供了一条通向多变量时间序列基础模型的可扩展路径，有效解决了通道独立与通道依赖方法之间的权衡问题。

Abstract: Modern time series architectures face a fundamental trade-off: channel-independent models scale well with increasing data volume but ignore critical inter-channel dependencies, while channel-dependent models are expressive but remain ``dimension-bounded'', struggling to generalize across heterogeneous datasets.To bridge this gap, we introduce Unicorn (Universal Correlation Network), a framework for scalable, multi-dataset pretraining on high-dimensional time series. At the core of Unicorn is a latent prototype codebook that decouples correlation modeling from specific channel identities. By projecting heterogeneous channels into a shared latent space, UniCorN learns identity-agnostic, reusable interaction patterns that transfer across domains with diverse dimensionalities and semantics. Extensive experiments show that Unicorn significantly outperforms state-of-the-art forecasting architectures, particularly in few-shot transfer scenarios, offering a scalable path toward multivariate time series foundation models.

</details>


### [75] [When LLMs Learn to Be Consistently Wrong: A Multi-Model Study of Linear Representations of Synthetic Deception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30381)
*Vahideh Zolfaghari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过LoRA微调五种Transformer模型（Pythia-1.4B, Gemma-2-2B/9B, Qwen2.5-7B, Llama-3.1-8B），研究合成性不诚实（synthetic dishonesty）的表征基础。线性探针在早期层即可近乎完美检测不诚实（AUC≥0.99），支持线性表征假说；探针在TruthfulQA和MMLU间零损失泛化。机制分析揭示两种表征模式：Pythia/Llama/Qwen的表征坍缩与Gemma-2的高维保持。不诚实方向在深层逐步凝聚，1-4层可实现最优校准（ECE<0.01）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欺骗性对齐是AI安全的核心挑战，但真实世界中的战略性欺骗难以受控研究。本文通过直接优化错误答案的方式诱导"合成性不诚实"，以提供可复现、可控的实验框架，研究学习型欺骗的表征基础及其可检测性。

Method: 使用LoRA对五种Transformer模型（Pythia-1.4B, Gemma-2-2B/9B, Qwen2.5-7B, Llama-3.1-8B）分别在诚实/不诚实变体上进行微调。训练线性探针和MLP探针检测隐藏状态中的不诚实信号；进行TruthfulQA到MMLU的跨域泛化测试；系统分析Fisher判别比、有效秩、质心几何、方向稳定性、跨域对齐和校准误差（ECE）等机制指标。

Result: 线性探针在1-3层即可达到近完美检测（AUC≥0.99），Pythia仅达0.705；逻辑回归探针始终匹配或超越MLP探针；TruthfulQA训练的探针跨域泛化至MMLU几乎零损失（ΔAUC≈0）；Gemma-2模型深层表征对高斯噪声具有极强鲁棒性；机制分析发现Pythia/Llama/Qwen出现表征坍缩，而Gemma-2保持高维结构；不诚实方向在深层逐步凝聚，1-4层即可实现ECE<0.01的校准。

Conclusion: 通过适度的监督微调，鲁棒且域不变的不诚实表征可以快速固化在模型早期层中，这既为基于激活的监控提供了可行性路径（早期层检测），也警示了简单微调即可在多种架构中嵌入可泛化的欺骗表征。

Abstract: Deceptive alignment, in which models maintain accurate internal representations while deliberately producing false outputs, remains a central challenge in AI safety. While strategic deception is the primary long-term concern, synthetic dishonesty - induced via direct optimization on incorrect answers - provides a controlled testbed for studying the representational basis of learned deception. We introduce a multi-model paradigm in which honest and deceptive variants of five transformer models (Pythia-1.4B, Gemma-2-2B/9B, Qwen2.5-7B, Llama-3.1-8B) are fine-tuned using LoRA on the same question distribution. Linear probes trained on mean-pooled hidden states detect synthetic dishonesty with near-perfect AUC (greater than or equal to 0.99) as early as layers 1-3 in four architectures, while Pythia-1.4B reaches a peak of 0.705. Logistic regression probes consistently match or outperform MLP probes, supporting the Linear Representation Hypothesis. Probes trained on TruthfulQA generalize with near-zero loss (Delta AUC approx. 0) to held-out MMLU subjects. Late-layer representations show strong robustness to Gaussian noise, with Gemma-2 models exhibiting exceptional stability. Mechanistic analysis of Fisher Discriminant Ratio, effective rank, centroid geometry, directional stability, cross-domain alignment, and calibration (ECE) reveals two regimes: representational collapse in Pythia/Llama/Qwen versus high-dimensional preservation in Gemma-2. Across all models, the dishonesty direction consolidates progressively in deeper layers, with optimal calibration (ECE less than 0.01 except Pythia) achievable in layers 1-4. These results demonstrate that robust, domain-invariant dishonesty representations can be rapidly entrenched via modest supervised fine-tuning, with implications for activation-based monitoring.

</details>


### [76] [LLMs Without Deep Neural Networks: New Architecture, Benefits and Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30385)
*Vincent Granville*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于RBF网络的深度神经网络替代方案，该方案无需迭代训练，能在一次迭代中通过闭式解找到损失函数的全局最优解，从而消除了繁琐的训练步骤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为作者独立发现的深度神经网络替代方案提供验证，该方案在LLM背景下具有更高的可解释性和准确率。

Method: 基于RBF网络的机制，但与标准方法不同，无需深度神经网络，通过闭式解在单次迭代中找到损失函数的全局最优解。

Result: 消除了繁琐的训练步骤，实现了单次迭代的全局最优解求解。

Conclusion: 该技术可作为标准DNN的高效替代方案，尤其适用于LLM场景，具备更高的可解释性和准确性。

Abstract: The purpose of this article is to provide validation to my deep neural network alternative in the context of LLMs. Very recently, there has been a significant interest by Chinese researchers in a model called RBF network, as a substitute to standard DNNs, with increased explainability and higher accuracy. It turns out that my new model, discovered independently, is based on the exact same machinery. But with a major twist: it does not need DNN as it finds the global optimum of the loss function in closed form, in one iteration, thus eliminating the tedious training step. Here I provide a high-level overview of my technology, with case study and comparison to similar methods.

</details>


### [77] [Functional MRI Time Series Generation via Wavelet-Based Image Transform and Spectral Flow Matching for Brain Disorder Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30387)
*Hwa Hui Tew,Junn Yong Loo,Fang Yu Leong,Julia K. Lau,Ding Fan,Hernando Ombao,Raphaël C. -W. Phan,Chee Pin Tan,Chee-Ming Ting*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出双频谱流匹配（DSFM）框架，通过级联离散小波变换和离散余弦变换对fMRI BOLD信号进行频谱表示，并训练条件流匹配模型生成类条件余弦频率表示，经逆变换重建生理上可信的时域BOLD信号，以解决fMRI数据生成中非平稳性、复杂时空动态和生理变异性的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: fMRI数据采集成本高、高保真样本有限，现有生成模型难以复制BOLD信号固有的非平稳性、复杂时空动态和生理变异性。

Method: 首先通过离散小波变换（DWT）将BOLD信号转换为小波分解图以捕获全局瞬态和多尺度变化，再投影到离散余弦变换（DCT）空间以利用低频主导BOLD系数的局部能量压缩；随后训练频谱流匹配模型生成类条件余弦频率表示；最后通过逆DCT和逆DWT重建时域BOLD信号。

Result: 通过改进的下游fMRI脑网络分类任务验证了生成数据的有效性。

Conclusion: 双变换方法施加了结构化频率先验，保留了关键的生理脑动态，生成的fMRI数据在脑网络分类等下游任务中表现优异。

Abstract: Functional Magnetic Resonance Imaging (fMRI) provides non-invasive access to dynamic brain activity by measuring blood oxygen level-dependent (BOLD) signals over time. However, the resource-intensive nature of fMRI acquisition limits the availability of high-fidelity samples required for data-driven brain analysis models. While modern generative models can synthesize fMRI data, they often remain challenging in replicating their inherent non-stationarity, intricate spatiotemporal dynamics, and physiological variations of raw BOLD signals. To address these challenges, we propose Dual-Spectral Flow Matching (DSFM), a novel fMRI generative framework that cascades dual frequency representation of BOLD signals with spectral flow matching. Specifically, our framework first converts BOLD signals into a wavelet decomposition map via a discrete wavelet transform (DWT) to capture globalized transient and multi-scale variations, and projects into the discrete cosine transform (DCT) space across brain regions and time to exploit localized energy compaction of low-frequency dominant BOLD coefficients. Subsequently, a spectral flow matching model is trained to generate class-conditioned cosine-frequency representation. The generated samples are reconstructed through inverse DCT and inverse DWT operations to recover physiologically plausible time-domain BOLD signals. This dual-transform approach imposes structured frequency priors and preserves key physiological brain dynamics. Ultimately, we demonstrate the efficacy of our approach through improved downstream fMRI-based brain network classification. The code is available at https://github.com/htew0001/DSFM.git .

</details>


### [78] [A Novel Evaluation Metric for Unsupervised Learning in AIS-Based Maritime Anomaly Detection: MADQI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30388)
*Ismet Gocer,Zakirul Bhuiyan,Raza Hasan,Shakeel Ahmad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为MADQI的新型质量指标，用于在无标签数据情况下评估海上AIS数据中的异常检测性能。该框架结合了四个互连的度量标准（ARC、PPS、SDS、ECE），并通过多块评估和自适应缩放实现自动归一化，在AIS数据集上取得了80.37%的MADQI分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的无监督学习算法（如Isolation Forest）虽广泛用于检测船舶异常行为（速度异常、位置跳变、时间间隙、转向角异常），但缺乏系统且有意义的评估指标，因此需要一种无需标签数据的异常检测性能评价方法。

Method: 提出MADQI复合指数，包含四个子指标：异常率一致性（ARC）、物理合理性分数（PPS）、分数分布分离度（SDS）和极端案例证据（ECE）。通过多块评估和自适应缩放技术实现自动归一化，并利用Haversine距离计算分析AIS数据集的空间和行为特征。

Result: 在AIS数据集上，MADQI总体得分80.37%；ECE得分0.907，ARC得分1.000，表明在检测极端异常和保持异常率一致性方面表现优异。

Conclusion: 该框架为无监督异常检测在海上AIS数据中的应用提供了一种可靠且有意义的评估方法，实验结果表明其有效性强。

Abstract: This paper introduces a new systematic framework for detecting anomalies in maritime Automatic Identification System (AIS) datasets. These anomalies include abnormal vessel behaviours related to speed, position jumps, time gaps, and turn angles. Although unsupervised learning algorithms such as Isolation Forest are widely used for detecting anomalous vessel movements, they often lack systematic and meaningful evaluation measures. To address this limitation, we propose a novel quality metric called Maritime Anomaly Detection Quality Index (MADQI). The prosed MADQI is a composite index designed to evaluate the anomaly detection performance of machine learning models without requiring labelled data. The proposed framework uses Haversine distance calculations to analyse AIS datasets and identify anomalies based on their spatial and behavioural characteristics. The proposed MADQI evaluation framework integrates four interconnected metrics: Anomaly Rate Consistency (ARC), Physical Plausibility Score (PPS), Score Distribution Separation (SDS), and Extreme Case Evidence (ECE). These metrics are combined through automatic normalisation using multi-chunk evaluation and adaptive scaling techniques. Experimental results on the AIS dataset show that the proposed framework achieved a MADQI score of 80.37%, demonstrating its effectiveness for unsupervised anomaly detection. In particular, the algorithm performed strongly in identifying abnormal vessel behaviour. Among the individual MADQI components, ECE and ARC achieved scores of 0.907 and 1.000, respectively, indicating excellent capability in detecting extreme anomalies and maintaining anomaly rate consistency. Overall, these results are encouraging and demonstrate that the proposed framework provides a reliable and meaningful approach for evaluating unsupervised anomaly detection in maritime AIS data.

</details>


### [79] [NumLeak: Public Numeric Benchmarks as Latent Labels in Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30393)
*Anany Kotawala*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了NumLeak评估框架，发现顶级大模型能高精度记忆金融基准、经济指标和气候数据，并揭示了记忆渠道导致的"拒绝-回忆"不对称性，且闭源API的黑盒探测低估了这一现象；最后提出一行系统提示防御可阻断99.8%的单轮后缀攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公开数值基准可能已出现在预训练数据中，导致基于日期的评估可能衡量的是记忆回忆而非样本外能力，需要系统测量和防御这一记忆通道。

Method: 结合API边界探针（对生产模型）和白盒受控验证（在开放因果语言模型上）的双层评估框架NumLeak，以及使用logprob排名检测记忆化。

Result: 顶级模型在Fama-French市场超额回报上达到3种子池化Pearson r=0.97-0.99，五个兄弟因子在25bps内；失业率、CPI通胀、NOAA温度也有类似保真度。新发布的保留集上解析率降至21-57%，但回答月份r仍约为0.99。白盒实验复现了剂量-反应关系，logprob排名可检测到开放式生成遗漏的记忆化。"日期到市场情绪"回归与真实Mkt-RF的r从0.74降至0.02（残差化后）。一行系统提示防御以近零效用成本阻断了99.8%的非自适应单轮后缀攻击。

Conclusion: 大模型存在显著的数值记忆通道，闭源API的黑盒探针低估了这一现象，但可通过简单的系统提示防御有效缓解。

Abstract: Public numeric benchmarks appear in pretraining, so an evaluation that conditions on a date may be measuring memorized recall rather than out-of-sample skill. We introduce NumLeak, a measurement framework that combines API-boundary probes on production models with a white-box controlled validation on an open causal LM. Top-tier frontier LLMs recall the Fama-French market excess return at 3-seed pooled Pearson r=0.97-0.99 while staying within 0.15 within-25bps on the five sibling factors; comparable fidelity appears on U.S. unemployment, CPI inflation, and NOAA temperature. On a recent-release holdout, parse rate collapses to 21-57% but r stays at approximately 0.99 on months answered, the refuse-or-recall asymmetry a memorized channel predicts. The white-box experiment reproduces the dose-response, and logprob ranking detects memorization that open-ended generation misses, implying closed-API black-box probes understate the channel. A Sonnet "date to market-sentiment" regression that correlates with true Mkt-RF at r=0.74 collapses to r=0.02 once the model's own recall is residualized out. A one-line system-prompt defense blocks 99.8% of a non-adaptive single-turn suffix attack set at near-zero utility cost on conceptual and historical-narrative queries

</details>


### [80] [LongDS-Bench: On the Failure of Long-Horizon Agentic Data Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30434)
*Kewei Xu,Xiaoben Lu,Shuofei Qiao,Zihan Ding,Haoming Xu,Lei Liang,Ningyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LongDS是一个用于评估长程、多轮数据分析智能体的基准测试，包含68个基于Kaggle笔记本构建的任务（共2,225轮），覆盖6个领域。最佳模型平均准确率仅48.45%，从早期到后期轮次性能下降近47个百分点，长程错误占失败的52%-69%。关键瓶颈在于维护正确的分析状态而非增加交互次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要评估孤立或短交互任务，缺乏对智能体在长程分析中追踪演化上下文能力的测试，因此需要构建一个专门评估长程、多轮数据状态演化跟踪能力的基准。

Method: 从真实Kaggle笔记本构建68个任务（2,225轮），覆盖地球科学、商业、教育等6个领域。任务围绕状态演化模式设计（如反事实扰动、回滚、多状态组合），平均依赖跨度11.3轮。评估了5个最先进模型。

Result: 最佳模型平均准确率仅48.45%，从早期到后期轮次性能下降近47个百分点，长程错误占失败的52%-69%。额外智能体步骤不一定提升性能。

Conclusion: 长程数据智能体的关键瓶颈在于维护正确的分析状态而非增加交互预算，LongDS可用于支持可靠长程智能体数据分析的研究。

Abstract: Real-world data analysis is inherently iterative, yet existing benchmarks mostly evaluate isolated or short interactive tasks, leaving agents' ability to track evolving analytical context over long horizons untested. We introduce LongDS, a benchmark for long-horizon, multi-turn data analysis where agents must maintain, update, restore, and compose evolving analytical states. LongDS comprises 68 tasks constructed from real-world Kaggle notebooks, spanning 2,225 turns across six domains including Geoscience, Business, and Education. Tasks are designed around state-evolution patterns (e.g., counterfactual perturbation, rollback, multi-state composition), with an average dependency span of 11.3 turns. Evaluating five state-of-the-art models, we find that the best model reaches only 48.45% average accuracy, performance drops nearly 47 points from early to late turns, and long-horizon errors account for 52%--69% of failures. Further analysis shows that additional agent steps do not necessarily improve performance, suggesting that the key bottleneck is maintaining a correct analytical state rather than increasing interaction budget. We release LongDS to support research on reliable long-horizon agentic data analysis. Code and data will be released at https://github.com/zjunlp/DataMind.

</details>


### [81] [Calibrated Preference Learning: The Case of Label Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30447)
*Santo M. A. R. Thies,Viktor Bengs,Timo Kaufmann,Sebastian J. Vollmer,Eyke Hüllermeier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次形式化定义了概率标签排序（Label Ranking）中的校准问题，建立了涵盖全排序、子排序和Top-k排序的校准层次体系，并实验发现主流模型校准不足，且校准性与基准准确率存在强相关但不完全一致的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 校准已在分类和回归中被广泛研究，但在概率标签排序（预测标签集合上的排序分布）中尚未被正式定义。简单将排序视为类别会忽略其结构特性，无法捕捉成对预测、Top-k预测等重要模态，因此需要为标签排序建立正式的校准理论框架。

Method: 形式化定义了标签排序的校准概念，构建了包含全排序校准、子排序校准和Top-k校准的层次化体系；通过理论证明各校准概念之间的蕴含关系（全排序校准蕴含其他两种，反之不成立；子排序校准与Top-k校准不可比较）；并在真实模型上进行实证评估。

Result: 理论证明了全排序校准可推出子排序和Top-k校准，但逆命题不成立；子排序校准与Top-k校准之间不可比较。实验发现流行标签排序模型通常校准不佳，子排序与Top-k指标之间存在显著差异。在RLHF奖励模型上，校准性与基准准确率强相关但不完全一致，表明校准捕获了超越Top-1准确率的有意义的模型质量维度。

Conclusion: 标签排序模型普遍存在校准不良问题，校准作为一个质量维度不同于传统准确率指标，值得未来进一步研究误校准的下游影响及开发相应的校正方法。

Abstract: Calibration, the alignment of predicted probabilities with true outcome frequencies, is essential for reliable decision-making. While extensively studied for classification and regression, calibration has not been formally addressed for probabilistic label ranking, where the goal is to predict a distribution over orderings of a label set. Naively treating rankings as classes ignores their structure and fails to capture important modalities such as pairwise and top-k predictions. We formalize calibration for label ranking and develop a hierarchy of notions covering full rankings, sub-rankings, and top-k rankings. We prove that full-rank calibration implies the others but not conversely, and sub-ranking and top-k calibration are incomparable. Empirically, we find popular label ranking models are often poorly calibrated, with substantial differences between sub-ranking and top-k metrics. Applying our framework to RLHF reward models, we find that calibration correlates strongly but not perfectly with benchmark accuracy, suggesting it captures a meaningful quality dimension beyond top-1 accuracy. These findings motivate future work on understanding the downstream effects of miscalibration and developing methods to correct it.

</details>


### [82] [Bounded Behavioral Indistinguishability for Black-Box LLM Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30448)
*Munawar Hasan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出有界行为不可区分性概念，用于评估黑盒LLM蒸馏效果。通过在Qwen和Llama教师-学生对上测试发现，LoRA蒸馏虽提升语义相似度，但对抗评估显示学生与教师仍存在可区分的风格、鲁棒性和领域技术提示差异。研究表明语义保真度不足，需要结合有界、对抗和类别感知的评估方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统黑盒LLM蒸馏评估仅关注输出相似性（语义相似或任务一致），但输出相似不代表学生模型在行为上与教师不可区分。需要更严格的评估框架来捕捉蒸馏后模型间的行为差异。

Method: 提出$(ε,q,t,\mathbb{A})$-有界行为不可区分性形式化框架，其中ε为区分优势界，q为查询次数界，t为计算界，A为对抗类别。在Qwen和Llama系列模型上，使用5,000提示的探测套件，通过对抗性判别器、成对类别分析、教师身份识别对抗器等评估基础学生与LoRA蒸馏学生的行为差异。

Result: LoRA将语义相似度从Qwen的0.788提升至0.862，Llama的0.814提升至0.874；但对抗评估显示区分优势仍然存在，风格/格式、鲁棒性和领域技术提示类别的伪迹最明显；跨族Llama判断器下Qwen区分优势从0.158降至0.081；查询预算实验中，分歧引导采样未持续优于分层随机采样。

Conclusion: 语义保真度对黑盒LLM蒸馏评估是必要但不充分的，需要结合有界、对抗和类别感知的多维度评估方法。

Abstract: Black-box LLM distillation is usually evaluated as an output-matching problem: a student is considered successful when its responses are semantically similar to, or task-consistent with, those of a teacher. However, output similarity does not imply that the student is behaviorally indistinguishable from the model it imitates. We introduce bounded behavioral indistinguishability, formalized as $(ε,q,t,\mathbb{A})$-behavioral indistinguishability over an explicit prompt distribution, where $ε$ bounds distinguishing advantage, $q$ bounds oracle queries, $t$ bounds computation, and $\mathbb{A}$ denotes the adversary class.
  We instantiate this notion on Qwen and Llama teacher-student pairs using a controlled $5,000$-prompt behavioral probe suite. For each family, we compare the teacher with both the base student and the LoRA-distilled student, measuring whether distillation reduces distinguishability rather than merely improving similarity. LoRA raises semantic similarity from $0.788$ to $0.862$ for Qwen and from $0.814$ to $0.874$ for Llama. Yet adversarial evaluation reveals remaining behavioral differences: learned discriminators retain nonzero advantage, and pairwise category analysis shows artifacts concentrated in style/format, robustness, and domain-technical prompts. A pairwise teacher-identification adversary confirms this trend. With a different-family Llama judge and A/B-swap consistency filtering, Qwen distinguishing advantage drops from $0.158$ for the base student to $0.081$ after LoRA distillation. Query-budget experiments show that disagreement-guided acquisition does not consistently outperform stratified random sampling, indicating that coverage and diversity remain strong baselines. Our results show that semantic fidelity is useful but insufficient: black-box LLM distillation requires bounded, adversarial, and category-aware evaluation.

</details>


### [83] [VeriGate: Verifier-Gated Step-Level Supervision for GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30451)
*Aakriti Agrawal,Minghui Liu,Furong Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VeriGate是一种在GRPO基础上引入验证器门控的逐步奖励扩展方法，通过在验证器奖励退化时使用过程监督、设计未来累积奖励和标记级优势，显著提升了推理模型的训练效果和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO使用基于验证器的结果奖励训练推理模型，但其监督信号稀疏：当所有采样轨迹获得相同验证器奖励时，组相对优势归零，学习停滞；结果奖励无法提供步骤级信用分配，限制了探索和鲁棒推理的学习。

Method: 提出VeriGate方法，包含三个设计：1) 当验证器奖励能区分轨迹时由验证器主导，否则使用过程监督；2) 将PRM步骤分数转换为未来累积奖励以赋予延续感知的信用分配；3) 将这些奖励转化为组归一化的标记级优势，恢复信息性梯度并减少奖励破解。

Result: 在MATH上使用1.5B和7B Qwen2.5-Instruct模型训练并在六个推理基准上评估，VeriGate分别将平均准确率提升约20%（1.5B）和12%（7B），显著减少零梯度故障、降低奖励破解行为，并提升推理质量。

Conclusion: VeriGate有效解决了GRPO的奖励稀疏和缺乏步骤级信用分配问题，在多种规模和基准上均表现出优于结果-only GRPO和PRM-as-outcome基线的性能。

Abstract: Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) is an effective recipe for training reasoning models with verifier-based outcome rewards, but its supervision is sparse: when all sampled trajectories for a prompt receive the same verifier reward, the group-relative advantage collapses to zero and learning stalls. Outcome-only rewards also provide no step-level credit assignment, limiting exploration and making it harder to learn robust reasoning. We present VeriGate (Verifier-Gated Step-Level GRPO), a verifier-gated extension of GRPO that addresses these limitations with three design choices. First, VeriGate keeps the verifier in charge whenever verifier rewards induce a meaningful preference among sampled trajectories, and uses process supervision only when verifier rewards are degenerate. Second, instead of collapsing Process Reward Model (PRM) step scores into a single trajectory reward, VeriGate converts them into future-cumulated rewards to assign continuation-aware credit. Third, VeriGate transforms these rewards into group-normalized token-level advantages, restoring informative gradients and fine-grained credit assignment while remaining less susceptible to reward hacking than methods that optimize aggregated PRM scores. Empirically, training on MATH with 1.5B and 7B Qwen2.5-Instruct models and evaluating on six reasoning benchmarks, VeriGate improves average accuracy by about 20% and 12% for 1.5B and 7B models respectively, substantially reduces zero-gradient failures, decreases reward-hacking behavior, and improves reasoning quality relative to outcome-only GRPO and PRM-as-outcome baselines.

</details>


### [84] [A Unified Framework for Gradient Aggregation in Multi-Objective Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30452)
*Zeou Hu,Kelvin Ho,Yaoliang Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了多目标优化中梯度聚合的统一框架，证明了非冲突方向是收敛的充分条件，并引入基于CVaR的capped MGDA方法，在对抗性联邦学习中展现出鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有梯度聚合方法动机各异、逐案分析、算法差异大，缺乏统一的理论框架来理解其收敛性保证和相互关系。

Method: 提出了一个统一的梯度聚合框架，核心是充分对齐条件（sufficient alignment condition），证明凸包内非冲突方向是收敛的基本充分条件；通过投影到对偶锥确保可行性；从原始优化视角统一现有算法并衍生新变体；引入基于CVaR的capped MGDA。

Result: 建立了到达Pareto平稳性的（最优）收敛速率；capped MGDA在对抗性联邦学习中表现出鲁棒性；合成问题与实用基准的实验验证了理论。

Conclusion: 非冲突方向是梯度聚合收敛的充分条件，对偶锥投影可扩展收敛保证的适用范围，capped MGDA是一种鲁棒的新方法。

Abstract: Many machine learning problems involve multiple inherent trade-offs that are best addressed by gradient-based multi-objective optimization (MOO) algorithms. Existing methods are often proposed with various motivations, analyzed case by case, and differ algorithmically in how the component gradients are aggregated at each step. In this work, we develop a unifying framework for gradient aggregation in MOO, establishing (optimal) rates of convergence to Pareto stationarity, the standard measure of performance in MOO. Central to our analysis is a sufficient alignment condition, from which we derive a theorem showing that non-conflicting directions, when chosen within the convex hull of gradients, form a fundamental sufficient condition for convergence. We further show that feasibility can be ensured through projection onto the dual cone, broadening the scope of methods that admit convergence guarantees. In parallel, we present a primal optimization perspective of gradient aggregation that encompasses established algorithms, clarifies their theoretical relationships, and enables the design of new variants. As an illustration, we introduce capped MGDA, derived from a CVaR-based formulation, and demonstrate its robustness in adversarial federated learning. Finally, we validate our theory through experiments on synthetic problems and practical benchmarks.

</details>


### [85] [Active Timepoint Selection for Learning Measure-Valued Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30625)
*Nicolas Huynh,Mihaela van der Schaar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将主动学习扩展到概率测度空间的方法，利用线性化最优传输（LOT）将分布快照映射到切空间，结合高斯过程建模来构建概率路径的替代模型，从而策略性地选择最优测量时间，在单细胞生物学等场景中显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在单细胞生物学等场景中，从稀疏快照推断连续概率路径面临挑战：数据采集具有破坏性且测序成本高昂，需要主动学习策略来选择最优测量时间。然而，现有方法面临两大难题：目标对象位于无限维Wasserstein空间，标准欧氏度量不适用；当前插值方法缺乏认知不确定性量化。

Method: 提出一个将主动实验扩展到测度空间的框架：利用线性化最优传输（LOT）将分布快照映射到切空间，在该空间中应用高斯过程（GP）建模，构建概率路径的易处理概率替代模型，进而设计获取函数（acquisition policy）迭代选择测量时间以最小化不确定性。

Result: 在合成数据集和真实数据集上，所提策略均优于忽视不确定性的基线方法。

Conclusion: 基于LOT和高斯过程的主动学习框架能有效解决Wasserstein空间中概率路径推断的测量时间优化问题，为单细胞生物学等高成本数据采集场景提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Inferring continuous probability paths from sparse snapshots is a fundamental challenge in domains like single-cell biology, where high-fidelity data acquisition is often destructive and constrained by prohibitive sequencing costs. This motivates the need for active learning strategies to strategically select optimal measurement times. However, designing active learning policies for this setting remains an open problem: the target objects reside on the infinite dimensional Wasserstein space where standard Euclidean metrics are ill-defined, and current interpolation methods lack epistemic uncertainty quantification. We introduce a framework which extends active experimentation to the space of measures. By leveraging Linearized Optimal Transport (LOT), we map distributional snapshots into a tangent space amenable to Gaussian Process modeling, allowing us to construct a tractable probabilistic surrogate for the underlying probability path. This yields an acquisition policy that iteratively selects measurement times to minimize uncertainty. Empirical results demonstrate that our strategy outperforms uncertainty-agnostic baselines on both synthetic and real-world datasets.

</details>


### [86] [DisjunctiveNet: Neural Symbolic Learning via Differentiable Convexified Optimization Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30456)
*Shraman Pal,Can Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个端到端框架，通过在神经网络中嵌入基于层次凸松弛的混合整数线性约束可微优化层，实现硬约束满足，在稀疏数据集上达到完美规则遵守和强预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学与工程中的学习任务常面临稀疏数据集，纯数据驱动方法效果有限；同时这些任务蕴含丰富的领域知识（物理定律、操作规则、专家经验），常表达为逻辑命题和线性不等式。现有神经符号方法通常采用软惩罚近似或非可微后处理，无法在端到端框架中实现硬约束满足。

Method: 将规则表示为析取约束，应用层次凸松弛得到凸包络公式，将可处理的线性约束嵌入为神经网络中的可微优化层，实现精确规则满足。

Result: 在真实数据集上实现了完美的规则满足率和强预测性能。

Conclusion: 提出的端到端框架能够有效融合领域知识与数据驱动学习，在处理混合整数线性约束方面优于现有方法。

Abstract: Many learning tasks in science and engineering are characterized by sparse datasets, which limits the effectiveness of purely data-driven approaches. At the same time, these problems are often accompanied by rich domain knowledge derived from physical laws, operational requirements, and expert heuristics. Such knowledge is frequently expressed as rules involving logical propositions and linear inequalities. Existing neuro-symbolic methods typically enforce these rules approximately through soft penalties, assume input-independent rules when designing specialized architectures, or rely on non-differentiable post-processing at inference time to achieve hard constraint satisfaction. While recent advances in differentiable optimization layers enable end-to-end feasibility enforcement within neural networks, extending these approaches to logical or mixed-integer rules remains challenging due to inherent nonconvexity. In this work, we propose a unified end-to-end framework for enforcing hard, input-dependent mixed integer linear constraints within neural networks. Our approach represents rules as disjunctive constraints and applies hierarchical convex relaxations to obtain convex hull formulations. These relaxations yield tractable linear constraints that can be embedded as differentiable optimization layers while enabling exact rule satisfaction. We demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework on real-world datasets, achieving perfect rule satisfaction and strong predictive performance.

</details>


### [87] [Kalimati Vegetable Price Index Forecasting with a Momentum Corrected Online Stacking Ensemble](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30720)
*Sahaj Raj Malla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Kalimati蔬菜价格指数(KVPI)，一种基于逆波动率加权的复合指数，用于预测尼泊尔等新兴经济体的农产品价格。通过构建64个因果有效特征，评估了14种预测模型，提出的动量校正在线堆叠集成方法在90天预测期内实现了RMSE为1.771、MAPE为0.68%、R²为0.845的最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新兴经济体的农产品价格预测因高波动性、频繁的供应中断和强烈的文化需求影响而面临巨大挑战，现有方法难以有效处理个体商品的噪声信号。

Method: 构建KVPI指数（基于逆波动率加权的135种日度批发商品综合指数），设计64个因果有效特征（包括节日超前滞后效应、滚动统计量和日历变量），系统评估14种预测模型（涵盖统计、树集成、深度学习、混合和Transformer架构），提出动量校正在线堆叠集成方法。

Result: 树集成模型表现稳健，经典统计模型和复杂Transformer在噪声数据中表现不佳。动量校正在线堆叠集成在90天预测期实现最优：RMSE=1.771，MAPE=0.68%，R²=0.845。

Conclusion: 该开源管道为尼泊尔及类似市场的政策制定者和供应链参与者提供了实用、可靠的价格预测工具，有助于加强粮食安全。

Abstract: Forecasting agricultural commodity prices in emerging economies is difficult due to high volatility, frequent supply disruptions, and strong cultural influences on demand. This study introduces the Kalimati Vegetable Price Index (KVPI), a new inverse-volatility weighted composite index that aggregates 135 daily wholesale commodities from Kathmandu over ten years (2013-2023). By creating a stable macro-level signal, the KVPI reduces the noise inherent in modelling individual crops. A rich set of 64 causally valid features was developed, including festival lead-lag effects, rolling statistics, and calendar variables.
