Fourteen forecasting models spanning statistical, tree-based, deep learning, hybrid, and transformer architectures were rigorously evaluated across short (7-day), medium (14- and 30-day), and long-term (90-day) horizons. Tree-based ensembles proved notably robust, while classical statistical models and complex transformers struggled with the noisy dataset. The proposed Momentum-Corrected Online Stacking Ensemble achieved the strongest performance, yielding a Root Mean Square Error (RMSE) of 1.771, an exceptionally low Mean Absolute Percentage Error (MAPE) of 0.68%, and explaining 84.5% of the variance (R-squared = 0.845) at the 90-day horizon. This open-source pipeline provides policymakers and supply chain actors in Nepal and similar markets with a practical, reliable tool for anticipating price movements and strengthening food security.

</details>


### [88] [Scalable Constrained Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning via State Augmentation and Consensus for Separable Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30461)
*Santiago Amaya-Corredor,Miguel Calvo-Fullana,Anders Jonsson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合状态增强策略学习与分布式对偶变量共识的约束多智能体强化学习方法，通过轻量级邻接共识实现全局约束协调，证明了对偶变量共识误差有界且约束违反随连通性递减，实验显示该方法在智能电网需求响应中能扩展到数千智能体，而集中训练分散执行方法仅能处理数十个。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 独立学习无法产生可行解，因为智能体无法确定各自对全局约束的适当贡献；现有集中训练分散执行方法复杂度随智能体数量呈二次增长，扩展到大规模系统时受限。

Method: 每个智能体学习一个条件于局部状态和编码约束反馈的对偶变量的单一增广策略；在执行阶段通过局部通信在智能体间达成对偶变量共识；利用分布式共识算法更新拉格朗日乘子。

Result: 在温和连通性假设下，智能体间乘子的共识误差有界，约束违反程度随图连通性和共识轮次增加而降低；方法在训练和执行阶段均呈线性扩展；在智能电网需求响应中，无共识时智能体无限推迟需求导致退化解，有共识时满足电网约束和需求满足，可扩展至数千智能体。

Conclusion: 轻量级邻接共识足以实现全局协调约束执行，同时保持独立训练的可扩展性，为大规模约束多智能体强化学习提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We present a distributed approach for constrained Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL) that combines state-augmented policy learning with distributed consensus over dual variables. Our method targets systems where agents have separable dynamics but must coordinate to satisfy global resource constraints, a setting in which, as we demonstrate empirically, independent learning fails to produce feasible solutions because agents cannot determine appropriate individual contributions toward collective constraint satisfaction. The key technical contribution is showing that lightweight neighbor-to-neighbor consensus over Lagrange multipliers suffices for globally coordinated constraint enforcement while preserving the scalability of independent training. Each agent learns a single augmented policy offline, conditioned on both its local state and a dual variable encoding constraint feedback. During execution, agents reach agreement on this dual variable through local communication alone. We prove that under mild connectivity assumptions, the consensus error among agents' multipliers is bounded, and show that this translates to a bounded constraint violation that decreases with graph connectivity and the number of consensus rounds. Unlike centralized training with decentralized execution (CTDE) approaches, whose complexity grows at least quadratically with agent count, our method scales linearly in both training and execution. Experiments on smart grid demand response demonstrate that consensus coordination is \emph{essential for feasibility}: without it, agents satisfy grid capacity constraints only by indefinitely postponing demand, a degenerate non-solution. With consensus, agents converge to a shared dual variable and satisfy both grid constraints and demand fulfillment, scaling to thousands of agents while CTDE baselines are limited to dozens.

</details>


### [89] [Unlearning in Diffusion Models: A Unified Framework with KL Divergence and Likelihood Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30825)
*Shervin Khalafi,Alejandro Ribeiro,Dongsheng Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于约束优化框架的扩散模型遗忘学习方法，通过KL散度和似然约束实现遗忘与保留之间的最优权衡，并建立了强对偶性理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型遗忘学习面临两个根本冲突的目标：移除不期望的数据/概念，同时保持预训练模型的效用。现有方法缺乏 principled 的理论框架来平衡这一权衡。

Method: 构建了基于反向KL散度、前向KL散度和似然约束的三个约束优化问题，证明了非凸KL约束下的强对偶性，显式刻画了最优解作为遗忘目标，并开发了对应的原始-对偶算法。

Result: KL约束方法在概念遗忘和数据遗忘任务上，相比于基于权重的基线方法实现了更优的保留-遗忘权衡；基于似然的方法在保持遗忘效果的同时更好地保留了保留概念。

Conclusion: 约束优化框架为扩散模型遗忘学习提供了理论严谨且实践有效的方法，能够系统性地处理遗忘与保留之间的根本冲突。

Abstract: Unlearning in diffusion models aims to remove undesirable data or concepts while preserving the utility of pretrained models -- two fundamentally conflicting objectives. We propose a principled constrained optimization framework that formulates unlearning as minimizing the deviation from a pretrained model, subject to explicit separation constraints from the unlearning distributions. Specifically, we formulate three constrained optimization problems based on reverse and forward KL divergences, and likelihood constraints. The first two generalize existing approaches for concept and data unlearning, while the third offers a novel and natural formulation for unlearning. Despite the nonconvexity of the KL constraints, we establish strong duality for all three problems, enabling us to explicitly characterize their optimal solutions as unlearning targets and develop primal-dual algorithms for each formulation. Experimental results demonstrate that our KL-constrained approach achieves superior retention-unlearning tradeoffs compared to weight-based baselines for concept and data unlearning, and that our likelihood-based approach matches unlearning effectiveness while better preserving retained concepts compared to baselines.

</details>


### [90] [idSCD: Identifying Training Datasets through Semantic Correlation Descriptors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30462)
*Andrada Gobeaja,Ionut Hodoroaga,Elena Burceanu,Marius Leordeanu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于语义相关性描述符（SCD）的白盒方法，通过模型学习到的语义相关性结构来推断数据集成员身份，在多个实验设置中优于现有黑盒与白盒基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据集成员推断方法依赖于行为或分布证据（如置信度、损失、生成样本等），但未能利用数据集在训练过程中留下的特有痕迹——模型内部化的虚假相关性。作者希望证明数据集可以通过其诱导的语义相关性结构被识别，从而实现更精确的成员推断。

Method: 提出语义相关性描述符（SCDs），这是一种白盒方法，捕捉模型学习到的语义相关性结构，使其在不同数据集混合间可比较。设计了一个基于SCD的成员评分函数，仅需目标模型的SCD和目标数据集的独立SCD，无需留一法模型。在NLI、情感分类、医学文本分类三个场景下进行实验。

Result: 在留一数据集诊断中，SCD能完美分离匹配与非匹配数据集对。基于SCD的成员评分分类器在平均性能和最低标准差上均最优，超过RMIA、Attack-P、LiRA等黑盒基线以及SIF白盒基线。当数据集组暴露不同的语义特殊性时，ROC-AUC相对提升超过60%。

Conclusion: 数据集成员身份可以通过模型内化的语义相关性进行追踪，SCD方法在多个任务上有效且鲁棒，尤其当数据集间存在显著语义差异时效果更佳。

Abstract: Can a dataset be recognized from the spurious correlations it induces during training? We argue that datasets leave dataset-specific traces in a model's learned semantic correlation structure: incidental regularities that are predictive within a dataset, but not causal for the underlying task, can be internalized during training. We use this insight to study dataset-level membership inference, moving beyond existing methods that rely on behavioral or distributional evidence such as confidence scores, losses, margins, generated samples, or query responses. We introduce a white-box semantic fingerprinting approach based on semantic correlation descriptors (SCDs), which capture the semantic correlation structure learned by a model and make it comparable across dataset mixtures. In a controlled leave-one-dataset-out diagnostic, SCDs recover dataset-specific changes and perfectly separate matching from non-matching dataset pairs. We then propose a practical SCD-based membership score that tests whether a target dataset is part of a model's training mixture using only the model's SCD and the target dataset's standalone SCD, without requiring leave-one-dataset-out models. Across three diverse experimental settings, with dataset groups for natural language inference, emotion classification, and medical text classification, we test both the advantages and limitations of SCD-based membership inference with different degrees of semantic separation and keyword support between dataset splits. On average, the classifier based on this score achieves the highest performance and the lowest std, outperforming black-box baselines RMIA, Attack-P, and LiRA, as well as the white-box SIF baseline. These results show that dataset membership can be traced through internal semantic correlations, with the largest relative gain exceeding 60% in ROC-AUC when dataset groups expose distinct semantic particularities.

</details>


### [91] [Local linear convergence of gradient methods for overparameterized Gaussian mixtures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30936)
*Jingxing Wang,Vasileios Charisopoulos,Maryam Fazel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究过参数化下高斯混合模型的学习问题，提出了一种交替使用短步长梯度下降和长步长Polyak步长的优化方法，在标准散度测度上实现了局部线性收敛速度，克服了过参数化带来的慢收敛问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过参数化对于避免伪局部最优和使用梯度EM算法全局恢复真实模型至关重要，但会显著减慢局部收敛速度。本文旨在解决这一矛盾，证明慢收敛并非过参数化的固有挑战，而可以通过利用损失景观的有利结构来克服。

Method: 提出了一种交替优化方法：交替执行若干"短"梯度下降步（逼近慢增长流形）和"长"Polyak步（收缩到最小化器的距离）。在混合权重满足一定假设时，利用Polyak步长在标准散度测度的慢增长流形上实现几何级损失下降；对于任意权重的混合，该方法能收敛到接近最优的解（达到自然误设阈值）。

Result: 在混合权重满足一定假设下，方法实现了局部线性收敛到最小化器；对于任意权重的混合，收敛到接近最优的解（达到自然误设阈值）。

Conclusion: 过参数化导致的慢收敛并非固有挑战，通过利用损失景观中慢增长流形的有利结构，可以设计高效的优化方法实现线性收敛，为过参数化模型的学习提供了理论保证和实用算法。

Abstract: We study the problem of learning Gaussian mixture models under overparameterization. Prior work has shown that while overparameterization is essential for avoiding spurious local optima and enables global recovery of the ground-truth model using the gradient-EM (expectation-maximization) algorithm, it can dramatically slow down the local rate of convergence. Under certain assumptions on the mixture weights, we show that a standard divergence measure minimized by statistical learning procedures possesses a manifold of slow growth on which the well-known Polyak stepsize reduces the loss geometrically, and design a gradient-based method that converges to minimizers at a locally linear rate. Additionally, we show that our method converges to nearly optimal solutions -- up to a natural misspecification threshold -- for mixtures with arbitrary weights. At a high level, the method alternates between several "short" gradient descent steps that approach the manifold and "long" Polyak steps that contract the distance to minimizers. Our results suggest that slow convergence is not an intrinsic challenge of overparameterization, but can be overcome by exploiting the favorable structure of the loss landscape.

</details>


### [92] [Can Subgraph Explanations Be Weaponized to Steal Graph Neural Networks?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30470)
*Ojas Nimase,Jiate Li,Yue Zhao,Yushun Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次提出针对严格黑盒图分类场景的模型提取攻击方法XSTEAL，利用模型解释接口（二元解释掩码）指导蒙特卡洛边灵敏度估计，在仅观察离散标签和解释掩码的条件下高效逼近决策边界，实验证明该方法优于现有基线，揭示了可解释AI接口的安全漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GMLaaS平台为实现监管透明度要求部署了解释性接口，但这种透明度为模型提取攻击创造了可利用的漏洞。现有攻击方法通常需要概率分数、梯度或置信度值，但在严格黑盒设定下攻击者只能观察到离散类别标签和二元解释掩码，亟需针对该场景的专用攻击方法。

Method: 提出XSTEAL方法：(1)利用模型解释输出指导蒙特卡洛边灵敏度估计，逼近决策边界，并带有Hoeffding集中不等式保证估计精度；(2)利用解释子图高效缩小边界搜索空间。该方法仅需离散类别标签和二元解释掩码即可实现模型提取。

Result: 在多个领域的基准图数据集上进行了广泛实验，结果表明该方法在严格黑盒约束下显著优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 可解释性接口创造了可利用的攻击面，该发现对防御机制和可解释AI强制要求的政策框架具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: Graph Machine Learning as a Service (GMLaaS) platforms increasingly implement explainability interfaces to meet regulatory transparency requirements. However, this transparency creates exploitable vulnerabilities for model extraction attacks. We present the first model extraction attack specifically designed for graph classification under strict black-box constraints where the attacker observes only discrete class labels and binary explanation masks (no probability scores, gradients, or confidence values). Our method (1) uses model explanation outputs to guide Monte Carlo edge sensitivity estimation toward decision boundaries, with Hoeffding concentration guarantees on estimation accuracy and (2) exploits explanation subgraphs to efficiently narrow the boundary search space. Extensive experiments on benchmark graph datasets across multiple domains demonstrate our method's superiority over comparable baselines. These findings demonstrate that such explainability interfaces create exploitable attack surfaces, informing both defensive mechanisms and policy frameworks for explainable AI mandates. The implementation code is provided in https://github.com/LabRAI/XSTEAL/.

</details>


### [93] [Convergence of Two-Timescale Markovian Stochastic Approximations with Applications in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31172)
*Vagul Mahadevan,Claire Chen,Shuze Daniel Liu,Shangtong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在马尔可夫噪声下双时间尺度随机逼近算法的稳定性与收敛性，并首次证明了TDC算法在离策略线性函数逼近下的几乎必然收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前双时间尺度SA算法的稳定性与收敛性仅在i.i.d.噪声下被证明，而强化学习中更实际的噪声类型是马尔可夫噪声。现有方法依赖投影算子或要求噪声在紧集上，限制了应用范围。

Method: 提出用慢时间尺度参数的running max来控制快时间尺度参数，替代传统方法中使用当前慢时间尺度参数的方式，从而在不依赖投影算子且不要求噪声紧集条件下建立稳定性。

Result: 在马尔可夫噪声下建立了双时间尺度SA的稳定性与收敛性；作为关键应用，首次证明了带资格迹的TDC算法在离策略线性函数逼近下的几乎必然收敛。

Conclusion: 该方法去除了对投影算子和噪声紧集假设的依赖，为强化学习中的双时间尺度算法提供了更通用的理论基础。

Abstract: This work studies the convergence of two-timescale stochastic approximations (SA), a class of iterative algorithms that update two sets of parameters in fast and slow timescales respectively. Notable examples of two-timescale SA in reinforcement learning (RL) include temporal difference learning with gradient correction (TDC) and actor-critic methods. Previously, the stability (i.e., boundedness) and convergence of two-timescale SA were only established under i.i.d. noise. This work instead establishes the stability and convergence of two-timescale SA under Markovian noise, a setup that is more realistic in RL. Notably, we do not need to use any projection operator and the noise does not need to live in a compact space. Our key technical novelty is to control the fast timescale parameter with the running max of the slow timescale parameter, instead of with the current slow timescale parameter, as most prior works do. As a key application, we establish the first almost sure convergence of TDC with eligibility traces under off-policy learning with linear function approximation.

</details>


### [94] [Universal Multiclass Transductive Online Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30479)
*Steve Hanneke,Hongao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究通用直推式在线分类问题（标签空间可能无界），刻画了可学习性，发现可学习类的最优错误率只有两种：有界或对数增长。引入LCLL树和 indifference property 来表征可学习性，并将结果扩展到不可知情况和随机过程已知情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在直推式在线学习（实例序列预先已知）中，标签空间可能无界的情况下，缺乏对可学习性及其最优收敛率的完整刻画。

Method: 提出"Level-Constrained-Littlestone-Littlestone (LCLL) tree"这一新组合结构，结合indifference property，对可学习性进行组合刻画。

Result: 可学习类的错误率只有两种最优速率：有界（常数）或对数增长；此外将结果推广到不可知情形和实例序列由随机过程生成的情形。

Conclusion: 在直推式在线分类中，即使标签空间无界，可学习性仍可由LCLL树刻画，且最优速率仅有两种可能。

Abstract: We consider the problem of universal transductive online classification with a possibly unbounded label space. This setting considers online learning, with the sequence of instances (without labels) known to the learner in advance. We say a concept class $\mathcal{H}$ is learnable if there is a learning algorithm $\mathcal{A}$, such that for every realizable sequence, the number of mistakes made by $\mathcal{A}$ grows at most sublinearly with the number of predictions. We characterize the learnability of this setting and show that there are only two possible optimal rates for the learnable classes: either bounded or increasing logarithmically. We introduce a new combinatorial structure, called ``Level-Constrained-Littlestone-Littlestone (LCLL) tree'', which, along with the indifference property, characterizes the learnability. We also extend the learnability result to the agnostic case and the case where only the stochastic process that generates the instance sequence is known.

</details>


### [95] [Beyond Additive Decompositions: Interpretability Through Separability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31200)
*Jinyang Liu,Munir Eberhardt Hiabu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出张量分离学习（TSL），一种通过贪婪阶段式正交重新拟合学习秩-1乘积之和的回归模型，避免了加法模型在强交互下的信号取消和外推问题，并保证可视化忠实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释机器学习方法（如GAM、SHAP、函数ANOVA）依赖加法表示，在存在强交互作用时会出现信号取消和支撑外推问题，导致信息丢失。

Method: 提出TSL回归模型，通过阶段式贪婪过程结合正交重新拟合，学习一组秩-1的单变量特征函数乘积之和，强制可分离性以避免加法投影的信息损失。

Result: 建立了具有有界混合p阶偏导数的函数的逼近率保证，并在回归基准测试中证明TSL可与黑箱模型竞争。

Conclusion: TSL模型可完全由一阶部分依赖函数重构（至多常数因子），确保可视化忠实于拟合组件，从而在保持可解释性的同时达到与黑箱模型相竞争的性能。

Abstract: Interpretable machine learning requires models that are accurate and structurally faithful to the data.Existing explainability methods rely heavily on additive representations (e.g., Generalized Additive Models (GAMs), SHapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP), functional ANOVA), which can suffer from signal cancellation and off-support extrapolation in the presence of strong interactions. We propose Tensor Separation Learning (TSL), a regression model that learns a sum of rank-1 products of univariate per-feature functions via a stagewise greedy procedure with orthogonal refitting. By enforcing separability, TSL avoids the information loss inherent in additive projections caused by marginalizing higher-order interactions. The learned TSL model can be fully reconstructed from first-order partial dependence functions, up to constant factors. This stage-wise correspondence ensures that the resulting visualizations are faithful to the fitted components. We establish approximation-rate guarantees for functions with bounded mixed $p$-th order partial derivatives and demonstrate that TSL competes with black-box models on regression benchmarks.

</details>


### [96] [Discovering a Zeta Map Algorithm on Dyck Paths via Mechanistic Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30482)
*Xiaoyu Huang,Blake Jackson,Kyu-Hwan Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过训练一个小型编码器-解码器Transformer模型来学习Dyck路径上的zeta映射，并利用机械可解释性工具分析其计算机制，从中提取出一个新的显式组合算法（scaffolding map），并证明该算法与zeta映射等价（模逆序约定）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习在数学发现中的应用日益广泛，但数学中所需的往往不是预测本身，而是可独立验证的显式构造。本文旨在探索如何从训练好的模型中提取出可验证的数学算法，以zeta映射作为案例研究。

Method: 训练一个单层、单头的小型编码器-解码器Transformer模型学习Dyck路径上的zeta映射；使用机械可解释性工具（解码器交叉注意力分析、线性探针、因果干预）分析模型计算过程；将分析得到的信号翻译为组合学语言，提出scaffolding map算法；通过数学证明验证该算法与zeta映射的一致性。

Result: 揭示了基于层级的机制：编码器表示使路径层级线性可访问，解码器以结构化方式选择和遍历输入位置；从模型行为中提取出显式的scaffolding map算法，并证明该算法与zeta映射等价（模逆序约定）。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个AI辅助数学发现的受控案例，展示了机械可解释性如何将模型的行为转化为精确、人类可验证的组合算法，为从神经网络中提取数学知识提供了方法论参考。

Abstract: Machine learning is increasingly used in mathematical discovery, but in mathematics the desired output is often not a prediction itself, but an explicit construction that can be checked independently. We study this setting through the zeta map on Dyck paths, a classical bijection in the combinatorics of the q,t-Catalan numbers. We train a deliberately small one-layer, one-head encoder-decoder transformer on this map and analyze its learned computation using mechanistic interpretability tools, including decoder cross-attention analysis, linear probing, and causal intervention. The analysis reveals a level-based mechanism: encoder representations make path levels linearly accessible, while the decoder selects and traverses input positions in a structured way. Translating these signals into combinatorics leads to the scaffolding map, an explicit peak-centered traversal algorithm for Dyck paths. We prove that this algorithm agrees with the zeta map, modulo a reversal convention in the labeling. This gives a controlled example of AI-assisted mathematical discovery in which mechanistic interpretability turns model behavior into a precise, human-verifiable combinatorial algorithm.

</details>


### [97] [Fraud Type Decomposition and the Observation-Mechanism Taxonomy:Class-Specific Detection Limits in Payment Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31257)
*Gaurav Dhama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出将欺诈检测视为同质二元变量的现有方法存在结构性错误，提出了一种将欺诈分为五类的观察机制分类法，证明了按类别分别估计欺诈率并聚合的方法严格优于统一估计，效率差距由异质观察率导致的Jensen惩罚决定，并推导了每类欺诈检测的约束条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有欺诈检测方法将欺诈视为同质二元变量，忽略了标签生成过程的异质性和不完美性，导致结构性错误和可证明的低效。

Method: 提出观察机制分类法，将欺诈分为五类（每类由不同的审查和标注流程定义）；通过理论证明按类分别估计欺诈率并聚合优于统一估计；推导每类欺诈的检测理论约束（包括内生标签污染、结构性不可观测性和特征无信息性）。

Result: 按类别分别估计欺诈率并聚合严格优于统一估计，效率差距由异质观察率导致的Jensen惩罚表征；为每类欺诈确定了检测的理论边界。

Conclusion: 欺诈检测本质上是多个不同的估计问题，每个问题由其自身的观察结构和检测极限所支配，需要针对不同欺诈类型设计差异化的检测策略。

Abstract: Fraud detection in payment networks relies on labels generated through heterogeneous and imperfect observation processes, yet existing approaches treat fraud as a homogeneous binary variable. We show that this assumption is structurally incorrect and leads to provable inefficiency. We introduce an observation-mechanism taxonomy that partitions fraud into five classes, each defined by a distinct censorship and labeling pipeline. We prove that estimating fraud rates separately by class and aggregating strictly dominates pooled estimation, with the efficiency gap characterized as a Jensen penalty arising from heterogeneous observation rates. For each class, we derive the binding theoretical constraint on detection, including endogenous label corruption, structural non-observability, and feature non-informativeness. These results establish that fraud detection is fundamentally a collection of distinct estimation problems, each governed by its own observation structure and detection limit.

</details>


### [98] [Graph-Conditioned Mixture of Graph Neural Network Experts for Traffic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30486)
*Amirhossein Ghaffari,Saeid Sheikhi,Ekaterina Gilman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GC-MoE，一种基于图条件的混合专家框架，为传感器图上的时空预测任务分配每个节点个性化的预训练专家组合。仅训练约17K参数的路由模块，在四个标准基准上提升MAE指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统时空预测方法对所有节点统一使用单一骨干架构，忽略了图区域中不同节点（如道路段）在功能类别、结构及交通行为上的动态差异，因此需要节点级别的专家专业化。

Method: GC-MoE框架包含：冻结的预训练时空GNN专家池、基于图拓扑和近期交通输入窗口的输入感知空间上下文路由模块（仅训练该轻量模块）、可选的图条件输出精炼层，以及作为消融诊断的节点自适应ST-LoRA适配器。

Result: 在PEMS04、PEMS07、METR-LA和PEMS-BAY四个标准基准上，GC-MoE相比零参数集成基线提升了MAE，并取得了有竞争力的RMSE和MAPE，仅需在150万冻结专家权重上训练约17K参数。

Conclusion: GC-MoE通过图条件的专家混合机制，以极小的可训练参数开销实现了节点级别的专业化时空预测，验证了感知输入和拓扑结构的路由策略的有效性。

Abstract: Spatio-temporal forecasting on sensor graphs is commonly tackled with a single backbone architecture applied uniformly across all nodes, although graph regions can exhibit different dynamics. Road segments differ in functional class, structure, and traffic behavior, suggesting that node-wise expert specialization can be useful. We propose GC-MoE, a graph-conditioned mixture of experts framework that assigns each node a personalized combination of frozen forecasting experts based on graph topology and the recent traffic input window. GC-MoE combines frozen pretrained spatio-temporal GNN experts with an input-aware, spatially contextualized router while training only a lightweight routing module. We also study a bounded graph-conditioned output refinement layer as an optional extension and include node-adaptive ST-LoRA adapters only as an ablation diagnostic. Across four standard benchmarks (PEMS04, PEMS07, METR-LA, and PEMS-BAY), GC-MoE improves MAE over a zero-parameter ensemble baseline, with competitive RMSE and MAPE, while training only ~17K parameters on top of 1.5M frozen expert weights. The implementation is available at https://github.com/Ahghaffari/gc_moe.

</details>


### [99] [Why Linear Recurrent Memory Works in Partially Observable Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31261)
*Yike Zhao,Onno Eberhard,Malek Khammassi,Ali H. Sayed,Michael Muehlebach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论上证明了线性循环神经网络在部分可观测强化学习中作为记忆单元的有效性，通过构造两种线性滤波器：一种精确复现HMM的信念向量logits（确定性转移矩阵下），另一种在近确定性转移矩阵下实现状态解码误差趋零。结果扩展至动作控制的HMM，并通过实验验证了线性滤波器作为特征提取器的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性RNN在部分可观测强化学习中表现出色，但缺乏理论解释。本文旨在为其经验有效性提供理论依据。

Method: 构造两种线性滤波器：第一种在确定性转移矩阵下精确复现HMM信念向量的softmax前logits；第二种在近确定性转移矩阵下使状态解码误差趋零；并扩展到动作控制的时变HMM场景。

Result: 线性滤波器在确定性HMM下可作为最优策略学习的充分统计量；在近确定性HMM下可几乎消除状态模糊性；数值实验和小型强化学习游戏验证了其作为特征提取器的有效性。

Conclusion: 线性RNN家族的理论有效性得到证明，其构造的线性滤波器在特定条件下等价于最优信念状态或近似最优，为线性记忆单元在部分可观测强化学习中的应用提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: The family of linear recurrent neural networks has shown strong performance as recurrent memory units in partially observable reinforcement learning. We provide a theoretical justification for their empirical effectiveness by constructing and studying two linear filters: (i) the first exactly reproduces the pre-softmax logits of the belief vector in a hidden Markov model (HMM) under a deterministic transition matrix, thereby serving as a sufficient statistic for optimal policy learning, (ii) the second achieves vanishing state-decoding error under a nearly deterministic transition matrix, thus reducing state ambiguity to near zero. The results extend to action-controlled HMMs, where the corresponding linear filters become time-varying with action-dependent dynamics. We illustrate our main results through numerical experiments and further show that the constructed linear filter serves as a strong feature extractor in a small reinforcement learning game.

</details>


### [100] [MAAT: Multi-phase Adapter-Aware Targeted Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30514)
*Suryash Yagnik,Shubham Gaur,Saksham Thakur,Vinija Jain,Aman Chadha,Amitava Das*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 现有机器遗忘评估因"为什么"类因果知识样本占比极低（<1.3%）而产生结构性偏差。5WBENCH平衡基准使因果遗忘失败首次可量化，揭示现有方法无法同时实现高遗忘与高保留。MAAT通过多阶段LoRA适配器操作首次突破该Pareto前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器遗忘评估基准中"为什么"类因果/关系型问题的样本占比极低（CounterFact中<0.06%，ZSRE中0.6%，TOFU/MUSE/WMDP-Cyber中<1.3%），导致在因果知识上失败的方法在整体评分中仍可高分，且该失败无法通过非平衡评估检测。

Method: 提出5WBENCH平衡基准（5类各1000样本），以及MAAT框架：三阶段LoRA适配器操作，包括梯度投影上升、SVD秩维剪枝、任务向量消减、混合KL-隐状态保留修复。

Result: 现有方法在"为什么"类问题上均无法同时实现高遗忘与高保留。MAAT首次在因果知识的遗忘-保留Pareto前沿上达到新的操作点，同时实现高遗忘与高保留。

Conclusion: "为什么"类问题的困难源于多跳推理链（44% vs. ≤2%）和40.1 token长答案跨度的梯度稀释。MAAT是首个在因果知识上同时实现高遗忘与高保留的方法。

Abstract: Machine unlearning evaluation is structurally skewed: Why-type questions, which probe causal and relational knowledge, comprise less than 0.06% of CounterFact, 0.6% of ZSRE, and less than 1.3% of TOFU, MUSE, and WMDP-Cyber. This near-zero representation means that methods that fail on causal knowledge can score highly in aggregate, and this failure is undetectable without balanced evaluation. We present 5WBENCH, a balanced 5,000-sample benchmark with 1,000 examples per 5W category (Who, What, When, Where, Why), making causal unlearning failures quantifiable for the first time. Using 5WBENCH, we show that no existing baseline simultaneously achieves high forgetting and high retention on Why-type questions: aggressive forgetting degrades retained knowledge, while conservative methods fail to forget causal facts. Why-type difficulty stems from multi-hop reasoning chains (44% of Why entries vs. less than or equal to 2% for others) and gradient dilution over 40.1-token answer spans. We present MAAT (Multi-phase Adapter-Aware Targeted Unlearning), a three-phase framework operating on LoRA adapter weights, combining gradient-projected ascent, SVD rank-dimension pruning, task vector negation, and hybrid KL-hidden-state retain repair. MAAT is the first method to simultaneously achieve high forgetting and high retention on Why-type causal knowledge, reaching a new operating point on the forget-retain Pareto frontier. We make our code publicly available.

</details>


### [101] [Non-Asymptotic Convergence of Stochastic Iterative Algorithms: A Lyapunov Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31309)
*Zaiwei Chen,Siva Theja Maguluri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了基于Lyapunov技术的随机迭代算法（随机逼近）在有限时间分析中的应用，重点介绍了广义Moreau包络作为通用Lyapunov函数的方法，涵盖压缩算子、i.i.d.噪声、Markov噪声、半范数压缩算子、耗散算子及高概率界等场景，并应用于随机梯度下降、线性SA和强化学习算法（Q学习、时序差分学习）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为随机逼近算法的有限时间分析提供统一且自洽的理论框架，解决现有方法在不同范数和噪声假设下缺乏通用Lyapunov函数的问题，并推动在强化学习等领域的应用。

Method: 利用广义Moreau包络构造通用Lyapunov函数，在不同范数下统一分析压缩算子的随机逼近算法；从标准i.i.d.噪声情形拓展至Markov噪声、半范数压缩算子、耗散算子等复杂场景，并推导均方收敛保证和高概率界。

Result: 建立了基于广义Moreau包络的通用Lyapunov分析框架，给出随机梯度下降、线性SA、Q学习、时序差分学习等算法的均方收敛保证；成功将方法推广至Markov噪声、半范数压缩、耗散算子等情形，并得到高概率收敛界。

Conclusion: 该综述为随机逼近的有限时间分析提供了统一路线图，展示了广义Moreau包络作为通用工具的潜力，并指出了若干开放问题供后续研究。

Abstract: We survey Lyapunov-based techniques for the finite-time analysis of stochastic iterative algorithms, also known as stochastic approximation (SA) algorithms, for solving fixed-point equations $\bar{F}(x)=x$, where the operator $\bar{F}(\cdot)$ can only be accessed through a noisy oracle. We first focus on the standard setting in which $\bar{F}(\cdot)$ is contractive with respect to some norm and the noise is i.i.d., and explain how generalized Moreau envelopes serve as universal Lyapunov functions, regardless of the underlying norm. We then show how this framework yields mean-square convergence guarantees and applies to stochastic gradient descent, linear SA, and value-based reinforcement learning algorithms such as Q-learning and temporal-difference learning. Finally, we discuss extensions to Markovian noise, seminorm-contractive operators, dissipative operators, and high-probability bounds, and conclude with open problems. The goal is to present a unified and self-contained roadmap for the finite-time analysis of SA and its applications, especially in reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [102] [Revisiting Padded Transformer Expressivity: Which Architectural Choices Matter and Which Don't](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30523)
*Anej Svete,William Merrill,Ryan Cotterell,Ashish Sabharwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了填充Transformer（padded transformers）的表达能力，发现其在实用假设下对注意力类型、模型宽度和均匀性具有鲁棒性，而数值精度和模型深度是影响表达性的主要因素。具体证明了：多项式填充的常数精度Transformer等价于L-uniform AC⁰，增长精度达到L-uniform TC⁰；循环机制使对数深度循环的常数精度达到FO-uniform AC^d，增长精度达到FO-uniform TC^d；宽度或精度超过对数增长不会提升表达性，且结果对softmax和平均硬注意力均成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通过布尔电路描述Transformer的计算能力，但缺乏精确刻画且对建模选择敏感。填充Transformer虽可作为建立与电路类等价的有效工具，但已有研究仅覆盖有限类型的填充Transformer理想化模型，未充分探讨注意力类型、模型宽度和均匀性变化下等价性的鲁棒性。

Method: 采用形式化理论分析方法，在多项式填充设置下，分别考虑常数精度和增长精度两种数值精度情形，以及不同注意力类型（softmax和平均硬注意力）、模型宽度和均匀性条件，通过电路复杂度理论建立Transformer与电路类之间的等价关系，并进一步分析循环机制对表达能力的影响。

Result: 1）多项式填充的常数精度Transformer等价于L-uniform AC⁰，增长精度等价于L-uniform TC⁰，不受宽度影响；2）循环机制下，log^d N次循环的常数精度Transformer达到FO-uniform AC^d，增长精度达到FO-uniform TC^d；3）宽度或精度超过对数增长不会提升表达性；4）上述结果对softmax和平均硬注意力均成立。

Conclusion: 填充Transformer在实用假设下对注意力类型、模型宽度和均匀性具有出乎意料的鲁棒性，数值精度和模型深度是影响表达性的核心因素。该工作为理解Transformer的计算能力提供了更精确和鲁棒的理论刻画。

Abstract: Recent work describes what transformers can and cannot compute through connections to boolean circuits, but existing results lack exact characterizations and are sensitive to modeling choices. Padded transformers -- to whose input filler symbols such as ``...'' are appended -- emerge as a useful gadget for establishing equivalences to circuit classes by providing polynomial space for adaptive parallel computation. However, only a limited set of padded transformer idealizations has been studied, leaving open how robustly these equivalences hold under changes to attention type, model width, and uniformity. We find that, under practical assumptions, padded transformers are surprisingly robust to all of these, and identify numeric precision and model depth as the main factors affecting expressivity. Concretely, we prove that polynomially padded $\text{L-uniform}$ constant-precision transformers are equivalent to $\text{L-uniform AC}^0$, while growing-precision ones achieve $\text{L-uniform TC}^0$ regardless of width. Furthermore, looping enables sequential processing analogous to circuits: $\log^d N$-looped constant-precision transformers reach $\text{FO-uniform AC}^d$, and growing-precision ones reach $\text{FO-uniform TC}^d$. Interestingly, growing width or precision beyond logarithmic does not increase expressivity, and all our results hold for both softmax and average hard attention transformers.

</details>


### [103] [Convergence of Steepest Descent and Adam under Non-Uniform Smoothness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30648)
*Sharan Vaswani,Yifan Sun,Reza Babanezhad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在目标函数曲率与其函数值成仿射关系的非均匀光滑性假设下，最速下降法、RMSProp和Adam的确定性变体及符号梯度法的收敛性，证明它们在逻辑回归、softmax策略梯度等任务中比梯度下降更快，并给出了RMSProp和Adam优于AdaGrad等方法的理论下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将非均匀光滑性假设推广到目标函数曲率与函数值成仿射关系的更广泛场景，以更好地建模机器学习任务中的损失景观，并分析一阶方法在此类假设下的收敛行为。

Method: 提出目标函数曲率与目标值成仿射关系的假设（affine curvature assumption），结合梯度主导条件，推导最速下降法、确定性对角RMSProp和Adam的收敛率；通过理论分析证明符号梯度下降在线性收敛且快于梯度下降；给出两层神经网络上RMSProp和Adam线性收敛的条件；并构建下界证明所提方法优于AdaGrad、AMSGrad等。

Result: 1) 逻辑回归（可分数据）和softmax策略梯度目标下，符号梯度下降线性收敛且快于梯度下降；2) 特定两层神经网络在可分数据上，RMSProp和Adam以恒定步长和动量参数线性收敛；3) 下界表明RMSProp和Adam在本文假设下比AdaGrad、AMSGrad、梯度下降和重球动量法更快。

Conclusion: 本文建立的曲率-目标值仿射关系假设能统一解释多种机器学习任务中自适应方法的加速现象，为RMSProp和Adam在特定问题上的优势提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Recent work has analyzed the convergence of first-order methods under non-uniform smoothness assumptions that better model the loss landscape in machine learning tasks. We generalize this assumption to objectives whose curvature is an affine function of the objective value. This property is satisfied by a broad class of problems, including logistic regression, generalized linear models with a logistic link function, softmax policy gradient in reinforcement learning, and a class of neural networks. Under this assumption and gradient domination conditions, we establish a general convergence rate for the steepest descent method, and deterministic, diagonal variants of RMSProp and Adam. Our results imply that for logistic regression on separable data and the softmax policy gradient objective, sign GD converges linearly and is provably faster than GD. Furthermore, we show that for a class of two-layer neural networks on separable data, RMSProp and Adam can converge at a linear rate with a constant step-size and momentum parameter. Finally, we present a lower bound demonstrating that, under our assumption, RMSProp and Adam are provably faster than AdaGrad, AMSGrad, gradient descent, and heavy-ball momentum.

</details>


### [104] [Assign and Add: A Mechanistic Study of Compositional Arithmetic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31497)
*Brady Exoo,Alberto Bietti,John Sous*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究Transformer在变量赋值与模加法任务中的组合泛化机制，发现模型通过复用模块化加法MLP实现组合，训练呈现三阶段动力学：先学模加法，再学变量赋值结构，最后精炼泛化。理论框架解释了组合性如何从训练动态中涌现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型能够组合技能完成复杂任务，但组合发生的具体机制尚不明确。作者希望揭示Transformer中组合泛化的内在机理。

Method: 通过变量赋值与模加法的受控实验设置，将训练数据划分为不相交集合，观察小规模Transformer在未见过的变量与数字组合上的泛化表现。进行机制分析（发现同一模加法MLP模块被复用）和训练动力学分析（识别三阶段学习过程），并提供理论框架解释组合性涌现。

Result: 1) 小型Transformer能泛化到训练中未见过的变量与数字组合；2) 同一"模加法"MLP模块在输入直接给出或通过变量赋值机制间接给出时均被使用；3) 训练呈现三个阶段：模加法学习→变量赋值结构学习→精炼泛化阶段。

Conclusion: 组合泛化可以是Transformer内部机制组合性的自然结果，为理解大型语言模型的组合能力提供了机制层面的解释。

Abstract: Large language models are able to compose skills in order to perform complex tasks, many of which might not have been seen during training. The details of how exactly this composition occurs remain elusive. In this paper, we study a mechanism for compositional generalization in transformers by considering a simple controlled setting involving variable assignment and modular addition. By partitioning our training data into disjoint sets, we observe that small transformers are able to generalize to previously unseen combinations of variables and numbers. Our mechanistic analysis shows that the same ``modular addition'' MLP module is used whether the inputs are given directly or indirectly through a separate variable assignment mechanism. We also analyze the training dynamics from an empirical lens, which reveals three phases of learning: first, modular addition is learned, then the structure required for variable assignment, and finally a refinement phase where the model generalizes to some hard sequences not seen in training. Finally, we provide a theoretical framework to explain how compositionality emerges from training dynamics. These results suggest that compositional generalization can be a natural consequence of the compositionality of internal mechanisms in~transformers.

</details>


### [105] [Representation Collapse in Sequential Post-Training of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30524)
*Yichen Liu,Mingyu Chen,Hao Wang,Xiaoran Xu,Chenxi Lin,Rui Zhang,Yutong Zhou,Yuxin Yang,Jiarui Wu,Wei Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM在多阶段后训练过程中，表征是否会逐渐退化为低秩、各向异性且同质的特征空间，并发现这种过度集中化会降低后续可塑性、泛化能力和校准性能，同时探讨了若干轻量化干预措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究多阶段后训练（如SFT、偏好优化、安全微调、数学/代码专业化、长链思维微调等）是否导致内部表征逐渐压缩为低秩、各向异性且同质的特征空间，以及这种压缩是否对后续学习能力产生负面影响。

Method: 定义了一套针对隐藏状态、logits、token轨迹和LoRA更新的测量指标，在受控阶段顺序下分析多种后训练范式，并评估混合域回放、特征刷新、表征多样性正则化和LoRA更新去相关等轻量干预措施。

Result: 过度的表征集中化确实会降低后续适应的可塑性、削弱域外泛化能力并导致校准性能变差。

Conclusion: 轻量级干预措施可以在不牺牲后训练行为收益的前提下，有效保留模型未来的可学习性。

Abstract: Large language models are now adapted through chains of post-training stages rather than through a single instruction-tuning pass. This paper studies whether such sequential post-training gradually compresses internal representations into low-rank, anisotropic, and homogeneous feature spaces. We define a measurement suite for hidden states, logits, token trajectories, and LoRA updates, and we use it to analyze supervised fine-tuning, preference optimization, safety/refusal tuning, math and code specialization, and long chain-of-thought tuning under controlled stage orderings. The central hypothesis is that excessive representation concentration is not merely a geometric curiosity: it predicts reduced plasticity during later adaptation, weaker out-of-domain generalization, and poorer calibration. We further evaluate lightweight interventions, including mixed-domain replay, feature refresh, representation diversity regularization, and LoRA update decorrelation, as ways to preserve future learnability without giving up the behavioral gains of post-training.

</details>


### [106] [When Are Multimodal Predictions Biologically Supported? A Diagnostic Evaluation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31504)
*Dylan Steiner,Gustavo Arango-Argoty,Gerald Sun,Etai Jacob*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DECAT框架，用于在事后评估多模态模型是否学到共享生物信息、单模态生物信息或虚假相关性，通过五个零假设参考指标和规则决策，在合成数据和TCGA真实数据上验证，发现CLIP等纠缠模型在无共享生物时也会错误宣称共享。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态肿瘤学模型能产生准确预测，但无法区分模型学到的是跨模态共享的生物信息、单一模态特有的生物信息，还是反映混淆变量而非真正生物学的虚假相关性。需要一种方法诊断模型表征的性质。

Method: 提出DECAT，一个模型无关的事后评估框架，通过五个零假设参考指标和基于规则的决策过程，将多模态表征分类为四个诊断场景。该框架作用于学到的表征，无需知道具体混淆变量，证据不足时返回不确定。

Result: 在合成数据（超2500个训练表征，四种多模态模型类）和真实数据（8979名TCGA患者，多模态嵌入和五个预训练病理基础模型）上验证。纠缠模型（如CLIP）在共享生物学检测上近乎完美，但在其实基础模型嵌入中多数情况下无共享生物时也会错误宣称共享，错误率随混淆强度增加。DECAT能在无需混淆标签的情况下检测到AUROC无法发现的混淆。

Conclusion: DECAT框架能有效诊断多模态模型表征的性质，揭示纠缠模型可能错误宣称共享生物学的问题，且能检测到传统指标无法发现的混淆效应。

Abstract: Multimodal models in oncology can produce accurate predictions, but accurate prediction does not reveal whether the model has learned biology that is shared across modalities, biology confined to one modality, or spurious correlations that reflect confounders rather than genuine biology. We introduce DECAT, a model-agnostic post-hoc evaluation framework that classifies multimodal representations into four diagnostic scenarios for a given task and modality, using five null-referenced metrics and a rule-based decision procedure. The framework operates on learned representations, requires no knowledge of which specific confounder is present, and returns indeterminate when the evidence is insufficient. We validate DECAT on synthetic data across four multimodal model classes (over 2,500 trained representations) and on real data from 8,979 TCGA patients, evaluating both multimodal embeddings and five pretrained pathology foundation models. Entangled models (e.g., CLIP) achieve near-perfect shared biology detection but falsely claim shared biology in the majority of cases where it is absent on real foundation model embeddings. This false claim rate increases with confound strength so that larger cohorts and stronger representations produce more confident but still incorrect diagnoses. Applied to both multimodal TCGA embeddings and five pathology foundation models without paired RNA, DECAT detects confounding invisible to AUROC without requiring the confounder labels, as confirmed by post-hoc stratification.

</details>


### [107] [Measuring, Localizing, and Ablating Alignment Signatures in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30526)
*Aniket Anand,Janvijay Singh,Zhewei Sun,Dilek Hakkani-Tür,Nick Feamster*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现后训练（post-training）会使语言模型产生可检测的AI风格，并提出PASTA方法通过对齐-基础残差对比来估计并消融这种风格方向，从而降低AI检测率，同时保持生成文本的相关性和连贯性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对齐语言模型通常表现出可识别的AI风格，但后训练与这种风格及内部表征之间的关系尚不清楚。作者希望探究后训练是否引入或放大了AI风格规律性，以及这些规律性是否存在局部化的内部特征。

Method: 1) 比较人类文本、基础模型生成和对齐模型生成在匹配前缀下的风格差异；2) 提出PASTA（Post-training Alignment Signature Targeted Ablation），一种无需训练的方法，通过对齐-基础残差对比估计后训练对齐特征，在解码时消融对应方向。

Result: 对齐生成的文本相比基础生成显示出更低的人类语料库亲和力和更高的AI检测率。在11个对齐模型和6个AI检测器上，PASTA降低了对齐模型的检测率；该效果跨检测器泛化良好，且不能被随机方向复现。PASTA生成文本保持相关性和连贯性，同时表现出更大的风格多样性。

Conclusion: 后训练的AI风格效应可以被测量、定位，并通过激活消融进行因果检验。

Abstract: Aligned language models often exhibit a recognizable AI-like style, yet its connection to post-training and internal representations remains poorly understood. In this work, we study whether post-training introduces or amplifies AI-like stylistic regularities and whether these regularities have a localized internal signature. To this end, we compare human text, base-model generations, and aligned-model generations under matched human-source prefixes. Aligned generations show lower human-corpus affinity and higher AI-detection rates than base generations, suggesting that post-training shifts generated text away from human-corpus style and toward detector-visible AI-like text. We then introduce PASTA (Post-training Alignment Signature Targeted Ablation), a training-free method that estimates a post-training alignment signature from aligned-base residual contrasts and ablates the corresponding direction during decoding. Across 11 aligned models and 6 AI detectors, PASTA lowers the detection rate for most aligned models; this effect transfers well across detectors and is not reproduced by random directions. Qualitative analysis suggests that PASTA generations remain relevant and coherent while exhibiting greater stylistic variation. Together, these results show that AI-like stylistic effects of post-training can be measured, localized, and causally tested through activation ablation.

</details>


### [108] [The Dynamic-Probabilistic Consistency Gap in Chaotic Surrogate Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31547)
*Andre Herz,Matthijs Pals,Daniel Durstewitz,Georgia Koppe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了动力系统重建中的动态-概率一致性差距，即有限时域概率目标会导致动力学退化或预测不确定性偏离局部切向动力学。作者提出了KAFFEE框架，通过可微分扩展卡尔曼滤波器训练来缩小这一差距，在随机超混沌Lorenz-96系统上减少了识别出的失败模式，并在13个混沌系统的零样本适应中保持了良好的动力学性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动力系统重建中，现有方法追求有限时域概率目标（如开环高斯分布）会导致模型动力学退化，或使预测不确定性不再反映局部切向动力学。作者希望弥合这一动态-概率一致性差距，使重建模型既能准确捕捉动力学，又能提供符合系统本质的不确定性估计。

Method: KAFFEE框架基于可微分扩展卡尔曼滤波（EKF）训练，通过评估局部预测残差（新息）的似然来计算损失，同时利用学习到的局部雅可比矩阵传输协方差。该方法将滤波过程融入训练，避免了开环目标导致的雅可比生成协方差增长被惩罚的问题。

Result: 在随机超混沌Lorenz-96系统上，KAFFEE相比于开环目标方法，减少了核心坍塌、噪声掩盖和盲目不确定性三种失败模式，改进了动力学不变量重建，并保持了有竞争力的预测分数。在13个混沌系统的DSR基础模型概率适应中，KAFFEE实现了上下文贝叶斯滤波，同时基本保留了零样本动力学性能。

Conclusion: 动态-概率一致性差距是动力系统重建中一个根本性问题，KAFFEE通过将EKF训练框架引入DSR提供了一种有效缓解方案，为高维混沌系统中不确定性感知的动力学建模提供了新方向。

Abstract: Dynamical systems reconstruction (DSR) aims to learn surrogate models that capture the dynamics underlying time-series data. Reliably deploying these surrogates requires uncertainty estimates consistent with the learned dynamics. We expose a dynamic-probabilistic consistency (DPC) gap: the pursuit of finite-horizon probabilistic objectives can degrade dynamics or decouple predictive uncertainty from the local tangent dynamics it ought to reflect. We isolate three mechanisms behind this gap: core collapse, noise masking, and blind uncertainty. Specifically, we show that open-loop Gaussian rollout objectives can penalize Jacobian-generated covariance growth in chaotic systems, encouraging optimization shortcuts that weaken physical expansion or decouple uncertainty from it. To mitigate this gap, we propose KAFFEE (Kalman-Aware Framework For Ergodic Emulation), a differentiable extended Kalman filter-based training framework that evaluates likelihood on local predictive residuals (innovations) while transporting covariance through learned local Jacobians. On stochastic hyperchaotic Lorenz-96, KAFFEE reduces the identified failure modes, improves reconstruction of dynamical invariants relative to open-loop objectives, and maintains competitive predictive scores. We further show that the DPC gap appears when probabilistically adapting a DSR foundation model across 13 chaotic systems, where KAFFEE enables in-context Bayesian filtering while largely preserving zero-shot dynamics.

</details>


### [109] [The Long-Term Effects of Data Selection in LLM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30537)
*Yuxin Yang,Aoxiong Zeng,Xiangquan Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出多阶段微调中数据选择的“长视距”视角，发现短视选择策略会出现排名反转（当前受益但后续学习变慢、遗忘加剧），并形式化该问题为“短视选择”，提出一种包含覆盖度、未来代理迁移和反集中项的长视距感知选择（LHAS）目标函数作为诊断工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据选择方法多关注单阶段效用（如损失、梯度、多样性等），忽略了在多阶段微调中当前最优策略可能损害模型后续的适应能力、遗忘控制和鲁棒性。

Method: 在统一的多阶段协议下对比了随机、基于损失、梯度、多样性、质量和效用-多样性六类选择策略；形式化定义了“短视选择”并给出局部分析；提出LHAS目标函数，在即时效用基础上加入覆盖度、未来代理迁移和反集中项。

Result: 短视选择策略存在排名反转现象：提升当前阶段性能的同时导致后续学习速度下降、遗忘加剧；LHAS目标可诊断并缓解此问题。

Conclusion: 数据选择应被视为一种塑造模型学习轨迹的训练干预，而非仅作为局部数据效率机制；评估需纳入长期指标（适应速度、遗忘、能力失衡、分布外鲁棒性）。

Abstract: Data selection is increasingly used to reduce the cost of large language model (LLM) fine-tuning, with recent methods prioritizing samples by current utility, diversity, quality, or influence. This paper studies a different question: when fine-tuning occurs over multiple stages, can selection strategies that look optimal now make the model less adaptable later? We introduce a long-horizon view of LLM data selection in which a selector is evaluated not only by immediate task performance, but also by future adaptation speed, forgetting, capability imbalance, and out-of-distribution robustness. We compare representative random, loss-based, gradient-based, diversity-based, quality-based, and utility-diversity selection families under a unified multi-stage protocol. Through controlled experiments designed to instantiate this protocol, we show how short-term selectors can exhibit rank reversal: they improve the current stage while slowing subsequent learning and increasing forgetting. We formalize this behavior as \emph{myopic selection}, provide a simple local analysis of why it can occur, and propose a diagnostic Long-Horizon Aware Selection (LHAS) objective that augments immediate utility with coverage, future-proxy transfer, and anti-concentration terms. The study argues that data selection should be evaluated as a training intervention that shapes the model's learning trajectory, rather than only as a local data-efficiency mechanism.

</details>


### [110] [A Tight Theory of Error Feedback Algorithms in Distributed Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31594)
*Daniel Berg Thomsen,Adrien Taylor,Aymeric Dieuleveut*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对分布式学习中的通信瓶颈问题，深入分析了两种主要的误差反馈算法（EF和EF21），通过识别最优步长选择和构建最优Lyapunov函数，提供了紧的收敛性分析，且结果与智能体数量无关，在单智能体场景下恢复了已知的最佳保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式学习中的通信开销是主要瓶颈，梯度压缩虽可缓解但会损害收敛性。误差反馈机制能有效补救这一问题，然而众多变体的相对性能尚不明确，缺乏统一的理论理解。

Method: 识别两种误差反馈算法（EF和EF21）的最优步长选择；构建针对每种算法量身定制的最优Lyapunov函数；进行紧的收敛性分析，确保结果与智能体数量无关。

Result: 提供了EF和EF21两种算法的紧收敛保证；在单智能体场景下恢复了已知的最佳收敛保证；结果具有与智能体数量无关的普适性。

Conclusion: 通过最优步长选择和定制化Lyapunov函数，可以显著提升误差反馈算法的收敛性分析质量，为分布式优化中的梯度压缩提供了更坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: Communication costs are a major bottleneck in distributed learning and first-order optimization. A common approach to alleviate this issue is to compress the gradient information exchanged between agents. However, such compression typically degrades the convergence guarantees of gradient-based methods. Error feedback mechanisms provide a simple and computationally cheap remedy for this issue, but numerous variants have been proposed, and their relative performance remains poorly understood. This paper provides tight convergence analyses for two of the main error-feedback algorithms from the literature, the classic Error Feedback method (EF) and Error Feedback 21 (EF21), by identifying optimal step-size choices and constructing optimal Lyapunov functions tailored to each method. The results hold independently of the number of agents and recover the known best guarantees possible in the single-agent regime.

</details>


### [111] [DisasterLex: An Expert Concept-to-Schema Knowledge Graph for Geospatial Reasoning in Disaster Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30538)
*Yiming Xiao,Ankit Basu,Kai Yin,Sahil Vartak,Christian Swords,Ali Mostafavi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DisasterLex 是一个知识图谱介导的文本到SQL框架，通过插入专家知识图谱（EKG）连接用户查询与灾难管理数据库，在75查询测试集上相较四个基线方法提升1.4倍至2.75倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灾难响应需要查询结构化表格数据，但现有文本到SQL方法在灾难领域迁移效果差，因为该领域查询涉及异构地理空间模式且需要因果推理。

Method: DisasterLex框架在用户查询与数据库之间插入专家知识图谱（EKG），包含概念、类型化因果边和概念到表的链接，通过四阶段编排（实体识别、领域路由、因果规划、SQL生成）逐步限制传递给模型的模式。

Result: 在7个基础模型上，DisasterLex相比LightRAG、HippoRAG 2、ReFoRCE、CHESS四个基线方法提升1.4倍至2.75倍，绝对得分1.65-3.56（满分5.0）。错误分析显示基线失败集中在路由和多表SQL组合操作上。

Conclusion: 知识图谱介导的框架能有效提升灾难领域文本到SQL的查询性能，代码、数据和EKG工件已开源。

Abstract: Disasters are inevitable and increasingly costly, and effective response depends on querying structured tabular data: precise, information-dense records of hazard, exposure, vulnerability, and lifeline infrastructure that underpin disaster management. Current text-to-SQL methods enable natural-language access to such tables but transfer poorly to the disaster domain, where queries span heterogeneous geospatial schemas and require reasoning over causal relations. We introduce DisasterLex, a knowledge-graph-mediated framework that inserts an Expert Knowledge Graph (EKG) of curated concepts and typed causal edges between the user query and the database, bridged to schema by concept-to-table links. The orchestration runs four stages (identifying query entities, routing to the operational domain, planning over causal edges, and grounding the SQL), restricting the schema passed to the model at each step. We instantiate it on a disaster-analytics database (36 geospatial tables, 150 columns) with an EKG of 107 concepts, 117 causal edges, and 52 concept-to-schema links, evaluated on a 75-query test set. On all seven base models spanning proprietary and open-weight families, DisasterLex beats four state-of-the-art baselines (LightRAG, HippoRAG 2, ReFoRCE, CHESS) by 1.4x to 2.75x, with absolute scores of 1.65 to 3.56 (of 5.0). Error analysis shows baseline failures cluster in routing and multi-table SQL composition, the operations our orchestration explicitly addresses. Code, data, and the EKG artifact are available at https://github.com/YimingXiao98/DisasterLex and on Zenodo at https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.20388029.

</details>


### [112] [SubsurfaceGen: Procedural Generation of Field-Scale Earth Models and Seismic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30541)
*Joseph Stitt,Pratik Rathore,Madeleine Udell,Ching-Yao Lai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了SubsurfaceGen，一个GPU加速的3D速度模型与地震数据生成器，并发布了包含4,276个2D速度切片、5秒波场和8秒炮集记录的数据集，覆盖六种地质环境，用于解决机器学习全波形反演训练数据在空间/时间尺度、地质多样性和物理真实性方面的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FWI训练数据资源（如Marmousi、SEAM、OpenFWI）在空间范围、时间范围、地质多样性和物理真实性方面存在严重不足，无法满足机器学习方法对场地尺度、地质多样且物理真实训练数据的需求。

Method: 开发了GPU加速的SubsurfaceGen生成器，构建了42个真实场地尺度的3D速度模型（10 km x 10 km横向范围，6.19 km深度，10 m分辨率），从中提取2D切片并生成波场和炮集记录，涵盖六种地质环境（四种由SubsurfaceGen构建，两种来自现有源）。

Result: 生成了包含4,276个2D速度切片、5秒波场和8秒炮集记录的数据集；利用该数据集评估了神经算子（波场预测）和编码器-解码器（端到端速度反演），揭示了场地尺度下的失效模式。

Conclusion: SubsurfaceGen及配套数据集能够为基于机器学习的FWI提供更真实、多样且尺度合适的数据支持，并揭示了现有方法在场地尺度下的局限性，具有推动ML-FWI发展的潜力。

Abstract: Full waveform inversion (FWI) is the gold standard for subsurface imaging, with applications from carbon sequestration to energy and mineral exploration to earthquake hazard assessment. Machine learning approaches to FWI need field-scale, geologically diverse, and physically realistic training data, but existing resources such as Marmousi, SEAM, and OpenFWI fall short on spatial extent, temporal extent, geological diversity, and physical realism. We address these limitations with SubsurfaceGen, a GPU-accelerated generator for 3D velocity models and seismic data. Along with SubsurfaceGen, we release a paired dataset of 4,276 2D velocity slices, 5 s wavefields, and 8 s shot gathers drawn from 42 realistic, field-scale 3D velocity models, each spanning 10 km x 10 km laterally and 6.19 km deep at 10 m resolution. The dataset spans six geological settings -- four built with SubsurfaceGen and two drawn from prior sources -- relevant for carbon sequestration and hydrocarbon exploration. We use this dataset to evaluate neural operators on wavefield prediction and encoder-decoders on end-to-end velocity inversion, holding out one geological setting for out-of-distribution testing. These experiments surface failure modes at field-scale and demonstrate how SubsurfaceGen and the associated dataset can impact ML-based FWI.

</details>


### [113] [Early Prediction of Future Behavioral Strategy from Process Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30550)
*Robert Kasumba,Dennis Barbour,Chien-Ju Ho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出过程级潜变量模型(PLVM)，通过跨任务的部分行为轨迹预测新任务中的策略模式。在PowerWash Simulator游戏数据中，PLVM利用两个清洁任务的部分轨迹，成功预测目标任务中Zone Planner与Zone Hopper两种策略。仿真实验表明跨任务融合在源任务揭示共享潜过程互补维度时效果最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应系统常需从有限证据中做出针对特定任务的个体决策，但个体层级的倾向难以从标准行为证据中推断。聚合结果摘要会混淆不同行为过程，而单任务内的过程建模会混淆稳定倾向与任务特定布局。

Method: 提出过程级潜变量模型(PLVM)，编码任务特定轨迹并将其融合为共享的个体层级潜表征，用于跨任务预测。

Result: 在PowerWash Simulator自然数据集上，PLVM利用两个清洁任务的局部轨迹预测Fire Station关卡中的Zone Planner与Zone Hopper行为。控制仿真显示跨任务融合在源任务揭示共享潜过程互补维度时效果最佳。

Conclusion: 过程级跨任务建模能在观察目标任务充分行为不可行时，支持目标任务策略的早期预测。

Abstract: Adaptive systems often need to make task-specific decisions about people from limited evidence: a tutor may need to anticipate how a learner will approach a new problem, a game may need to adapt when a player enters a new level, and a human-AI system may need to infer whether a partner will persist with a plan or switch goals. These decisions depend on person-level tendencies that shape how people solve related tasks, but such tendencies are difficult to infer from standard behavioral evidence. One approach is to use aggregate outcome summaries, such as scores, completion rates, or productivity; these summaries are compact and available across tasks, but can collapse distinct behavioral processes into similar outcomes. Another approach is to use process-level traces, which record how behavior unfolds; however, process modeling within one task can entangle stable person-level tendencies with task-specific layout and affordances. In this work, we study early cross-task behavioral inference: whether partial source-task process traces can reveal transferable person-level structure that predicts strategy in a held-out target task. We introduce a Process-Level Latent Variable Model (PLVM), which encodes task-specific traces and fuses them into a shared person-level latent representation for cross-task prediction. In PowerWash Simulator, a naturalistic telemetry dataset of human gameplay, PLVM uses partial traces from two cleaning tasks to predict locally persistent Zone Planner behavior versus frequent Zone Hopper behavior in the held-out Fire Station level. Controlled simulations with known latent types show that cross-task fusion helps when source tasks reveal complementary dimensions of a shared latent process. These results suggest that process-level cross-task modeling can support early prediction of target-task strategy when observing sufficient target-task behavior is impractical.

</details>


### [114] [Destruction is a General Strategy to Learn Generation; Diffusion's Strength is to Take it Seriously; Exploration is the Future](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30553)
*Pierre-André Noël*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将扩散模型置于信息掩蔽与重建的框架下理解，认为其“破坏式”信息掩蔽比手工设计的方式更灵活，尤其在数据稀缺场景下具有优势。同时讨论了强化学习方法迁移到扩散模型时的微妙问题，并探索了更具扩散原生特性的探索方法。文章附带教程，引入了一种新型概率图模型以辅助阐述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在统一扩散模型与更广泛的机器学习范式（信息掩蔽与重建），论证扩散模型“破坏式”信息掩蔽的灵活性优势，并指出强化学习技术迁移到扩散领域时存在的细微问题，探索更适配扩散的解决方案。

Method: 将扩散模型置于“破坏-生成”（destroy-then-generate）视角下分析；引入一种新型概率图模型以辅助教程阐述；指出强化学习技术移植到扩散上下文时需注意的微妙问题，并探讨扩散原生的探索方法。

Result: 论证了扩散模型作为信息掩蔽与重建框架的一部分，其破坏式信息掩蔽方式在数据稀缺场景下具有优势；识别了强化学习方法迁移至扩散模型时的潜在问题；提出了若干值得探索的研究方向。

Conclusion: 扩散模型的破坏式信息掩蔽是一种灵活且具有潜力的范式，尤其在数据稀缺场景下；强化学习技术的直接移植需谨慎，应发展更具扩散原生特性的探索方法；作者并未给出最终答案，但指出了值得继续深入的研究方向。

Abstract: I present diffusion models as part of a family of machine learning techniques that withhold information from a model's input and train it to guess the withheld information. I argue that diffusion's destroying approach to withholding is more flexible than typical hand-crafted information withholding techniques, providing a rich training playground that could be advantageous in some settings, notably data-scarce ones. I then address subtle issues that may arise when porting reinforcement learning techniques to the diffusion context, and wonder how such exploration problems could be addressed in more diffusion-native ways. I do not have definitive answers, but I do point my fingers in directions I deem interesting. A tutorial follows this thesis, expanding on the destroy-then-generate perspective. A novel kind of probabilistic graphical models is introduced to facilitate the tutorial's exposition.

</details>


### [115] [Supervised Training Rapidly Degrades Early Visual Cortex Alignment Across Biologically Plausible Learning Rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30556)
*Nils Leutenegger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 未经训练的随机神经网络在表征相似性上始终与早期视觉皮层相当甚至更优，这一反直觉发现挑战了"训练提升脑对齐"的假设。通过追踪四种学习规则（BP、FA、PC、STDP）在训练过程中与人类fMRI数据的对齐变化，发现单轮训练即导致V1对齐度下降25-90%，其中反向传播下降最严重，而预测编码和STDP保留更多脑样结构；在物体选择性皮层中则呈现相反趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究为何未经训练的随机神经网络在表征相似性上能与甚至超过训练过的网络与早期视觉皮层的对齐程度，挑战"学习提升脑对齐"的传统假设，并揭示不同学习规则如何重塑网络表征与大脑的对应关系。

Method: 采用表征相似性分析（RSA），使用THINGS数据库720张物体图像和三名被试的fMRI数据，计算六组视觉ROI中模型与大脑表征相异矩阵（RDM）之间的Spearman相关系数。在四种学习规则（反向传播BP、反馈对齐FA、预测编码PC、脉冲时序依赖可塑性STDP）下，追踪八个训练检查点（epoch 0-40）的对齐度变化。

Result: (1) 单轮训练使V1对齐度下降25-90%，因学习规则而异；(2) BP对V1对齐度损害最严重（Δr=-0.080），PC和STDP保留更多（Δr≈-0.04）；(3) 在物体选择性皮层（LOC）呈现较弱的相反趋势，BP训练后对齐度提升最大，但绝对变化较小。

Conclusion: 未经训练的网络仅通过归纳偏置即可捕获低级视觉统计特征；全局误差信号（如BP）对早期表征的重塑比局部学习规则（PC、STDP）更剧烈，后者能更好地保留脑样结构。

Abstract: Random, untrained neural networks consistently match or exceed trained networks in representational similarity to early visual cortex. This puzzling finding challenges the assumption that learning improves brain alignment. We investigate it by tracking representational similarity analysis (RSA) alignment to human fMRI data across training for four learning rules: backpropagation (BP), feedback alignment (FA), predictive coding (PC), and spike-timing-dependent plasticity (STDP). Using 720 object images from the THINGS database and fMRI data from three subjects across six visual ROIs, we measure Spearman correlations between model and brain representational dissimilarity matrices at eight training checkpoints (epochs 0-40). We find that (1) a single epoch of training reduces V1 alignment by 25-90%, depending on the learning rule; (2) backpropagation reduces V1 alignment most severely (delta r = -0.080), while predictive coding and STDP preserve substantially more (delta r ~ -0.04); and (3) a weaker, opposite tendency appears in object-selective cortex (LOC), where BP shows the largest increase in alignment during training, although the absolute change is small. These results suggest that untrained architectures capture low-level visual statistics through inductive biases alone, and that global error signals (BP) reshape early representations more aggressively than local learning rules (PC, STDP), which better preserve brain-like structure.

</details>


### [116] [Zeroth-Order Non-Log-Concave Sampling with Variance Reduction and Applications to Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30573)
*M. Berk Sahin,Behzad Sharif,Abolfazl Hashemi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种方差缩减的零阶Langevin采样方法，用于高维、非对数凹分布的黑箱采样，首次建立了零阶非对数凹采样的非渐近收敛保证，并应用于基于分数的生成先验的黑箱逆问题后验采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维非对数凹分布的黑箱采样（梯度不可获取或计算昂贵）是机器学习中的基础挑战。现有Langevin动力学方法在黑箱场景下方差大且缺乏非渐近收敛保证。

Method: 提出方差缩减的零阶Langevin采样方法，采用改进的梯度估计器降低经典批零阶估计器的方差，消除批大小对维度的依赖。同时提出ZO-APMC算法用于黑箱逆问题的后验采样。

Result: 建立了ε-相对Fisher信息下的首个非渐近收敛保证；在Poincaré不等式假设下，建立了平方总变差距离的收敛保证；为基于分数的生成先验的黑箱逆问题后验采样提供了首个非渐近收敛保证。合成实验和实际线性/非线性逆问题验证了理论有效性。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效解决了黑箱非对数凹采样的高方差和缺乏理论保证问题，在理论和实践上均表现优异。

Abstract: Sampling from high-dimensional, non-log-concave distributions with unnormalized densities remains a fundamental challenge in machine learning, particularly in black-box settings where gradient information is inaccessible or computationally prohibitive. While Langevin dynamics provides a principled framework for sampling when gradients are accessible, its extension to the black-box settings suffers from high variance and lacks non-asymptotic convergence guarantees for non-log-concave sampling. To address these limitations, we propose a variance-reduced zeroth-order Langevin sampling method. Our method employs a gradient estimator that substantially reduces the variance of the classical batched zeroth-order estimator and eliminates the unfavorable dimensional dependence of the batch size required for accurate estimation, enabling practical and stable sampling. We establish the first non-asymptotic convergence guarantees for zeroth-order non-log-concave sampling in terms of $\varepsilon$-relative Fisher information, and, under a Poincaré inequality assumption, squared total variation distance. We further propose ZO-APMC, a posterior sampling algorithm for black-box inverse problems with pre-trained score-based generative priors, establishing the first non-asymptotic convergence guarantees for such methods. We validate our theory through synthetic experiments and demonstrate strong empirical performance on practical linear and nonlinear inverse problems.

</details>


### [117] [Benchmarking Machine Learning Uncertainty Quantification Methodologies for Predicting Turbine Gas Temperature Degradation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30585)
*Jostein Barry-Straume,Changmin Son,Adrian Sandu,Gavan Burke,Rekha Sundararajan,Andrew Rimell,James G. Steinrock*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了五种构建预测区间的方法（Delta法、贝叶斯蒙特卡洛Dropout、Bootstrap法、下上界估计和均值方差估计），用于量化涡轮燃气温度的神经网络预测不确定性，并通过统一实验框架评估了各方法在覆盖概率、区间宽度和稳定性方面的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代发动机的预测与健康管理需要准确的涡轮燃气温度预测和稳健的不确定性量化，以确保可靠性和安全性。

Method: 在统一实验框架下实现五种预测区间构建方法，采用交叉验证进行超参数选择、重复训练-测试划分评估性能鲁棒性，并使用覆盖概率、归一化平均预测区间宽度和覆盖宽度准则三个指标综合评估各方法的准确性和紧致性。

Result: 五种方法在区间覆盖、宽度和稳定性方面表现出不同的权衡特性。

Conclusion: 研究结果为发动机健康管理和预测中预测区间方法的选择与调优提供了实用指南，确保实际应用中的可解释性和精度。

Abstract: Effective prognostics and health management of modern engines relies on accurate turbine gas temperature predictions and robust uncertainty quantification to ensure reliability and safety. This paper investigates five major approaches for constructing prediction intervals -- namely the Delta method, Bayesian Monte Carlo Dropout, Bootstrap method, Lower-Upper Bound Estimation, and Mean-Variance Estimation -- as a means of capturing the uncertainty in neural network predictions of turbine gas temperature. Each approach is implemented within a unified experimental framework that employs cross-validation for hyperparameter selection, repeated train-test splits for performance robustness, and multiple metrics to evaluate both the accuracy and tightness of the intervals. In particular, Coverage Probability, Normalized Mean Prediction Interval Width, and the Coverage Width-based Criterion are measured to comprehensively assess each method's reliability and sharpness. Experiments conducted on a representative turbine gas temperature dataset reveal distinct trade-offs among the five methods in terms of interval coverage, width, and stability. These findings provide a practical guide for selecting and tuning prediction interval methods in engine health management and prognostics, ensuring both interpretability and precision in real-world applications.

</details>


### [118] [Counterfactual Evaluation Reveals Hidden Capability Profiles in Clinical LLMs and Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30590)
*Matt Turk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出因果敏感性评分（CSS），一种通过沿五个临床维度（生物标志物翻转、既往治疗失败、生物标志物移除、手术状态变更、分期扰动）对肿瘤病例进行干预性变异来评估临床AI系统响应性的指标。与基于覆盖率的共识匹配评分（CMS）相比，CSS揭示了模型排序的彻底反转（CMS最差模型变成CSS最佳），并暴露了所有前沿模型在手术状态干预上的普遍安全盲区。CSS还可迁移至工具使用智能体，并具有跨评判者复现性和医学专家验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于覆盖率的评估指标（如CMS）无法区分表面上表现相似但实际行为截然不同的临床AI系统——一个系统能根据患者输入变化更新推荐，另一个则输出不变。这种评估盲区可能导致部署行为不可靠的AI系统，因此需要一种能直接测量模型对临床输入变化响应性的干预性指标。

Method: 设计CSS指标，对224个肿瘤多学科会诊病例沿五个临床维度（生物标志物翻转、既往治疗失败、生物标志物移除、手术状态变更、分期扰动）进行干预性变异，预先注册每个变异对应的正确推荐更新方向，用{0, 0.5, 1.0}三级评分评估模型推荐是否朝正确方向变化。在六款前沿模型上比较CSS与CMS（基于覆盖率的加权召回指标）的排序结果，并进行ReAct风格的工具使用实验和跨评判者复现验证。

Result: 六款模型在CSS与CMS上排序几乎完全相反：所有模型排名均发生变化，CMS最差模型变成CSS最佳，CMS中上等模型在CSS上垫底。所有前沿模型在手术状态变更干预上表现极差（最高仅17.2% CSS），这一缺陷未被CMS暴露。工具使用提升了五款模型的CSS（+2.5至+20.3个百分点），但最低CSS模型即使检索相同图表章节仍无法更新推荐，暴露出结构性响应不足。

Conclusion: 干预性预注册指标（如CSS）能捕捉覆盖率指标遗漏的模型响应性信息，可作为覆盖率的互补评估工具，并为未来的智能体强化学习系统提供密集奖励信号。

Abstract: Two clinical AI systems can score nearly identically on coverage-based rubrics yet behave radically differently when their patient inputs change: one updates its recommendations to match the new clinical signal, while the other produces the same output regardless. We introduce the Causal Sensitivity Score (CSS), a pre-registered interventional metric that mutates oncology tumor-board cases along five clinically meaningful dimensions - biomarker flips, prior-treatment failures, biomarker removals, surgery-status changes, and stage perturbations - and scores whether each model updates its recommendations in the pre-registered correct direction using a {0, 0.5, 1.0} scale. Benchmarked against the Consensus Match Score (CMS), a coverage-based weighted recall metric, six frontier models from three labs evaluated in single-shot inference across 224 cases rank in nearly opposite orders: all six models change rank, the CMS-worst model becomes CSS-best, and one upper-mid CMS model ranks last on CSS. We further surface a universal safety blind spot: every frontier model fails on surgery-status interventions (at most 17.2% CSS on Family D), a finding CMS does not expose. The metric also transfers to tool-using agents: in a ReAct-style experiment, tool use improves CSS for five of six models (+2.5 to +20.3 percentage points), yet the lowest-CSS model retrieves the same chart sections and still fails to update its recommendations - revealing a structural responsiveness deficit visible only under counterfactual evaluation. Cross-judge replication and three-rater medical-professional validation confirm the aggregate findings. Interventional pre-registered metrics like CSS complement coverage-based evaluation for clinical AI agents: they capture responsiveness that coverage metrics miss and offer a candidate dense reward signal for future agentic RL systems.

</details>


### [119] [Learning Transferable Predictability Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30592)
*Diyali Goswami,Auroop R. Ganguly*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GON模型，通过锚定-方差目标固定序数评分坐标，解决跨系统可预测性排序的尺度模糊问题，在五级可预测性阶梯上实现一致的序数评分，并验证了预训练初始化在跨系统迁移中的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法仅能在单一系统内区分确定性与随机动力学，且无法产生跨系统具有一致数值解释的评分。评分监督仅提供排序信息，导致评分坐标存在单调重参数化的不确定性（规范自由度），需要固定评分坐标以实现跨系统可迁移的序数评分。

Method: 提出GON（Gauge-Fixed Ordinal Network），采用时序卷积模型，结合锚定-方差目标函数将各层级得分均值固定到共享目标坐标；使用2-jet特征提取局部轨迹几何信息，该特征在光滑流下保持不变，而被随机替代过程破坏。

Result: 在五个留出动力系统上，预训练GON检查点初始化在所有窗口预算下均优于从头训练；零样本评分在随机边界保持序数结构；迁移深度反映与训练家族的几何接近性。

Conclusion: 成对判别与全局一致的序数评分是不同性质的任务，需要稳定的评分坐标实现跨系统迁移，这对可预测性评估、模型选择和自然/工程动力系统的早期预警诊断具有重要意义。

Abstract: We study the problem of assigning a scalar score to a short trajectory window that reflects its position on an ordered continuum of predictability regimes, spanning structured deterministic dynamics to unstructured stochastic noise. Existing methods address deterministic-versus-stochastic discrimination within a single system and do not produce scores with a consistent numerical interpretation across systems. We formalize this as ordinal estimation over a five-level predictability ladder and identify a structural source of cross-system ambiguity: ranking supervision alone leaves the score coordinate unfixed up to a monotone reparameterization, which we term the gauge freedom of ordinal scoring. We propose the Gauge-Fixed Ordinal Network (GON), a temporal convolutional model trained with an anchor-and-variance objective that pins level-wise score means to shared target coordinates. GON operates on 2-jet features that expose local trajectory geometry, preserved by smooth flows and disrupted by stochastic surrogate procedures. On five held-out dynamical systems, initializing from a pretrained GON checkpoint consistently outperforms training from scratch across all window budgets, with adaptation depth reflecting geometric proximity to the training family. Zero-shot scores retain ordinal structure at the stochastic boundary, where surrogate procedures most strongly disrupt nonlinear geometry, and pretrained initialization consistently beats scratch across all window budgets. Pairwise discrimination and globally coherent ordinal scoring are distinct properties requiring a stable score coordinate for cross-system transfer, with direct implications for predictability assessment, model selection, and early-warning diagnostics across natural and engineered dynamical systems.

</details>


### [120] [Scientific Machine Learning for Engine Health Management and Remaining Useful Life Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30593)
*Jostein Barry-Straume,Changmin Son,Adrian Sandu,Gavan Burke,Rekha Sundararajan,Andrew Rimell,James G. Steinrock*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个多任务科学机器学习框架，用于涡轮发动机的剩余寿命（RUL）和热指标（TGTU、DTGT）联合预测，并提供量化不确定性（预测区间）。框架采用共享序列编码器（卷积+残差双向LSTM+注意力池化）和任务特定头（均值-方差估计、可选生存头），支持通过少量参数调整以适应不同部署策略。评估使用点指标（MAE）和区间指标（PICP、MPIW、CWC），并按飞行阶段和维护段分层报告结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际机队数据存在异构性和非平稳性，仅靠点预测不足以支撑风险感知的维护决策，需要同时预测热指标和RUL并量化不确定性。

Method: 多任务框架：共享序列编码器（卷积前端、残差双向LSTM层、注意力池化）连接多个任务特定头，包括均值-方差估计头（概率回归）和可选的生存头（阈值事件建模）。支持通过少量参数（如DTGT阈值规则、RUL目标构建）进行调优。

Result: 使用MAE、PICP、MPIW、CWC进行评估，结果按聚合和按飞行阶段/维护段分层报告，展示了操作上下文对预测的影响。

Conclusion: 该框架能够同时预测涡轮热指标和RUL并提供不确定性量化，通过可调参数适应不同维护策略，支持不确定性感知的发动机健康监控。

Abstract: Engine Health Management (EHM) depends on reliable forecasting of Remaining Useful Life (RUL) and on tracking thermal indicators such as turbine gas temperature (TGT). In practice, real-world fleet data are heterogeneous and non-stationary, and point predictions alone are insufficient for risk-aware maintenance decisions. This paper presents a multi-task scientific machine learning framework for turbine prognostics that jointly predicts turbine gas temperature untrimmed (TGTU), Delta Turbine Gas Temperature (DTGT), and RUL, with quantified uncertainty in the form of prediction intervals whose empirical coverage is evaluated. A shared sequence encoder (convolutional front-end with residual bidirectional LSTM layers and attention pooling) feeds task-specific heads, including mean--variance estimation for probabilistic regression and, optionally, a survival head for threshold-based event modeling. The framework is designed to be tunable via a small set of practitioner-facing parameters (e.g., DTGT thresholding rules and RUL target construction) so that deployment can align with in-house policies and proprietary criteria. The predictive performance of the proposed framework is evaluated using both point and interval metrics, including mean absolute error (MAE), prediction interval coverage probability (PICP), mean prediction interval width (MPIW), and the coverage--width criterion (CWC). Results are reported both in aggregate and stratified by flight phase and maintenance segment to highlight operational-context effects and to support uncertainty-aware monitoring.

</details>


### [121] [Improving Relative Representations with Learned Anchors and Whitened Inner Products](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30596)
*Oscar Thorsted Svendsen,Nikolaj Holst Jakobsen,Fabian Mager,Hiba Nassar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于学习语义原型和几何感知相似度指标的跨模型通信框架，替代传统相对表示中的随机锚点和余弦相似度，实现了异构架构间近乎无损的信息传输和稳定的零样本通信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 独立训练的神经网络模型通常收敛到不兼容的潜在表示，阻碍了高度模块化AI系统的构建。传统相对表示方法使用随机锚点和余弦相似度，无法捕捉现代架构（如Transformer）的各向异性几何结构，导致跨模型通信效果不佳。

Method: 1) 通过学习鲁棒的语义原型替代随机采样的锚点；2) 采用几何感知的相似度度量，保留判别性幅值信息并对仿射变换具有不变性。

Result: 在视觉和语言任务上取得了显著的性能提升和一致性改善，特别是在异构架构（如不同规模的小语言模型）间实现了近乎无损的信息传输和稳定的零样本通信。

Conclusion: 通过改进相对表示中的锚点选择和相似度度量，可以有效解决跨模型通信中的表示不兼容问题，为构建高度模块化的AI系统提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Independently trained neural models typically converge to incompatible latent representations, creating a fundamental barrier to highly modular AI systems. While Relative Representations (RR) address this by mapping absolute coordinates to a shared space defined by similarities to common anchor points, traditional implementations rely on randomly sampled anchors and cosine similarity, which frequently fail to capture the anisotropic geometries of modern architectures like Transformers. In this work, we propose a robust framework for cross-model communication based on two improvements. We learn anchors as robust semantic prototypes and utilize a geometry-aware similarity metric which preserves discriminative magnitude information and is invariant to affine shifts. Our approach demonstrates significant gains in performance and consistency across vision and language tasks. Notably, it enables nearly lossless information transfer and stable zero-shot communication even between highly heterogeneous architectures, such as small language models of varying scales.

</details>


### [122] [When LLM Reward Design Fails: Diagnostic-Driven Refinement for Sparse Structured RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28918)
*Youting Wang,Yuan Tang,Bowen Liu,Xuan Liu,Dingyan Shang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将LLM生成的奖励塑形视为调试而非一次性生成，通过诊断驱动的迭代细化（基于训练诊断和失败模式分类）显著提升了稀疏结构化强化学习任务（MiniGrid）的性能，并揭示了该方法在密集奖励连续控制任务（MuJoCo）中的边界条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM生成奖励塑形方法多采用一次性生成（one-shot），但在稀疏结构化强化学习任务中失败率较高，缺乏系统性的诊断与改进机制。

Method: 审计识别出两种主要一次性失败模式——奖励泛滥（reward flooding）和语义/API误解，以及一种较罕见的弱塑形（weak-shaping）情况。提出基于训练诊断和失败模式分类的迭代细化方法，通过诊断指标指导目标导向的奖励函数修正。

Result: DoorKey-8x8从2.3%提升至97.6%，KeyCorridor从31.2%提升至86.7%（种子间方差大）。控制实验表明增益并非来自重试或额外训练：仅指标重新提示导致大幅下降，静态词汇控制恢复了大部分差距（87.6%; 70.7%），表明分类提示是主要机制，动态标签仅提供部分增量证据。预算匹配和Best-of-3比较将细化与选择和训练时间效应分离。连续控制结果显示成功诊断在密集奖励运动任务中可能失效。

Conclusion: LLM生成的奖励塑形更适用于PPO框架下的稀疏结构化任务（具有可靠接口），方法受限于这些条件；事件文本等领域的应用效果可能有益、有害或中性。

Abstract: For sparse, structured reinforcement-learning tasks with semantic reward-function interfaces, LLM-generated reward shaping is better framed as debugging than one-shot generation. We study PPO-trained agents using MiniGrid as core evaluation and MuJoCo as boundary stress test. Our audit finds two dominant one-shot failure modes -- reward flooding and semantic/API misunderstanding -- plus a rarer weak-shaping case. We propose diagnostic-driven iterative refinement, where training diagnostics and a failure-mode taxonomy guide targeted reward-function revision. Refinement improves DoorKey-8x8 from 2.3% to 97.6% and KeyCorridor from 31.2% to 86.7% with high seed-to-seed variance. Controls show these gains are not from retrying or extra training: metrics-only re-prompting yields large drops, while a static-vocabulary control recovers much of the gap (87.6%; 70.7%), showing the taxonomy prompt is a major mechanism and dynamic labels provide only partially isolated incremental evidence. Budget-matched and Best-of-3 comparisons separate refinement from selection and training-time effects. Component-removal tests, sensitivity analyses, and an audit against author labels provide converging evidence for the debugging interpretation while revealing calibration limits. Continuous-control results show the boundary: success-based diagnostics can misfire in dense-reward locomotion, and return-trend feedback removes one false-positive mechanism without robust gains. The low-call protocol is a cost contrast with population-based reward search, not a benchmark comparison. In four crossed-variance-design environments, point estimates suggest larger gains when LLM reward-function variance dominates but bootstrap intervals are wide. The method is bounded to sparse structured tasks with reliable interfaces under PPO; fields like event_text may help, hurt, or be neutral.

</details>


### [123] [ScaleMAP: Preserving Local Density and Neighborhood Structure in Low-Dimensional Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30597)
*Rajas Poorna,Marcus T. Cicerone*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ScaleMAP通过将嵌入位移除以原始空间局部半径的几何均值来重新注入尺度信息，替代了DensMAP中与UMAP力竞争的密度惩罚，在保持UMAP级邻域保真度的同时实现了与DensMAP相当的密度保留效果，并能恢复被UMAP压缩的稀疏桥接结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UMAP和PaCMAP等非线性降维方法在构建图时自适应归一化局部距离，抹除了邻域尺度信息，导致稀疏结构（如细胞类型间的桥接、高光谱图像中的窄谱峰）被抑制或丢失；DensMAP添加密度惩罚但引入竞争性力导致点分散。需要一种无需竞争目标即可保留密度信息的方法。

Method: ScaleMAP将每对嵌入位移除以两个端点原始空间局部半径的几何均值，以变量替换而非竞争目标的方式重新注入尺度信息；该原理同样适用于PaCMAP。

Result: 在转录组学、高光谱成像和流式细胞术等标准基准和科学数据集上，ScaleMAP在密度保留方面与DensMAP相当，同时保持UMAP级别的邻域保留。在转录组数据中恢复了UMAP压缩的稀疏桥接结构；在流式细胞术中忠实表示了跨越17个数量级的密度结构；应用于PaCMAP也持续改善了密度保留。

Conclusion: ScaleMAP通过变量替换而非竞争目标的方式有效解决了降维中的密度信息丢失问题，且该原理可推广至其他降维方法（如PaCMAP），为保留尺度信息的非线性降维提供了通用策略。

Abstract: Nonlinear dimensionality-reduction methods such as UMAP and PaCMAP adaptively normalize local distances during graph construction, erasing neighborhood scale from the data. This distorts more than relative cluster sizes: sparse structures like bridges between transitioning cell types and narrow spectral spikes in hyperspectral images can be suppressed or lost entirely. DensMAP adds a density penalty to correct this, but this penalty competes with UMAP's attraction-repulsion forces, scattering points far from their neighborhoods. ScaleMAP takes a different approach: each pairwise embedding displacement is divided by the geometric mean of the two endpoints' original-space local radii, re-injecting scale information as a change of variables rather than as a competing objective. Across standard benchmarks and scientific datasets from transcriptomics, hyperspectral imaging, and flow cytometry, ScaleMAP matches DensMAP on density preservation while maintaining UMAP-level neighborhood preservation. In transcriptomic data, it recovers sparse bridges between cell populations that UMAP collapses; in flow cytometry, it faithfully represents density structure across 17 orders of magnitude. The same principle applied to PaCMAP yields consistently improved density preservation, suggesting the approach generalizes beyond UMAP.

</details>


### [124] [AMNESIA: A Large Scale Medical Unlearning Benchmark Suite with Disease-Informed Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30599)
*Saeedeh Davoudi,Reihaneh Iranmanesh,Ophir Frieder,Nazli Goharian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AMNESIA是首个大规模医学机器遗忘开源基准测试，包含70,560个问答对，覆盖8,820份患者记录和11种疾病类别，用于评估遗忘方法在患者级别和疾病级别的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学知识持续演进，需要对已训练的大语言模型进行选择性遗忘（机器遗忘），但现有遗忘基准依赖合成或小规模通用数据，缺乏临床遗忘方向的系统研究。

Method: 构建AMNESIA基准，包含事实性（直接回忆）和推理性（临床推断）两类问题，使用四种主流遗忘方法在随机患者级别和疾病级别上进行评估，并引入检测医学术语泄露的新指标。

Result: 遗忘单个患者会侵蚀具有相同疾病的其他患者的知识，表明现有方法难以区分患者个体与共享的临床知识。

Conclusion: 需要开发能够更好分离患者个体信息与共享临床知识的遗忘方法。

Abstract: Medical knowledge is continuously evolving. This creates a need to update or selectively forget information encoded in already-trained medical LLMs. Machine unlearning aims to remove the influence of specific training data from a model without full retraining.
