### [163] [Trust-Region Behavior Blending for On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31159)
*Daniil Plyusov,Alexey Gorbatovski,Alexey Malakhov,Nikita Balagansky,Boris Shaposhnikov,Daria Korotyshova,Daniil Gavrilov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Trust-Region behavior Blending (TRB)方法，通过在预热阶段将学生策略替换为KL信任域内最接近教师的行为策略，解决on-policy蒸馏中早期学生 rollout 质量差的问题，预热后通过KL预算退火恢复纯学生rollout训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: On-policy蒸馏（OPD）中，早期学生策略生成的prefix质量较差，导致教师监督信号作用于弱或低质量的prefix上，影响蒸馏效果。

Method: TRB是一种预热方法：在学生为中心的KL信任域内，用最接近教师的行为策略替换早期rollout策略，保持per-prefix的反向KL OPD损失不变；通过将KL预算退火至零，预热后恢复纯学生rollout训练。

Result: 在两个数学推理蒸馏任务中，TRB在所有对比方法中取得了最强的平均表现。

Conclusion: TRB有效缓解了on-policy蒸馏中早期rollout质量差的问题，提升了蒸馏性能。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student on prefixes sampled from its own policy while matching a stronger teacher. This addresses the prefix mismatch of offline distillation, but early student rollouts can still be poor, placing teacher supervision on weak or low-quality prefixes. We propose Trust-Region behavior Blending (TRB), a warmup method that replaces the early rollout policy with the closest-to-teacher behavior policy inside a student-centered KL trust region, while keeping the per-prefix reverse-KL OPD loss unchanged. The KL budget is annealed to zero, so training returns to pure student rollouts after warmup. Across two math-reasoning distillation settings, TRB attains the strongest average among the compared methods.

</details>


### [164] [Learning Permutation-invariant Macroscopic Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30812)
*Zhichao Han,Mengyi Chen,Qianxiao Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种置换不变的自编码器框架，用于从无序粒子系统中学习低维宏观动力学，无需固定微观自由度顺序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维微观系统的宏观动力学建模是跨学科关注的问题。现有数据驱动方法（如自编码器）要求输入微观自由度按固定顺序排列，但在粒子系统等场景中，微观状态本质上是无序的。这促使需要一种置换不变的潜在表征学习框架。

Method: 采用置换不变的编码器，并设计解码器重建以观测点为中心的质分布而非逐样本重建；随后联合学习可观测量与潜在状态的宏观动力学。

Result: 在多个微观系统上验证了有效性和鲁棒性，包括：相互作用粒子系统的能量动力学学习、Lennard-Jones流体的混合动力学预测、聚合物在拉伸力场中运动的聚合物动力学建模。

Conclusion: 提出的置换不变自编码器框架能够有效地从无序微观系统中学习宏观动力学，为粒子系统等应用场景提供了鲁棒的建模方案。

Abstract: Accurately modeling the macroscopic dynamics of high-dimensional microscopic systems is of broad interest across the sciences. Many data-driven approaches learn a low-dimensional latent state through an autoencoder trained for pointwise input reconstruction. These methods typically assume a fixed ordering of microscopic degrees of freedom in the input. However, in many settings, such as particle systems, the microscopic state is inherently unordered. This motivates an autoencoder framework that learns permutation-invariant latent representations. To this end, we adopt a permutation-invariant encoder and design the decoder to reconstruct the mass distribution centered at the observed points rather than per-sample reconstruction. We then jointly learn the macroscopic dynamics of the observables together with the latent states. We demonstrate the effectiveness and robustness of the proposed method across a range of microscopic settings, including learning the energy dynamics in interacting particle systems, predicting mixing dynamics in Lennard-Jones fluids, and modeling the stretching dynamics from video data of polymers moving in an elongational force field.

</details>


### [165] [What changes after deployment? A survey on On-device Learning in TinyML](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31226)
*Massimo Pavan,Luca Pezzarossa,Fabrizio Pittorino,Manuel Roveri,Xenofon Fafoutis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该综述调查了约70篇TinyML设备上的在线学习（ODL）工作，首次从分布变化类型（分布偏移）的视角系统分析了不同变化对应用、硬件和解决方案结构的影响，并指出方法论基准与实际部署之间存在持续性差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决微控制器级设备（TinyML）上机器学习模型在部署后遭遇分布变化导致性能下降的问题，同时填补现有文献未系统刻画分布变化类型及其对ODL解决方案影响的空白。

Method: 对约70篇关于设备端学习（ODL）的工作进行系统综述，以"分布变化机制"为统一分析原则，考察不同类型分布变化如何影响可寻址的应用场景、所用硬件以及解决方案的架构设计。

Result: 识别出不同类型分布变化对TinyML的ODL设计有显著差异化影响；揭示了方法论基准测试与实际部署场景之间存在持久性差距。

Conclusion: 未来的TinyML研究需要更关注实际部署中的分布变化类型，缩小基准测试与真实场景之间的鸿沟，以推动ODL在资源受限设备上的有效应用。

Abstract: Machine learning models on microcontroller-class devices (TinyML) face a fundamental challenge: post-deployment distribution change undermines static models. On-device learning (ODL) addresses this by running the learning process directly on the device. The existing literature has not characterized how distribution change occurs or how different change types require different solutions. Approximately 70 ODL works are surveyed under one principle: the distribution change regime. The survey analyzes how different types of distribution change influence the applications addressable on-device, the hardware employed, and the structure of the solutions. A persistent gap between methodological benchmarks and real-world deployment scenarios is also identified.

</details>


### [166] [EchoRL: Reinforcement Learning via Rollout Echoing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31228)
*Jinhe Bi,Aniri,Minglai Yang,Xingcheng Zhou,Wenke Huang,Sikuan Yan,Yujun Wang,Zixuan Cao,Michael Färber,Xun Xiao,Volker Tresp,Yunpu Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对RLVR训练中优势退化（advantage-degenerated）导致策略梯度消失的问题，提出EchoRL模块，通过从验证成功的rollout中识别基于熵的EchoClip作为辅助监督信号，有效提升训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的RLVR（Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards）方法在训练过程中，随着验证成功rollout占比增加，奖励标准差降为零导致优势退化，策略梯度消失，训练收益边际化。但这些退化rollout中仍包含有价值的学习信号未被利用。

Method: 提出EchoRL轻量模块：1）基于外部专家模型生成的金色轨迹的熵模式分析；2）从验证成功的rollout中根据步骤级熵值识别EchoClip；3）将该clip作为辅助监督信号注入RL目标函数中。

Result: 在10个基准、5种LLM骨干网络、4种主流RLVR后训练方法上，EchoRL以最小开销一致提升RLVR后训练性能。

Conclusion: EchoRL通过挖掘被忽略的优势退化rollout中的学习信号，有效克服了RLVR训练中的奖励坍塌问题，是一种轻量且通用的改进方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards is an effective route for post-training to strengthen the reasoning capability of large language models. However, as training proceeds, the learning signal can collapse thus makes the training gain become marginal and ineffective. Specifically, a growing fraction of prompts' rollouts become advantage-degenerated: all the self-generated rollouts show verified-success, making the standard deviation over their rewards be zero; accordingly each rollout's advantage becomes degenerated (zero) as well. Given such rollouts' advantages, the policy-gradient for model optimization eventually vanishes, capping the training performance. We argue that some of these rollouts still contain valuable learning signals but unfortunately omitted with the existing RLVR methods. In this paper, inspired through analyzing the entropy pattern behind golden trajectories produced by external expert models, we propose EchoRL for better exploiting the advantage-degenerated rollouts to further improve the training performance. EchoRL is a lightweight module that first identifies an EchoClip from verified-success rollouts based on their step-level entropy values, and then feeds this clip back as an auxiliary supervision signal in the RL objective. Extensive experiments across 10 benchmarks, 5 LLM backbones, and 4 popular RLVR post-training methods demonstrate that EchoRL consistently improves RLVR post-training with minimal overhead.

</details>


### [167] [Cross-Layer Subspace Coupling for LLM Compression: A Unifying Framework and Its Empirical Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30836)
*Snigdha Chandan Khilar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现，基于SVD的LLM压缩方法（如SVD LLM和Basis Sharing）可统一为一个优化问题。尽管该统一方法在数学上能将权重重构误差降低高达46%，但在实际任务中完全失败，困惑度和准确率显著劣于标准逐层SVD LLM。作者从机制上解释：束方法在数学上耦合相邻层，但Transformer残差流在前向传播中实际上解耦了它们。因此逐层最优性比联合跨层优化更重要。结论：权重空间重构是跨层压缩的错误目标，未来方法必须聚焦于逐层激活重构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者发现SVD LLM和Basis Sharing等基于SVD的压缩方法可统一为同一优化问题，希望通过数学推导证明统一方法的优越性，并检验其在权重重构误差上的改进效果。

Method: 将SVD LLM和Basis Sharing统一为单一优化问题，在Pythia模型上进行数学证明和权重重构误差测试，并与标准逐层SVD LLM进行下游任务（困惑度、准确率）对比。

Result: 统一方法将权重重构误差降低高达46%，但在下游任务中完全失败，困惑度和准确率严重劣于标准逐层SVD LLM。

Conclusion: 权重空间重构是跨层压缩的 flawed objective，Transformer残差流在前向传播中解耦了相邻层，因此逐层最优性比联合优化更重要，未来方法应聚焦于逐层激活重构。

Abstract: Recent SVD based compression methods for large language models like SVD LLM and Basis Sharing can be unified under one optimization problem. While mathematical proofs and tests on Pythia models show this unified approach improves weight reconstruction error by up to 46% percent it fails in practical tasks. Downstream metrics like perplexity and accuracy severely degrade compared to standard per layer SVD LLM. The authors explain this failure mechanistically. Although the bundle method mathematically couples adjacent layers the transformer residual stream actually decouples them during forward passes. Thus per layer optimality matters more than joint cross layer optimization. The paper concludes that weight space reconstruction is a flawed objective for cross layer compression and future methods must focus on per layer activation reconstruction instead.

</details>


### [168] [CoMem: Context Management with A Decoupled Long-Context Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30842)
*Yuwei Zhang,Chengyu Dong,Shuowei Jin,Changlong Yu,Hejie Cui,Hongye Jin,Xinyang Zhang,Hamed Bonab,Colin Lockard,Jianshu Chen,Zhenyu Shi,Jingbo Shang,Xian Li,Bing Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CoMem 提出了一种解耦记忆管理与智能体工作流的框架，通过 k 步异步流水线将记忆模型的摘要生成与智能体推理并行化，配合奖励驱动的训练策略，在 SWE-Bench-Verified 上实现了 1.4 倍延迟提升，同时保持大部分性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上下文管理通过迭代摘要解决长时域任务，但额外的摘要 token 解码开销严重影响了端到端响应延迟，亟需一种机制来降低这一开销。

Method: 提出 CoMem 框架：(1) 将记忆管理从主智能体工作流中解耦，使两者可并行执行；(2) 设计 k 步异步流水线，用记忆模型的摘要重叠智能体的推理，掩蔽上下文处理延迟；(3) 引入奖励驱动的训练策略，确保异步设置下记忆模型捕获足够统计量以支持智能体决策。

Result: 在 SWE-Bench-Verified 上，CoMem 相比传统长上下文方案实现 1.4 倍延迟提升，同时保持大部分性能；且延迟优势随系统吞吐量增加而扩大。

Conclusion: CoMem 提供了更优的效率-效果权衡，为独立优化智能体推理与记忆压缩提供了一条模块化路径。

Abstract: Context management enables agentic models to solve long-horizon tasks through iterative summarization of previous interaction histories. However, this process typically incurs substantial decoding overhead for the extra summarization tokens, which significantly affect the end-to-end response latency at deployment. In this paper, we introduce CoMem, a novel framework that decouples memory management from the primary agent workflow, enabling these processes to execute in parallel. We propose a $k$-step-off asynchronous pipeline that overlaps the memory model's summarization with the agent's inference, effectively masking the latency of context processing. To ensure robustness under this asynchronous setting, we introduce a reward-driven training strategy that aligns the memory model to capture sufficient statistics for the agent's decision-making. Theoretical analysis confirms that CoMem offers a superior efficiency-effectiveness trade-off compared to coupled architectures. Our extensive experimental results on SWE-Bench-Verified show that CoMem provides 1.4x latency improvements upon vanilla long-context solutions while preserving most of the performance. Furthermore, we demonstrate that these latency gains scale favorably with increased system throughput, offering a modular path forward for the independent optimization of agent reasoning and memory compression.

</details>


### [169] [Learning Cardiac Latent Representations in Vectorcardiogram Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31249)
*Bosong Huang,Panzhen Zhao,Zengxiang Li,Patricia Lee,Wei Jin,Alan Wee-Chung Liew,Ming Jin,Shirui Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LVCG框架，在Frank向量心电图（VCG）空间而非传统心电图（ECG）信号空间学习心脏电活动的统一潜在表征，以解决ECG表示学习中的冗余、伪相关和过拟合问题，实验表明在域迁移场景下具有更好的鲁棒性和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ECG表征学习方法几乎都在可观测的ECG信号空间中操作，而标准十二导联ECG本质上是同一心脏电活动在不同空间方向上的多个投影，因此在ECG空间进行表征学习会引入大量冗余信息，导致伪相关和过拟合风险增加。

Method: 受Frank向量心电图（VCG）模型启发，提出LVCG——首个在物理可解释的VCG潜在空间中进行自监督表征学习的通用框架，通过学习视图不变的潜在VCG表征来减少冗余并改善泛化。

Result: LVCG在多项任务上普遍优于基于ECG空间的基线方法，尤其在域迁移场景下展现出更强的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 在VCG潜在空间学习心脏电活动的统一表征能有效减少ECG空间中的冗余信息，提升模型在域迁移等挑战性场景下的表现，为心脏电活动表征学习提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Electrocardiography (ECG) is a cornerstone of cardiac assessment, making the learning of informative ECG representations fundamental to tasks ranging from disease diagnosis to clinical report generation. However, existing methods operate almost exclusively in the observable ECG signal space. In practice, the standard twelve-lead ECG represents multiple projections of the same underlying cardiac electrical activity from different spatial orientations. Therefore, representation learning in the ECG space inevitably introduces substantial redundancy, which may lead to spurious correlations and increased risk of overfitting. To address this and motivated by the Frank vectorcardiogram (VCG) model, we propose learning a unified latent representation of cardiac electrical activity directly in the VCG space. We introduce LVCG, the first general self-supervised representation learning framework designed to operate in this physically grounded latent space. By learning view-invariant latent VCG representations rather than lead-specific artifacts, VCG minimizes redundancy and improves generalization. LVCG generally outperforms ECG-space baselines across tasks, demonstrating enhanced robustness and generalization, especially in domain shift settings.

</details>


### [170] [A Lecture Note on Offline RL and IRL, Part II: Foundations of Inverse Reinforcement Learning and Dynamic Discrete Choice Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30843)
*Enoch Hyunwook Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了结构计量经济学中的动态离散选择（DDC）模型与机器学习中的熵正则化逆强化学习（IRL）模型在数学上等价，系统梳理了两大领域的经典识别结果与计算范式，并分析了各自的局限性，最后给出了统一的经验风险最小化框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将DDC与IRL这两个各自独立发展但研究同一问题的领域统一起来，澄清其等价性，并系统对比传统计量方法与现代ML方法的识别能力与计算局限，以便于跨领域的方法迁移与改进。

Method: 首先证明DDC与熵正则化IRL的概率模型等价；然后梳理经典识别结果（Magnac & Thesmar）及四种计算范式（Rust嵌套不动点、Hotz-Miller条件选择概率、Adusumilli & Eckardt的两种时序差分方法）；接着分析现代ML/IRL方法（对抗IRL、occupancy matching、IQ-Learn、离线ML-IRL）的实际目标函数与识别性质；最后引入Kang等人的经验风险最小化框架，提供梯度估计器。

Result: 明确了DDC与IRL的等价性，厘清了各方法的识别内容与局限：传统方法受限于维度灾难、转移核估计、"致命三要素"或投影不动点偏差；现代方法虽灵活但需精确理解其实际识别目标。

Conclusion: 两大领域本质相同，Kang等人的经验风险最小化框架为离线IRL/DDC提供了统一的梯度估计途径，有望克服传统方法的计算瓶颈。

Abstract: In the forward reinforcement-learning problem, the reward is fixed and known; the learner is asked to find a good policy or value function. Here we turn the question around. Given offline data generated by an expert, can we recover the reward the expert was optimizing? This is the inverse reinforcement learning problem, and remarkably, two communities, structural econometricians studying dynamic discrete choice (DDC) and machine learners studying entropy-regularized IRL, have been working on exactly the same probabilistic model under different names. We begin by proving their equivalence. We then develop the classical identification result of Magnac and Thesmar and the classical computational paradigms that grew out of it: Rust's nested fixed-point algorithm, the conditional-choice-probability approach of Hotz and Miller, and the two temporal-difference approaches of Adusumilli and Eckardt: linear semi-gradient TD and approximate value iteration. Each route has its limits: dimensionality, transition-kernel estimation, the deadly triad, or projected fixed-point bias. We then walk through the modern ML/IRL strand: adversarial IRL, occupancy matching, IQ-Learn, and offline ML-IRL, deriving each method's actual objective and stating precisely what it does and does not identify. We close with the empirical-risk-minimization framework of Kang et al., which yields a gradient-based estimator for offline IRL/DDC.

</details>


### [171] [ForecastCompass: Guiding Agentic Forecasting with Adaptive Factor Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30858)
*Yurui Chang,Yongkang Du,Yuanpu Cao,Jinghui Chen,Lu Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 ForecastCompass (FoCo)，一种基于自适应因子记忆的智能体预测框架，通过层次化预测任务分类、因子记忆和推理记忆两个互补组件，以及迭代记忆修正机制，使智能体能够积累可迁移的预测知识，在 Prophet Arena 和 FutureX 基准上显著提升概率预测精度和校准效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体在动态环境中进行预测时，需要从不完整、有时限的证据中推理并生成校准后的概率，但现有智能体记忆方法并非为预测任务设计，通常仅存储过往交互、反思或事实关联，缺乏对可复用预测因子和校准知识的显式表达，因此需要一种专门针对预测任务的记忆框架。

Method: FoCo 包含三个核心设计：(1) 层次化预测任务分类法，用于组织预测经验并支持任务相关知识的检索；(2) 两个互补记忆组件——因子记忆（捕获可复用的预测维度）和推理记忆（编码概率更新、不确定性处理及校准原则）；(3) 利用回顾性分析作为学习信号，通过语言化的记忆修正程序迭代更新记忆，使智能体随时间积累可迁移的预测知识。

Result: 在 Prophet Arena 和 FutureX 基准上，使用 GPT-5-mini 和 Gemini-2.5-Flash 的实验表明，FoCo 在概率预测准确性和校准效果上均优于基线方法。

Conclusion: FoCo 通过将预测经验以因子和推理知识的形式结构化存储并迭代优化，有效提升了智能体在动态环境中的预测能力，验证了专门针对预测任务设计的记忆框架的有效性。

Abstract: Agentic forecasting is important for decision-making in dynamic environments, but it remains challenging because agents must reason from incomplete, time-limited evidence and produce calibrated probabilities before outcomes are resolved. Memory provides a natural mechanism for transferring experience from resolved forecasts to future prediction tasks. However, existing agent-memory methods are not tailored to forecasting, as they typically store past interactions, reflections, or factual associations without explicitly representing reusable predictive factors or calibration knowledge. We propose ForecastCompass (FoCo), an adaptive factor-based memory framework for agentic forecasting. FoCo organizes forecasting experience with a hierarchical forecasting-task taxonomy, enabling retrieval task-relevant forecasting knowledge. It maintains two complementary memory components: factor memory, which captures reusable predictive dimensions, and reasoning memory, which encodes probability updating, uncertainty handling, and calibration principles. Using retrospective analyses as learning signals, FoCo iteratively revises memory through a verbalized memory-revision procedure, enabling the agent to accumulate transferable forecasting knowledge over time. Experiments on Prophet Arena and FutureX with GPT-5-mini and Gemini-2.5-Flash show that FoCo improves both probabilistic accuracy and calibration.

</details>


### [172] [The Terminal Representation in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31289)
*Amir Esterhuysen,Anders Jonsson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出终端表征（TR），一种无需特征分解即可实现奖励加权轨迹编码的低维表征学习框架，理论上证明了其与默认表征（DR）的等价性，并在多个下游任务中验证了其计算效率优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有成功表征（SR）和默认表征（DR）在强化学习中的表征学习虽有效，但DR依赖特征分解且要求对称转移动力学假设，计算开销大。作者希望设计一种无需特征分解、更低维度且保持奖励加权轨迹编码能力的表征方法。

Method: 提出终端表征（TR），在DR基础上重新形式化奖励加权轨迹编码，作为低维对象直接学习；推导了TR的理论基础（推导、收敛性、零样本组合性、不同奖励公式的等价性）；证明TR隐含在DR的顶部特征向量中，无需特征分解即可捕获相同知识。

Result: 理论上证明了TR两种学习算法的收敛性；建立了TR与DR顶部特征向量的嵌入关系；实验表明TR在下游应用（选项发现、奖励塑形、迁移学习、探索）中达到与现有表征相当的效果，同时计算、存储和学习开销更低。

Conclusion: TR作为SR和DR的可行替代方案，在保持表征能力的前提下显著降低了计算复杂度，且避免了特征分解所需的对称动力学假设，为强化学习中的表征学习提供了更高效的新范式。

Abstract: Representation learning is a powerful tool for spatio-temporal abstraction within reinforcement learning (RL). Two well established approaches are through the successor representation (SR) and the default representation (DR). The SR encodes states by the future trajectories they induce, capturing information flow decoupled from reward. The DR builds on this by weighting trajectories with reward, integrating credit-assignment structure into the representation. Eigenvectors of both representations have been used to support a range of downstream tasks -- including option discovery, reward shaping, transfer learning, and exploration. We introduce a structurally distinct formulation: the terminal representation (TR). The TR encodes reward-weighted trajectories similarly to the DR, but can be learned as a lower-dimensionality object, and can be used directly for the mentioned applications without eigenvector computations. Eigendecomposition also imposes the assumption of symmetric transition dynamics, which the TR can bypass. In this work we develop the theoretical foundations of the TR: its derivation, convergence of two learning algorithms, its use for zero-shot compositionality, and equivalences between alternative reward formulations. We further show the TR is embedded in the top DR eigenvector, allowing it to capture the same underlying knowledge without eigendecomposition. Additionally, we provide empirical evidence of the TR as a viable alternative to existing representations in subsidiary applications, while requiring less computational overhead to learn, store, and use.

</details>


### [173] [GlucoFM: A Dual-Stream Foundation Model for Continuous Glucose Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30865)
*Zechen Li,Keerthana Natarajan,Weizhi Zhang,Menglian Zhou,Simon A. Lee,Yuwei Zhang,Maxwell A. Xu,Zeinab Esmaeilpour,Flora D. Salim,Mark Malhotra,Lindsey Sunden,Shwetak Patel,Yuzhe Yang,Ahmed A. Metwally*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GlucoFM 是一种轻量级 CGM 基础模型，通过将血糖记录对齐到24小时时间网格、保留观测掩码，并将血糖动态分解为慢变生理状态流和瞬时事件流，实现了可迁移的血糖监测表示学习。在477名受试者、109,066小时的未标记数据上预训练后，在7项临床预测任务中平均 PR-AUC 提升4.1个点，尤其在糖尿病风险和β细胞功能障碍任务上表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用时间序列模型和CGM专用基础模型通常将血糖轨迹编码为纠缠的单流序列，未能显式建模血糖动态的不同时间尺度结构，导致对血糖生理状态的表征能力有限。

Method: 将不规则CGM记录对齐到24小时时间网格并保留观测掩码；将血糖动态分解为慢变生理状态流和瞬时事件流；采用两个互补预训练目标：掩码上下文潜在预测（融合日表示）和时间动态预测（状态和事件流）。

Result: 在四个不同队列和七项临床预测任务中，GlucoFM在所有基线中取得了最强的subject-disjoint线性探测性能，平均PR-AUC比最佳CGM专用基础模型提升4.1个点。在所有糖尿病风险和β细胞功能障碍任务以及3/4的胰岛素抵抗任务上取得最优PR-AUC，同时实现了最佳的跨数据集迁移性能和强少样本适应能力。

Conclusion: 生理感知分解（将血糖动态分解为慢变状态流和瞬时事件流）是一种有效的归纳偏置，能够提升CGM表示学习的可迁移性，尤其在核心代谢结局预测上表现突出。

Abstract: Continuous glucose monitoring (CGM) provides a dense view of daily metabolic physiology, yet existing generic time-series and CGM-specific foundation models often encode glucose traces as entangled single-stream sequences, leaving the distinct temporal structure of glycemic dynamics only implicitly modeled. We present GlucoFM, a lightweight CGM foundation model that aligns irregular recordings to a 24-hour chronological grid, preserves observation masks, and decomposes glucose dynamics into slow physiological state and transient event streams, capturing low-frequency glycemic baselines and short-term deviations that may reflect acute physiological responses or sensor artifacts. GlucoFM is pretrained on 109,066 hours of unlabeled CGM recordings from 477 subjects with two complementary objectives: masked contextual latent prediction over fused daily representations and temporal dynamics prediction over state and event streams. Across four diverse cohorts and seven clinical prediction tasks, GlucoFM achieves the strongest subject-disjoint linear-probing performance among evaluated baselines, improving average PR-AUC by 4.1 points over the best CGM-specific foundation model. Its gains are most pronounced on core metabolic outcomes, leading PR-AUC on all diabetes-risk and $β$-cell dysfunction tasks and on 3 of 4 insulin-resistance tasks. GlucoFM also achieves the best overall cross-dataset transfer performance and strong few-shot adaptation among evaluated methods, and consistent gains when aggregating multiple days for subject-level prediction, highlighting physiology-aware decomposition as an effective inductive bias for transferable CGM representation learning.

</details>


### [174] [Inconsistency-Aware Minimization: Improving Generalization with Unlabeled Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31324)
*Hee-Sung Kim,Hyeonseong Kim,Sungyoon Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需标签即可计算的泛化度量——局部不一致性（local inconsistency），该度量基于参数空间的信息几何视角，并与Fisher信息矩阵和损失Hessian矩阵存在理论联系。基于此，作者提出了不一致性感知最小化（IAM）优化方法，在标准监督学习、半监督和自监督学习场景中均能提升泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 泛化差距的估计和提升泛化能力的优化方法对深度学习模型至关重要。利用无标签数据来实现这些目标在实际场景中具有显著优势，因此需要一种无需标签即可计算的泛化度量。

Method: 从参数空间的信息几何视角出发，推导出局部不一致性作为泛化度量，并将其与Fisher信息矩阵和损失Hessian矩阵建立理论联系。在此基础上提出IAM方法，将局部不一致性纳入训练目标。

Result: 实验表明局部不一致性与泛化差距相关；IAM在标准监督学习下达到与SAM等现有方法相当的性能，在半监督和自监督学习场景中也表现出有效性。

Conclusion: 局部不一致性是一种有效的无标签泛化度量，IAM作为一种结合该度量的优化方法，在多种学习范式下均能提升泛化性能。

Abstract: Estimating the generalization gap and developing optimization methods that improve generalization are crucial for deep learning models, for both theoretical understanding and practical applications. Leveraging unlabeled data for these purposes offers significant advantages in real-world scenarios. This paper introduces a novel generalization measure, local inconsistency, derived from an information-geometric perspective on the parameter space of neural networks. A key feature of local inconsistency is that it can be computed without explicit labels. We establish theoretical underpinnings by connecting local inconsistency to the Fisher information matrix and the loss Hessian. Empirically, we demonstrate that local inconsistency correlates with the generalization gap. Based on these findings, we propose Inconsistency-Aware Minimization (IAM), which incorporates local inconsistency into the training objective. We demonstrate that in standard supervised learning settings, IAM enhances generalization, achieving performance comparable to that of existing methods such as Sharpness-Aware Minimization. Furthermore, IAM exhibits efficacy in semi- and self-supervised learning scenarios, where the local inconsistency is computed from unlabeled data.

</details>


### [175] [dashi: A Python library for Dataset Shift Characterization to Support Trustworthy AI Development and Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31360)
*David Fernández-Narro,Pablo Ferri,Ángel Sánchez-García,Juan M. García-Gómez,Carlos Sáez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了dashi，一个用于数据集偏移（dataset shifts）探索、量化和表征的开源Python库。dashi提供无监督（基于信息几何和非参数统计流形）与有监督（量化模型性能退化）两种方法，支持时间跨度和多源批次分析，并通过三个健康AI案例（妊娠糖尿病、COVID-19、紧急医疗调度）验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据集偏移（包括时间维度和多源场景）会严重降低AI模型性能并影响数据质量，尤其在健康AI领域可能危及患者安全与基本权利。尽管协变量偏移、先验偏移和概念偏移的理论基础已较为成熟，但缺乏易于使用且全面的软件工具来进行实际分析。

Method: dashi采用双模态方法：1）无监督方法利用信息几何和非参数统计流形进行数据变异性表征与分析（如信息几何时间图、全局概率偏差、源概率异常度等指标）；2）有监督方法量化并表征模型性能退化。两种方法均支持用户自定义的时间批次和领域/源批次。

Result: 在三个健康AI案例（妊娠糖尿病、COVID-19、紧急医疗调度）上验证了dashi的实用性，通过交互式可视化和变异性指标，有效支持了AI生命周期各阶段的鲁棒性与安全性评估。

Conclusion: dashi通过评估数据一致性和AI性能，为构建鲁棒且安全的机器学习管道提供了支持，增强了AI生命周期各阶段的可信度。

Abstract: The Artificial Intelligence (AI) life cycle requires a thorough understanding of the underlying data dynamics for robust, safe and cost-effective AI development and use. Dataset shifts are defined as changes between train and test data distributions. Whether occurring over time (temporal) or across different sites (multi-source), they can severely degrade model performance and compromise data quality. This is particularly important in health AI, where the safety and fundamental rights of patients can be severely affected by uncontrolled shifts both at training and operational stages. While the theoretical foundations of covariate, prior, and concept shifts are well established, there is a lack of accessible and comprehensive software tools to perform their analysis. We introduce dashi, an open-source Python library designed for the exploration, quantification, and characterization of dataset shifts. dashi provides a dual approach: an unsupervised approach that leverages information geometry and non-parametric statistical manifolds to data variability characterization and analysis (e.g., Information Geometric Temporal plots and Multi-Source Variability metrics like Global Probabilistic Deviation and Source Probabilistic Outlyingness), and a supervised approach that quantifies and characterizes model performance degradation. Both unsupervised and supervised approaches work across user-defined temporal and domain/source batches. We demonstrate the utility of dashi on three simulated and real-world health AI case studies on gestational diabetes mellitus, COVID-19 and emergency medical dispatch. By providing interactive visual analytics and variability metrics, dashi supports trustworthiness of AI life cycle stages enabling robust and safe machine learning pipelines through the assessment of data coherence and AI performance.

</details>


### [176] [Bandwidth Allocation with Device Partitioning for Federated Learning over Industrial IoT networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30892)
*Kangmin Kim,Jaeyoung Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对工业物联网联邦学习系统中的通信瓶颈问题，提出一种基于设备计算能力异质性的带宽分配策略，通过将参与设备分组成有序子集并依次授予独占全带宽权限，严格证明该策略相比无分区方案可降低训练时间，同时减少上行能耗，在真实数据集上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习系统中，通信时间是制约训练效率的主要瓶颈，而传统的带宽分配方法侧重于满足个体服务质量需求，忽略了FL系统整体快速收敛的目标，需要一种全新的带宽分配方法。

Method: 提出了一种基于分区的带宽分配策略：将参与设备按计算能力异质性划分为有序子集，依次让每个子集独占全带宽进行通信，而非同时为所有设备分配带宽。从理论上证明了该策略的训练时间严格低于任何无分区方案。

Result: 在GC10-Det（工业表面缺陷检测）和CIFAR-10（图像分类）等真实数据集上的实验表明，该策略持续降低了训练时间和能量消耗，逼近轮次时间的理论下界。

Conclusion: 所提出的分区带宽分配策略有效解决了FL系统中的通信瓶颈问题，利用设备计算能力异质性实现了更低的训练时间和能耗，尤其适用于电池受限的工业物联网设备。

Abstract: We consider a federated learning (FL) system in which Industrial Internet-of-Things (IIoT) devices collaboratively train a global model over wireless channels without sharing local data. In such systems, communication time is a primary bottleneck that constrains overall training efficiency. Unlike conventional networks that prioritize individual quality-of-service requirements, FL systems collectively aim to converge to an optimal global model as efficiently as possible, which calls for a fundamentally different approach to bandwidth allocation. In this paper, we propose a novel bandwidth allocation policy that exploits the heterogeneity of device computing capabilities to minimize total training time. Rather than distributing bandwidth among all selected devices simultaneously, the proposed policy partitions the participating devices into ordered subsets and sequentially grants each subset exclusive access to the full bandwidth. We formally prove that this partitioning-based policy achieves a strictly lower training time than any bandwidth allocation scheme without partitioning, irrespective of the underlying scheduling algorithm. Furthermore, by reducing per-device transmission duration, the proposed policy also minimizes uplink energy consumption, which is particularly beneficial for battery-constrained IIoT devices. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets - including GC10-Det, an industrial surface defect benchmark, and CIFAR-10, a standard image classification benchmark - demonstrate that the proposed policy consistently reduces training time and energy consumption compared to existing bandwidth allocation schemes, approaching the theoretical lower bound on round time.

</details>


### [177] [Scaling Higher-Order Graph Learning with Maximal Clique Complexes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31373)
*Antoine Vialle,Aref Einizade,Fragkiskos D. Malliaros,Jhony H. Giraldo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了简化与分解的细胞Weisfeiler-Lehman测试（sCWL和fCWL），在保持CWL表达力的同时提升计算效率；引入最大团复形来降低CWN的时空复杂度；并提出CliqueWalk偏置随机游走算法，避免显式团枚举，实现线性扩展。整体构建了一个可扩展的高阶图表示拓扑学习框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图神经网络（GNN）局限于建模成对交互，而基于细胞复形的高阶模型虽表达力更强但可扩展性差，亟需在保持高阶表达力的同时提升计算效率。

Method: 1) 提出简化CWL（sCWL）和分解CWL（fCWL），在保留CWL表达力的前提下降低计算复杂度；2) 引入最大团复形（maximal clique complex），减少时间和内存复杂度；3) 提出CliqueWalk，一种偏置随机游走算法，用于采样最大团，避免显式团枚举，复杂度与图规模呈线性关系。

Result: 所提方法在保持与CWL测试相当的表达力的同时，显著提升了计算效率；基于最大团复形的CWN在时间与内存复杂度上均有降低，且保持了强实证性能；CliqueWalk实现了线性可扩展性。

Conclusion: 该工作通过理论化简与算法创新，成功弥合了高阶图表示学习在表达力与可扩展性之间的鸿沟，构建了一个实用的可扩展拓扑学习框架。

Abstract: Graph neural networks (GNNs) are limited to modeling pairwise interactions, while higher-order models based on cell complexes achieve greater expressivity but often suffer from poor scalability. We introduce simplified and factored cellular Weisfeiler Leman tests (sCWL and fCWL), which preserve the expressivity of the CWL test while improving computational efficiency. We further introduce the maximal clique complex, enabling scalable CWNs with reduced time and memory complexity while retaining strong empirical performance. To avoid explicit clique enumeration, we propose CliqueWalk, a biased random walk that samples maximal cliques and scales linearly with graph size. These contributions yield a scalable topological learning framework for higher-order graph representation.

</details>


### [178] [Zero Collapse: A Failure Mode of Policy Gradient Methods in Discontinuous Reward Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30896)
*Nishant Kumar,Enrique Areyan Viqueira,Amy Greenwald*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了在重复拍卖竞价的强化学习场景中，由于拍卖奖励函数具有不连续、“悬崖式”的特性，导致策略梯度方法出现“零塌陷”失效模式——策略一旦陷入零奖励平坦区域便难以恢复。论文从机制上解释了这一现象，分析了策略随机性与步长的交互影响，并通过REINFORCE和Actor-Critic变体进行了实证验证，提出了初始化和架构选择等缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在重复拍卖竞价中面临挑战：连续控制与数字广告策略复杂性并存，而策略梯度和基于价值的方法在拍卖的不连续奖励景观（如第一价格拍卖中零奖励区域与陡峭边界的并存）中表现不佳，亟需理解并解决这一失效模式。

Method: (1) 从机制上解释了不连续奖励如何导致信号消失和零塌陷；(2) 分析了策略随机性与步长之间的交互作用；(3) 在REINFORCE和Actor-Critic变体上进行了实证演示；(4) 提出了基于初始化和架构选择的实用缓解策略；(5) 引入了针对拍卖环境的正式RL框架，突出其独特结构特性。

Result: 识别出“零塌陷”这一根本性失效模式：随机探索和基于梯度的更新会使策略越过最优高奖励区域进入平坦零奖励区域，由于缺乏信息梯度信号，恢复过程极其样本低效；Actor-Critic方法尤其易受影响，有偏的价值估计会加速向不稳定区域移动。

Conclusion: 零塌陷是拍卖竞价RL中不连续奖励导致的关键失效模式，需要通过初始化选择、架构设计等策略加以缓解；本文为拍卖环境建立了正式的RL框架，有助于理解和应对此类具有独特结构属性的问题。

Abstract: Bidding in repeated auctions is a central challenge for reinforcement learning (RL), combining continuous control with the strategic complexities of digital advertising. While policy gradient and value-based methods seem well-suited for these settings, they often struggle with the discontinuous, "cliff-like" nature of auction reward landscapes. In a first-price auction, for example, a bidder receives zero reward until they cross a specific threshold, after which the reward decreases as the bid increases. This creates a landscape of flat, zero-reward regions separated by sharp boundaries.
  We identify a fundamental failure mode in this setting termed "zero collapse." We show that stochastic exploration and gradient-based updates can cause policies to overshoot optimal high-reward regions and enter flat, zero-reward regimes. Once there, the lack of an informative gradient signal makes recovery extremely sample-inefficient, effectively trapping the agent. We find that actor-critic methods are particularly susceptible, as biased value estimates can accelerate this movement toward unstable regions.
  Our contributions include: (1) a mechanistic explanation of how discontinuous rewards lead to vanishing signals and zero collapse; (2) an analysis of the interaction between policy stochasticity and step size; and (3) an empirical demonstration of this phenomenon across REINFORCE and actor-critic variants. We propose practical mitigation strategies involving initialization and architectural choices to improve stability. Finally, we introduce a formal RL framework for auction environments highlighting their unique structural properties.

</details>


### [179] [PithTrain: A Compact and Agent-Native MoE Training System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31463)
*Ruihang Lai,Hao Kang,Haozhan Tang,Akaash R. Parthasarathy,Zichun Yu,Junru Shao,Todd C. Mowry,Chenyan Xiong,Tianqi Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MoE训练框架的"智能体任务效率"(ATE)这一新评估维度，并构建了面向AI编码智能体的原生框架PithTrain和评估基准ATE-Bench，在保持与生产框架同等吞吐量的同时，将智能体交互轮次减少62%、GPU活跃时间降低64%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE训练框架的演进成本高昂，且仅用吞吐量评估无法反映AI编码智能体在理解、操作和扩展框架时的隐性成本。

Method: 提出智能体任务效率(ATE)概念，基于四个智能体原生设计原则构建PithTrain框架，并设计ATE-Bench基准测试。

Result: PithTrain吞吐量匹配生产框架；在ATE-Bench上，智能体交互轮次减少62%，GPU活跃时间降低64%。

Conclusion: 面向智能体原生的框架设计能显著提升编码智能体在MoE训练任务中的效率，ATE应成为训练框架评估的必要维度。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) has become the dominant architecture for frontier language models. To meet this demand, production frameworks have built optimized MoE training stacks over years of engineering effort. Yet evolving these stacks for new architectures and system optimizations remains expensive. With the rise of AI coding agents, they could automate parts of training-framework development and accelerate this evolution. But applying them to these existing frameworks carries hidden costs, invisible to today's throughput-only evaluations. We name this missing dimension agent-task efficiency (ATE): the cost of using coding agents to understand, operate, and extend a framework. Grounded in four agent-native design principles, we build PithTrain, a compact, agent-native MoE training framework. We further introduce ATE-Bench, covering real-world training-framework tasks. Our evaluation shows PithTrain matches the throughput of production frameworks, and on ATE-Bench, PithTrain enables higher agent-task efficiency, with up to 62% fewer Agent Turns and 64% less Active GPU Time.

</details>


### [180] [Density-Guided Robust Counterfactual Explanations on Tabular Data under Model Multiplicity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30901)
*Jun Tan,Qing Guo,Zicheng Xu,Jinglin Li,Qi Fang,Ning Gui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DensityFlow是一种基于生成式框架的反事实解释方法，通过神经ODE和密度评分引导生成过程，避免低密度区域的不确定性，并在黑盒场景下使用局部代理蒸馏实现高效优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反事实解释在低密度区域可靠性差，分类器方差高，而基于集成的方法计算成本昂贵。需要一种既能保证稳定性又能降低查询成本的CE生成方法。

Method: 1) 将反事实生成建模为神经ODE参数化的连续时间动力学；2) 通过噪声对比估计（NCE）学习可微密度评分，利用(K+1)路判别器估计密度比，引导生成避开低密度区域；3) 针对黑盒场景，提出局部代理蒸馏机制，仅在CE生成轨迹上对齐轻量级代理模型与目标模型，实现高效梯度优化。

Result: 在模型多重性（model multiplicity）下实现更高的有效性（validity），同时相比基于集成的基线方法显著降低查询成本。

Conclusion: DensityFlow通过密度感知的生成式框架有效提升了反事实解释的鲁棒性和效率，为黑盒场景下的可解释AI提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Counterfactual explanations (CEs) are essential for actionable recourse, yet their reliability is often compromised in low-density regions, where classifiers exhibit high variance. Unlike existing methods that rely on expensive ensemble intersections to define stability, we propose \textit{DensityFlow}, a generative framework that constructs robust CEs by adhering to the high-confidence data manifold. Specifically, we model the counterfactual generation as continuous-time dynamics parameterized by Neural ODE, guided by a differentiable density score to actively avoid uncertain, low-density areas. This density score is learned via Noise Contrastive Estimation, effectively leveraging a $(K{+}1)$-way discriminator to estimate density ratios. For black-box settings, we introduce a local proxy distillation mechanism that aligns a lightweight surrogate with the target model strictly within the trajectory of CE generation, enabling efficient gradient-based optimization with minimal queries. Experiments demonstrate that \textit{DensityFlow} achieves superior validity under model multiplicity while significantly reducing query costs compared to ensemble-based baselines. Our implementation is available at https://github.com/G-AILab/DensityFlow.

</details>


### [181] [GPU Forecasters: Language Models as Selective Surrogates for Kernel Runtime Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31464)
*Zaid Khan,Justin Chih-Yao Chen,Jaemin Cho,Elias Stengel-Eskin,Mohit Bansal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究如何利用LLM作为GPU性能预测的替代模型（surrogate），通过预测GPU内核性能来减少实际硬件评估的成本，使内核搜索在相同GPU评估预算下能考虑更多候选方案，从而找到更快的内核。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU内核优化需要反复在硬件上测量性能，每次评估都需要编译和执行，成本高昂。随着LLM驱动的内核搜索规模扩大，这种on-device评估成为瓶颈。因此需要一种替代方案来减少对GPU测量的依赖。

Method: 提出LLM作为选择性GPU替代模型（selective GPU surrogate），用于预测内核性能。要求替代模型既准确又能识别何时可能出错并将评估交回GPU。通过准确性、校准性和在有限GPU预算下恢复快速内核的实际效用三个维度评估替代模型，并探索使用强化学习提升预测精度和置信度校准。

Result: LLM能够准确预测相对内核性能，强化学习可以进一步提升其效用。在内核搜索中使用该替代模型，在相同GPU评估预算下可考虑数倍候选方案，从而找到比同等预算基线更快的内核。

Conclusion: LLM不仅可作为内核生成器，还能充当GPU的虚拟模型（virtual model），在核优化中扮演更广泛的角色，通过减少真实硬件评估次数来加速内核搜索。

Abstract: GPU kernels are the workhorse of modern deep learning, and optimizing them (via evolutionary search or coding agents) usually requires repeated measurement on target hardware. While these measurements provide the ground-truth signal necessary for kernel search, they are costly, because each evaluation of a kernel requires compilation and repeated execution on a GPU. As improvements in LLM inference reduce the cost of writing novel kernels and LLM-driven searches scale to large search budgets, on-device evaluation becomes a bottleneck. To address this, we study how LLMs can serve as selective GPU surrogates for kernel evaluation, by forecasting the performance of proposed kernels. A useful surrogate should be accurate, and it should be selective, by knowing when it could be wrong, and deferring to the GPU. To evaluate surrogates, we measure whether their forecasts are accurate, calibrated, and practically useful for recovering fast kernels under limited GPU-measurement budgets. Next, we study whether reinforcement learning can improve forecast accuracy and confidence calibration. Our experiments demonstrate that LLMs can accurately forecast relative kernel performance, that their utility can be improved through reinforcement learning. Used inside a kernel search, the surrogate lets the search consider several times as many candidates under the same GPU evaluation budget, and that leads to finding faster kernels than an equal-budget baseline. These results suggest that LLMs can play a broader role in kernel optimization, by acting as virtual models of a GPU rather than solely as kernel generators for search.

</details>


### [182] [On Efficient Scaling of GNNs via IO-Aware Layers Implementations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31500)
*Daria Fomina,Daniil Krasylnikov,Alexey Boykov,Andrey Dolgovyazov,Vyacheslav Zhdanovskiy,Fedor Velikonivtsev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于I/O和算术强度分析的GPU内核优化方法，将常用GNN层分为三种内核族（SpMM卷积、归约聚合、注意力层），分别设计融合内核以减少数据移动和提升局部性，实验表明在多种模型上可获得高达10倍加速和76倍显存压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNN框架（如DGL、PyG）在通用消息传递中存在边级中间变量物化导致的显存流量过大和稀疏不规则访存瓶颈，限制了大规模图上的可扩展性。

Method: 从I/O和算术强度视角出发，将常用GNN层归类为三种内核族：基于SpMM的卷积、基于归约的聚合、基于注意力的层（GATv2/Graph Transformer）。针对每类设计专用GPU融合内核，并研究了图重排序对不同内核映射的影响差异。

Result: 融合注意力内核在Graph Transformer上最高达3.9倍加速（中位数1.6倍），Tensor Core块稀疏变体在局部稠密图上达7.3倍；GATv2最高8.5倍（中位数2.0倍），峰值显存最高减少76倍（中位数6倍）；度感知归约内核最高10倍加速（中位数2.6倍）；优化缓存的cuSPARSE在SpMM层上最高8倍加速。

Conclusion: 所提出的GPU内核优化方案能有效加速多种GNN层，且作为即插即用替代方案发布，支持可重现的硬件感知GNN加速。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) are bottlenecked by sparse, irregular memory access. Popular frameworks such as DGL and PyTorch Geometric support general message passing, but complex layers often materialize edge-wise intermediates, increasing memory traffic and limiting scalability on large graphs. We take an I/O- and arithmetic-intensity--centric view and show that widely used layers fall into three kernel families: SpMM-based convolutions, reduction-based aggregations, and attention-based layers (GATv2/Graph Transformer). For each family, we develop GPU kernels that reduce data movement, improve locality, and remain robust across realistic graphs. We also study graph reordering and find that its impact depends on the kernel mapping: it benefits neighbor-parallel (gather-dominated) kernels more consistently than feature-parallel designs. Empirically, our fused attention kernels reach up to $\textbf{3.9}\times$ speedup for Graph Transformer (median $\textbf{1.6}\times$), with Tensor Core (block-sparse) variants up to $\textbf{7.3}\times$ on locally dense graphs; for GATv2 we reach up to $\textbf{8.5}\times$ speedup (median $\textbf{2.0}\times$) while reducing peak memory by up to $\textbf{76}\times$ (median $\textbf{6}\times$). Our degree-aware reduction kernels achieve up to $\textbf{10}\times$ speedup (median $\textbf{2.6}\times$). For SpMM-based layers, properly cached cuSPARSE achieves up to $\textbf{8}\times$ speedup over DGL and outperforms evaluated custom baselines in the majority of evaluations. We release our implementations as drop-in replacements to support reproducible, hardware-aware GNN acceleration.

</details>


### [183] [PINNs Failure Modes are Overfitting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30910)
*Nigel T. Andersen,Takashi Matsubara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现PINNs的失败模式源于过拟合（残差在配点处小但其他地方大），通过正则化可消除失败模式，并提出了基于双反向传播的方法，在标准失败模式方程上以23倍更少配点和普通架构达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINNs在部分简单方程上会收敛到错误解（失败模式），尽管损失很低。现有工作提出了多种架构和优化方案，但缺乏对失败根本原因的理解。本文旨在揭示失败模式的本质原因并提出更简洁有效的解决方案。

Method: 1) 直接可视化残差，证明失败模式是过拟合导致——残差仅在配点处小，其他地方大。2) 应用正则化消除失败模式。3) 扩展双反向传播（double backpropagation）到全部残差集，实现高效训练。

Result: 在四个标准失败模式方程上，所提方法以普通架构和最多23倍更少的配点达到了SOTA性能。

Conclusion: PINNs失败模式的根源是过拟合而非优化困境，正则化能有效消除失败模式；基于双反向传播的简单方法即可大幅超越复杂定制方案。

Abstract: Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) are a common class of machine learning-based partial differential equation (PDE) solvers which train a network to represent a solution by minimizing a residual loss that encodes the PDE. Despite their successes, they are known to fail on certain simple equations, converging to an incorrect solution despite low loss. These failure modes have garnered significant attention in the literature over the past several years, motivating both architectural and optimization based solutions. By directly visualizing the residual, we show that failure modes are the result of overfitting: the loss is minimized on the collocation points, but not elsewhere. Applying regularization causes the failure modes to vanish. Finally, we extend double backpropagation over the full set of residuals, and use it to achieve state-of-the-art performance on four standard failure mode equations with up to $23\times$ fewer collocation points and a vanilla architecture.

</details>


### [184] [Skill Reuse as Compression in Agentic RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31509)
*Zhikun Xu,Yu Feng,Jacob Dineen,Taiwei Shi,Jieyu Zhao,Ben Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ReuseRL基于最小描述长度原理，通过提取可重用的技能字典并惩罚低压缩性的行为，显著提升了LLM智能体在分布内和外分布任务上的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准RL训练的LLM智能体容易学习脆弱、任务特定的捷径，导致泛化能力差。作者假设，当成功轨迹在结构上可压缩（即可分解为一小组可重用抽象模式）时，智能体泛化能力更强。

Method: 提出ReuseRL框架：1）从成功轨迹中提取共享技能字典；2）在RL目标中添加分割代价（segmentation cost），显式惩罚那些编码效率低下的特异行为；3）基于最小描述长度（MDL）原理进行正则化；4）证明了该压缩惩罚的PAC-Bayes泛化界限。

Result: 在ALFWorld、TextWorld-Cooking和Countdown-Stepwise三个基准上，ReuseRL在分布内和分布外成功率上均优于vanilla GRPO和强round-length基线方法。

Conclusion: 轨迹的结构可压缩性（通过MDL原理量化）是提升智能体泛化能力的有效正则化信号，通过惩罚低压缩性的特异性行为可以学习到更通用的技能。

Abstract: Large language model agents trained with reinforcement learning (RL) often learn brittle, task-specific shortcuts. We hypothesize that agents generalize better when their successful trajectories are structurally compressible, decomposed into a small set of reusable abstract patterns. To formalize this, we introduce ReuseRL, which grounds agentic RL in the Minimum Description Length (MDL) principle. ReuseRL extracts a shared skill dictionary from successful trajectories and augments the RL objective with a segmentation cost, explicitly penalizing idiosyncratic behaviors that encode poorly. We prove a PAC-Bayes generalization bound for this compression penalty. Across ALFWorld, TextWorld-Cooking, and Countdown-Stepwise, ReuseRL improves in- and out-of-distribution success over vanilla GRPO and strong round-length baselines.

</details>


### [185] [Automating Formal Verification with Reinforcement Learning and Recursive Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30914)
*Max Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本论文研究了如何利用验证器环境（通过强化学习和推理时搜索）提升大语言模型生成经过验证的程序和证明的能力。在Dafny中使用GRPO进行多轮RLVR训练，将验证通过率从9.7%提升至31.1%；在Lean中构建验证器引导的推理框架，通过子目标分解和证明修正器将通过率从46.2%提升至69.2%，并解决了42个此前未解决任务中的7个。同时提出了Dalek-Bench基准测试集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化形式化验证对于大语言模型仍然具有挑战性，因为证明助理和验证感知语言的数据稀缺，且正确性取决于满足精确的机器可检查规范而非生成合理的代码。现有模型在生成经过验证的程序和证明方面表现不足，需要探索如何利用验证器环境来改进LLM的生成能力。

Method: 采用两阶段方法：(1) 在Dafny中使用GRPO及其变体进行RLVR训练，将生成的候选程序组装为完整程序并用编译器和验证器结果评分，经过规范过滤后执行多轮RLVR；(2) 在Lean中构建验证器引导的推理脚手架，将证明生成视为对分解子目标的结构化搜索，结合验证器反馈、诊断和修复，并使用证明修正器进行迭代改进。

Result: 初始Dafny实验中验证奖励从2.2%提升至58.1%，但出现规范利用问题；经过滤后的多轮RLVR将验证通过率从9.7%提升至31.1%。Lean推理框架在VeriCoding上从46.2%提升至69.2%，在VERINA上解决了7个此前未解决的任务。Dalek-Bench的初步结果仍然较弱。

Conclusion: 验证器环境（RLVR和推理时搜索）能有效提升LLM生成经过验证的程序和证明的能力，但面临规范利用（hacking）等挑战。Dalek-Bench的弱结果说明需要更强的进度评估和任务特定的工具使用策略。

Abstract: Automated formal verification remains challenging for large language models because data for proof assistants and verification-aware languages is scarce, and correctness depends on satisfying precise machine-checkable specifications rather than producing plausible code. This thesis studies how verifier environments can improve LLM generation of verified programs and proofs through reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR) and verifier-guided inference-time search. First, we train open-source models in Dafny with RLVR using Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) and related variants, assembling generated candidates into complete programs and scoring them with compiler and verifier outcomes. Initial experiments on an APPS-derived Dafny dataset increased verified reward from 2.2% to 58.1%, but revealed specification hacking, where models exploit weak formal specifications instead of implementing the intended solutions. After filtering underspecified and vulnerable tasks, multi-turn RLVR on the refined benchmark improves the verified pass rate from 9.7% to 31.1%. Second, we develop a verifier-guided inference scaffold in Lean that treats proof generation as structured search over decomposed subgoals, verifier feedback, diagnostics, and repair. With a fixed base model, the full scaffold with proof reviser improves pass rate on an initial VeriCoding pilot set from 46.2% under direct repair to 69.2%. On the larger VERINA dataset, whole-task decomposition plus proof reviser solves 7 of 42 previously unsolved tasks. We also introduce Dalek-Bench, a repository-scale Lean benchmark derived from the Rust $\texttt{curve25519-dalek}$ verification project; preliminary results remain weak, indicating that stronger progress evaluation and task-specific tool-use policies are still needed.

</details>


### [186] [Positional versus Symbolic Attention Heads: Learning Dynamics, RoPE Geometry, and Length Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31558)
*Felipe Urrutia,Juan José Alegría,Cinthia Sanchez Macias,Jorge Salas,Cristian B. Calderon,Cristobal Rojas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过多跳推理任务研究Transformer注意力头的学习动态，发现成功学习与纯注意力头（位置型或符号型）的出现相关。尽管任务结构等价，但数字任务需要位置头和符号头，字母任务仅需符号头。符号机制在长序列外推中更可靠，位置机制则面临更严重的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Transformer在结构化任务中的求解机制及其在新场景中的行为，对于安全部署具有重要意义。

Method: 在GPT-J上训练两个结构等价的多跳推理任务（数字位置推理和字母符号推理），利用注意力头行为分类指标识别纯头，并通过理论构造和几何解释分析RoPE注意力机制的功能实现。

Result: 成功学习时出现纯注意力头；数字任务需要位置头+符号头，字母任务仅需符号头；符号机制在长序列外推中更鲁棒，位置机制存在更尖锐的限制。

Conclusion: 位置机制与符号机制在长序列外推上存在定量分离，符号机制更可靠，该预测在受控和真实模型中得到验证。

Abstract: Transformer-based language models are widespread in today's society. As such, understanding the mechanisms by which they solve structured tasks and predicting how they may behave in novel scenarios is of great importance for safe deployment. We study the learning dynamics of attention heads in a controlled setting by training a decoder-only Transformer (GPT-J) on two structurally equivalent multi-hop reasoning tasks: a number task requiring positional reasoning and a letter task requiring symbolic reasoning. Using a recently introduced metric that classifies attention-head behavior as positional or symbolic for a given prompt, we show that successful learning is associated with the emergence of pure heads, i.e., heads that express themselves as either positional or symbolic. Despite the tasks' structural equivalence, they impose different mechanistic demands: the number task requires both positional and symbolic heads, whereas the letter task requires only symbolic heads. We then identify the computational roles of these heads, characterize the basic functions they implement, and give theoretical constructions showing how single-layer RoPE-based attention can realize these functions through geometrically interpretable query, key, and value operations. This analysis yields a quantitative separation between positional and symbolic mechanisms in their robustness to longer sequences, formalized through a novel notion of discrepancy. We empirically validate the resulting predictions in both controlled and real-world models, showing that symbolic mechanisms extrapolate more reliably to longer sequences while positional mechanisms face sharper limitations.

</details>


### [187] [Welfare, Improvability, and Variance: A Principal-Agent Approach to Optimal Benchmark Item Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30916)
*Andreas Haupt,Justin Hartenstein,Anka Reuel,Mykel Kochenderfer,Sanmi Koyejo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将基准测试建模为多任务主-代理人博弈，论证基准测试的社会福利损失由项目层级的三个基本要素共同决定：与福利优先级的对齐、边际可改进性以及性能方差。基于此理论，开发了一个审计框架，用于沿这三个维度对基准测试项目进行排序，并在OLMES项目上进行了实证应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI基准测试的局限性研究主要集中在污染、饱和和构造欠规范等问题上，而对聚合方法的关注严重不足——基准测试通常通过对项目分数进行简单平均来汇总，隐含地假设每个测试项目具有同等价值。作者旨在揭示这种聚合方式的缺陷，并提供更科学的审计方法。

Method: 将基准测试建模为多任务主-代理人博弈，推导出社会福利损失由三个项目层级的基本要素决定：与规范福利优先级的对齐程度、边际可改进性和性能方差。将此理论转化为一个审计框架，沿这三个维度对项目进行排序。实证中使用WORKBank衡量福利、EvoLM 4B模型套件衡量可改进性、PolyPythias 410M模型面板衡量方差，对OLMES项目进行分析。

Result: 该框架能够识别出OLMES中在亲工作者福利操作化假设下属于帕累托劣势的项目。所有代码已开源。

Conclusion: 基准测试中对项目的简单平均聚合存在根本性问题，作者提出的三要素审计框架为评估和设计更科学的基准测试提供了理论和实践工具。

Abstract: AI benchmarks have well-documented limitations, with prior work examining contamination, saturation, and construct underspecification. Aggregation has received far less attention: benchmarks are typically summarized by uniformly averaging item-level scores, implicitly treating every test item as equally valuable. We model benchmarking as a multitask principal-agent game and show that the welfare loss from a benchmark is determined jointly by three item-level primitives: alignment with normative welfare priorities, marginal improvability, and performance variance. We translate the theory into an audit framework that ranks items along each of these three axes, and apply it to OLMES items using WORKBank for welfare, the EvoLM 4B suite for improvability, and the PolyPythias 410M panel for variance. The framework surfaces items that are Pareto-inferior within OLMES subject to a pro-worker welfare operationalization. All code is available at https://github.com/stair-lab/principal-agent-benchmarks.

</details>


### [188] [Unsupervised Diffusion Solver for Combinatorial Optimization via Combinatorial Adjoint Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30920)
*Shengyu Feng,Tarun Suresh,Yiming Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Combinatorial Adjoint Matching (CAM)，一种针对离散扩散组合优化求解器的无监督训练框架，通过将扩散模型建模为连续时间马尔可夫链上的随机控制问题并引入离散伴随动力学，实现了结构化、低方差的轨迹级优化信号传播，在多种组合优化问题上性能优于现有无监督扩散基线，并达到与有监督扩散求解器及传统求解器相当的水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的组合优化神经求解器通常依赖大量近最优解进行有监督训练，缺乏无监督训练方法，且离散组合域上的轨迹级优化信号难以有效传播。

Method: 将基于扩散的组合优化建模为连续时间马尔可夫链上的随机控制问题，引入离散伴随动力学来传播优化信号，提出Combinatorial Adjoint Matching (CAM)无监督训练框架。

Result: CAM在多种组合优化问题上一致优于现有无监督扩散基线方法，性能与强有监督扩散求解器乃至传统求解器竞争。

Conclusion: 离散伴随方法能够有效实现无监督训练下的扩散组合优化求解器，为摆脱对大量最优解标注的依赖提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Diffusion-based neural solvers have shown strong promise for combinatorial optimization (CO), but existing methods typically rely on supervised training with large collections of near-optimal solutions. In this work, we extend adjoint-based trajectory optimization methods to discrete combinatorial domains. We formulate diffusion-based CO as a stochastic control problem over Continuous-Time Markov Chains and introduce discrete adjoint dynamics for propagating optimization signals through discrete generative trajectories. Building on this formulation, we propose Combinatorial Adjoint Matching (CAM), an unsupervised training framework for discrete diffusion solvers with structured and low-variance trajectory-level optimization signals. Empirically, CAM consistently outperforms existing unsupervised diffusion baselines and achieves performance competitive with strong supervised diffusion solvers and even traditional solvers across diverse combinatorial optimization problems. Our code is available at https://github.com/Shengyu-Feng/CAM.

</details>


### [189] [Spectral Anatomy of Quantum Gaussian Process Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30952)
*Jian Xu,Chao Li,Guang Lin,Yuning Qiu,Delu Zeng,John Paisley,Qibin Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现量子高斯过程（QGP）中看似无关的“HHL-QGP指数加速被否定”与“量子核后验病态破坏贝叶斯优化”两个现象，实际由同一量——核Gram矩阵的归一化谱熵 S(K)/log n——控制。作者证明了Nyström近似误差的Cauchy–Schwarz尾界、基于Bach自由度dσ(K)的有限样本方差收缩恒等式，以及目标内在维度的最优熵刻画。实验表明该诊断与核族无关，在多种量子/经典核上均呈现一致的S/logn曲线，并成功从模拟器迁移到IBM Heron硬件（中位绝对误差3.2%），无需错误缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示HHL-QGP回归指数加速被否定与量子核后验病态这两个看似独立的现象背后的统一数学机制，建立普适的诊断量来评估量子高斯过程的可行性与性能边界。

Method: （1）证明Nyström近似误差的Cauchy–Schwarz尾界；（2）导出以Bach自由度dσ(K)表达的有限样本方差收缩恒等式；（3）通过目标在核本征基下的内在维度刻画目标依赖的最优熵；（4）在模拟器和IBM Heron量子硬件上对多种核族（硬件高效、matchgate、IQP、RBF/Matérn/RFF/深度核）进行实证验证。

Result: 所有核族在去量子化、ECE和方差收缩面板上均坍缩到相同的S/logn曲线；NLL最优位于高熵（光滑目标）或低熵（带限量子数据目标）；从模拟器迁移到IBM Heron硬件中位绝对误差3.2%（nq=4），扩展到nq=6时平均误差1.7%；无需错误缓解。

Conclusion: 归一化谱熵S(K)/logn是量子高斯过程的核心诊断量，统一解释了去量子化可行性、后验病态与最优性能边界，并具有良好的硬件可迁移性。

Abstract: Two recent results have reshaped quantum Gaussian processes (QGPs). On the one hand, \citet{lowe2025assessing} rule out the exponential speedups claimed by HHL-based QGP regression in the typical, well-conditioned regime; on the other, an independent line of work shows that highly expressive quantum kernels suffer posterior pathologies that break Bayesian optimization. We show that these seemingly unrelated phenomena are governed by the same quantity: the normalized spectral entropy $S(K)/\log n$ of the kernel Gram matrix. We prove a Cauchy--Schwarz tail bound on Nyström approximation error, a finite-sample variance-contraction identity in terms of Bach's degrees of freedom $d_σ(K)$, and a characterization of the \emph{target-dependent} optimal entropy via the intrinsic dimension of the target in the kernel eigenbasis. Empirically, the diagnostic is kernel-agnostic: hardware-efficient, matchgate, IQP \emph{and} RBF/Matérn/RFF/deep-kernel families all collapse onto identical $S/\log n$ curves on dequantization, ECE, and variance-contraction panels. The NLL sweet spot lives at high entropy for smooth targets and at low entropy for band-limited quantum-data targets. The diagnostic transfers from simulator to IBM Heron hardware with median absolute error $3.2\%$ and mean $5.2\%$ in $S/\log n$ across $24$ configurations at $n_q = 4$, with matchgate and IQP within $5\%$ mean and a single HE configuration returning a $30\%$ outlier that drops to $0.5\%$ on rerun (attributed to calibration drift); the same diagnostic transfers to a second Heron backend (mean error $2.7\%$) and to a $n_q = 6$ scale-up on the original backend (mean error $1.7\%$). No error mitigation is applied throughout.

</details>


### [190] [Revisiting Zeroth-Order Hessian Approximation: A Single-Step Policy Optimization Lens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30960)
*Junbin Qiu,Zhaowei Hong,Renzhe Xu,Yao Shu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ZoVH框架，通过将零阶Hessian估计重新解释为单步策略优化的视角，统一了多种经典随机估计器，并开发了一套包含全Hessian矩阵、正则化逆和偏差校正逆Hessian-梯度积分的方差缩减估计器套件，理论上证明了无偏性、方差最优性和收敛保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维场景下零阶Hessian及其逆的低方差估计仍然是一个重大挑战，现有方法缺乏统一的框架来系统性地降低估计方差。

Method: 提出ZoVH框架：(1) 通过单步策略优化视角重新解释零阶Hessian近似，建立了一般零阶Hessian估计器与平滑策略优化目标Hessian之间的理论等价性；(2) 推导出理论上方差最优的基线；(3) 引入历史函数查询重用的查询复用策略以提升样本效率。

Result: 严格理论分析证明了Hessian估计器的无偏性、基线的方差最优性、完整ZoVH套件的误差界以及曲率感知零阶算法的收敛保证；大量实证结果验证了ZoVH在真实应用中具有更优的估计精度和收敛性能。

Conclusion: ZoVH为零阶Hessian估计提供了一种统一且高效的方差缩减框架，在双层优化、贝叶斯推断和不确定性量化等任务中具有广泛应用前景。

Abstract: Accurate Zeroth-Order (ZO) Hessian estimation is a cornerstone of derivative-free methods, essential for tasks such as bilevel optimization, Bayesian inference, and uncertainty quantification. However, obtaining a complete suite of low-variance estimators for the Hessian and its inverse in high-dimensional settings remains a significant challenge. To address this, we propose a unified framework that reinterprets ZO Hessian approximation through the lens of single-step Policy Optimization (PO). This perspective establishes a theoretical equivalence between general ZO Hessian estimators and the Hessian of a smoothed PO objective, unifying distinct classical randomized estimators as specific instances of baseline selection. Building on this foundation, we introduce ZoVH, a comprehensive suite of variance-reduced estimators for the full Hessian matrix, its regularized inverse, and the bias-corrected inverse Hessian-gradient product. ZoVH leverages two key techniques: (1) a unique optimal baseline derived to provably minimize variance, and (2) a query reuse strategy that incorporates historical function queries to enhance sample efficiency without inflating costs. Our rigorous theoretical analysis confirms the unbiasedness of the Hessian estimator, validates the variance optimality of our baseline, provides error bounds for the entire ZoVH suite, and establishes convergence guarantees for the resulting curvature-aware ZO algorithm. Extensive empirical results validate our theoretical findings, demonstrating that ZoVH achieves superior estimation accuracy and convergence performance in real-world applications. Code is available at https://github.com/Qjbtiger/ZoVH

</details>


### [191] [Parallel Tempering Initial Sampling in Inference-Time Reward Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30991)
*Myeongjun Oh,Gwangho Kim,Sungyoon Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对扩散模型推理时奖励对齐中SMC方法因初始化不佳和易陷局部模式的问题，提出PATHS方法，通过并行退火耦合多个采样链，在平坦化奖励景观中高效探索稀有高奖励区域，在布局到图像等任务上取得一致性提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SMC方法在复杂奖励景观中从标准先验初始化粒子，导致高奖励区域采样极难；即使采用奖励感知初始化，也容易陷入多模态奖励景观的局部模式，缺乏全局探索能力。

Method: PATHS维护一个奖励退火链的阶梯结构，通过周期性的Metropolis交换操作耦合多个采样链，利用平坦化的奖励景观促进跨模式探索，缓解模式捕获问题。

Result: 在布局到图像生成和数量感知生成任务中，PATHS在奖励对齐质量上持续优于基线方法，尤其在复杂提示场景下提升显著。

Conclusion: PATHS作为一种初始化方法，通过并行退火机制显著增强了有限预算下对稀有高奖励区域的探索能力，有效解决了现有SMC方法在复杂奖励对齐中的性能瓶颈。

Abstract: Inference-time reward alignment steers pretrained diffusion and flow-based generative models to satisfy user-specified rewards without retraining. Recently, Sequential Monte Carlo (SMC) has emerged as a powerful framework for this task by iteratively filtering and propagating multiple particles. However, we show that standard SMC-based methods often suffer from poor performance because they initialize particles from a standard prior, whereas high-reward regions in complex reward landscapes are extremely rare. Further, we show that even recent reward-aware initial sampling approaches remain vulnerable to getting trapped in local modes, as complex reward landscapes are often multi-modal. To overcome these limitations, we propose PATHS (PArallel Tempering for High-complexity reward Sampling), a novel initialization method that couples multiple sampling chains through parallel tempering. PATHS maintains a ladder of reward-tempered chains and periodically performs Metropolis swaps, enabling efficient exploration across flattened reward landscapes, thereby mitigating the mode-trapping issues. Our analysis reveals that this mechanism substantially enhances the finite-budget exploration of rare, high-reward regions that are typically challenging to sample. Experiments on layout-to-image and quantity-aware generation show that PATHS achieves consistent gains in alignment quality, particularly on complex prompts.

</details>


### [192] [Eigenvectors of Experts are Training-free Non-collapsing Routers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30992)
*Giang Do,Hung Le,Truyen Tran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SSMoE，一种无需训练的框架，通过利用专家权重的奇异值分解（SVD）谱特性来解决SMoE模型中的专家崩溃问题，从而提升模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SMoE模型在训练和推理中面临专家崩溃问题，现有方法（如改进路由）需要从头训练或微调，成本高昂，且无法完全解决预训练模型中的崩溃问题。

Method: SSMoE：基于专家权重矩阵的特征向量编码丰富语义信息的观察，利用奇异值分解的谱特性，提出一种无需训练的框架来替代传统路由策略。

Result: 在多种语言和视觉任务上，在干净和损坏数据设置下，SSMoE均展现出强泛化能力和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 对模型内部结构的深入理解可以指导开发更有效的SMoE架构，SSMoE为无需训练即可缓解专家崩溃问题提供了新途径。

Abstract: Sparse Mixture of Experts (SMoE) architectures improve the training efficiency of Large Language Models (LLMs) by routing input tokens to a selected subset of specialized experts. Despite their remarkable success, both training and inference in SMoE models suffer from the expert collapse issue (Chi et al., 2022), which degrades model performance. Prior studies primarily focus on improving the router; however, such methods rely on training from scratch or fine-tuning, which requires high computational and data-processing costs. Furthermore, we demonstrate that, despite these efforts, the issue persists when advancing well-pretrained SMoE models, as evidenced by both theoretical and empirical results. To fill that gap, we analyze the advanced SMoE models and observe that the eigenvectors of expert weight matrices encode rich semantic information, pointing to an effective alternative to conventional routing strategies. Building on this insight, we propose Singular Value Decomposition SMoE (SSMoE), a novel and training-free framework that leverages spectral properties of the expert weights to address the collapse issue and enhance model performance. Extensive experiments across diverse language and vision tasks, under both clean and corrupt data settings, demonstrate the strong generalization and robustness of SSMoE. Our findings highlight how a deeper understanding of model internals can guide the development of more effective SMoE architectures. Our implementation is publicly available at https://github.com/giangdip2410/SSMoE.

</details>


### [193] [Learning Multi-Agent Coordination via Sheaf-ADMM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31005)
*Jeffrey Seely,Bartłomiej Cupiał,Llion Jones*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个可微优化框架，通过将输入分解为局部视图并由神经编码器参数化的凸子问题，利用ADMM和细胞层（cellular sheaf）协调多智能体，实现端到端训练。在迷宫寻路、图像分类和数独任务上验证了有效性，相比标准CNN和MPNN展现出更好的鲁棒性和求解率，且ADMM结构提供了可分析、可干预的协调动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于消息传递的多智能体架构缺乏可解释性和可干预性，且难以处理智能体间异构的全局一致性需求。本文旨在通过结合优化理论（ADMM）和拓扑结构（细胞层），构建一个可微、可分析、可干预的多智能体协调框架。

Method: 输入分解为重叠局部视图，每个智能体通过神经编码器参数化求解一个凸子问题；智能体间通过ADMM协调，协调约束由细胞层（cellular sheaf）指定，允许异构的全局一致性；通过展开优化过程进行反向传播，实现端到端联合训练。

Result: 在迷宫寻路、MNIST图像分类和数独任务上验证：局部视图分解使MNIST上对分布偏移的鲁棒性优于标准CNN；数独求解率显著高于参数匹配的MPNN基线；ADMM结构暴露了原始、共识和对偶三种状态变量，提供了消息传递架构不具备的可分析性和可干预性。

Conclusion: 基于ADMM和细胞层的可微优化框架能有效实现多智能体协调，在鲁棒性、求解率和可解释性方面均优于现有方法，为构建可分析、可干预的多智能体系统提供了新范式。

Abstract: We present a differentiable optimization framework for multi-agent coordination. An input is decomposed into overlapping local views, each processed by an agent that solves a convex subproblem parameterized by a neural encoder. Agents coordinate through the Alternating Direction Method of Multipliers (ADMM) with inter-agent constraints specified by a cellular sheaf. The sheaf specifies which aspects of neighboring solutions must agree, allowing for heterogeneous notions of global consensus. Backpropagating through the unrolled optimization jointly trains all components of the multi-agent system. We evaluate on maze pathfinding, image classification, and Sudoku, where agents with individually insufficient local views learn to coordinate to produce correct global outputs. On MNIST, the local-view decomposition yields improved robustness to distribution shifts relative to a standard CNN. On Sudoku, the optimization-derived structure yields markedly higher solve rates than parameter-matched MPNN baselines. Finally, the ADMM structure exposes distinct primal, consensus, and dual state variables, opening the coordination dynamics to direct analysis and intervention -- a property unavailable in standard message-passing architectures.

</details>


### [194] [Physics-Informed Coarsening for Multigrid Graph Neural Surrogates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31013)
*Amir Bazzi,David Cardinaux,Ramy Nemer,Jose Alaves,Arjun Kalkur Matpadi Raghavendra,Elie Hachem*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于固体力学的多尺度图神经网络替代模型，采用基于残差的物理信息粗化策略替代传统几何降采样，在保持长程交互的同时提升了长期推演的稳定性与精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基于学习的偏微分方程替代模型在流体和规则几何领域取得了显著进展，但在可变形固体力学领域（涉及非线性弹性、塑性及瞬态行为）仍缺乏鲁棒的替代模型，现有标准架构难以应对这些挑战。

Method: 设计了一种多尺度图神经网络，包含编码器-处理器-解码器架构，并引入基于残差的局部物理活动度量（而非几何启发式）对节点进行评分，优先保留高应变或高应力集中区域，从而实现物理信息驱动的粗化策略和多尺度消息传递。

Result: 在线性、非线性和瞬态多个固体力学数据集上，该方法相比标准采样基线在精度和长期推演稳定性上均取得了一致的提升。

Conclusion: 物理信息驱动的粗化策略对于构建可扩展的固体力学替代模型至关重要，为基于学习的固体力学仿真提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Learning-based surrogates for partial differential equations have recently matched the accuracy of classical solvers while achieving orders-of-magnitude speedups, predominantly in fluid settings and structured geometries. In contrast, robust surrogates for deformable solids remain underexplored, despite the presence of nonlinear elasticity, plasticity, and transient behavior that challenge standard architectures. We introduce a multigrid graph neural network for solid mechanics that couples an encoder-processor-decoder backbone with a physics-informed coarsening strategy. Instead of downsampling via geometric heuristics, our method scores nodes using a residual-based measure of local physical activity and preferentially retains regions of high strain or stress concentration, allocating multiscale capacity where it is most needed. This preserves long-range interactions through hierarchical message passing while improving stability over long rollouts. We evaluate on multiple datasets covering linear, nonlinear, and transient regimes, and observe consistent gains in accuracy and rollout stability compared to standard sampling baselines. Our results highlight the importance of physics-informed coarsening for scalable surrogate modeling in solid mechanics.

</details>


### [195] [SDM-Q: Cost-Aware Staged Decision-Making for Multi-Omics Classification with Deep Q-Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31014)
*Nan Mu,Xiaoyang Fan,Chen Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SDM-Q，一个基于强化学习的框架，通过将多组学诊断建模为有限时域序贯决策问题，在分类性能与模态获取成本之间取得平衡，有效减少冗余模态获取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习方法在推理时假设完全模态可用，导致大量冗余且临床实用性有限；完整多组学数据获取昂贵且耗时，亟需一种成本感知的自适应多组学分类方法。

Method: 将多组学诊断建模为有限时域序贯决策问题，使用动作-价值函数决定是否获取额外模态或终止决策并输出预测；奖励在终止阶段由分类正确性和累计模态获取成本共同决定；采用逆向阶段优化策略提升策略一致性和训练稳定性。

Result: 在ROSMAP、LGG、BRCA和KIPAN四个公共数据集上，SDM-Q有效减少冗余模态获取；BRCA和KIPAN中超过99%和95%的受试者仅用单模态即可准确分类；ROSMAP和LGG的平均获取模态数不超过2。

Conclusion: 成本感知的序贯决策为提升精准医学工作流效率提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Multi-omics data provide complementary molecular characterizations of disease phenotypes and play an important role in disease diagnosis and subtype classification in precision medicine. However, acquiring complete multi-omics profiles is expensive and time-consuming, while most existing deep learning methods assume full modality availability during inference, resulting in substantial redundancy and limited practicality in clinical settings. To address this issue, we propose SDM-Q, a reinforcement learning framework for adaptive and cost-aware multi-omics classification. Specifically, multi-omics diagnosis is reformulated as a finite-horizon sequential decision problem, where the currently acquired omics modalities define the diagnostic state at each stage. An action--value function determines whether to acquire an additional modality or terminate the decision process and output the final prediction. To balance diagnostic utility and acquisition cost, the reward is defined only at the terminal stage and jointly determined by classification correctness and cumulative modality acquisition cost. A backward stage-wise optimization strategy is introduced to improve policy consistency and training stability. Experiments on four public multi-omics datasets, including ROSMAP, LGG, BRCA, and KIPAN, demonstrate that SDM-Q effectively reduces redundant modality acquisition while maintaining competitive classification performance compared with methods using complete multi-omics inputs. In the BRCA and KIPAN datasets, more than 99\% and 95\% of subjects, respectively, achieve accurate classification using only a single omics modality, while the average number of acquired modalities remains below two for ROSMAP and LGG. These results suggest that cost-aware sequential decision-making provides an effective paradigm for improving the efficiency of precision medicine workflows.

</details>


### [196] [An Efficient and Scalable Graph Condensation with Structure-Preserving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31016)
*Yulin Hu,Fuyan Ou,Ye Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高效且可扩展的图压缩方法SP-ESGC，通过解耦设计分离节点压缩与图结构生成，利用热核特征传播、混合聚类和预训练边预测器实现高效且跨GNN架构泛化良好的图压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图压缩方法存在计算效率低下（耦合优化导致）以及跨GNN架构泛化能力差的问题，亟需一种高效且可扩展的解决方案。

Method: 提出SP-ESGC方法，包含三个关键步骤：1) 基于谱图理论的热核特征传播生成节点表示；2) 设计新型混合聚类策略从节点表示中提取判别性类内质心；3) 使用预训练边预测器从原始图中推断可迁移的结构模式，生成精确的合成图。

Result: 在真实图数据集上的大量实验表明，SP-ESGC实现了精确的图压缩，显著提升了计算效率，并且在不同GNN架构上展现出良好的泛化性能。

Conclusion: SP-ESGC通过解耦设计和结构保持策略，有效解决了传统图压缩方法计算效率低和泛化性差的问题，为资源受限场景下GNN的部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Graph condensation (GC) is pivotal for enabling Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) deployment in resource-constrained scenarios by compressing large-scale graphs into compact synthetic counterparts. Existing GC methods commonly suffer from computational inefficiency due to coupled optimization as well as encountering poor generalization across GNN architectures. To address these challenges, this study proposes an Efficient and Scalable Graph Condensation with Structure-Preserving (SP-ESGC), which possesses a decoupled design that separates node condensation from graph structure generation. Specifically, it first employs heat kernel feature propagation to generate node representation via spectral graph theory-inspired diffusion. Further, a novel hybrid clustering strategy is designed to extracts discriminative intra-class centroids from the node representation. Finally, a pre-trained edge predictor infers transferable structural patterns from the original graph, ensuring accurate synthetic graph generation. Extensive experiments on real-world graph datasets demonstrate that the proposed SP-ESGC implementes a precise GC with significantly high computational efficiency. Moreover, SP-ESGC also generalizes well across diverse GNN architectures.

</details>


### [197] [Augmented Lagrangian Predictive Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31022)
*Jeffrey Seely,Julian Gould*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出增强拉格朗日预测编码（PC-ALM），通过层局部拉格朗日乘子累积约束误差，使预测编码的权重更新方向对齐反向传播，在保持推理预算的同时匹配BP性能，并揭示了弹道式信用传播机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测编码作为反向传播的局部学习替代方案，在深度窄网络中性能不佳，其信用传播速度慢且呈扩散性。现有方法无法在不增加推理预算的前提下使权重更新与BP对齐，因此需要一种既能保持PC局部性又能实现BP梯度等价传播的框架。

Method: 将增强拉格朗日方法引入预测编码，为每层约束误差引入层局部拉格朗日乘子；通过乘子的对偶上升驱动激活值的循环动力学，替代PC中基于标量能量的热流动力学；利用乘子累积历史约束误差使权重更新方向逐渐对齐BP梯度。

Result: 在线性PC网络中，PC-ALM收敛到精确BP梯度分布在网络各层的均衡态；在深度达128层的非线性网络中，PC-ALM在所有宽深比下匹配BP性能，尤其解决了深度窄网络中PC表现不佳的问题；观察到信用信号在极深网络中呈弹道式均匀传播，而非PC的慢速扩散式传播。

Conclusion: 增强拉格朗日框架为预测编码提供了一般化推广，展示了分布式系统如何通过纯局部动力学计算和传播类BP信用信号，为局部学习算法的设计提供了新视角。

Abstract: Predictive coding (PC) is a local-learning alternative to backpropagation (BP), training deep networks via local energy-minimization dynamics rather than a global backward pass. We introduce Augmented Lagrangian Predictive Coding (PC-ALM), which maintains PC's inference budget but aligns each weight update toward BP by accumulating per-layer constraint errors into a layer-local Lagrange multiplier. In linear PC networks, PC-ALM converges to an equilibrium with exact BP gradients distributed across the network via only layer-local updates. We analyze PC-ALM in nonlinear PC networks up to depth 128 and show that it matches BP performance across all width-depth regimes, notably in deep narrow networks where PC underperforms. PC-ALM introduces recurrent dynamics in each layer's activations. Compared to PC's heat flow on a scalar energy, PC-ALM dynamics are driven by dual ascent on the augmented Lagrangian. We observe "ballistic" credit propagation across very deep networks, with credit signals evenly distributed across layers, compared to PC's slow, diffusive credit propagation. Beyond the algorithm itself, the augmented Lagrangian framework offers a generalization of PC, and may yield insights into how distributed systems could compute and propagate BP-like credit signals through purely local dynamics.

</details>


### [198] [Multi-Scale Separable Fourier Neural Networks for Solving High-Frequency PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31027)
*Qihong Yang,Qiaolin He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出多尺度可分傅里叶神经网络(MS-SFNN)，通过可分离表示、随机固定权重、余弦激活的傅里叶特征及高效批处理QR分解，实现对高维高频PDE的高精度求解，显著优于PINN和SV-SNN。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理信息神经网络(PINN)在处理高频、高维偏微分方程时面临精度不足和计算效率低下的问题，亟需一种兼具高精度和高效率的替代方案。

Method: 1) 采用可分离表示结构，d维输入对应d个独立子网络，每个子网络仅处理单个坐标，通过逐元素乘法构造基函数；2) 网络参数从均匀分布随机初始化后固定不变，仅通过最小二乘求解线性组合系数；3) 引入可调缩放因子调制基函数频率成分，使用余弦激活函数嵌入傅里叶特征；4) 推导解析基函数导数替代自动微分，设计内存高效的批处理QR分解算法求解大规模最小二乘系统。

Result: 在多个具有挑战性的线性与非线性高频PDE数值实验中，MS-SFNN达到了前所未有的精度，显著优于PINN和SV-SNN等当前最先进方法。

Conclusion: MS-SFNN通过可分离网络结构、随机固定权重与傅里叶特征嵌入的结合，在保持计算效率的同时突破了传统神经网络方法在高频PDE求解上的精度瓶颈，为高维高频PDE的高效求解提供了新范式。

Abstract: We propose a novel neural network architecture, termed Multi-Scale Separable Fourier Neural Networks (MS-SFNN), for the accurate and efficient solution of linear and nonlinear high-frequency partial differential equations (PDEs). MS-SFNN exploits a separable representation: given a $d$-dimensional input, it employs $d$ independent subnetworks -- each acting on a single coordinate -- and constructs basis functions via element-wise multiplication of their outputs. The PDE solution is approximated as a linear combination of these basis functions, with coefficients determined by least squares. Critically, all network weights and biases are randomly initialized once, from a uniform distribution with unit variance, and remain fixed thereafter. To enhance expressivity, a tunable scaling factor is introduced in each subnetwork to modulate the frequency content of the resulting basis functions. Fourier features are explicitly embedded through cosine activations, endowing the method with strong spectral approximation capabilities. To mitigate the memory bottleneck associated with dense collocation in high-frequency or three-dimensional problems, we replace automatic differentiation with analytically derived basis function derivatives and develop a memory-efficient batched QR decomposition algorithm for solving large-scale least-squares systems. Numerical experiments demonstrate that MS-SFNN achieves unprecedented accuracy across a range of challenging PDEs, significantly outperforming state-of-the-art methods such as Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINN) and Separated-Variable Spectral Neural Networks (SV-SNN).

</details>


### [199] [UniRTL: Unifying Code and Graph for Robust RTL Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31040)
*Yi Liu,Hongji Zhang,Lei Chen,Mingxuan Yuan,Qiang Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: UniRTL提出了一种多模态预训练框架，通过联合利用RTL代码和控制数据流图（CDFG）进行细粒度对齐，在性能预测和代码检索任务上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RTL表示学习方法仅依赖单一数据模态（代码或图结构），限制了表示的表达能力和泛化能力。作者认为整合代码和CDFG两种互补模态对全面理解RTL设计至关重要。

Method: 提出UniRTL多模态预训练框架：(1) 通过互掩码建模实现代码与CDFG的细粒度对齐；(2) 采用分层训练策略，包含预训练的图感知分词器，以及先对齐文本（功能摘要）与代码、再集成图结构的分阶段对齐方法。

Result: 在性能预测和代码检索两个下游任务、多种设置下，UniRTL均持续优于先前方法，展现出更强的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: UniRTL通过多模态融合为硬件设计自动化提供了更强大、更鲁棒的基础表示方法，验证了代码与CDFG联合表征的有效性。

Abstract: Developing effective representations for register transfer level (RTL) designs is crucial for accelerating the hardware design workflow. Existing approaches, however, typically rely on a single data modality, either the RTL code or its associated graph-based representation, limiting the expressiveness and generalization ability of the learned representations. For RTL, the control data flow graph (CDFG) offers a comprehensive structural representation that preserves complete information, while the code modality explicitly encodes semantic and functional information. We argue that integrating these complementary modalities is essential for a thorough understanding of RTL designs. To this end, we propose UniRTL, a multimodal pretraining framework that learns unified RTL representations by jointly leveraging code and CDFG. UniRTL achieves fine-grained alignment between code and graph through mutual masked modeling and employs a hierarchical training strategy that incorporates a pretrained graph-aware tokenizer and staged alignment of text (i.e., functional summary) and code prior to graph integration. We evaluate UniRTL on two downstream tasks, performance prediction and code retrieval, under multiple settings. Experimental results show that UniRTL consistently outperforms prior methods, establishing it as a more robust and powerful foundation for advancing hardware design automation.

</details>
