### [200] [The Challenges of Using Reinforcement Learning for Controlling Industrial Energy Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31044)
*Tobias Lademann,Théo Vincent,Jan Peters,Matthias Weigold*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了强化学习在真实工业热能网络中的部署挑战，发现尽管能实现稳定运行，但与仿真结果相比存在显著的性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习在工业能源系统优化控制中的研究大多局限于仿真环境，缺乏对真实部署挑战的系统性分析。

Method: 将任务形式化为马尔可夫决策过程，并沿该形式化描述的结构系统地分析了部分可观测性、动作空间设计、奖励设计以及仿真到现实差距等挑战，基于真实工业热能网络的部署案例进行验证。

Result: 强化学习在真实部署中能够实现运行稳定性，但与仿真环境相比存在显著的性能差距。

Conclusion: 真实工业环境中强化学习部署面临诸多挑战，仿真与现实的性能差距是主要瓶颈，未来需针对这些差距开展进一步研究。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has shown promising results for optimizing the control of industrial energy systems, yet most existing studies remain limited to the application in simulation environments. We investigate the challenges of deploying reinforcement learning in a real-world industrial energy system, considering a thermal heating network as a use case. We formulate the task as a Markov Decision Process and systematically analyze the associated challenges along the structure of the formal description, including partial observability, action space design, reward design, and the simulation-to-reality gap. The challenges are grounded in an existing real-world deployment, where reinforcement learning achieves operational stability but shows a significant performance gap compared to simulation.

</details>


### [201] [Best-Arm Identification-Based Trust Region Selection for Bayesian Optimization on Multimodal Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31050)
*Nobuo Namura,Sho Takemori*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种轨迹感知的贝叶斯优化框架，结合最佳臂识别与信任区域方法，通过预测局部优化器的最终性能并逐步淘汰次优候选，加速多模态问题的全局优化收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准贝叶斯优化在处理复杂多模态或高维问题时性能下降，信任区域方法虽能缓解但区域选择效率有待提升，现有研究将区域选择形式化为多臂老虎机问题但缺乏理论收敛保障。

Method: 在信任区域贝叶斯优化中引入最佳臂识别机制，外推多个局部初始化优化器的优化轨迹以预测其最终性能，利用BAI逐步淘汰次优候选。

Result: 在温和假设下理论上证明了所提方法比传统贝叶斯优化更快收敛到全局最优，合成和真实基准实验验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 轨迹感知的BAI引导信任区域贝叶斯优化能有效加速多模态优化问题的全局收敛。

Abstract: Gaussian process-based Bayesian optimization (BO) is a popular approach for expensive black-box optimization, but its performance often degrades on complex multimodal or high-dimensional problems. Trust region-based BO mitigates this issue by focusing on local regions, and recent studies suggest that selecting an effective region can be formulated as a multi-armed bandit problem. We propose a trajectory-aware framework that integrates best-arm identification (BAI) with trust region-based BO to efficiently solve multimodal optimization problems. Our method extrapolates the optimization trajectories of multiple locally initialized optimizers to predict their final performance and progressively eliminates suboptimal candidates via BAI. We theoretically show that the proposed BAI-guided BO converges faster to the global optimum than conventional BO under mild assumptions, and demonstrate its effectiveness through extensive experiments on synthetic and real-world benchmarks.

</details>


### [202] [Learning to Bid in FCR Markets: A Best-of-Both-Worlds Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31070)
*Marius Potfer,Cheng Wan,Pierre Gruet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对欧洲频率包含储备（FCR）市场的投标问题，将多国FCR出清问题重新表述为针对内生对手投标向量的重复多单位统一价格拍卖，进而转化为在线学习问题，并采用一种结合组合半强盗算法的"两全其美"方法。该投标者在随机环境下实现对数伪遗憾，在对抗环境下实现O(√T)遗憾。合成实验验证了理论缩放，对历史欧洲FCR数据的回测显示了竞争性表现：该方法在稳定产品上表现尤佳，而EXP3基线在强非平稳性下更安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧洲FCR市场中，灵活性提供者面临竞争对手报价隐藏、仅能观察到清算价格和成交数量等部分反馈信息的挑战，使得投标决策困难。

Method: 将多国FCR出清问题重新表述为针对内生对手投标向量的重复多单位统一价格拍卖，转化为在线学习问题，并采用Best-of-Both-Worlds组合半强盗算法，仅利用标准市场反馈（清算价格和成交数量）即可实现学习。

Result: 在随机环境下实现对数伪遗憾，在对抗环境下实现O(√T)遗憾；合成实验验证了理论缩放；对历史欧洲FCR数据的回测显示了竞争性能，在稳定产品上表现尤佳。

Conclusion: 基于学习的FCR市场投标在理论上是有依据的，实践中也是有用的，但需要学习规则与产品层面的市场稳定性相匹配。

Abstract: Bidding in the European Frequency Containment Reserve (FCR) market is challenging for flexibility providers because competing offers are hidden and bidders observe only partial feedback form the market, such as, clearing price and awarded quantity. For a participant active in a single country, we show that the multi-country FCR clearing problem can be recast as a repeated multi-unit uniform-price auction against an endogenous vector of opposing bids. This reformulation yields an online learning problem and allows us to adapt a Best-of-Both-Worlds combinatorial semi-bandit algorithm implementable from this standard market feedback. The resulting bidder achieves logarithmic pseudo-regret in stochastic environments and $\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{T})$ regret in adversarial ones. Synthetic experiments confirm the expected scaling, and backtests on historical European FCR data show competitive performance in practice: the method performs especially well on stable products, while EXP3-type baselines can be safer under stronger non-stationarity. Overall, the results show that learning-based bidding in FCR markets is theoretically grounded and practically useful when the learning rule matches product-level market stability.

</details>


### [203] [Riemannian Diffusion Models on General Manifolds via Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31106)
*Gyeonghoon Ko,Juho Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出利用物理信息神经网络（PINN）直接求解流形热方程来近似热核，从而将黎曼扩散模型推广到任意显式指定的流形上，实现了前向加噪和条件分数评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黎曼扩散模型需要从流形热核中采样并对其求导，但除少数高度对称流形外，热核几乎不存在闭式解，限制了模型的通用性。

Method: 给定显式流形后，选择坐标系并推导对应的热（Fokker-Planck）方程及短时渐近近似，用PINN训练学习对数热核，从而替代闭式热核进行前向加噪和去噪分数匹配中的条件分数评估。

Result: 在S²、SO(3)、SPD(n)以及置换商点云等多种流形上验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过PINN近似热核，可以将基于分数的生成建模推广到更一般的黎曼流形上，突破了闭式热核可用的局限。

Abstract: Riemannian diffusion models generalize score-based generative modeling to manifold-supported data via stochastic diffusion equations on the manifold. However, training requires sampling from and differentiating the manifold heat kernel, which is rarely available in closed form beyond a few highly symmetric manifolds. We propose a general approach that approximates the heat kernel by directly solving the manifold heat equation with a physics-informed neural network (PINN). Given an explicit manifold specification, we choose a coordinate system, derive the corresponding heat (Fokker--Planck) equation and a short-time asymptotic approximation, and then train a PINN to learn the log heat kernel. The resulting surrogate enables both forward noising (heat-kernel sampling) and conditional-score evaluation for denoising score matching. We demonstrate the method on diverse manifolds including $S^2$, $SO(3)$, $\mathrm{SPD}(n)$, and permutation-quotiented point clouds.

</details>


### [204] [Subspace-Decomposed JEPAs: Disentangling Progression and Content in Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31111)
*Lucas Thil,Jesse Read,Rim Kaddah,Guillaume Doquet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SD-JEPA，将JEPA潜变量分解为正交的两个子空间：低维进度子空间（余弦间隔三元组损失）和高维内容子空间（SIGReg正则化）。理论上证明两个防坍塌力作用于不相交坐标，可加性组合而非竞争。在多个控制基准上超越LeWM基线，1D角进度坐标可作为场景感知指南针，在惊喜检测上优于预测误差标量（AUROC提升+0.18），进度子空间解释72-95%任务进度方差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准JEPA学习紧凑潜世界模型，但潜变量中没有指定任何坐标专门编码任务进度信息，导致难以直接感知任务进展状态。

Method: 将JEPA潜变量分解为两个正交子空间：低维进度子空间（余弦间隔三元组损失约束）和高维内容子空间（SIGReg目标正则化）。证明两个防坍塌力作用于不相交坐标，加性组合。

Result: 在多数控制基准上超越LeWM基线，在Push-T上超越最强非LeWM JEPA基线。消融实验证实子空间分裂是关键成分。惊喜检测AUROC提升+0.18，97.5%胜率。进度子空间（占潜变量4.2%）解释72-95%任务进度方差。

Conclusion: SD-JEPA通过解耦进度与内容子空间，不仅提升了控制任务性能，还获得了场景感知的进度指南针，能分离"惊喜时刻"与"含义"，优于传统预测误差标量。

Abstract: Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) learn compact latent world models by predicting future embeddings, but no single coordinate of the latent is designated to encode task progression. We carve the JEPA latent into two orthogonal subspaces with disjoint roles: a low-dimensional progression subspace shaped by a cosine-margin triplet loss, and a high-dimensional content subspace regularised by the existing SIGReg objective of LeWM. We prove that the two anti-collapse forces act on disjoint coordinates, so they compose additively rather than competing on the same dimensions. Our method, SD-JEPA improves over the LeWM baseline on the majority of its control benchmarks at matched compute, and outperforms the strongest non-LeWM JEPA baseline on Push-T; a subspace-ablation falsifier confirms the split is the load-bearing ingredient. Beyond planning, the resulting 1-D angular progression coordinate functions as a scene-aware compass on the latent. It advances with task progress, regresses when the agent backtracks, and under controlled perturbations both spikes and relocalises to a semantically appropriate new task-phase sector, separating the moment of surprise from its meaning in a way that prediction-error scalars cannot. Three quantitative tests back this up: $|Δθ_t|$ outperforms the standard latent-prediction-error surprise at localising semantic events on 40 held-out cube episodes by up to +0.18 pooled AUROC (97.5% per-episode win rate at $\pm 1$-step tolerance); a within-episode linear probe across all four environments (40 episodes per env) shows the 8-dimensional progression subspace (4.2% of the latent) explains 72-95% of task-progress variance..

</details>


### [205] [Scalable Bayesian Inference for Nonlinear Conservation Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31127)
*Tim Weiland,Philipp Hennig*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于高斯过程先验的数值守恒方法，用于非线性守恒律的不确定性量化，在前向模拟中继承经典求解器精度并提供结构化不确定性，在逆问题中能在数秒内恢复非参数源场的后验分布，优于需要数分钟且精度更低的神经网络基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性守恒律在科学与工程中至关重要，但实际应用中常受稀疏或含噪测量等不确定性因素影响，使得推理物理量成为不适定问题，传统数值方法和深度学习方法都难以妥善处理。

Method: 将经典数值方法框架为高斯过程先验下的贝叶斯推断，结合稀疏近似技术扩展到大规模正问题和逆问题，开发了一种数值守恒的不确定性感知模拟方法。

Result: 前向模拟中继承经典求解器精度并提供结构化不确定性量化；逆问题中数秒内恢复非参数源场后验分布，远优于需要数分钟且精度更低的神经网络基线方法。

Conclusion: 该方法有效结合了经典数值方法的精确性与贝叶斯推断的不确定性量化能力，为非线性守恒律的大规模正问题和逆问题提供了高效、准确的解决方案。

Abstract: Nonlinear conservation laws are at the heart of many of the most important dynamical systems in science and engineering. In practical applications, such systems are often subject to various sources of uncertainty, e.g. due to sparse or noisy measurements. Inferring physical quantities and fields of interest then becomes an ill-posed problem which both classical numerical methods and modern deep learning-based methods struggle to treat appropriately. Recent work has framed classical numerical methods as Bayesian inference under Gaussian process priors, resulting in a physics-aware treatment of uncertainties. Following this line of work, we develop a novel numerically conservative method for uncertainty-aware simulations of nonlinear conservation laws. We use recent sparse approximation techniques to scale up to large-scale forward and inverse problems. For forward simulation, we inherit the accuracy of classical solvers while providing structured uncertainty quantification. On inverse problems, we recover posteriors over nonparametric source fields in seconds -- outperforming neural baselines that take minutes to produce a less accurate point estimate.

</details>


### [206] [Generalizing Multi-Scale Time-Series Modeling with a Single Operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31129)
*Cheonwoo Lee,Dooho Lee,Doyun Choi,Jaemin Yoo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SiGMA（单一广义多尺度架构），通过可学习离散高斯（LDG）核实现距离感知缩放，解决了现有时间序列多尺度建模依赖固定离散缩放的局限性，在长短期预测任务上均达到最优性能，同时训练速度提升最高5.3倍，内存消耗降低最高3.8倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列多尺度建模方法缺乏理论基础，且存在依赖固定离散缩放的局限性，无法实现灵活的距离感知缩放。

Method: 将现有缩放方法统一为缩放算子族，提出SiGMA架构，基于尺度空间理论设计可学习离散高斯（LDG）核，实现距离感知的多尺度建模。

Result: 在长短期预测基准上全面超越最先进的多尺度基线；长期预测16个设置中13个取得最佳；训练速度提升最高5.3倍，内存消耗降低最高3.8倍。

Conclusion: SiGMA通过理论驱动的可学习多尺度设计，在预测精度和计算效率上均显著优于现有方法，为时间序列多尺度建模建立了原则性基础。

Abstract: Multi-scale modeling has emerged as an effective design principle for time-series forecasting by capturing temporal dynamics at multiple resolutions. As no principled foundation has been established in the literature, we unify existing scaling methods into a scaling operator family, revealing a fundamental limitation of existing approaches: reliance on fixed and discrete scaling. To address this limitation, we propose SiGMA (Single Generalized Multi-scale Architecture), which enables distance-aware scaling via the learnable discrete Gaussian (LDG) kernel grounded in scale-space theory. We evaluate SiGMA comprehensively on long- and short-term forecasting benchmarks against state-of-the-art multi-scale baselines. SiGMA outperforms all competitors on both tasks, especially achieving the best performance in 13 out of 16 long-term evaluation settings. Beyond accuracy, SiGMA significantly improves training speed by up to 5.3 times and reduces memory consumption by up to 3.8 times over the strongest competitors. Code is available at https://github.com/cheonwoolee/SiGMA.

</details>


### [207] [Learning Hyperspherical Time-Frequency Representations for Time-Series Out-of-Distribution Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31155)
*Willian T. Lunardi,Samridha Shrestha,Martin Andreoni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于超球面嵌入的时序列异常检测方法HypertF，使用von Mises-Fisher分布建模类别条件结构，结合时域和频域编码器学习联合嵌入空间，并在UCR/UEA数据集上取得优于对比学习和事后基线方法的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时序列数据的分布外检测相比视觉和语言领域探索不足，缺乏关于如何利用监督式时序列表示进行可靠分布偏移检测的原则性理解。

Method: 将时序列OOD检测形式化为超球面嵌入上的表示学习，通过基于von Mises-Fisher似然的目标函数在单位球面上诱导类别条件结构；使用时域和频域编码器分别处理输入信号，集成到联合嵌入空间；检测阶段使用基于距离的评分（k-NN和马氏距离）。

Result: 在完整的UCR和UEA时序列存档上进行跨数据集评估，结果表明在k-NN和马氏距离评分下均一致优于强对比学习和事后基线方法。

Conclusion: 基于超球面嵌入和vMF似然目标的方法能有效利用监督式时序列表示进行OOD检测，时域-频域联合嵌入有助于提升检测性能。

Abstract: Out-of-distribution (OOD) detection for time-series data remains comparatively underexplored compared to vision and language, with a limited principled understanding of how supervised time-series representations can be leveraged for reliable detection under distributional shifts. This work formulates time-series OOD detection as representation learning with hyperspherical embeddings, where class-conditional structure is induced by a von Mises-Fisher (vMF) likelihood-based objective on the unit sphere. The learned representation combines time- and frequency-domain views of the input signal via domain-specific encoders, integrating them into a joint embedding space for OOD detection. Detection uses distance-based scores over the learned embeddings, including k-nearest neighbors (k-NN) and Mahalanobis scores. We evaluate the approach at scale on the complete UCR and UEA time-series archives under a cross-dataset protocol. Empirical results show consistent improvements under both k-NN and Mahalanobis scoring over strong contrastive learning and post-hoc baselines in the same setting. Code is available at https://github.com/tiiuae/hypertf-time-series-ood.

</details>


### [208] [TabCausal: Pretraining Across Causal Environments for Tabular Causal Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31156)
*Zi-Rong Li,Si-Yang Liu,Tian-Zuo Wang,Han-Jia Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TabCausal，一种因果发现基础模型，通过动态任务构造策略在多样化图先验、结构机制、噪声模型等环境下进行广泛因果预训练，从而在单次前向传播中实现数据集到因果图的映射。在合成基准和语义因果环境基准上，TabCausal优于多种基线方法，尤其在干预数据下表现突出，表明广泛因果预训练是可迁移的摊销式因果发现的关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果发现基础模型（CDFMs）性能有限，常无法匹配经典方法，其关键在于因果预训练任务的构造方式存在瓶颈。作者希望构建一种更有效的预训练策略，使模型能够从观测数据和混合干预数据中学习可迁移的结构知识。

Method: 提出TabCausal，采用数据驱动的CDFM框架，在多样化的图先验、结构机制、噪声模型、维度、样本量和干预机制下进行广泛因果预训练。动态任务构造策略将上述因果环境组合为多样的发现任务，实现从观测数据和混合干预数据中学习可迁移的结构模式。此外，引入协议引导+LLM审计的语义因果环境基准，生成领域可解释的SCM数据用于分布外分析。

Result: 在大规模合成基准上，TabCausal的宏平均性能优于多种因果发现基线方法。在合成和语义环境基准中，TabCausal均表现出稳健的结构恢复能力，尤其在干预证据下表现突出。

Conclusion: 广泛因果预训练是可迁移的摊销式因果发现的关键组成部分，TabCausal通过动态任务构造有效弥合了抽象合成生成器与现实因果推理场景之间的差距。

Abstract: Causal discovery aims to recover directed causal relations from observational and interventional data, providing a basis for mechanistic understanding and reliable decision-making. Causal discovery foundation models (CDFMs) seek to amortize this problem by mapping a dataset directly to a causal graph in a single forward pass, avoiding per-dataset testing, search, or optimization. However, existing CDFMs remain limited, often failing to consistently match strong classical methods, and we find that a key bottleneck is how causal pretraining tasks are constructed. Based on this observation, we propose TabCausal, a data-driven CDFM trained with broad causal pretraining over diverse graph priors, structural mechanisms, noise models, dimensions, sample sizes, and intervention regimes. A dynamic task construction strategy composes these causal environments into varied discovery tasks, enabling more transferable structural learning from observational and mixed-interventional data. On large-scale synthetic benchmarks, TabCausal achieves better macro-averaged performance than a diverse set of causal discovery baselines. To further bridge abstract synthetic generators and realistic causal reasoning scenarios, we introduce a protocol-guided and LLM-audited semantic causal environment benchmark, where domain-grounded SCMs generate interpretable observational and interventional datasets for out-of-distribution analysis. Across both synthetic and semantic environments, TabCausal demonstrates robust structure recovery, especially under interventional evidence, highlighting broad causal pretraining as a key ingredient for transferable amortized causal discovery.

</details>


### [209] [Retriever Portfolios: A Principled Approach to Adaptive RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31176)
*Miltiadis Stouras,Vincent Cohen-Addad,Silvio Lattanzi,Ola Svensson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种自动选择小型多样检索器组合（portfolio）的方法，以覆盖异构查询分布，并通过期望best-of-k目标实现高效构建，在多个QA基准上优于单检索器和朴素多检索器基线，同时降低延迟和token成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统通常使用单一检索器和固定超参数，无法有效处理从简单事实性问题到复杂多跳推理的高度异构查询，缺乏针对查询分布自适应的检索策略。

Method: 从候选池中自动选择小型多样的检索器组合（portfolio），以覆盖目标查询分布的不同区域；通过期望best-of-k目标形式化该问题，并设计具有近优保证的高效构建算法；搭配路由器流水线进行查询分配。

Result: 在多个QA基准上，学习的组合和路由器流水线在检索指标和答案质量上均优于单检索器和朴素多检索器基线；与推理时超参数调优方法相比，固定组合实现了相当或更好的精度，同时显著降低延迟和token成本。

Conclusion: 通过自动选择检索器组合可以有效适应异构查询分布，在提升性能的同时保持推理效率，为RAG系统的实际部署提供了一种实用方案。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems typically rely on a single retriever and a single set of hyperparameters, despite facing highly heterogeneous queries that range from simple factoid questions to complex multi-hop reasoning. We propose a method that automatically selects a small, diverse subset of retrievers (a portfolio) from a large pool of candidates, to cover different regions of the target query distribution. We formalize this setting via an expected best-of-$k$ objective over the query distribution and show that it admits an efficient portfolio construction algorithm with near-optimal guarantees. Across multiple QA benchmarks, our learned portfolios and router pipeline consistently outperform single-retriever and naive multi-retriever baselines on both retrieval metrics and answer quality. In addition, compared to inference-time hyperparameter tuning approaches, fixed portfolios enable parallel retrieval and LLM calls, achieving comparable (and sometimes better) accuracy with substantially lower latency and token cost.

</details>


### [210] [How well does Classification Accuracy capture Concept Drift Detection Quality? An overview of Concept Drift Detection evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31186)
*Joanna Komorniczak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了合成非平稳数据流中概念漂移检测质量指标与分类性能之间的关系，分析了八种漂移检测质量指标在七种数据流生成工具下的表现，并考虑漂移动态因素，旨在为漂移检测评估建立统一框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前学术界缺乏统一的评估框架来衡量概念漂移检测质量，现有研究多依赖分类质量指标，但这些指标受多种因素干扰，无法可靠反映漂移检测的真实效果。

Method: 对八种漂移检测质量指标与分类器性能之间的关系进行深入分析，使用七种合成非平稳数据流生成工具生成数据，同时将漂移动态纳入考量因素进行系统研究。

Result: 识别出最具信息量的漂移检测质量指标集合，并提供了对评估方法的深入理解。

Conclusion: 为概念漂移检测评估提供了更可靠的指标选择依据，有助于建立统一的评估框架。

Abstract: Data streams are nowadays among the most frequently analyzed data structures, with the concept drift posing a major challenge encountered by processing systems. Despite the proposition of numerous solutions to counteract the accuracy degeneration due to concept drift, the scientific community has not yet established a unified framework for evaluating the concept drift detection task. Existing research often relies on classification quality metrics, but these can be affected by multiple factors and may not reliably reflect drift detection quality. In this work, we present an in-depth overview of the relationship between metrics for quantifying drift detection quality and classification performance in synthetic nonstationary data streams. The proposed research studies eight drift detection quality metrics in relation to the classifier's performance across seven synthetic data stream generation tools, additionally considering drift dynamics as a factor. The studies aim to identify the most informative set of drift detection quality metrics and provide a deep understanding of the method's evaluation.

</details>


### [211] [FlagGAM: Rule-Based Generalized Additive Modeling for Explainable Tabular Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31189)
*Zijie Zhao,Roy E. Welsch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlagGAM提出了一种基于规则定义基函数的框架，将特征级别的规则构建与预测分离，通过稀疏、可读的单变量基函数（包括阈值标志、类别标志、尾偏差基和分类阶梯函数）进行表示，在保持可解释性的同时提升了表格数据预测的准确性和对缺失/噪声输入的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高风险领域的表格预测需要模型兼具准确性、透明性和对不完美输入的鲁棒性，现有方法难以同时满足这些要求。

Method: 提出FlagGAM框架，包含Flag Core Module（将数值和类别变量转换为稀疏、可读的单变量基函数）和默认的加性预测头（类GAM），并支持灵活的预测头扩展，保留稀疏规则基矩阵而非压缩为计数统计量。

Result: 默认FlagGAM在透明加性模式下接近EBM；混合类型回归上显著优于岭回归；在缺失和噪声扰动下AUROC下降小于常见基线；灵活预测头进一步提升准确率，接近强树基线模型。

Conclusion: FlagGAM为需要竞争性准确率、可沟通规则和对不完美输入鲁棒性的表格预测场景提供了一个实用的折中方案。

Abstract: Tabular prediction in high-stakes domains requires models that are accurate, transparent, and robust to imperfect inputs. We propose FlagGAM, a rule-defined basis framework that separates feature-level rule construction from prediction. A Flag Core Module converts numerical and categorical variables into sparse, human-readable univariate bases, including threshold flags, category-level flags, tail-deviation bases, and categorical step functions; a default additive head then combines these bases as a restricted GAM-style predictor. Rather than reducing triggered rules to compact count summaries, FlagGAM retains a sparse rule-basis matrix that supports mixed-type classification and regression, feature-specific weighting, and optional flexible prediction heads. Across tabular benchmarks, default FlagGAM remains close to EBM in transparent additive mode, improves substantially over ridge regression on mixed-type regression, and shows smaller AUROC degradation than common baselines under missing and noisy perturbations. Flexible heads further improve accuracy and approach strong tree-based baselines, with the caveat that the resulting model should be interpreted as a rule-basis representation followed by a nonlinear predictor rather than as a fully additive GAM. Overall, FlagGAM provides a practical middle ground for tabular settings that require competitive accuracy, communicable rules, and robustness to imperfect inputs.

</details>


### [212] [Student Capacity Moderates Knowledge Distillation Effectiveness: A Systematic Study Across ResNet Teacher-Student Pairs on CIFAR-10](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31191)
*Umut Onur Yasar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了ResNet图像分类中师生容量关系对知识蒸馏效果的影响，发现学生容量是关键调节因素、实现正确性对特征蒸馏至关重要、以及输入分辨率感知架构是有效蒸馏的前提条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究师生模型之间的容量关系如何调节知识蒸馏的有效性，澄清以往研究中因实现错误和架构问题导致的混淆结论。

Method: 在CIFAR-10上使用三组师生对（R50->R18、R34->R18、R50->R34），比较Logit-KD和Feature-KD两种蒸馏方法，在受控可复现条件下（3个随机种子，报告均值±标准差）进行实验。

Result: (1) R34学生从KD中获益显著高于R18学生，即使师生准确率差距相当；(2) 修正梯度裁剪bug后，Feature-KD在两个师生对中匹配或优于Logit-KD；(3) 修正32x32输入的ResNet主干使教师准确率提升超过5pp，远大于任何KD增益。

Conclusion: 学生容量是知识蒸馏效果的关键调节因素；实现正确性和架构适配对蒸馏方法比较和有效性至关重要。

Abstract: We investigate how teacher-student capacity relationships modulate knowledge distillation (KD) effectiveness in ResNet-based image classification on CIFAR-10. Across three teacher-student pairs -- R50->R18, R34->R18, and R50->R34 -- we compare Logit-KD and Feature-KD under controlled, reproducible conditions (3 seeds, mean+/-std reported throughout). We report three main findings. First, student capacity is a key moderating factor in distillation gain: R34 students benefit substantially more from KD than R18 students even when teacher-student accuracy gaps are comparable, with the strongest gain of +0.30pp observed for R50->R34 Feature-KD versus +0.18pp for R34->R18 Feature-KD and +0.00pp for R34->R18 Logit-KD. Second, implementation correctness critically affects Feature-KD: a gradient clipping bug that excluded projection layers suppressed Feature-KD performance and produced misleading comparisons with Logit-KD. After correction, Feature-KD matches or outperforms Logit-KD in two of three pairs, reaching 95.55% on R50->R34 against a baseline of 95.25%. Third, input-resolution-aware architecture is a prerequisite for effective distillation: correcting the ResNet stem for 32x32 inputs raises teacher accuracy by over 5pp -- an order of magnitude larger than any KD gain. All code and results are available at github.com/umutonuryasar/kd-capacity-gap.

</details>


### [213] [Geometry-based Schrödinger Bridges for Trustworthy Multimodal Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31193)
*Jiayu Xiong,Jing Wang,Qi Zhang,Wanlong Wang,Jun Xue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于几何的多模态融合方法（GMF），通过扩散薛定谔桥和修正流计算隐空间中输入所需的传输修正量（即初始速度平方范数），作为独立于模型预测的数据质量评估信号，从而打破传统置信度方法的循环依赖，显著提升对传感器噪声、不完整数据和语义冲突的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可信融合方法依赖模型自身的预测置信度来判断数据质量，但当模型自信但错误时，这些方法无法检测到错误，存在循环依赖问题。

Method: 提出GMF方法，通过扩散薛定谔桥结合修正流计算输入数据在隐空间中所需的传输修正量，以初始速度的平方范数作为可靠性评分，低值表示有效数据，高值表示噪声/不完整/冲突数据。

Result: 在严重传感器噪声和语义冲突场景下，GMF相比基于置信度的基线方法显著提升了鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 基于几何的可靠性信号可作为独立于预测的评判标准，有效识别不可靠输入，打破循环依赖，提升多模态融合系统的可信度。

Abstract: Real-world multimodal systems must be robust against low-quality data, such as sensor noise, incomplete multimodal data and conflicting inputs. However, existing trustworthy fusion methods rely on the model's own prediction confidence to judge data quality. This creates a circular dependency: when a model is confident but wrong, these methods fail to detect the error. To break this loop, we propose Geometry-based Multimodal Fusion (GMF). Instead of relying on predictions, we evaluate reliability by measuring how much transport correction the input needs in latent space. We implement Diffusion Schrödinger Bridge transport with Rectified Flow, where the squared initial velocity gives an efficient learned correction score. Valid data has low squared velocity magnitude, while noisy, incomplete data or conflicting data requires stronger transport correction. This geometry-based reliability signal acts as an independent judge, effectively flagging unreliable inputs even when the classifier is fooled. Extensive experiments demonstrate that GMF significantly improves robustness against severe sensor noise and semantic conflicts compared to confidence-based baselines.

</details>


### [214] [Fixed-Point Masked Generative Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31215)
*Andrea Miele,Yiming Qin,Alba Carballo-Castro,Justin Deschenaux,Pascal Frossard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CoFRe框架，通过将掩码生成模型中的部分去噪器替换为固定点求解器、引入跨步一致性损失和三状态重用的热身策略，实现了自适应深度、更少参数和更低训练成本，同时在低采样预算下提升了生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有掩码生成模型每一步都需要完整的双向Transformer前向传播，训练成本高且在低采样预算下质量下降；已有的改进方案仍为每个细化步骤分配固定的去噪计算量，缺乏自适应的计算分配机制。

Method: 1) 将部分去噪器替换为共享注意力层上的固定点求解器，实现自适应深度和更少参数；2) 引入跨步一致性损失，对齐相邻去噪步骤的隐藏表示；3) 提出三状态重用(3SR)，对未改变、仍掩码和新揭示的令牌分别处理，利用前一步解对求解器进行热启动。这三个组件构成了完整的训练到推理框架CoFRe。

Result: 在OpenWebText上，相比MDLM，参数量减少38.8%，训练时间减少11.5%，VRAM减少16.9%，生成困惑度从830.8降至101.8（在96个Transformer块前向传播预算下）；在ImageNette上，训练时间减少48.6%，VRAM减少50.7%，所有采样预算下的FID均有提升。此外，预训练MGMs可通过短时间微调转换为FP-MGMs，无需完整重训练。

Conclusion: CoFRe提供了一种实用的框架，实现了更经济的训练和更强的低预算掩码生成能力，在质量和计算成本之间取得了更好的权衡。

Abstract: Masked Generative Models (MGMs) enable parallel decoding and achieve strong performance across modalities, but require full-sequence bidirectional transformers at every step, making training costly and degrading quality under low sampling budgets. Existing work improves efficiency via better samplers or cheaper fixed-depth denoisers, but they still allocate a fixed amount of denoiser computation to each refinement step. We introduce Fixed-Point Masked Generative Models (FP-MGMs), which replace part of the denoiser with a fixed-point solver over shared attention layers to enable adaptive depth with fewer parameters. To make it more effective for masked generation, we first introduce a cross-step consistency loss, which aligns hidden representations at neighboring denoising steps and, second, three-state reuse (3SR) which warm-starts the solver using the previous solution by treating differently unchanged, still-masked, and newly revealed tokens respectively. Together, these components define our complete training-to-inference framework for fixed-point masked generation, \emph{CoFRe}. We also show that pre-trained MGMs can be converted into FP-MGMs with short fine-tuning, avoiding full retraining. Across modalities, CoFRe improves the quality and cost trade-off. On OpenWebText, CoFRe reduces parameters by 38.8\%, training time by 11.5\%, and VRAM by 16.9\%, while improving generative perplexity from 830.8 to 101.8 at a budget of $96$ transformer-block forward passes, compared to MDLM. In ImageNette, CoFRe reduces training time by 48.6\% and VRAM by 50.7\%, while improving FID in all sample budgets tested. Overall, CoFRe offers a practical framework for cheaper training and stronger low-budget masked generation.

</details>


### [215] [Multivariate Distributional Reinforcement Learning Using Sliced Divergences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31222)
*Baptiste Debes,Tinne Tuytelaars*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出切片分布强化学习（SDRL），通过将高维回报分布投影到一维，利用可计算的一维散度进行多变量分布强化学习，并证明了在标量折扣和一般矩阵折扣下的贝尔曼压缩性，在玩具问题、Gridworld和部分Atari游戏上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布强化学习在单变量场景取得显著成功，但扩展到多变量（多目标/多回报）时，常用度量难以自然推广或丧失计算可行性，且矩阵折扣场景下缺乏压缩性保证。

Method: SDRL：将多变量回报分布通过随机或最大切片投影到一维，利用Wasserstein/Cramér/MMD等一维散度计算距离；分别针对标量折扣（均匀切片）和密集矩阵折扣（最大切片）证明了贝尔曼压缩性，并分析了不同散度对单样本贝尔曼更新的适用性。

Result: 在玩具链问题、基于图像的Gridworld环境以及部分Atari游戏上进行了实验评估，验证了SDRL方法的可行性与效果。

Conclusion: SDRL为多变量分布强化学习提供了一种通用且理论完备的框架，通过切片技术将一维工具推广到高维，并首次在密集矩阵折扣下建立了压缩性结果。

Abstract: Distributional reinforcement learning (DRL) models the full return distribution rather than expectations, but extending it to multivariate settings remains challenging. Many common metrics do not naturally generalize beyond one dimension or lose computational tractability, and the multivariate case introduces additional difficulties such as general matrix discounting, for which no contraction results are available. We introduce Sliced Distributional Reinforcement Learning (SDRL), which lifts tractable one-dimensional divergences to multivariate return distributions via projections. We prove Bellman contraction for uniform slicing under shared scalar discounting, and introduce a maximum-slicing variant with contraction under general dense discount matrices. SDRL supports a broad class of base divergences; we analyze Wasserstein, Cramér, and Maximum Mean Discrepancy (MMD), and characterize which SDRL variants suit the standard single-sample Bellman update used in distributional RL. We evaluate SDRL on a toy chain problem and a gridworld image-based environment as well as a subset of Atari games.

</details>


### [216] [Bifurcated Remaining Useful Life Prediction: A Hybrid Approach for Realistic Uncertainty Characterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31241)
*Xabier Belaunzaran,Antonio Nappa,Arkaitz Artetxe,Basilio Sierra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种混合 prognostic 框架，通过区分健康/退化阶段、LSTM自编码器状态分类、条件Weibull生存分析（健康期）和概率神经网络+MC Dropout（退化期），实现涡扇发动机RUL的物理一致性不确定性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RUL预测方法通常缺乏对不确定性的量化，且未区分发动机不同运行阶段（健康期vs退化期）的不同预测需求，导致早期预测方差大、末期置信度低的问题。

Method: 1) LSTM自编码器在名义数据(RUL>150)上训练，通过重构误差作为状态分类器；2) 健康期：条件Weibull生存分析估计平均剩余寿命；3) 退化期：概率神经网络+MC Dropout捕获偶然和认知不确定性；4) 校准sigmoid函数将自编码器输出转换为连续状态概率，动态加权集成预测。

Result: 生成的置信区间具有物理一致性：生命周期末期高置信度（窄区间），早期运行阶段准确反映固有方差（宽区间）。

Conclusion: 该框架为风险感知维护提供了鲁棒工具，优势在于生成物理一致的不确定性带，能够支持风险知情维护决策。

Abstract: This study presents a novel hybrid prognostic framework for uncertainty-aware Remaining Useful Life (RUL) estimation in turbofan engines using the NASA C-MAPSS dataset. The framework employs a state-aware strategy that bifurcates the engines operational lifespan into "healthy" and "degraded" regimes. An LSTM-based autoencoder, trained strictly on nominal data (RUL > 150 cycles), monitors reconstruction error to act as a robust state classifier. For the healthy regime, a Conditional Weibull Survival Analysis is used for Mean Residual Life estimation. For the degraded regime, a Probabilistic Neural Network with Monte Carlo Dropout captures both aleatoric and epistemic uncertainties. Rather than using rigid binary labels, a calibrated sigmoid function converts the autoencoders output into continuous state probabilities, dynamically weighting the final ensemble prediction. The primary strength of this framework is its generation of physically consistent uncertainty bands, yielding high-confidence predictions near end-of-life while accurately reflecting the inherent variance of early operation, providing a robust tool for risk-informed maintenance.

</details>


### [217] [Spectral Reach: Understanding Neural Scaling as Progress into the Spectral Tail](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31244)
*Konstantin Nikolaou,Jonas Scheunemann,Sven Krippendorf,Samuel Tovey,Christian Holm*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"谱位置"这一可扩展度量，用于分析神经切线核特征值在训练中的变化，发现大模型具有更强的"谱到达"能力，能够持续学习弱谱信号，而特征学习是关键使能因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经标度律虽指导大模型发展，但其背后的机制尚不清晰，缺乏可扩展的分析工具。

Method: 引入"谱位置"度量，追踪经验神经切线核（eNTK）特征值在训练中对损失降低的贡献，并通过标度实验进行分析。

Result: 训练中谱位置下降，学习从主导特征模转向谱尾；大模型能到达更深的谱尾（谱到达），从而在弱谱信号上持续学习；特征学习通过自适应放大梯度维持谱到达。

Conclusion: 谱到达解释了大模型低损失的原因，特征学习是其关键，为架构和优化器设计提供了具体干预方向。

Abstract: Neural scaling laws describe predictable power-law relationships between model size, dataset size, compute, and performance. While these laws guide the development of modern foundation models, the mechanisms underpinning them remain poorly understood, in part due to the absence of scalable analysis tools. To close this gap, we introduce "spectral position": a scalable measure of which eigenvalues of the empirical neural tangent kernel (eNTK) currently drive loss reduction. Applying this measure to scaling experiments, we find that spectral position decreases throughout training: learning shifts from dominant eigenmodes into the spectral tail. Larger models reach further into the tail than smaller models, revealing a size-dependent capacity we call "spectral reach". This suggests why larger models achieve lower losses: they sustain learning on weak spectral signals inaccessible to smaller models. We further identify feature learning as a key enabler of spectral reach. It adaptively amplifies gradient magnitudes as learning advances, sustaining progress where frozen representations stall. This points to concrete interventions through architecture and optimizer design.

</details>


### [218] [Toward Identifiable Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31245)
*Walter Nelson,Theofanis Karaletsos,Francesco Locatello*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论和实验角度证明稀疏自编码器（SAE）存在高度不稳定性问题，并提出了改进版本iSAE，通过最小化架构和训练流程的改动，在降低重构误差的同时显著提升了稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAE虽然被广泛用于解释和交互神经网络表征，但经验上存在训练不稳定的问题——不同训练轮次会产生不同的概念词典和稀疏编码，这一现象缺乏理论解释和系统解决方案。

Method: 首先从理论上证明SAE的不稳定性，然后分析导致不稳定的模型属性，针对每个问题对架构和训练流程进行最小化修改，提出两种版本的identifiable SAE (iSAE)。通过连接SAE与传统字典学习方法，并结合实际学习到的字典满足近似受限等距性质（Restricted Isometry Property）的理论分析，证明iSAE的稀疏编码具有近似可辨识性。

Result: iSAE相比标准TopK SAE在重构误差更低的同时，模型稳定性显著提升。实际学习到的字典满足近似受限等距条件，使得相应稀疏编码具备近似可辨识性。

Conclusion: 通过理论分析和工程实践的结合，iSAE有效解决了SAE的不稳定问题，为可解释神经网络表征提供了更可靠的工具。

Abstract: Recently, sparse autoencoders (SAEs) have emerged as an attractive tool for interpreting and interacting with representations in practical neural networks. While it is common empirical folklore, we also show theoretically that SAEs are highly unstable: different training runs are likely to produce different concept dictionaries and sparse codes. We characterize the model properties that hinder the stability of real-world SAEs, and address each of these problems through minimal changes to the architecture and training procedure. Together, these changes yield two versions of an \textbf{i}dentifiable SAE (iSAE), a variant of the standard TopK SAE with lower reconstruction error and improved stability. We explain this improvement theoretically by connecting SAEs with traditional dictionary learning approaches, and show that the dictionaries learned in practice satisfy an approximate restricted isometry condition, rendering the corresponding sparse codes in those models near-identifiable.

</details>


### [219] [Lightweight CNN-Based Anomaly Detection for High Voltage Converter Modulators in the Spallation Neutron Source](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31259)
*Alberto D. Cencillo,Leonardo Concepción,Julián Luengo,Isaac Triguero*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究高功率脉冲变换器故障检测，通过改变时序滤波与跨通道混合的顺序并引入自适应通道重加权，在SNS的HVCM数据集上实现了优于现有方法的性能（AUC-PR 0.816，AUC-ROC 0.934）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高功率脉冲变换器的非计划停机是大型加速器设施停机的主要来源，SNS的HVCM模组是导致束流损失的第二大因素。现有深度学习方法从第一层就纠缠时序和跨通道操作，缺乏显式表示通道独立性或结构化通道间交互的机制。

Method: 提出改变时序滤波与跨通道混合的应用顺序，并引入逐脉冲自适应通道重加权机制。通过消融实验识别三个主导输入通道，并分析各故障家族的性能与故障前兆表现形式（单通道幅度偏移 vs. 需联合通道表征的细微模式）之间的关系。

Result: 在SNS全部四个子系统（RFQ, DTL, CCL, SCL）的公开HVCM数据集上，最优变体达到池化AUC-PR 0.816和AUC-ROC 0.934，在大多数子系统和六个故障家族中的五个上优于现有技术水平。

Conclusion: 架构归纳偏置（时序滤波与跨通道混合的顺序）对这类数据的检测性能起关键作用，自适应通道重加权可进一步提升灵敏度。不同故障类型的前兆表现方式（单通道幅度变化或跨通道联合模式）直接影响模型性能。

Abstract: Unscheduled trips of high-power pulsed converters are a leading source of downtime at large accelerator facilities. At the Spallation Neutron Source (SNS), the High Voltage Converter Modulators (HVCMs) are consistently the second-largest contributor to lost beam time. Each HVCM pulse is recorded across sensor channels spanning currents, voltages, and magnetic fluxes, whose mutual interactions encode the operating state of the system. Fault precursors do not manifest uniformly across these channels: depending on fault type, they may alter the temporal structure of individual signals, change the statistical dependencies among channels, or both. Existing deep-learning approaches typically process multi-channel signals with standard convolutional pipelines that entangle temporal and cross-channel operations from the first layer, giving the model no explicit mechanism to represent channel independence or structured inter-channel interaction. We hypothesise that architectural inductive bias, specifically the ordering of temporal filtering and cross-channel mixing, plays a central role in detection performance on this class of data. To test this, we vary the order in which these two operations are applied, and examine whether per-pulse adaptive channel reweighting further improves sensitivity. Evaluated on the public HVCM dataset across all four SNS subsystems (RFQ, DTL, CCL, SCL), our best variant achieves a pooled AUC-PR of 0.816 and AUC-ROC of 0.934, outperforming the state of the art on most subsystems and five of the six fault families. Ablations identify three dominant input channels and link per-fault-family performance to whether precursors manifest as amplitude shifts in individual channels or as subtler patterns requiring joint channel representations to surface.

</details>


### [220] [Algorithmic Recourse of In-Context Learning for Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31272)
*Wenshuo Dong,Jiaming Zhang,Shaopneg Fu,Hongbin Lin,Di Wang,Lijie Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次研究上下文学习（ICL）下表格数据的算法反事实解释，提出自适应子空间反事实框架ASR-ICL，通过零阶优化高效生成可操作的稀疏反事实解释，理论证明了解的存在性与有界性，实验验证了效果与收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高 stakes 决策场景（如信贷审批）中，被影响个体需要可追溯的反事实解释，但现有算法反事实方法未覆盖基于上下文学习的表格预测模型。

Method: 理论分析证明ICL下反事实定义良好且有界，刻画了随上下文增大向经典解收敛的行为；实践上提出ASR-ICL框架，采用自适应子空间零阶优化，高效生成稀疏且可操作的反事实解释，支持多分类任务。

Result: 在多个真实数据集和模型上，ASR-ICL以更少查询达到与现有方法相当的反事实质量，并实验验证了理论预测的收敛行为。

Conclusion: ASR-ICL在ICL场景下高效生成高质量反事实解释，理论与实验共同验证了方法的有效性，填补了该方向的研究空白。

Abstract: As predictive models are increasingly deployed in high-stakes settings such as credit approval, there is a growing need for post-hoc methods that provide recourse to affected individuals. Many such models operate on tabular data, where features correspond to real-world attributes. Recently, in-context learning (ICL) has enabled large language models to perform tabular prediction by conditioning on labeled examples at inference time, without explicit training. However, algorithmic recourse for tabular decision-making under ICL remains largely unexplored. In this work, we present the first study of algorithmic recourse for tabular data under ICL. We carry out a theoretical analysis, showing that recourse remains well-defined and bounded, and we characterize how recourse converges toward classical solutions as the context size increases. In practice, we propose a novel zeroth-order recourse framework, Adaptive Subspace Recourse for In-Context Learning (ASR-ICL), that efficiently generates actionable and sparse recourse for black-box ICL models. The proposed framework naturally extends to multi-class tabular tasks. Experiments across multiple real-world datasets and models demonstrate that ASR-ICL achieves recourse quality comparable to existing methods with fewer queries and empirically confirm the predicted convergence behavior, supporting our theoretical analysis.

</details>


### [221] [Survival Reinforcement Learning: Toward Scalable Self-Supervised RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31273)
*Franki Nguimatsia-Tiofack,Fabian Schramm,Théotime Le Hellard,Justin Carpentier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出生存强化学习（SRL），一种基于在线分类的替代方法，通过最大化智能体在目标状态的停留时间来解决对比强化学习（CRL）在长视距目标条件规划中面临的一致性-容忍性困境，在机器人操作任务上匹敌SOTA，在长视距运动任务上性能提升2-8倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督对比强化学习（CRL）虽具有深度扩展能力，但在长视距目标条件规划中存在对比损失固有的一致-容忍困境（uniformity-tolerance dilemma），亟需一种能突破CRL结构限制的新方法。

Method: 提出生存强化学习（SRL），基于在线分类的框架，扩展生存价值学习框架，通过最大化智能体在目标状态（目标目标）的驻留时间（dwell time）来学习策略，规避CRL的结构约束，并缓解生存框架中固有的"bang-bang"控制问题。

Result: 在多种机器人基准测试中，扩展后的SRL在操作任务上与SOTA的CRL性能持平，在稳定长视距运动任务上性能提升2倍至8倍。

Conclusion: 分类方法可作为扩展强化学习的关键原语，为突破对比学习在RL中的局限性提供了有力证据。

Abstract: While self-supervised Contrastive Reinforcement Learning (CRL) has shown remarkable depth-scaling capabilities, successfully using networks over 64 layers, scaled CRL still struggles with long-horizon goal-conditioned planning due to the uniformity-tolerance dilemma inherent in contrastive losses. We introduce Survival Reinforcement Learning (SRL), an online classification-based alternative that extends the survival value learning framework by maximizing the agent's dwell time at target goals. SRL bypasses the structural constraints of CRL and mitigates the "bang-bang" control solutions inherent to survival frameworks, which often induce undesirable behavior in complex dynamical systems. Evaluated across diverse robotic benchmarks, scaled SRL matches state-of-the-art CRL on manipulation tasks and outperforms it by 2x to 8x on stable, long-horizon locomotion tasks. Our results provide strong additional evidence that classification-based methods may serve as a key primitive in the broader effort to scale reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [222] [Learning Parametric Nitrogen Fertilizer Response Curves Using Neuro Symbolic Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31276)
*Giorgio Morales,John Sheppard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于神经符号回归（Neuro SR）的方法，无需预定义函数形式即可从数据中学习作物对氮肥响应的参数化曲线，并在真实冬小麦数据上取得了优于传统模型的拟合效果，能够发现不同管理区域的特异性功能关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精准农业中准确建模作物对氮肥的响应是核心挑战，现有方法要么依赖预定义的参数形式，要么使用不透明的机器学习模型，限制了从数据中发现和解释位置特异性功能关系的能力。

Method: 提出神经符号回归方法，集成基于Transformer的多集符号骨架预测策略，通过构建多样化输入子集并强制执行一致性，发现跨多个子域（管理区）共享的符号骨架，再使用遗传算法拟合观测数据。

Result: 在合成一维问题上验证了方法在数据稀缺条件下恢复正确表达式的鲁棒性；在真实冬小麦数据上，发现的表达式不仅拟合误差低于二次-平台和指数函数等传统模型，还能捕捉不同空间区域的多样化功能行为。

Conclusion: 神经符号回归方法能够发现位置特异性的农学关系，为精准农业中的知情决策提供支持，展现了在数据驱动的农艺关系发现方面的潜力。

Abstract: Accurately modeling crop response to Nitrogen (N) fertilization is a fundamental challenge in precision agriculture, as it impacts both economic returns and environmental sustainability. Existing approaches either rely on predefined parametric forms or opaque machine learning models, limiting their ability to interpret or discover site-specific functional relationships from data. In this work, we propose a neuro symbolic regression (SR) approach to learn parametric N-response curves without assuming a predefined functional form. Our approach integrates a transformer-based Multi-Set Symbolic Skeleton Prediction strategy, enabling the discovery of shared functional structures across multiple subdomains or management zones (MZs). By constructing diverse input subsets and enforcing consistency across them, the method recovers robust symbolic skeletons that are subsequently fitted to observed data using a genetic algorithm. This framework was first evaluated on synthetic one-dimensional problems to assess its robustness under varying levels of epistemic uncertainty. The results demonstrate the ability of the proposed SR approach to recover correct expressions even in data-scarce regimes. In this work, we present the results of applying our method to real-world winter wheat data, learning distinct parametric N-response curves for different MZs within a field. The results show that the discovered expressions not only achieve lower fitting errors than traditional models such as quadratic-plateau and exponential functions, but also capture diverse functional behaviors across spatial regions. This demonstrates the potential that neuro SR has to enable the discovery of site-specific agronomic relationships and support informed decision-making in precision agriculture.

</details>


### [223] [Interpretability Without Tradeoffs: Disentangling Polysemanticity At Equal Predictive Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31304)
*Doğukan Bağcı,Bernt Schiele,Simone Schaub-Meyer,Jonas Fischer,Robin Hesse*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ELUDe是一种无需训练、无损、无监督的解缠方法，通过重组织层间信息流将DNN的混合表示分解为可解释的单语义子单元，在不改变模型输出的前提下显著提升可解释性，并支持表示操控等实际应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DNN的单个神经元常编码多个不相关概念（多语义性），导致模型决策过程难以解释。现有方法如稀疏自编码器虽能分离混合信号，但会改变模型结构并降低下游性能。

Method: ELUDe通过重组织神经网络层间信息流，将潜在表示分解为清晰、可检查的子单元，这些子单元行为类似可解释特征。该方法通过构造保证原始计算完全不变，无需显式训练或标签，可直接应用于预训练模型。

Result: 在DINOv2和监督ViT-B/16等多个视觉模型上，ELUDe显著提升可解释性，下游精度保持不变，运行高效，并支持模型表示操控等实际应用。

Conclusion: ELUDe实现了近乎无代价的可解释性：在不牺牲性能的前提下，提供更清晰、可扩展且可操作的模型洞察。

Abstract: Deep neural networks (DNNs) are widely used, but interpreting what they actually learn remains difficult. A major obstacle is that individual neurons often encode multiple unrelated concepts, obscuring the decision process of the network. While prior work, such as sparse autoencoders, can separate these mixed signals into more meaningful, "monosemantic" features, this typically requires altering the model in ways that can degrade downstream performance. To overcome this, we introduce ELUDe (explicit, lossless, unsupervised disentanglement), a method for improving the interpretability of DNNs while preserving their functional equivalence. ELUDe breaks latent representations into clear, inspectable sub-units that behave like interpretable features, while guaranteeing that the model's outputs remain exactly the same. It requires no explicit training, no labels, and can be applied to pretrained models. ELUDe works by reorganizing how information flows between layers, re-routing concept-specific contributions while preserving the original computation by construction. Across several vision models, including DINOv2 and supervised ViT-B/16, ELUDe improves interpretability, keeps downstream accuracy unchanged, runs efficiently, and supports practical uses such as steering model representations. In short, ELUDe offers interpretability (almost) without a tradeoff: clearer, scalable, and actionable model insights with no loss in performance.

</details>


### [224] [Graph Neural Networks Are Not Continuous Across Graph Resolutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31315)
*Christian Koke,Yuesong Shen,Abhishek Saroha,Marvin Eisenberger,Bastian Rieck,Michael Bronstein,Daniel Cremers*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了图神经网络(GNNs)在自然图收敛模式下不连续的缺陷，指出其对不同分辨率下同一对象的图会生成截然不同的潜在表示。作者追溯了这一问题源于常用信息传播方案的结构性障碍，并提出了一种原则性修改方案，使模型具备跨尺度的连续性，从而实现不同分辨率的稳定集成和可靠泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战图神经网络社区中关于GNNs在自然图收敛模式下具有连续性的传统观点，揭示GNNs在处理不同分辨率下的相似图时存在的表示不一致问题。

Method: 通过理论分析追溯GNN不连续性的根源——常用信息传播方案的结构性障碍，并在此基础上提出一种原则性的架构修改方法，赋予模型跨尺度的连续性。

Result: 验证了GNNs在自然图收敛模式下确实不连续，不同分辨率的同一对象图会产生显著不同的潜在表示。提出的修改方法能够实现不同分辨率的稳定集成和跨分辨率的可靠泛化。

Conclusion: GNNs存在因信息传播方案导致的跨尺度不连续性问题，通过原则性修改可以解决这一问题，实现模型对多尺度图数据的连续表征和泛化。

Abstract: We show that contrary to conventional wisdom in the community, graph neural networks (GNNs) are not continuous with respect to all natural modes of graph convergence. As a result, GNNs may generate substantially different latent representations for graphs that are very similar. In particular they assign vastly different latent embeddings to graphs that represent the same underlying object at different resolution scales. We trace this failure of continuity back to a structural obstruction arising from commonly used information-propagation schemes. Building on this insight we then derive a principled modification to standard GNN architectures which equips models with continuity across scales. The proposed modification enables consistent integration of distinct resolutions and reliable generalization between them. We systematically validate our theoretical findings in a wide range of numerical experiments.

</details>


### [225] [Forgetting Has Neighbors: Localized Collateral Forgetting in Machine Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31317)
*Polina Dolgova,Sebastian U. Stich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现机器遗忘中存在“局部连带遗忘”现象：未学习模型与重训练模型在预测上的逐点差异高度不均匀，靠近遗忘集的样本受影响更大。原因在于遗忘使用的替代目标与重训练局部预测结构不一致。提出“局部教师蒸馏”缓解策略，用仅在遗忘集邻居上训练的教师生成软标签替代随机目标，显著缩小与重训练模型的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器遗忘评估使用聚合指标（如准确率、遗忘分数）可能隐藏局部失败，作者希望在逐样本级别揭示未学习模型与重训练模型之间的预测差异及其非均匀分布特性。

Method: 1. 在逐样本级别比较未学习模型与重训练模型的预测差异，发现差异随与遗忘集的几何邻近度增大而增大（局部连带遗忘）。2. 分析机制：遗忘使用的替代目标（如梯度上升的随机标签）与重训练局部预测结构不一致，通过共享表征传播到邻近样本。3. 提出局部教师蒸馏（Local Teacher Distillation）：仅在遗忘集的保留邻居上训练一个小教师模型，用其软标签替代随机目标进行遗忘，缓解不一致性。

Result: 在CIFAR-100部分类别遗忘任务上，局部教师蒸馏使未学习模型在靠近遗忘集的区域显著更接近重训练模型，同时保持有竞争力的聚合遗忘指标。

Conclusion: 局部连带遗忘是机器遗忘中一个被聚合指标掩盖的重要问题，其根源在于替代目标与局部预测结构的不一致；局部教师蒸馏作为一种简单的缓解策略，能有效缩小与重训练模型的差距。

Abstract: Machine unlearning aims to remove the influence of selected training examples without full retraining. Standard evaluations often summarize unlearning quality with aggregate metrics, such as accuracy- and forgetting-based scores, which can hide localized failures. We study this failure mode at the example level by comparing the predictions of an unlearned model to those of the model retrained after deletion. We show that this pointwise discrepancy can be highly non-uniform: for gradient-ascent and random-labeling methods, with and without retain-set fine-tuning, it grows with geometric proximity to the forget set. We call this phenomenon localized collateral forgetting. Our analysis identifies a mechanism behind the effect: surrogate targets used during unlearning can be inconsistent with the local prediction structure induced by retraining, and this inconsistency propagates through shared representations to nearby examples. Motivated by this mechanism, we propose Local Teacher Distillation, a simple mitigation strategy that replaces random targets with soft labels from a small teacher trained only on retained neighbors of the forget set. On CIFAR-100 partial-class deletion, this local teacher brings the unlearned model substantially closer to retraining, especially near the forget set, while maintaining competitive aggregate unlearning metrics.

</details>


### [226] [Generalized Intention Modeling in Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31318)
*Mateusz Odrowaz-Sypniewski,Jasmine Bayrooti,Ajay Shankar,Amanda Prorok*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种任务自适应的对手建模框架，通过学习多种意图表示的绩效驱动混合，并引入一种与自我智能体未来回报互信息最大化的新意图表示，在多种任务中达到或超越现有最优方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对手建模方法预先选择特定信息（如对手下一动作或未来环境状态）作为意图嵌入，但这类信息并非普遍适用于所有任务，意图具有任务和环境依赖性，导致建模效果不稳定。

Method: 提出任务自适应对手建模框架，学习一种绩效驱动的多种意图表示的混合；同时引入一种新的意图表示，通过最大化与自我智能体未来回报的互信息来捕捉与性能最直接相关的对手信息。

Result: 在多种任务上一致达到或超越现有最优基线方法的性能，并揭示了不同对手建模策略何时以及为何成功。

Conclusion: 任务自适应的意图混合表示比单一预设意图更有效，且最大化与回报互信息的意图表示能更直接地提升决策性能。

Abstract: Modeling an opponent's intent is critical for effective decision-making in non-cooperative, competitive, and general-sum multi-agent reinforcement learning. Existing opponent modeling methods encode intent using an embedding derived from episode information chosen a priori, such as the opponent's next action or a future environment state, and use this to guide the ego-agent's behavior. These approaches assume that the chosen information is universally representative of intent; however, we show empirically that this is not the case as intentions are often task- and environment-dependent. To address this, we introduce a task-adaptive opponent modeling framework that learns a performance-driven mixture of multiple intent representations. We further introduce a new intention representation that maximizes mutual information with the ego-agent's future returns, thereby capturing opponent information that is most directly relevant to performance. Our approach consistently matches or exceeds the performance of state-of-the-art baselines across diverse tasks and yields insights into when and why different opponent modeling strategies succeed.

</details>


### [227] [Trading Complexity for Expressivity Through Structured Generalized Linear Token Mixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31367)
*Erwan Fagnou,Paul Caillon,Blaise Delattre,Alexandre Allauzen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一框架，将token混合层的两大核心特征——（i）输入对输出的直接单步影响和（ii）通过过去输出的循环信息传播——分离开来，从而设计出新的循环模式，在运行时与表达能力之间实现有原则的权衡，并通过合成任务和语言建模进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的token混合层（如注意力机制和状态空间模型）在解码速度和内存需求（包括缓存大小）之间存在必要的权衡。本文旨在探索新的权衡方案，通过统一框架分离直接影响和循环传播两个特征，设计更高效的token混合器。

Method: 提出一个统一框架，将token混合层分解为两个独立特征：直接输入-输出影响和通过过去输出的循环信息传播。该框架不仅涵盖注意力机制和状态空间模型等主流架构，还通过允许每个状态依赖多个过去状态（而非仅前一个）来泛化循环方程。基于此框架设计新的循环模式，并理论分析其表达能力与运行时的权衡。

Result: 新设计的循环模式在理论上实现了所需的复杂度，并在合成任务和语言建模上得到了实证验证。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个统一的工具包，用于理解和设计跨模型家族的高效且富有表现力的token混合器，能够在运行时和表达能力之间进行有原则的权衡。

Abstract: Token mixing layers play a key role in how language models can learn and generate long-range dependencies. Their efficiency relies on the necessary trade-off between decoding speed and the memory requirements, along with the cache size. Considering causal generation, this paper explores new trade-offs thanks to a unified framework which separates two crucial features: (i) the direct influence of inputs on outputs in one generation step; (ii) the recurrent propagation of information through past outputs. This framework encompasses major architectures such as attention and state-space models, but also generalizes the recurrence equations by allowing each state to depend on multiple past states rather than only the immediate predecessor. By introducing structure, we design new recurrence patterns that provably achieve the desired complexity, while providing theoretical insights on their expressivity -- trading runtime for expressivity in a principled way. Empirical validation is performed on synthetic tasks, along with language modeling. Together, these results provide a unified toolkit for the understanding and design of efficient and expressive token mixers across model families.

</details>


### [228] [A Unifying View of Variational Generative Wasserstein Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31369)
*Paul Caucheteux,Clément Bonet,Anna Korba*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了生成式 Wasserstein 流（GWF）的统一理论框架，将多种生成模型视为参数化 JKO 格式的实例，涵盖 f-散度、IPM 和平方 MMD，并建立了与 GAN 等方法的联系，同时通过实验分析了 JKO 正则化的影响以及参数化 Wasserstein 流的动力学特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代生成模型虽然都可视为最小化概率分布之间的散度，但依赖于不同的算法和几何原理，缺乏统一的理论框架来解释其内在联系与差异。

Method: 基于 Wasserstein 梯度流的连续时间优化视角，利用 Jordan-Kinderlehrer-Otto (JKO) 格式的隐式离散化，将生成模型建模为参数化 JKO 格式；将框架从 f-散度扩展到积分概率度量（IPM）和平方最大均值差异（MMD），推导出新的 JKO 生成算法。

Result: 证明了大量现有方法可推导为 f-散度目标下参数化 JKO 格式的实例，建立了多个近期提出算法之间的等价性；通过实验验证了 JKO 正则化对多种目标的实际影响；分析了限制在参数化映射诱导分布下的 Wasserstein 流动力学。

Conclusion: GWF 为生成模型提供了统一的理论视角，揭示了不同算法（包括 GAN）之间的内在联系，并为设计新的生成算法提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Many modern generative models can be viewed as minimizing divergences between probability distributions, yet they rely on different algorithmic and geometric principles. Wasserstein gradient flows provide a continuous-time formulation for optimizing over distributions, and can be approximated through their implicit discretization via the Jordan-Kinderlehrer-Otto (JKO) scheme. In this work, we present a unified theoretical framework for generative modeling based on Wasserstein gradient flows, which we refer to as Generative Wasserstein Flows (GWF). We show that a broad class of existing methods can be derived as instances of parametric JKO schemes for $f$-divergence objectives, and we establish equivalences between several recently proposed algorithms. We extend this framework beyond f-divergence to Integral Probability Metrics and squared Maximum Mean Discrepancy, deriving new JKO-based generative algorithms, and clarifying their connections with GANs. We study empirically the impact of the JKO regularization for a wide set of objectives. Finally, we analyze parametric Wasserstein flows, where the dynamics are restricted to distributions induced by parametrized maps.

</details>


### [229] [Softsign: Smooth Sign in Your Optimizer For Better Parameter Heterogeneity Handling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31371)
*Dmitrii Feoktistov,Timofey Belinsky,Andrey Veprikov,Amir Zainullin,Aleksandr Beznosikov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SoftSignum和SoftMuon，用平滑的soft-sign替代硬符号映射，结合自适应分位数温度调度，解决了符号优化器在终端收敛时的振荡问题，在多种深度学习任务（包括LLM预训练）中优于AdamW和硬符号方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于符号和LMO启发的优化器虽然内存占用低且性能强，但其固定幅度的更新机制在终端收敛阶段表现不佳：更新与梯度幅度解耦且未考虑参数异质性，导致振荡而非收敛。

Method: 提出SoftSignum，用温度可控的soft-sign变换替代hard sign，实现从符号式更新到幅值敏感的SGD式更新的逐参数平滑过渡；配合自适应分位数温度调度；将相同原理扩展到矩阵值优化器得到SoftMuon；基于强凸正则化子和Fenchel共轭开发了通用的几何松弛框架，并证明其在随机非凸设置下的收敛性。

Result: 在多种深度学习任务（包括LLM预训练）上，SoftSignum和SoftMuon一致优于对应的硬符号优化器以及标准AdamW。

Conclusion: 通过平滑松弛符号映射并引入自适应温度调度，可以有效解决符号优化器终端收敛振荡问题，几何松弛框架为这类方法提供了理论收敛保证。

Abstract: Sign-based and LMO-inspired optimizers have recently attracted substantial attention in deep learning due to their strong performance and low memory footprint. However, their fixed-magnitude updates can hurt terminal convergence: they decouple update mechanisms from gradient magnitudes and fail to account for parameter heterogeneity, often leading to oscillation rather than convergence. We propose SoftSignum, a smooth relaxation of sign-based optimization that replaces the hard sign map with a temperature-controlled soft-sign transformation, enabling a parameter-wise transition from sign-like updates to magnitude-sensitive SGD-like steps. We complement it with an adaptive quantile-based temperature schedule and extend the same principle to matrix-valued optimizers, obtaining SoftMuon. We also develop a generalized geometry-relaxation framework based on strongly convex regularizers and Fenchel conjugates, proving convergence in stochastic non-convex setting. Experiments on diverse deep learning tasks, including LLM pretraining, show that SoftSignum and SoftMuon consistently improve over their hard sign-based counterparts and standard AdamW.

</details>


### [230] [Constrained Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning with Max-Min Criterion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31388)
*Giseung Park,Hyunyoung Nam,Woohyeon Byeon,Amir Leshem,Youngchul Sung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合最大最小准则与显式约束满足的多目标强化学习框架，通过理论分析、收敛性证明及表格环境验证，并在建筑热控制、多目标运动控制和温室气体排放感知交通管理三个仿真领域展示了其在平衡公平性与约束满足方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最大最小MORL方法虽能促进公平性，但在需要约束满足的场景中适用性有限，因此需要一种既能优化多目标公平性又能显式处理约束的强化学习框架。

Method: 提出一个将最大最小准则与约束满足相集成的MORL框架，建立理论基础，并通过收敛性分析和表格设置下的实验验证算法有效性。

Result: 在模拟建筑热控制、多目标运动控制和温室气体排放感知交通管理这三个领域，该方法有效地平衡了公平性与约束满足。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架为约束条件下的公平多目标决策提供了可行的理论支撑与算法实现，具有广泛的应用潜力。

Abstract: Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning (MORL) extends standard RL by optimizing policies with respect to multiple, often conflicting, objectives. While max-min MORL has emerged as an effective approach for promoting fairness, its applicability remains limited, particularly when constraints must be incorporated. In this paper, we propose a MORL framework that integrates the max-min criterion with explicit constraint satisfaction. We establish a theoretical foundation for the proposed framework and validate the resulting algorithm through convergence analysis and experiments in tabular settings. We further demonstrate the practical relevance of our approach in simulated building thermal control, multi-objective locomotion control, and greenhouse-gas-emission-aware traffic management. Across these domains, our method effectively balances fairness and constraint satisfaction in multi-objective decision-making.

</details>


### [231] [Fixed Universal Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31423)
*Jingwen Liu,Alexandr Andoni,Daniel Hsu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出通用Transformer（Universal Transformers），即固定参数的Transformer可以通过适当的输入嵌入模拟给定类别中的任何Transformer，类似于通用图灵机。研究发现，当嵌入维度足够大时，存在显式的稀疏构造实现通用性，且随机初始化的Transformer几乎必然具有通用性。实验在括号平衡和多跳推理任务上验证了理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Transformer的表达能力是否主要来自于输入表示而非学习到的权重，类比通用图灵机的概念，研究是否存在固定参数的Transformer可以模拟任意其他Transformer。

Method: 提出通用Transformer框架，通过输入嵌入编码目标模型的描述，内部参数保持固定。提供显式稀疏构造证明通用性（当嵌入维度足够大时），并证明随机初始化的Transformer几乎必然具有通用性。在括号平衡和多跳推理任务上进行实证验证。

Result: 证明了通用Transformer的存在性：固定参数的Transformer可以模拟任意目标Transformer；随机初始化的Transformer几乎必然具有通用性；实证验证了理论在算法任务上的有效性。

Conclusion: Transformer的大部分表达能力可能 resides in 输入表示而非学习到的权重参数，这一发现对理解Transformer的泛化能力和设计更高效的模型具有重要启示。

Abstract: We introduce \emph{universal transformers}: fixed transformers that can simulate any transformer in a given class via a suitable input embedding. Analogous to a universal Turing machine, the input embedding encodes a description of the target model while all internal parameters remain fixed. We provide explicit sparse constructions achieving universality when the embedding dimension is sufficiently large, and further show that universality is generic: randomly initialized transformers are universal almost surely, which aligns with recent empirical results of Zhong and Andreas (2024). We empirically validate our theory on the algorithmic tasks of parenthesis balancing and multi-hop reasoning. Our results suggest that much of a transformer's expressive power may reside in its input representation rather than its learned weights.

</details>


### [232] [DG-CoLearn: An Efficient Collaborative Learning Framework for Dynamic Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31427)
*Ashley Hoi-Ting Au,Zikun Zhang,Ligang He,Qiang Ni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DG-CoLearn框架，通过增量图快照处理、客户端无感知的协作学习和服务器中介的嵌入交换机制，在保护跨客户端结构隐私的同时，实现高效、可扩展的协作式动态图学习，训练速度提升最高33.8倍，通信开销降低最高27.4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态图学习方法因重复全快照重训练导致计算开销大，且不适合数据分区的协作场景；跨分区边在真实图系统中不可避免，但直接共享图结构违反隐私约束。

Method: 提出DG-CoLearn，核心包括：增量图快照处理（仅计算受时间更新影响的图区域，通过时间建模保留历史信息）、客户端无感知的协作学习框架、服务器中介的嵌入交换机制（实现准确的多跳消息传递，无需暴露原始跨客户端结构信息）。

Result: 训练时间最高加速33.8倍，通信开销最高降低27.4倍；节点分类F1提升最高13.36%，链接预测MAP提升最高8.27%。

Conclusion: DG-CoLearn在协作动态图学习中有效桥接了效率、可扩展性和客户端间结构隐私保护。

Abstract: Dynamic graph learning (DGL) is essential for modelling evolving graph data, but existing methods suffer from significant computational overhead due to repeated full-snapshot retraining and are not well-suited for collaborative settings with partitioned data. In realistic graph systems, cross-partition edges are unavoidable, but direct sharing of graph structure between clients may violate privacy constraints. We propose DG-CoLearn, a client-oblivious collaborative dynamic graph learning framework built on incremental graph snapshot processing, which focuses computation on graph regions affected by temporal updates while preserving historical information through temporal modelling. This incremental design is consistently applied across the entire graph processing pipeline, including a server-mediated embedding exchange mechanism to enable accurate multi-hop message passing without exposing raw cross-client structural information. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DG-CoLearn achieves up to 33.8$\times$ speedup in training time and 27.4$\times$ reduction in communication overhead, while consistently improving predictive performance on both node classification (up to 13.36% F1 improvement) and link prediction (up to 8.27% MAP improvement) tasks. These results highlight the effectiveness of DG-CoLearn in bridging efficiency, scalability, and client-to-client structural privacy in collaborative dynamic graph learning.

</details>


### [233] [Flow map learning in nonlinear vector autoregressive models: influence of the feature-library structure on the training error](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31438)
*Markus Gross*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了非线性向量自回归（NVAR/NG-RC）模型在时间序列预测中的可辨识性问题，揭示了训练误差随时间分辨率变化的（前）渐近标度律，并指出当特征库与真实动力学模型不匹配时，小训练误差与弱泛化能力并存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列预测通常需要学习非线性和时滞依赖关系，而NVAR/NG-RC模型作为一类重要模型，其可辨识性问题尚未被充分理解——即模型能否从有限数据中正确识别出真实的非线性动力学系统。

Method: 通过理论分析，推导了训练误差作为时间分辨率函数的特征标度律，该定律取决于特征库能否精确表示流映射（传播子）的李级数早期系数。针对多项式向量场驱动的动力系统，使用单项式和傅里叶特征库在NVAR/NG-RC模型上进行验证，并分析了时间分辨率、非线性度、延迟项数对误差的影响。

Result: 延迟项可以降低最优单步训练误差，但只有当特征库提供足够非线性度时才能改善长时程预测精度。当模型类与真实数据生成过程不匹配时，小训练误差与弱泛化能力同时存在。多种混沌动力系统的数值实验验证了理论预测。

Conclusion: 特征库的表示能力是决定NVAR/NG-RC模型泛化性能的关键因素，仅凭训练误差小不足以保证良好的长期预测能力，必须确保模型类与真实动力学结构匹配。

Abstract: Time series forecasting often requires learning nonlinear and time-delayed dependencies. A paradigmatic class of forecasting models are nonlinear vector autoregressive processes (NVAR), also known as next-generation reservoir computers (NG-RCs). These models approximate the Koopman operator on the space spanned by their explicit feature library. We consider the identifiability problem for learning Markovian nonlinear dynamical systems and show that the training error as a function of time resolution follows characteristic (pre-)asymptotic scaling laws. These laws depend on whether the feature library can represent the early Lie-series coefficients of the flow map (propagator) exactly or merely approximately. For dynamical systems governed by polynomial vector fields, we demonstrate the mechanism for NVAR/NG-RC models with monomial and Fourier feature libraries. We determine the dependence of the training error on the temporal resolution, the involved nonlinear degree, and the number of delay terms. While delay terms reduce the optimal one-step training error, they improve long-horizon forecasts only when the library provides sufficient nonlinearity. Thus, small training error coexists with weak generalization as the model class is mismatched to the true data-generating process. Numerical experiments on various chaotic dynamical systems confirm the theoretical predictions.

</details>


### [234] [DRIFT: Decoupled Rollouts and Importance-Weighted Fine-Tuning for Efficient Multi-Turn Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31455)
*Jian Mu,Tianyi Lin,Chengwei Qin,Zhongxiang Dai,Yao Shu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DRIFT框架通过将KL正则化RL目标重写为重要性加权监督学习，将离线交互轨迹与固定参考策略解耦，以SFT的效率实现在线RL的多轮优化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线强化学习能有效处理多轮交互动态，但生成完整修正轨迹成本过高；离线SFT效率高但存在分布偏移和行为崩溃问题。现有方法在效率与效果之间存在尖锐矛盾。

Method: DRIFT将KL正则化RL目标等价转化为重要性加权监督学习；从固定参考策略离线采样交互轨迹；基于回报计算重要性权重；通过加权SFT优化策略。

Result: DRIFT在性能上匹配或超越多轮强化学习基线，同时保持了标准SFT的训练效率和简洁性。

Conclusion: DRIFT成功弥合了在线RL与离线SFT之间的效率-效果差距，提供了一种兼具在线RL多轮动态处理能力与SFT高效性的实用框架。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed in multi-turn interactive settings where users or environments can iteratively provide lightweight feedback. Unfortunately, optimizing such behavior presents a sharp dilemma in practice: online reinforcement learning is able to effectively address multi-turn dynamics but is prohibitively expensive due to the cost of generating full correction trajectories at every update, whereas offline supervised fine-tuning (SFT) is efficient but suffers from distribution shift and behavioral collapse. To this end, we novelly propose DRIFT (Decoupled Rollouts and Importance-Weighted Fine-Tuning), a framework that operationalizes the theoretical insight that the KL-regularized RL objective is equivalent to importance-weighted supervised learning. DRIFT decouples rollout from optimization by sampling offline interaction trajectories from a fixed reference policy, deriving return-based importance weights, and optimizing the policy via weighted SFT on the resulting dataset. Empirically, we demonstrate that DRIFT matches or exceeds the performance of multi-turn reinforcement learning baselines while maintaining the training efficiency and simplicity of standard supervised fine-tuning. Code is available at https://github.com/2020-qqtcg/DRIFT.

</details>


### [235] [Balanced LoRA: Removing Parameter Invariance to Accelerate Convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31484)
*Valérie Castin,Kimia Nadjahi,Pierre Ablin,Gabriel Peyré*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现LoRA存在过参数化问题，不同低秩因子对的条件数差异大，影响收敛速度。为此提出BaLoRA，通过将迭代投影到平衡流形上改善损失景观的条件数，在计算开销很小的前提下实现更快的收敛和更好的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA虽然广泛应用但存在过参数化问题：多个低秩因子对可得到相同的适配权重矩阵，但这些对的 condition number 差异显著，导致收敛到不同损失极小值时收敛速度不同。

Method: 提出BaLoRA，通过将迭代投影到平衡流形（balanced manifold）上，在保持适配矩阵不变的同时改善损失景观的条件数。该投影步骤计算量轻量，可无缝集成到现有微调流程中。

Result: 理论和实验均证明：不同低秩因子对的条件数差异大，影响收敛率；BaLoRA比标准LoRA收敛更快，在多种微调任务上性能更优。

Conclusion: BaLoRA通过平衡流形投影有效改善了LoRA的收敛特性，是一种计算高效且性能更优的LoRA变体。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) is the most widely adopted method for fine-tuning large language models. Notably, LoRA is inherently overparameterized: multiple pairs of low-rank factors can yield the same adapted weight matrix. We show--both theoretically and empirically--that these pairs exhibit significantly different condition numbers. As a result, converging to different loss minimizers directly impacts the convergence rate of LoRA. Building on this observation, we introduce Balanced Low-Rank Adaptation (BaLoRA), a variant of LoRA that projects iterates onto a balanced manifold. This manifold improves the conditioning of the loss landscape while preserving the adapted matrix. The projection step is computationally lightweight and integrates seamlessly into existing fine-tuning pipelines. Empirically, BaLoRA converges faster than standard LoRA and achieves superior performance across a range of fine-tuning tasks.

</details>


### [236] [Graphical einops: bridging tensor networks and computation graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31485)
*Vincent Wang-Maścianica,Nikhil Khatri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种形式化的图形演算系统，用于einops风格的张量编程结构片段，将架构图从纯表征性工具转变为可证明的推理工具。该演算通过嵌套分级管表示张量轴，核心规则是分级自然性（滑动眼镜），可实现标准等变性的简短图解推导，并能将注意力掩码转换为预处理操作，恢复稀疏注意力块的高效实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习中的架构图通常只是表征性的，其蕴含的张量程序恒等式仍依赖文字描述和张量轴操作来证明，缺乏形式化的证明工具。

Method: 提出一种形式化图形演算：将张量轴表示为嵌套在基础类型周围的分级管（graded tubes），管边界恢复无向张量网络视图，管内保留计算图的操作性读取；核心重写规则为"分级自然性"（滑动眼镜）。

Result: 标准等变性证明可简化为简短的图解推导；该重写系统可将注意力掩码转换为预处理操作，从而恢复稀疏注意力块的高效实现。

Conclusion: 该图形演算使架构图具有证明能力，为einops风格的张量编程提供了形式化基础，并在注意力机制优化中展示了实际应用价值。

Abstract: Architecture diagrams are ubiquitous in deep learning, but they are usually only representational: the tensor-program identities they suggest are still proved by prose and tensor-axis manipulation. We introduce a formal graphical calculus for the structural fragment of tensor programming underlying einops, making such diagrams proof-enabling. Our calculus represents tensor axes as nested graded tubes around a base type. The tube boundary recovers the undirected tensor-network view of axes, while the directed interior retains the operational reading of computation graphs. The key rewrite is grade-naturality: sliding spectacles over tubes. Standard equivariance proofs become short diagrammatic derivations. We additionally demonstrate how our rewrite system may be applied to convert attention masks into pre-processing operations, recovering efficient implementations of sparse attention blocks.

</details>


### [237] [Scalable Inference-Time Annealing with Surrogate Likelihood Estimators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31498)
*Daniel Peñaherrera,Rishal Aggarwal,David Ryan Koes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SITA（可扩展推理时退火）方法，通过能量模型构建快速代理似然，对基于流的生成模型进行迭代微调，以逐步在更低温度下采样分子构象，避免了传统方法中昂贵的散度计算开销，在丙氨酸二肽和三肽上取得了最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算化学中高效采样玻尔兹曼分布一直是长期挑战，现有扩散模型结合温度退火重要性采样方法需要计算分数场的散度来估计重要性权重，对大规模系统计算不可行。

Method: 提出SITA方法，使用基于能量的模型构建快速代理似然，在推理时退火过程中对基于流的生成模型进行迭代微调，逐步从高温到低温生成样本，避免了直接计算分数场散度。

Result: 在丙氨酸二肽和丙氨酸三肽体系上取得最优性能，同时避免了昂贵的散度计算项。

Conclusion: SITA方法通过能量模型代理似然替代散度计算，实现了对较大分子体系的高效玻尔兹曼采样。

Abstract: A long standing challenge in computational chemistry and biophysics is efficiently sampling the Boltzmann distribution of molecules. Advances in generative modeling have been proposed to address the limitations of conventional sampling techniques by eliminating the computational cost of simulation. A promising direction is iteratively finetuning diffusion models along a temperature ladder whereby training data is generated via importance sampling during inference-time annealing. Unfortunately, these methods require computing a divergence over the score field to estimate importance weights, rendering them intractable for larger systems. Here we present scalable inference-time annealing (SITA), which retrains flow-based models to generate samples at progressively lower temperatures using an energy-based model to facilitate fast surrogate likelihoods. We demonstrate state-of-the-art performance on both Alanine Dipeptide and Alanine Tripeptide while avoiding costly divergence terms. Our code is available at: https://github.com/countrsignal/sita.git

</details>


### [238] [On the Relationship Between Activation Outliers and Feature Death in Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31518)
*Elana Simon,Etowah Adams,James Zou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 稀疏自编码器（SAE）在分解神经网络激活时存在特征死亡问题，研究发现激活中的维度级离群值（由激活均值与每token变异之比γ衡量）是导致初始化时特征死亡的根本原因。均中心化预处理可消除该问题，为SAE训练提供了理论依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏自编码器在分解神经网络激活时，许多学习到的特征从不激活（特征死亡），这浪费了字典容量并可能重新引入叠加问题。不同模型间特征死亡率差异巨大（GPT-2接近0%，AlphaFold3超过70%），其根本原因尚不明确，需要系统性的分析和解决方案。

Method: 1) 发现维度级激活离群值（维度平均幅度远大于每token变化）导致特征死亡，机制是特征与激活均值的对齐程度影响初始化时预激活值。2) 形式化离群值严重程度为γ = ‖μ‖/‖σ‖，并在454个模型-层组合上验证其预测初始死亡率的能力。3) 提出均中心化预处理方法消除离群值引起的特征死亡。

Result: γ能有效预测初始特征死亡率（Spearman ρ: dead-by-TopK为0.89，dead-by-ReLU为0.82）。死特征可在训练中复活，但需要SAE偏置学习激活均值，在高γ下该过程极其缓慢。均中心化消除了所有测试模型中的离群值诱导死亡。

Conclusion: 维度级激活离群值是导致SAE特征死亡的核心机制，均中心化是解决该问题的有效预处理手段，为SAE训练中何时以及为何需要该预处理提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) decompose neural network activations into interpretable features, but many learned features never activate, a problem called feature death that wastes dictionary capacity and can reintroduce superposition. Death rates vary dramatically between models: near-zero on GPT-2, over 70% on AlphaFold3 with identical configurations. We find that dimension-level activation outliers (dimensions whose mean magnitude is large relative to per-token variation) cause this by shifting pre-activations at initialization based on each feature's alignment with the activation mean. Features anti-aligned with the mean receive permanently negative pre-activations and never fire. We formalize outlier severity as $γ= \|μ\|/\|σ\|$; it predicts initial death rates (Spearman $ρ= 0.89$ for dead-by-TopK, $0.82$ for dead-by-ReLU) across 454 model-layer combinations spanning language, vision, protein, and genomic models. Dead features can revive during training, but recovery requires the SAE bias to learn the activation mean, a process that is prohibitively slow at high $γ$. Mean-centering (subtracting the activation mean) sidesteps this and eliminates outlier-induced death across all tested models, confirming the mechanism and providing a principled basis for when and why this preprocessing step is necessary.

</details>


### [239] [Chem-PerturBridge: a harmonized compendium of small molecule perturbation transcriptomic effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31522)
*Artur Szałata,Olga Novitskaia,Maiia Shulman,Matthew Mella,Altynbek Zhubanchaliyev,Fabian J. Theis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Chem-PerturBridge是一个包含37k+化合物、136个细胞背景、125万转录组样本的大型多数据集资源。研究发现跨数据集间同化合物的logFC排序和幅度一致性较弱，但方向一致性较好。该资源作为预训练数据能提升化合物表示学习的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有小分子转录组资源分散在不同技术、元数据标准、对照、剂量和预处理流程中，缺乏统一的大规模资源来支持大型扰动模型的训练。

Method: 构建Chem-PerturBridge资源，统一8种检测类型的数据集，标准化标识符和元数据，计算重复感知的条件级效应。然后评估匹配条件跨数据集的一致性和同数据集内重复一致性，并作为预训练资源评估化合物表示学习性能。

Result: 跨数据集同化合物条件的logFC排序和幅度一致性弱，但方向一致性稳定。Chem-PerturBridge预训练嵌入在化合物保留的OP3评估和11个数据集的分子保留评估中均优于L1000-only嵌入、Morgan指纹和无描述符基线。

Conclusion: Chem-PerturBridge既支持跨数据集签名一致性的诊断评估，也支持异质性扰动转录组数据的模型导向复用。

Abstract: Large perturbation models require training data encompassing chemical, cellular, and assay diversity. Current transcriptomic resources for small-molecule modeling, however, are fragmented across technologies, metadata conventions, controls, doses, and preprocessing pipelines. We introduce Chem-PerturBridge, a harmonized multi-dataset resource comprising over 37k compounds, 136 cellular contexts, and 1.25M transcriptomic samples across eight assay types, with standardized identifiers, metadata, and replicate-aware condition-level effects. We use the resource to evaluate matched-condition agreement across datasets and replicate agreement within datasets. Matched same-compound conditions generally show weak agreement in fine-grained logFC rankings and magnitudes across most dataset pairs, often falling below same-context different-compound baselines. In contrast, logFC direction agreement is substantially more stable and usually exceeds these baselines. We further evaluate Chem-PerturBridge as a pretraining resource for compound representation learning. Under a compound-held-out OP3 evaluation split, embeddings pretrained on Chem-PerturBridge improve over L1000-only embeddings, Morgan fingerprints, and the descriptor-free OP3 baseline across metrics. An extensive molecule-holdout evaluation across 11 datasets further shows that models trained on Chem-PerturBridge outperform or match those that are not. Chem-PerturBridge therefore supports both diagnostic evaluation of cross-dataset signature agreement and model-oriented reuse of heterogeneous perturbation transcriptomic data.

</details>


### [240] [Value Functions as Supermartingale Certificates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31524)
*Alessandro Abate,Daniel Contro,Mirco Giacobbe,Agustín Martínez-Suñé,Diptarko Roy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了随机系统认证方法与强化学习之间的理论联系，证明在适当奖励下，几乎必然满足ω-正则属性的策略的价值函数编码了Streett超鞅证书，为通过强化学习进行证书合成提供了原则性途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 认证方法提供形式化保证但难以自动化，而强化学习方法虽能处理ω-正则任务但缺乏形式化保证，尤其是在无限状态空间上。本文旨在弥合这两条研究线之间的差距。

Method: 建立理论联系：证明在适当奖励函数下，几乎必然满足ω-正则属性的策略的价值函数编码了一个Streett超鞅证书。该结果在有限、可数无限和连续状态空间上均成立。

Result: 通过有限马尔可夫决策过程上的实验验证了理论结果，表明价值函数确实编码了Streett超鞅证书，从而为通过RL进行证书合成提供了可行性证据。

Conclusion: 该理论联系为通过强化学习合成形式化证书提供了原则性路线，使RL方法可以获得与认证方法相当的形式化保证。
